              '// the finalize worker can persist them — stderr is lost in detached device\n'
              '// runs. Requires a libglomap_core.a built from the same source state.\n'
              'namespace colmap {\n'
              'void AetherLastBaSolveInfo(std::string* solver_used,\n'
              '                           std::string* sparse_backend,\n'
              '                           int* mixed,\n'
              '                           int* threads,\n'
              '                           std::string* dense_backend);\n'
              '}  // namespace colmap\n'
              '\n'
              'namespace {\n'
              '\n'
              '// Production contract: the shipping mobile route ends at COLMAP\'s final global\n'
              '// BA and official point filtering. The historical PocketWorld repair/detail\n'
              '// passes remain in this translation unit only for reproducibility of old\n'
              '// experiments; production must not execute them, even if a stale environment\n'
              '// variable attempts to re-enable one.\n'
              'constexpr bool kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly = true;\n'
              '\n'
              'double NowMs() {\n'
              '  using namespace std::chrono;\n'
              '  return duration<double, std::milli>(steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch())\n'
              '      .count();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              'uint64_t NewDescriptorResidencySessionNonce() {\n'
              '  static std::atomic<uint64_t> next{1};\n'
              '  uint64_t nonce = next.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '  if (nonce == 0) nonce = next.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '  return nonce;\n'
              '}\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '\n'
              '// Per-frame record kept on the session for the streaming surface so we can\n'
              '// match a new frame against its k_neighbors candidates without re-reading the\n'
              '// db (candidates are spatial-first — see SelectStreamCandidates).\n'
              'struct FrameRecord {\n'
              '  int frame_id = -1;\n'
              '  colmap::image_t image_id = 0;\n'
              '  // Immutable DB identity retained even when remove_frame marks image_id=0 to\n'
              '  // withdraw the frame from all live candidate/reconstruction paths.\n'
              '  colmap::image_t persisted_image_id = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t frame_identity_digest = 0;\n'
              '  // The official route stores one calibrated PINHOLE camera per captured\n'
              '  // image. ARKit reports this calibration for the same 12 MP ARFrame as the\n'
              '  // JPEG, so autofocus-induced changes remain attached to their own image.\n'
              '  colmap::camera_t camera_id = colmap::kInvalidCameraId;\n'
              '  colmap::Camera camera;\n'
              '  int n_keypoints = 0;\n'
              '  std::vector<uint8_t> descriptors;  // 128 * n_keypoints, RootSIFT\n'
              '  // Keypoint positions (pixel coords at the fed resolution), kept so the\n'
              '  // two-view geometry of every new pair can be estimated without a sqlite\n'
              '  // read-back. ~1.5k pts × 16 B ≈ 24 KB/frame — negligible.\n'
              '  std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> points;\n'
              '  // ARKit CamFromWorld for this frame, ALREADY flipped into COLMAP camera axes\n'
              '  // (C = diag(1,-1,-1)); used ONLY by the throwaway live-preview triangulation.\n'
              '  colmap::Rigid3d cam_from_world;\n'
              '  // Normalized physical-gravity direction in COLMAP camera coordinates.\n'
              '  Eigen::Vector3d gravity_cam = Eigen::Vector3d::Zero();\n'
              '  bool has_pose = false;\n'
              '  // [THERMAL-THROTTLE 2026-07-11] True when this frame was matched against a\n'
              '  // REDUCED candidate window (thermal serious → live K 12→6, GPU relief for\n'
              '  // the camera pipeline). FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames treats such frames as\n'
              '  // starved so the full temporal-K pair topology is restored at finish time\n'
              '  // (cooler device, no 2 s/frame budget) — delivered quality is unchanged by\n'
              '  // construction, only capture-time pacing differs.\n'
              '  bool fed_throttled = false;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'std::array<double, 3> FrameGravityArray(const FrameRecord& frame) {\n'
              '  return {frame.gravity_cam.x(), frame.gravity_cam.y(),\n'
              '          frame.gravity_cam.z()};\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'uint64_t FrameIdentityDigestV1(\n'
              '    const std::string& name,\n'
              '    const colmap::Camera& camera,\n'
              '    const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points,\n'
              '    const std::vector<uint8_t>& descriptors) {\n'
              '  uint64_t hash = UINT64_C(1469598103934665603);\n'
              '  const auto mix_byte = [&hash](const uint8_t byte) {\n'
              '    hash ^= byte;\n'
              '    hash *= UINT64_C(1099511628211);\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  const auto mix_u64 = [&mix_byte](const uint64_t value) {\n'
              '    for (int shift = 0; shift < 64; shift += 8) {\n'
              '      mix_byte(static_cast<uint8_t>((value >> shift) & 0xff));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  const auto mix_double = [&mix_u64](const double value) {\n'
              '    uint64_t bits = 0;\n'
              '    static_assert(sizeof(bits) == sizeof(value));\n'
              '    std::memcpy(&bits, &value, sizeof(bits));\n'
              '    mix_u64(bits);\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  for (const char ch : name) mix_byte(static_cast<uint8_t>(ch));\n'
              '  mix_byte(0);\n'
              '  mix_u64(static_cast<uint64_t>(camera.model_id));\n'
              '  mix_u64(camera.width);\n'
              '  mix_u64(camera.height);\n'
              '  mix_u64(camera.params.size());\n'
              '  for (const double value : camera.params) mix_double(value);\n'
              '  mix_u64(points.size());\n'
              '  for (const Eigen::Vector2d& point : points) {\n'
              '    mix_double(point.x());\n'
              '    mix_double(point.y());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  mix_u64(descriptors.size());\n'
              '  for (const uint8_t value : descriptors) mix_byte(value);\n'
              '  return hash == 0 ? 1 : hash;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewResultV1\n'
              'EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& frame1,\n'
              '    const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points1,\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& frame2,\n'
              '    const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points2,\n'
              '    const colmap::FeatureMatches& matches,\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions& options) {\n'
              '  if (!frame1.has_pose || !frame2.has_pose) {\n'
              '    aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewResultV1 result;\n'
              '    result.status =\n'
              '        aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewStatusV1::kInvalidInput;\n'
              '    result.geometry.config = colmap::TwoViewGeometry::DEGENERATE;\n'
              '    return result;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return aether::sfm::EstimateMandatoryGravityTwoViewGeometryV1(\n'
              '      frame1.camera, points1, FrameGravityArray(frame1), frame2.camera,\n'
              '      points2, FrameGravityArray(frame2), matches, options);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(\n'
              '    const aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewResultV1& result) {\n'
              '  return result.status == aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewStatusV1::kValid ||\n'
              '         result.status == aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewStatusV1::kPlanar;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'struct PointIdPair {\n'
              '  colmap::point3D_t a = 0;\n'
              '  colmap::point3D_t b = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool operator==(const PointIdPair& other) const {\n'
              '    return a == other.a && b == other.b;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'struct PointIdPairHash {\n'
              '  size_t operator()(const PointIdPair& p) const {\n'
              '    const size_t h1 = std::hash<colmap::point3D_t>{}(p.a);\n'
              '    const size_t h2 = std::hash<colmap::point3D_t>{}(p.b);\n'
              '    return h1 ^ (h2 + 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL + (h1 << 6) + (h1 >> 2));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'PointIdPair CanonicalPointPair(colmap::point3D_t p1, colmap::point3D_t p2) {\n'
              '  return p1 < p2 ? PointIdPair{p1, p2} : PointIdPair{p2, p1};\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool TracksHaveDisjointImages(const colmap::Track& t1,\n'
              '                              const colmap::Track& t2) {\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::image_t> images;\n'
              '  images.reserve(t1.Length() + t2.Length());\n'
              '  for (const auto& el : t1.Elements()) {\n'
              '    if (!images.insert(el.image_id).second) return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  for (const auto& el : t2.Elements()) {\n'
              '    if (!images.insert(el.image_id).second) return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool ReprojectsCleanlyToTrack(const colmap::Reconstruction& recon,\n'
              '                              const colmap::Track& track,\n'
              '                              const Eigen::Vector3d& xyz,\n'
              '                              double max_reproj_px) {\n'
              '  for (const auto& el : track.Elements()) {\n'
              '    if (!recon.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) return false;\n'
              '    const colmap::Image& image = recon.Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '    if (!image.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= image.NumPoints2D()) return false;\n'
              '    const colmap::Camera* camera = image.CameraPtr();\n'
              '    if (!camera) return false;\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam = image.CamFromWorld() * xyz;\n'
              '    if (x_cam.z() <= 0.0) return false;\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> px = camera->ImgFromCam(x_cam);\n'
              '    if (!px) return false;\n'
              '    if ((*px - image.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy).norm() > max_reproj_px) {\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Why a live merge attempt was rejected — surfaced via aether_sfm_live_diag so\n'
              '// the acceptance rate is attributable (the 2026-07-10 device run accepted\n'
              '// 3/13021 merge-needed signals and the reason was invisible).\n'
              'enum class MergeReject {\n'
              '  kNone = 0,        // merge is acceptable\n'
              '  kMissing,         // point deleted / empty track / broken obs (defensive)\n'
              '  kSharedImage,     // both tracks observe the same image (opt-in gate, off)\n'
              '  kReproj,          // union refit fails cheirality/reproj on some observation\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              '// [MERGE-GATE 2026-07-11] Rewritten around a UNION-TRACK REFIT. The original\n'
              '// gate reprojected the length-weighted AVERAGE of the two current positions\n'
              '// (COLMAP\'s IncrementalTriangulator::Merge recipe) — correct in the mapper,\n'
              '// where both fragments are already optimized against consistent poses, but\n'
              '// nearly always wrong here: live fragments are independent low-parallax\n'
              '// 2-view triangulations under ARKit-seeded, windowed-BA poses, so their depth\n'
              '// error is large and the midpoint reprojects off BOTH tracks. Host replay of\n'
              '// the cap47 device db attributed 98% of merge rejections to exactly that\n'
              '// midpoint-reproj failure (12,892/13,142; the once-suspected disjoint-images\n'
              '// gate accounted for only 250). Refitting one DLT triangulation over the\n'
              '// UNION of observations uses the merged track\'s full (wider) baseline — the\n'
              '// very benefit the merge exists to unlock — and the acceptance test stays as\n'
              '// strict as before: EVERY observation of BOTH tracks must be in front of its\n'
              '// camera and reproject within max_reproj_px of the REFIT position. Two\n'
              '// physically-distinct points still cannot pass (no single 3D point fits both\n'
              '// tracks\' rays within the gate). On success *out_refit_xyz carries the fitted\n'
              '// position for the caller to install on the merged point.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// The disjoint-images requirement is OPT-IN and OFF at the call site: COLMAP\'s\n'
              '// merge has no such requirement (Reconstruction::MergePoints3D concatenates\n'
              '// tracks that share images without complaint), and duplicate fragments of the\n'
              '// same physical point routinely co-observe an image (DSP-SIFT emits\n'
              '// near-identical keypoints at several scales, each seeding its own track).\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A ③ 2026-07-11] Enrichment-targeting hint: the delivered model\'s\n'
              '// 2-view / low-parallax (θ_max < 3°) tracks, snapshotted from the live recon\n'
              '// BEFORE the finalize enrichment thread starts (the refine worker mutates the\n'
              '// model concurrently under the overlap schedule, so the enrichment pass must\n'
              '// never read the model directly). Consumed by AddSpatialRevisitMatches\' top-up\n'
              '// ordering when OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TARGETED=1: pairs are ranked by how many of\n'
              '// these tracks the pair can hand a ≥5°-parallax third observation — the exact\n'
              '// upgrade the TrackUpgrade pass then banks — instead of by camera-center\n'
              '// proximity (which is blind to WHY the extra pair is wanted).\n'
              'struct EnrichTargetHint {\n'
              '  struct LowTrack {\n'
              '    Eigen::Vector3d xyz;          // current (pre-refine) triangulated position\n'
              '    std::vector<int> frame_idxs;  // observing frames (indices into s->frames)\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  std::vector<LowTrack> tracks;\n'
              '  std::vector<std::vector<int32_t>> by_frame;  // frame idx -> track ids\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'MergeReject CanMergeLivePoints(const colmap::Reconstruction& recon,\n'
              '                               colmap::point3D_t pid1,\n'
              '                               colmap::point3D_t pid2,\n'
              '                               double max_reproj_px,\n'
              '                               bool require_disjoint_images,\n'
              '                               Eigen::Vector3d* out_refit_xyz) {\n'
              '  if (pid1 == pid2) return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '  if (!recon.ExistsPoint3D(pid1) || !recon.ExistsPoint3D(pid2)) {\n'
              '    return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const colmap::Point3D& p1 = recon.Point3D(pid1);\n'
              '  const colmap::Point3D& p2 = recon.Point3D(pid2);\n'
              '  if (p1.track.Length() == 0 || p2.track.Length() == 0) {\n'
              '    return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (require_disjoint_images &&\n'
              '      !TracksHaveDisjointImages(p1.track, p2.track)) {\n'
              '    return MergeReject::kSharedImage;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // DLT refit over the union of observations.\n'
              '  std::vector<Eigen::Matrix3x4d> cams_from_world;\n'
              '  std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_points;\n'
              '  cams_from_world.reserve(p1.track.Length() + p2.track.Length());\n'
              '  cam_points.reserve(p1.track.Length() + p2.track.Length());\n'
              '  for (const colmap::Track* track : {&p1.track, &p2.track}) {\n'
              '    for (const auto& el : track->Elements()) {\n'
              '      if (!recon.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& image = recon.Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!image.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= image.NumPoints2D()) {\n'
              '        return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera* camera = image.CameraPtr();\n'
              '      if (!camera) return MergeReject::kMissing;\n'
              '      const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> np =\n'
              '          camera->CamFromImg(image.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy);\n'
              '      if (!np) return MergeReject::kReproj;\n'
              '      cams_from_world.push_back(image.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix());\n'
              '      cam_points.push_back(*np);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  Eigen::Vector3d refit_xyz;\n'
              '  if (!colmap::TriangulateMultiViewPoint(\n'
              '          colmap::span<const Eigen::Matrix3x4d>(cams_from_world.data(),\n'
              '                                                cams_from_world.size()),\n'
              '          colmap::span<const Eigen::Vector2d>(cam_points.data(),\n'
              '                                              cam_points.size()),\n'
              '          &refit_xyz)) {\n'
              '    return MergeReject::kReproj;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const bool ok =\n'
              '      ReprojectsCleanlyToTrack(recon, p1.track, refit_xyz, max_reproj_px) &&\n'
              '      ReprojectsCleanlyToTrack(recon, p2.track, refit_xyz, max_reproj_px);\n'
              '  if (!ok) return MergeReject::kReproj;\n'
              '  if (out_refit_xyz) *out_refit_xyz = refit_xyz;\n'
              '  return MergeReject::kNone;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] One repaid probe-debt pair, parked until the\n'
              '// live model can be grown from it safely. The repay legs ([PROBE-DEBT]) run\n'
              '// either on the capture worker (idle repay) or on the finalize ENRICHMENT\n'
              '// thread, and neither may touch live_recon: the model is being read/mutated\n'
              '// by the streaming local BA resp. the stage-1 refinement at that moment. So\n'
              '// the pair\'s verified TVG inliers are parked here and consumed once, after\n'
              '// the enrichment join, when the finalize worker is the model\'s sole owner.\n'
              '// Empty whenever the probe gate is off → every hook is a no-op.\n'
              'struct ProbeDebtGrowPair {\n'
              '  int j = 0;  // frame index (earlier)\n'
              '  int f = 0;  // frame index (later)\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches inliers;  // TVG-verified inliers, as persisted\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              '}  // namespace\n'
              '\n'
              'struct aether_sfm_session {\n'
              '  aether_sfm_options_t options{};\n'
              '  std::string db_path;\n'
              '  std::string image_path;\n'
              '  bool owns_db_file = false;  // streaming sessions delete their temp db on free\n'
              '  uint64_t descriptor_residency_nonce = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Streaming state (NULL for pure-batch sessions until finalize fills recon).\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::Database> db;\n'
              '  // [TAIL-CACHE-FIRST V1 2026-08-01] Default-off mutable master cache. It is\n'
              '  // never a DatabaseCache::Create result (that graph is finalized). The epoch\n'
              '  // policy owns fail-closed identity; this field owns COLMAP objects only.\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> tail_cache_master;\n'
              '  aether::sfm::TailCacheEpochV1 tail_cache_epoch;\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::image_pair_t> tail_cache_seen_db_pairs;\n'
              '  std::mutex tail_cache_mutex;\n'
              '  std::vector<FrameRecord> frames;\n'
              '  // [VISUAL-LOOP V1 2026-08-03] Portable P10/L4 retrieval state. This owns\n'
              '  // only bounded descriptor sketches and candidate scores; exact matching and\n'
              '  // COLMAP TVG below remain the sole graph-edge authority.\n'
              '  aether::sfm::PortableVisualLoopIndexV1 visual_loop_index;\n'
              '  aether_preclamp_instr_v1::SessionRecords preclamp_instr_records;\n'
              '  // Non-zero once at least one per-image camera has been written. Geometry\n'
              '  // never reads this ID; each FrameRecord owns the camera for its image.\n'
              '  colmap::camera_t camera_id = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Rough live-preview cloud (throwaway): triangulated during capture from the\n'
              '  // per-frame matches + ARKit poses. World frame = ARKit world. The finalize\n'
              '  // pipeline is unchanged and still yields the authoritative model.\n'
              '  std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> preview_points;\n'
              '  std::mutex preview_mutex;  // add_frame writer vs. get_preview_points reader\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ── Streaming live local-BA preview (replaces the raw-triangulation cloud) ──\n'
              '  // Incrementally grown Reconstruction: one calibrated camera+trivial rig per\n'
              '  // image, one registered image per posed frame (ARKit pose), tracks grown from the\n'
              '  // cross-checked matches, refined by COLMAP\'s upstream six-image local\n'
              '  // refinement after each successfully registered frame.\n'
              '  // Separate from `recon` (finalize output); owned solely by add_frame (worker).\n'
              '  //\n'
              '  // [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY 2026-07-11] Held via shared_ptr so finalize_async can\n'
              '  // MOVE the pointer into the refine worker instead of deep-copying the whole\n'
              '  // model (colmap::Reconstruction has a user-defined copy ctor and NO move —\n'
              '  // std::move on a by-value member would silently copy; transferring the\n'
              '  // shared_ptr relocates nothing, so the Images\' internal camera pointers stay\n'
              '  // valid). After the move live_recon is nullptr and live_recon_ready=false:\n'
              '  // every reader gates on the flag (and defensively on the pointer).\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> live_recon;\n'
              '  bool live_recon_ready = false;                                // camera+rig added\n'
              '  // [LIVE-CLOUD-SNAPSHOT-DIAG V2] Telemetry-only prior snapshot. Exact COLMAP\n'
              '  // point IDs never cross the public ABI, so the comparison lives beside the\n'
              '  // native model and is never read by reconstruction or display code.\n'
              '  uint64_t live_cloud_diag_snapshot_seq_v2 = 0;\n'
              '  bool live_cloud_diag_has_previous_points_v2 = false;\n'
              '  uint64_t live_cloud_diag_previous_snapshot_seq_v2 = 0;\n'
              '  aether::sfm::LiveCloudDiagPointMapV2 live_cloud_diag_previous_points_v2;\n'
              '  // [PAIR-DRAFT 2026-08-09] 显示层临时配对云(第 2 张专用)。单写者=add_frame/\n'
              '  // remove_frame/finalize(worker isolate),读者=同 isolate 的 previewTracked\n'
              '  // —— 与 live_recon 完全相同的线程契约,无锁。live 模型一旦有点即被清空,\n'
              '  // previewTracked 兜底条件(live 零点 && 草稿非空)自动失效 ⇒ 无缝换正式云。\n'
              '  std::vector<aether_sfm_point_t> draft_pair_points;\n'
              '  std::vector<int32_t> draft_pair_obs_offsets;\n'
              '  std::vector<aether_sfm_track_obs_t> draft_pair_obs;\n'
              '  std::vector<colmap::image_t> reg_order;                       // registration order → window\n'
              '  int ba_window = 6;     // COLMAP default: six most-connected local images\n'
              '  int ba_every_n = 1;    // run the windowed BA every Nth frame (raise under thermal)\n'
              '  // ── [INCREMENTAL-GLOBAL-BA 2026-07-13] Rolling capture-time global BA ────────\n'
              '  // DEFAULT OFF (OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA=1). Every N registered frames the\n'
              '  // worker runs a bounded, observation-capped, pose-only global BA over live_recon\n'
              '  // (MaybeIncrementalGlobalRefine) so accumulated ARKit-pose drift is corrected AS\n'
              '  // IT GROWS — a sliding window at a time — instead of in one 74s finalize solve.\n'
              '  // When any incremental refine has fired, the finalize global solve COLLAPSES its\n'
              '  // round budget (see RefineGlobalBA). Written+read only on the capture-worker\n'
              '  // isolate that owns live_recon, so no lock (mirrors aether_sfm_global_refine).\n'
              '  size_t last_global_refine_reg = 0;       // reg_order.size() at the last refine\n'

              '  // [GPU-TIMESTAMP-PROBE V1] Writer-owned identity and the complete native\n'
              '  // frame snapshot pulled immediately after the serialized GPU extraction.\n'
              '  // The extractor POD intentionally contains none of these Session IDs.\n'
              '  uint64_t gpu_timestamp_run_id = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t gpu_timestamp_frame_ordinal = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t gpu_timestamp_next_record_id = 1;\n'
              '  bool gpu_timestamp_probe_written = false;\n'
              '  uint64_t gpu_timestamp_probe_instance_id = 0;\n'
              '  bool gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = false;\n'
              '  uint64_t gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ordinal = 0;\n'
              '  AetherGpuTimestampFrameV1 gpu_timestamp_pending_frame{};\n'
              '  // [A1B-ASYNC-PVBA 2026-07-27] Async preview-BA experiment arm state\n'
              '  // (default OFF). Ownership discipline: the WORKER thread does every kick\n'
              '  // (snapshot copy) and harvest (merge-back); the background thread touches\n'
              '  // ONLY its own model copy and its own db read connection, then parks the\n'
              '  // refined copy behind async_pvba_mutex and flips async_pvba_done.\n'
              '  std::thread async_pvba_thread;\n'
              '  std::atomic<bool> async_pvba_running{false};\n'
              '  std::atomic<bool> async_pvba_done{false};\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> async_pvba_result;\n'
              '  std::mutex async_pvba_mutex;\n'
              '  int apvba_snapshot_nframes = 0;  // [A1B-V2] frames at kick time\n'
              '  // [A1B-V4] Pre-BA poses of every frame in the snapshot, kept so the\n'
              '  // background pass\'s correction can be PROPAGATED onto the frames accepted\n'
              '  // while it ran (ORB-SLAM2 §: "propagating the correction of updated\n'
              '  // keyframes ... to non-updated keyframes"). ~56 B/frame.\n'
              '  std::unordered_map<colmap::frame_t, colmap::Rigid3d> apvba_snapshot_poses;\n'
              '  double stat_apvba_last_corr_mm = 0.0;   // |t| of the propagated correction\n'
              '  double stat_apvba_last_corr_deg = 0.0;  // its rotation angle\n'
              '  // [A1B-V3] consecutive-drop backoff + last background error (v2\'s drop rate\n'
              '  // was a self-amplifying loop: a failed harvest returns NOT_REGISTERED, the\n'
              '  // next frame re-triggers, kicks doubled and so did the contention).\n'
              '  int apvba_consecutive_drops = 0;\n'
              '  int apvba_skip_kicks = 0;\n'
              '  bool apvba_kick_armed = true;  // [A1B-V5] cadence match: one pass/publish\n'
              '  std::string apvba_last_error;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_apvba_kicks = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_apvba_merges = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_apvba_dropped = 0;   // bg failure / result discarded\n'
              '  double stat_apvba_last_ba_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '  double stat_apvba_merge_ms_total = 0.0;\n'
              '  double stat_apvba_copy_ms_total = 0.0;\n'
              '  // [P1-LIVE-REPAY 2026-07-11] Capture-idle debt-repayment pass counters\n'
              '  // (see aether_sfm_live_repay). Same worker-thread ownership as add_frame.\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_calls = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_attempted = 0;        // matcher invocations by repay\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_written = 0;          // pairs persisted to the db\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_inliers = 0;          // TVG inliers persisted by repay\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_failed = 0;           // matcher rc!=0 (left for finalize)\n'
              '  int64_t stat_repay_skipped_thermal = 0;  // repay calls refused at serious+\n'
              '  // Live pair bookkeeping for the repay scan (add_frame worker thread only):\n'
              '  // per-frame valid-window-pair counters mirroring FinalizeRematchStarved-\n'
              '  // Frames\' win_valid (gap <= K, TVG inliers >= gate), plus the set of frame\n'
              '  // pairs already RESOLVED live (db row written, matched-empty, or repaid) so\n'
              '  // an idle scan never re-touches sqlite or the matcher for them. GPU-failed\n'
              '  // add_frame pairs are deliberately NOT in the set — they are the debt.\n'
              '  std::vector<int> live_win_valid;\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<uint64_t> live_pairs_done;\n'
              '  // [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET 2026-07-11] Finalize-enrichment time gate state (see\n'
              '  // EnrichBudgetExhausted). enrich_start_ms is written by the thread that\n'
              '  // runs the enrichment passes right before they start; stage1_done is the\n'
              '  // AUTO-mode window signal set by the refine worker after its stage-1 block.\n'
              '  double enrich_start_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '  std::atomic<bool> enrich_stage1_done{false};\n'
              '  int64_t stat_enrich_budget_stopped = 0;  // fresh attempts skipped by the gate\n'
              '  // [P2-FRAG-MERGE 2026-07-11] Finalize-tail fragment-merge attribution (see\n'
              '  // MergeFragmentTracks). Conservation invariant the pass must uphold:\n'
              '  // frag_post_npts + frag_accepted == frag_pre_npts (a merge DEDUPLICATES two\n'
              '  // fragments into one multi-view point — the count drop is not deletion).\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_links = 0;              // TVG-connected track-pair candidates\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_attempted = 0;          // pairs entering the union refit\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_accepted = 0;           // merges installed\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_obs_merged = 0;         // union track sizes of accepted merges\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_reject_reproj = 0;      // union refit reproj/cheirality fail\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_reject_theta = 0;       // θ_max did not strictly rise\n'
              '  // [P2-FRAG-MERGE-REPRICE] union θ_max below OFFICIAL_AETHER_FRAG_MERGE_MIN_THETA_DEG\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_reject_min_theta = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_reject_degenerate = 0;  // broken obs / DLT failure\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_pre_npts = 0, stat_frag_pre_2view = 0, stat_frag_pre_lt3 = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t stat_frag_post_npts = 0, stat_frag_post_2view = 0, stat_frag_post_lt3 = 0;\n'
              '  double frag_theta_p50_pre_deg = 0.0;\n'
              '  double frag_theta_p50_post_deg = 0.0;\n'
              '  double frag_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Result of finalize()/run(): the largest reconstruction.\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::ReconstructionManager> recon_manager;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;  // best model, or null\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Async finalize (aether_sfm_finalize_async): phase 1 fills `recon` with a\n'
              '  // LOCAL-only result (instant), then `refine_thread` runs the global BA on a\n'
              '  // copy and atomically swaps `recon` to the refined model. `recon_mutex` guards\n'
              '  // every read/write of `recon` once the worker may be running; `finalize_status`\n'
              '  // is the lock-free progress flag the caller polls.\n'
              '  std::mutex recon_mutex;\n'
              '  std::thread refine_thread;\n'
              '  std::atomic<int> finalize_status{0};  // aether_sfm_finalize_status_t\n'
              '  double refine_ms = 0.0;               // worker global-BA wall time\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Per-frame telemetry read back by aether_sfm_debug_last (perf diagnostics).\n'
              '  // The exact values behind the device log line\n'
              '  //   extract=<..>ms match=<..>ms cand=<..> gpuM=<..> cpuM=<..>\n'
              '  // Written at the end of each aether_sfm_add_frame; describe the LAST frame.\n'
              '  double last_extract_ms = 0.0;   // DSP-SIFT extract wall time (>~2000 => GPU->CPU fallback)\n'
              '  double last_match_ms = 0.0;     // total per-pair matching wall time this frame\n'
              '  int last_n_cand = 0;            // # previous frames in the window matched against\n'
              '  int last_gpu_matches = 0;       // matches accepted via the GPU GEMM matcher\n'
              '  int last_cpu_matches = 0;       // matches accepted via CPU fallback (>0 => GPU matcher failed)\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [GPU-HANG-B1 2026-08-06] Chromium 式 watchdog(见 official_gpu_watchdog_v1.h)。\n'
              '  // 巡逻线程在 aether_sfm_create 启动、aether_sfm_free(join 完 refine/apvba\n'
              '  // 线程后)停止。一期纯观测:hang_detected 只落 jsonl,不改任何行为。\n'
              '  aether_gpu_watchdog_v1::WatchdogV1 gpu_watchdog;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'namespace aether_preclamp_instr_v1 {\n'
              '\n'
              'void ResetOfficialSessionRecords(::aether_sfm_session* session) noexcept {\n'
              '  if (session == nullptr) return;\n'
              '  ResetSessionRecords(&session->preclamp_instr_records);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool CopyOfficialSessionRecords(\n'
              '    const ::aether_sfm_session* session, std::vector<FrameCounts>* out) {\n'
              '  if (session == nullptr) return false;\n'
              '  return CopySessionRecords(&session->preclamp_instr_records, out);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool DrainOfficialSessionRecords(\n'
              '    ::aether_sfm_session* session, std::vector<FrameCounts>* out) noexcept {\n'
              '  if (session == nullptr) return false;\n'
              '  return DrainSessionRecords(&session->preclamp_instr_records, out);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '}  // namespace aether_preclamp_instr_v1\n'
              '\n'
              'namespace {\n'
              '\n'
              'enum class TailCacheModeV1 { kOff, kShadow, kOn };\n'
              '\n'
              '// [TAIL-CACHE 2026-08-08 SHIPPED ON — user-signed "装 tail-cache"] Reuse the\n'
              '// DatabaseCache across frames instead of rebuilding it from the db inside every\n'
              '// add_frame. The rebuild is O(db size), so it is the term that makes capture\n'
              '// get slower the longer you shoot; caching flattens that curve.\n'
              '//   Correctness (the reason this can be default-ON): shadow mode replays the\n'
              '//   local refinement on the reused cache next to the authoritative fresh-cache\n'
              '//   run and compares the LocalBundle call sequences. cap201 on this exact code:\n'
              '//   tail_shadow_compare_v1 = 199/199 EXACT. Delivered points +27 (+0.023%,\n'
              '//   inside the run-to-run band). Earlier host verdict (2026-08-02): off/shadow/on\n'
              '//   semantically identical on every metric; 144/144 EXACT.\n'
              '//   Speed, cap201 host, 9 interleaved pairs: tail segment 15.5s -> 8.8s (-43.5%,\n'
              '//   9/9 same-signed), stream paired-diff median -11.32%. Per-frame tail p50\n'
              '//   80 -> 45 ms; growth slope 0.617 -> 0.273 ms/frame. Device (2026-08-03,\n'
              '//   200/201 frames): tail p50 247 -> 107 ms (-56.7%), slope 2.182 -> 1.009.\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_TAIL_CACHE_V1=0 restores the pre-08-08 rebuild-every-frame\n'
              '//   path; =shadow re-arms the exactness comparison.\n'
              'TailCacheModeV1 TailCacheMode() {\n'
              '  static const TailCacheModeV1 cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* value = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TAIL_CACHE_V1");\n'
              '    if (!value) return TailCacheModeV1::kOn;  // DEFAULT ON since 2026-08-08\n'
              '    if (std::strcmp(value, "shadow") == 0) return TailCacheModeV1::kShadow;\n'
              '    if (std::strcmp(value, "0") == 0 || std::strcmp(value, "off") == 0) {\n'
              '      return TailCacheModeV1::kOff;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return TailCacheModeV1::kOn;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool TailCacheActive() { return TailCacheMode() != TailCacheModeV1::kOff; }\n'
              '\n'
              'bool DescriptorResidencyEnabledCore() {\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* value =\n'
              '        std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1");\n'
              '    return value && value[0] == \'1\' && value[1] == \'\\0\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '#else\n'
              '  return false;\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool TailCacheTraceEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* value = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TAIL_CACHE_V1");\n'
              '    if (!value) return false;\n'
              '    return std::strcmp(value, "off") == 0 ||\n'
              '           std::strcmp(value, "shadow") == 0 ||\n'
              '           std::strcmp(value, "1") == 0 || std::strcmp(value, "on") == 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'const char* TailCacheDirtyReasonName(\n'
              '    aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1 reason) {\n'
              '  using Reason = aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1;\n'
              '  switch (reason) {\n'
              '    case Reason::kNone: return "none";\n'
              '    case Reason::kPairOverwrite: return "pair_overwrite";\n'
              '    case Reason::kRemoveFrame: return "remove_frame";\n'
              '    case Reason::kLatePair: return "late_pair";\n'
              '    case Reason::kModelReplacement: return "model_replacement";\n'
              '    case Reason::kFinalizeMove: return "finalize_move";\n'
              '    case Reason::kReconstructionSwap: return "reconstruction_swap";\n'
              '    case Reason::kConfigChange: return "config_change";\n'
              '    case Reason::kCacheInconsistency: return "cache_inconsistency";\n'
              '    case Reason::kExceptionRetry: return "exception_retry";\n'
              '    case Reason::kDeviceReset: return "device_reset";\n'
              '    case Reason::kUnknownMutation: return "unknown_mutation";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return "unknown_mutation";\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int64_t EpochMs();\n'
              'void AppendMatchFailJsonl(aether_sfm_session* s, const std::string& line);\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendTailCacheEpochEventLockedV1(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                                       const char* phase) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !phase || !TailCacheTraceEnabled()) return;\n'
              '  const auto& epoch = s->tail_cache_epoch;\n'
              '  const std::string line =\n'
              '      "{\\"t\\":" + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '      ",\\"type\\":\\"tail_cache_epoch_event_v1\\",\\"phase\\":\\"" + phase +\n'
              '      "\\",\\"state\\":" + std::to_string(static_cast<int>(epoch.state())) +\n'
              '      ",\\"generation\\":" + std::to_string(epoch.generation()) +\n'
              '      ",\\"invalidated_generation\\":" +\n'
              '      std::to_string(epoch.invalidated_generation()) +\n'
              '      ",\\"rebuild_parent_generation\\":" +\n'
              '      std::to_string(epoch.rebuild_parent_generation()) +\n'
              '      ",\\"dirty_reason\\":\\"" + TailCacheDirtyReasonName(epoch.dirty_reason()) +\n'
              '      "\\"}";\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1 reason,\n'
              '    const char* phase = "dirty_before_mutation") {\n'
              '  s->tail_cache_epoch.MarkDirty(reason);\n'
              '  AppendTailCacheEpochEventLockedV1(s, phase);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void TailCacheInitialize(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || !TailCacheActive()) return;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  s->tail_cache_master = std::make_shared<colmap::DatabaseCache>();\n'
              '  s->tail_cache_epoch.StartEmpty();\n'
              '  s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs.clear();\n'
              '  if (s->db->NumImages() != 0) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kConfigChange);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void TailCacheMarkDirty(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                        aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1 reason) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheActive()) return;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(s, reason);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'struct TailCacheMutableBuildV1 {\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> cache;\n'
              '  std::vector<uint32_t> image_ids;\n'
              '  std::vector<uint64_t> qualifying_pair_ids;\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::image_pair_t> seen_db_pairs;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'TailCacheMutableBuildV1 BuildMutableTailCacheV1(\n'
              '    const colmap::Database& database) {\n'
              '  TailCacheMutableBuildV1 out;\n'
              '  out.cache = std::make_shared<colmap::DatabaseCache>();\n'
              '  const bool has_rigs = database.NumRigs() > 0;\n'
              '  const bool has_frames = database.NumFrames() > 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  if (has_rigs) {\n'
              '    for (auto rig : database.ReadAllRigs()) out.cache->AddRig(std::move(rig));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  for (auto camera : database.ReadAllCameras()) {\n'
              '    if (!has_rigs) {\n'
              '      colmap::Rig rig;\n'
              '      rig.SetRigId(camera.camera_id);\n'
              '      rig.AddRefSensor(camera.SensorId());\n'
              '      out.cache->AddRig(std::move(rig));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    out.cache->AddCamera(std::move(camera));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (has_frames) {\n'
              '    for (auto frame : database.ReadAllFrames()) {\n'
              '      out.cache->AddFrame(std::move(frame));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  for (auto image : database.ReadAllImages()) {\n'
              '    if (!has_frames) {\n'
              '      colmap::Frame frame;\n'
              '      frame.SetFrameId(image.ImageId());\n'
              '      frame.SetRigId(image.CameraId());\n'
              '      frame.AddDataId(image.DataId());\n'
              '      image.SetFrameId(frame.FrameId());\n'
              '      out.cache->AddFrame(std::move(frame));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    image.SetPoints2D(colmap::FeatureKeypointsToPointsVector(\n'
              '        database.ReadKeypoints(image.ImageId())));\n'
              '    out.image_ids.push_back(static_cast<uint32_t>(image.ImageId()));\n'
              '    out.cache->AddImage(std::move(image));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  for (auto& [pair_id, geometry] : database.ReadTwoViewGeometries()) {\n'
              '    out.seen_db_pairs.insert(pair_id);\n'
              '    if (geometry.inlier_matches.size() < 15) continue;\n'
              '    const auto [image1, image2] = colmap::PairIdToImagePair(pair_id);\n'
              '    out.cache->CorrespondenceGraph()->AddTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '        image1, image2, std::move(geometry));\n'
              '    out.qualifying_pair_ids.push_back(pair_id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return out;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool TailCacheRebuildLocked(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db ||\n'
              '      s->tail_cache_epoch.state() !=\n'
              '          aether::sfm::TailCacheEpochStateV1::kDirty ||\n'
              '      !s->tail_cache_epoch.BeginRebuild()) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  AppendTailCacheEpochEventLockedV1(s, "rebuild_begin");\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // Keep the finalized fresh route as a rebuild oracle. The returned cache is\n'
              '    // never mutated and is deliberately discarded after construction.\n'
              '    colmap::DatabaseCache::Options oracle_options;\n'
              '    oracle_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    oracle_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '    const auto oracle = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, oracle_options);\n'
              '    (void)oracle;\n'
              '\n'
              '    TailCacheMutableBuildV1 rebuilt = BuildMutableTailCacheV1(*s->db);\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_epoch.CompleteRebuild(rebuilt.image_ids,\n'
              '                                       rebuilt.qualifying_pair_ids);\n'
              '    if (!s->tail_cache_epoch.readable()) return false;\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master = std::move(rebuilt.cache);\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs = std::move(rebuilt.seen_db_pairs);\n'
              '    AppendTailCacheEpochEventLockedV1(s, "rebuild_complete");\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency,\n'
              '        "rebuild_failed");\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void TailCacheMirrorImage(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                          const colmap::Camera& camera,\n'
              '                          colmap::image_t image_id,\n'
              '                          const char* name,\n'
              '                          const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheActive()) return;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  if (!s->tail_cache_epoch.readable() || !s->tail_cache_master) return;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    colmap::Rig rig;\n'
              '    rig.SetRigId(camera.camera_id);\n'
              '    rig.AddRefSensor(camera.SensorId());\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master->AddRig(std::move(rig));\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master->AddCamera(camera);\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::Image image;\n'
              '    image.SetImageId(image_id);\n'
              '    image.SetName(name);\n'
              '    image.SetCameraId(camera.camera_id);\n'
              '    image.SetPoints2D(points);\n'
              '    colmap::Frame frame;\n'
              '    frame.SetFrameId(image_id);\n'
              '    frame.SetRigId(camera.camera_id);\n'
              '    frame.AddDataId(image.DataId());\n'
              '    image.SetFrameId(frame.FrameId());\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master->AddFrame(std::move(frame));\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master->AddImage(std::move(image));\n'
              '    if (!s->tail_cache_epoch.AdmitImage(static_cast<uint32_t>(image_id))) {\n'
              '      TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '          s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool TailCacheBeginPairWrite(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                             colmap::image_t image1,\n'
              '                             colmap::image_t image2,\n'
              '                             bool late_write = false) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheActive()) return false;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  const colmap::image_pair_t pair_id =\n'
              '      colmap::ImagePairToPairId(image1, image2);\n'
              '  const bool first_write = s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs.insert(pair_id).second;\n'
              '  if (!first_write) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kPairOverwrite);\n'
              '  } else if (late_write) {\n'
              '    // Idle repay/prepay inserts a pair after the newest frame\'s normal\n'
              '    // add-frame epoch. Rebuilding preserves the fresh SQLite pair ordering;\n'
              '    // incrementally appending here could change correspondence adjacency and\n'
              '    // a later FindLocalBundle tie-break.\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kLatePair);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return first_write;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void TailCacheCommitPair(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                         colmap::image_t image1,\n'
              '                         colmap::image_t image2,\n'
              '                         const colmap::TwoViewGeometry& geometry,\n'
              '                         bool first_write) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheActive() || !first_write ||\n'
              '      geometry.inlier_matches.size() < 15) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  if (!s->tail_cache_epoch.readable() || !s->tail_cache_master) return;\n'
              '  const colmap::image_pair_t pair_id =\n'
              '      colmap::ImagePairToPairId(image1, image2);\n'
              '  if (!s->tail_cache_epoch.AdmitPair(pair_id)) return;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    s->tail_cache_master->CorrespondenceGraph()->AddTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '        image1, image2, geometry);\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'struct SpatialRevisitCandidate {\n'
              '  int i = -1;\n'
              '  int j = -1;\n'
              '  double distance_m = 0.0;\n'
              '  double angle_rad = 0.0;\n'
              '  double score = 0.0;\n'
              '  int anchor_rank = 0;\n'
              '  bool quadratic = false;\n'
              '  // [KNIFE-A ③] top-up targeting: # of 2-view/low-parallax tracks this pair\n'
              '  // can hand a ≥5°-parallax third observation (0 when the hint is absent).\n'
              '  int target_score = 0;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'Eigen::Vector3d CameraForwardWorld(const colmap::Rigid3d& cam_from_world) {\n'
              '  return cam_from_world.rotation().inverse() * Eigen::Vector3d(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [SPATIAL-FIRST 2026-07-11] Legacy capture-time match-candidate selection.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHY: the previous policy matched each new frame against the last K frames by\n'
              '// TIME. Track length then depends on the user\'s walking pattern: revisiting a\n'
              '// region after more than K frames (backtracking, hopping between areas, uneven\n'
              '// pacing) never re-matches against the earlier frames that SEE the same\n'
              '// surface, so tracks fragment and finalize delivers fewer track>=3 points.\n'
              '// Camera-center proximity is the property that actually predicts covisibility\n'
              '// — 空间邻近应为第一标准,你没法控制用户的步伐 (architecture sign-off).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// POLICY (match budget unchanged — still at most K candidates per frame, same\n'
              '// matcher chain and handoff §0:12 contract: 8192 features / mutual cross-check\n'
              '// / a GPU-matcher failure skips the pair):\n'
              '//   1. Spatial-first: the K nearest previous frames by ARKit camera-center\n'
              '//      distance, gated on viewing-direction compatibility (forward-axis angle\n'
              '//      < 45° — a same-position opposite-facing frame shares no surface).\n'
              '//   2. Temporal fill: if the spatial set is short (< K), fill with the most\n'
              '//      recent frames not already selected (per-pair dedup by frame index; a\n'
              '//      (j, new) pair can never pre-exist in the db because the new frame id is\n'
              '//      fresh, so in-set dedup is the complete dedup).\n'
              '//   3. Degraded fallback: a frame WITHOUT a usable ARKit pose (tracking\n'
              '//      limited / pose missing) selects the legacy pure-temporal window via\n'
              '//      the same fill loop — the no-pose path behaves exactly as before.\n'
              '// In a smooth continuous walk the K nearest ARE mostly the last K frames, so\n'
              '// this converges to the old policy; it diverges exactly when the user\'s path\n'
              '// makes time a bad proxy for space.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Exactness over pose_hash_grid.h: the orientation gate breaks the grid\'s\n'
              '// pure-kNN abstraction (the K compatible neighbours may sit arbitrarily many\n'
              '// shells out), and this linear scan costs ~ns per previous frame — invisible\n'
              '// next to the >=100 ms descriptor match each SELECTED candidate costs.\n'
              '// Revisit only if captures ever reach ~10^5 frames.\n'
              'constexpr double kStreamCandViewAngleMaxRad = 45.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '\n'
              'std::vector<int> SelectStreamCandidates(const aether_sfm_session& s,\n'
              '                                        const FrameRecord& rec, int frame_id,\n'
              '                                        int k, int* out_spatial,\n'
              '                                        int* out_temporal) {\n'
              '  std::vector<int> selected;\n'
              '  if (out_spatial) *out_spatial = 0;\n'
              '  if (out_temporal) *out_temporal = 0;\n'
              '  if (k <= 0 || frame_id <= 0) return selected;\n'
              '  selected.reserve(k);\n'
              '\n'
              '  // A frame is a usable candidate only when it is neither withdrawn nor\n'
              '  // featureless. image_id == 0 is the withdrawal marker set by remove_frame and\n'
              '  // mirrored by RebuildFrameRecordsForResume; it is tested explicitly rather\n'
              '  // than inferred from n_keypoints so that the three pair-producing paths\n'
              '  // (this one, AddOfficialQuadraticPairs, FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames) all\n'
              '  // state the same predicate.\n'
              '  // NOTE: the old comment here claimed resume-rebuilt records carry no\n'
              '  // descriptors. That is no longer true for ACTIVE resumed frames — the\n'
              '  // mandatory-ARKit recovery contract reads them back to recompute identity\n'
              '  // digests.\n'
              '  const auto usable = [&](int j) {\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& prev = s.frames[j];\n'
              '    return prev.image_id != 0 && prev.n_keypoints > 0 &&\n'
              '           !prev.descriptors.empty();\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Kill switch: OFFICIAL_AETHER_STREAM_TEMPORAL_ONLY=1 forces the legacy pure-temporal\n'
              '  // window (baseline arm of the host A/B; emergency same-binary revert on\n'
              '  // device via setenv before aether_sfm_create).\n'
              '  static const bool temporal_only = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STREAM_TEMPORAL_ONLY");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '\n'
              '  int n_spatial = 0;\n'
              '  if (!temporal_only && rec.has_pose) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d center = rec.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d forward = CameraForwardWorld(rec.cam_from_world);\n'
              '    const double min_dot = std::cos(kStreamCandViewAngleMaxRad);\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<double, int>> compatible;  // (center dist^2, j)\n'
              '    compatible.reserve(frame_id);\n'
              '    for (int j = 0; j < frame_id; ++j) {\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& prev = s.frames[j];\n'
              '      if (!prev.has_pose || !usable(j)) continue;\n'
              '      const double dot = std::max(\n'
              '          -1.0,\n'
              '          std::min(1.0, forward.dot(CameraForwardWorld(prev.cam_from_world))));\n'
              '      if (dot < min_dot) continue;\n'
              '      compatible.emplace_back(\n'
              '          (center - prev.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc()).squaredNorm(), j);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    n_spatial = std::min(k, static_cast<int>(compatible.size()));\n'
              '    std::partial_sort(compatible.begin(), compatible.begin() + n_spatial,\n'
              '                      compatible.end());  // (dist^2 asc, j asc) — deterministic\n'
              '    for (int t = 0; t < n_spatial; ++t) {\n'
              '      selected.push_back(compatible[t].second);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (out_spatial) *out_spatial = n_spatial;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Temporal fill / full fallback: most recent first, dedup\'d against the\n'
              '  // spatial picks.\n'
              '  if (static_cast<int>(selected.size()) < k) {\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<int> chosen(selected.begin(), selected.end());\n'
              '    for (int j = frame_id - 1;\n'
              '         j >= 0 && static_cast<int>(selected.size()) < k; --j) {\n'
              '      if (!usable(j) || !chosen.insert(j).second) continue;\n'
              '      selected.push_back(j);\n'
              '      if (out_temporal) ++*out_temporal;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // Chronological processing order — the caller\'s grow/merge sequencing stays\n'
              '  // oldest-first, exactly like the legacy ascending-j loop.\n'
              '  std::sort(selected.begin(), selected.end());\n'
              '  return selected;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [SPATIAL-K20 2026-08-03] Product-required pair graph.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Space is the primary source, not a hypothesis arm: select the twenty nearest\n'
              '// non-recent, view-compatible ARKit poses, then independently add the previous\n'
              '// two frames for short-term continuity.  The shared, platform-neutral C++ core\n'
              '// owns ordering, canonical pair identity, source preservation and dedup, so\n'
              '// iOS/Metal and future Vulkan/SIMD carriers cannot silently grow different\n'
              '// pairing semantics.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_STREAM_TEMPORAL_ONLY remains an emergency/reference override\n'
              '// and deliberately routes to the characterized legacy selector above.  The\n'
              '// shipping default does not depend on an environment variable.\n'
              'std::vector<int> SelectSpatialK20TemporalT2Candidates(\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_session& s, const FrameRecord& rec, const int frame_id,\n'
              '    const bool thermal_throttled, const int thermal_spatial_k,\n'
              '    int* out_spatial, int* out_temporal) {\n'
              '  if (out_spatial) *out_spatial = 0;\n'
              '  if (out_temporal) *out_temporal = 0;\n'
              '  if (frame_id <= 0) return {};\n'
              '\n'
              '  aether_pair_policy_config_v2 abi_config{};\n'
              '  aether_pair_policy_config_v2_default(&abi_config);\n'
              '  if (aether_pair_policy_config_v2_validate(&abi_config) !=\n'
              '      AETHER_PAIR_POLICY_V2_OK) {\n'
              '    return {};\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  static const bool temporal_only = [] {\n'
              '    const char* value = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STREAM_TEMPORAL_ONLY");\n'
              '    return value != nullptr && value[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  if (temporal_only) {\n'
              '    return SelectStreamCandidates(s, rec, frame_id, abi_config.spatial_k,\n'
              '                                  out_spatial, out_temporal);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::vector<aether::sfm::PairSelectionFrameV2> history;\n'
              '  history.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(frame_id));\n'
              '  for (int index = 0; index < frame_id; ++index) {\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& previous = s.frames[index];\n'
              '    aether::sfm::PairSelectionFrameV2 frame;\n'
              '    frame.frame_id = index;\n'
              '    frame.pose_valid = previous.has_pose;\n'
              '    frame.matchable = previous.n_keypoints > 0 && !previous.descriptors.empty();\n'
              '    if (previous.has_pose) {\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d center =\n'
              '          previous.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d forward =\n'
              '          CameraForwardWorld(previous.cam_from_world);\n'
              '      frame.center_xyz = {center.x(), center.y(), center.z()};\n'
              '      frame.forward_xyz = {forward.x(), forward.y(), forward.z()};\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    history.push_back(frame);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  aether::sfm::PairSelectionFrameV2 current;\n'
              '  current.frame_id = frame_id;\n'
              '  current.pose_valid = rec.has_pose;\n'
              '  current.matchable = rec.n_keypoints > 0 && !rec.descriptors.empty();\n'
              '  if (rec.has_pose) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d center = rec.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d forward = CameraForwardWorld(rec.cam_from_world);\n'
              '    current.center_xyz = {center.x(), center.y(), center.z()};\n'
              '    current.forward_xyz = {forward.x(), forward.y(), forward.z()};\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  aether::sfm::PairSelectionConfigV2 config;\n'
              '  // [K-REWIRE 2026-08-06 用户签决"K10上产,回环保留"] 常态空间 K 重新服从\n'
              '  // 调用方传入的 k(= base_k 链:env OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K > options.\n'
              '  // k_neighbors(生产 12)> 兜底 6)。此前这里常态用 abi_config.spatial_k\n'
              '  // (硬编码 20)⇒ 08-06 上午随尺度重编上机后,K10 回滚被静默旁路,真机\n'
              '  // cand=22(+回环尖峰 26),复现被判死的 K20 热债(未命名2:match 尖峰\n'
              '  // 7.3s、时长史上最长)。质量侧同场真机图 A/B 已证 12 对/帧饱和\n'
              '  // (K22→K12 点数 −0.34%)。thermal_spatial_k 形参实为"调用方解析后的\n'
              '  // 生效 K"(热降频时已被调用方降过),两种状态同走一条线。\n'
              '  // 回环检索(VISUAL-LOOP V1,每10帧+≤4)不受影响,按用户签决保留。\n'
              '  // k 是"总预算"(签决的 K10 臂 = 空间10+时间2=总12),空间名额 = k − 时间窗。\n'
              '  config.spatial_k =\n'
              '      std::max(0, thermal_spatial_k - abi_config.temporal_lookback);\n'
              '  (void)thermal_throttled;  // K 语义已并入 thermal_spatial_k,形参留作 ABI 稳定\n'
              '  config.temporal_lookback = abi_config.temporal_lookback;\n'
              '  config.spatial_recent_exclusion = abi_config.spatial_recent_exclusion;\n'
              '  const aether::sfm::PairSelectionResultV2 result =\n'
              '      aether::sfm::SelectSpatialTemporalCandidatesV2(history, current, config);\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::vector<int> selected;\n'
              '  selected.reserve(result.ordered_pairs.size());\n'
              '  int spatial = 0;\n'
              '  int temporal = 0;\n'
              '  const uint32_t spatial_mask =\n'
              '      static_cast<uint32_t>(aether::sfm::PairCandidateSourceV2::kSpatial);\n'
              '  const uint32_t temporal_mask =\n'
              '      static_cast<uint32_t>(aether::sfm::PairCandidateSourceV2::kTemporal);\n'
              '  for (const aether::sfm::CanonicalPairCandidateV2& pair :\n'
              '       result.ordered_pairs) {\n'
              '    if (pair.second_frame_id != frame_id || pair.first_frame_id < 0 ||\n'
              '        pair.first_frame_id >= frame_id) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    selected.push_back(pair.first_frame_id);\n'
              '    if ((pair.source_mask & spatial_mask) != 0U) ++spatial;\n'
              '    if ((pair.source_mask & temporal_mask) != 0U) ++temporal;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (out_spatial) *out_spatial = spatial;\n'
              '  if (out_temporal) *out_temporal = temporal;\n'
              '  return selected;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [MATCH-FAIL TELEMETRY 2026-07-11] Record one capture-time GPU matcher\n'
              '// failure (add_frame\'s fail-closed `mrc != 0 → continue`). Counts total +\n'
              '// per-rc buckets + the consecutive-failure streak, and emits ONE warning line\n'
              '// when a failure SEGMENT emerges (capture 43\'s thermal collapse failed whole\n'
              '// frames of pairs back-to-back — a single skipped pair is normal noise, a run\n'
              '// of them means the block behind it will not register without the finalize\n'
              '// re-match). Re-logs every 64 further consecutive failures so an ongoing\n'
              '// collapse stays visible without per-pair spam.\n'
              'constexpr int kGpuMatchFailStreakWarn = 8;\n'
              '\n'
              '// [RC7-FILELOG 2026-07-11] Epoch-ms wall clock for the jsonl lines below\n'
              '// (NowMs above is steady_clock — good for durations, useless for correlating\n'
              '// against telemetry_native/telemetry_dart timestamps after a detached run).\n'
              'int64_t EpochMs() {\n'
              '  using namespace std::chrono;\n'
              '  return duration_cast<milliseconds>(system_clock::now().time_since_epoch())\n'
              '      .count();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [RC7-FILELOG 2026-07-11] Append one JSONL line to <db_dir>/\n'
              '// sfm_match_fail.jsonl — the pull-able file record of capture-time GPU\n'
              '// matcher failures (and thermal-throttle transitions). Motivation: rc=7\n'
              '// (MTLCommandBufferStatusError) evidence previously lived only in NSLog +\n'
              '// in-memory counters; detached device runs (拔线) lose stderr/os_log, so the\n'
              '// cap44/45 freeze forensics had timestamps for everything EXCEPT the GPU\n'
              '// failures. The db directory is the app\'s Documents container on device —\n'
              '// exactly what `devicectl copy` already recovers. Best-effort by design:\n'
              '// open-append-close per event (events are rare; a thermal collapse writes a\n'
              '// few hundred ~150 B lines), any I/O failure is swallowed.\n'
              'void AppendMatchFailJsonl(aether_sfm_session* s, const std::string& line) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const std::filesystem::path p =\n'
              '        std::filesystem::path(s->db_path).parent_path() /\n'
              '        "sfm_match_fail.jsonl";\n'
              '    FILE* f = std::fopen(p.string().c_str(), "a");\n'
              '    if (!f) return;\n'
              '    std::fputs(line.c_str(), f);\n'
              '    std::fputc(\'\\n\', f);\n'
              '    std::fclose(f);\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    // telemetry only — never take add_frame down\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-CLOUD-SNAPSHOT-DIAG V2] Observe the exact native model at a publish\n'
              '// boundary. This function only reads Reconstruction/FrameRecord state and writes\n'
              '// a diagnostics sidecar plus its own previous-snapshot map. It is deliberately\n'
              '// fail-open: no allocation, fit, formatting, or I/O failure may affect capture.\n'
              'void AppendLiveCloudSnapshotDiagnosticsV2(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                                          const char* stage) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    if (!s || !stage || !s->live_recon_ready || !s->live_recon) return;\n'
              '    const double snapshot_start_ms = NowMs();\n'
              '    const uint64_t snapshot_seq = ++s->live_cloud_diag_snapshot_seq_v2;\n'
              '\n'
              '    std::vector<aether::sfm::LiveCloudArkitBaCenterPairV2> center_pairs;\n'
              '    center_pairs.reserve(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    for (const FrameRecord& frame : s->frames) {\n'
              '      if (!frame.has_pose || frame.image_id == 0 ||\n'
              '          !s->live_recon->ExistsImage(frame.image_id)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& image = s->live_recon->Image(frame.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!image.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d arkit_center =\n'
              '          frame.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d ba_center =\n'
              '          image.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '      center_pairs.push_back({\n'
              '          {arkit_center.x(), arkit_center.y(), arkit_center.z()},\n'
              '          {ba_center.x(), ba_center.y(), ba_center.z()},\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const aether::sfm::LiveCloudArkitBaSim3SummaryV2 sim3 =\n'
              '        aether::sfm::SummarizeLiveCloudArkitBaSim3V2(center_pairs);\n'
              '    const double sim3_compute_ms = NowMs() - snapshot_start_ms;\n'
              '    char sim3_line[3072];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(\n'
              '        sim3_line, sizeof(sim3_line),\n'
              '        "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"live_cloud_arkit_ba_sim3_v2\\","\n'
              '        "\\"contract\\":\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_SNAPSHOT_DIAG_V2_20260810\\","\n'
              '        "\\"stage\\":\\"%s\\",\\"snapshot_seq\\":%llu,"\n'
              '        "\\"valid\\":%s,\\"status\\":\\"%s\\",\\"input_pairs\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"pair_count\\":%zu,\\"inlier_count\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"ba_to_arkit_scale\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"ba_to_arkit_tx_m\\":%.12g,\\"ba_to_arkit_ty_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"ba_to_arkit_tz_m\\":%.12g,\\"ba_to_arkit_qx\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"ba_to_arkit_qy\\":%.12g,\\"ba_to_arkit_qz\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"ba_to_arkit_qw\\":%.12g,\\"ba_to_arkit_rotation_deg\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"arkit_to_ba_scale\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"arkit_to_ba_tx_m\\":%.12g,\\"arkit_to_ba_ty_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"arkit_to_ba_tz_m\\":%.12g,\\"arkit_to_ba_qx\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"arkit_to_ba_qy\\":%.12g,\\"arkit_to_ba_qz\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"arkit_to_ba_qw\\":%.12g,\\"arkit_to_ba_rotation_deg\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"residual_p50_m\\":%.12g,\\"residual_p90_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"residual_max_m\\":%.12g,\\"residual_all_max_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"compute_ms\\":%.3f,\\"observation_only\\":true}",\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), stage,\n'
              '        static_cast<unsigned long long>(snapshot_seq),\n'
              '        sim3.valid ? "true" : "false", sim3.status.c_str(),\n'
              '        sim3.input_pair_count, sim3.pair_count, sim3.inlier_count,\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_scale, sim3.ba_to_arkit_translation_m[0],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_translation_m[1],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_translation_m[2],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_quaternion_xyzw[0],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_quaternion_xyzw[1],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_quaternion_xyzw[2],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_quaternion_xyzw[3],\n'
              '        sim3.ba_to_arkit_rotation_deg, sim3.arkit_to_ba_scale,\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_translation_m[0],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_translation_m[1],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_translation_m[2],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_quaternion_xyzw[0],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_quaternion_xyzw[1],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_quaternion_xyzw[2],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_quaternion_xyzw[3],\n'
              '        sim3.arkit_to_ba_rotation_deg, sim3.residual_p50_m,\n'
              '        sim3.residual_p90_m, sim3.residual_max_m,\n'
              '        sim3.residual_all_max_m, sim3_compute_ms);\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, sim3_line);\n'
              '\n'
              '    const double point_start_ms = NowMs();\n'
              '    aether::sfm::LiveCloudDiagPointMapV2 current_points;\n'
              '    current_points.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '    for (const auto& [point_id, point] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) {\n'
              '      current_points.emplace(\n'
              '          static_cast<std::uint64_t>(point_id),\n'
              '          std::array<double, 3>{point.xyz.x(), point.xyz.y(), point.xyz.z()});\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    const bool baseline = !s->live_cloud_diag_has_previous_points_v2;\n'
              '    aether::sfm::LiveCloudSameIdPointDeltaSummaryV2 point_delta;\n'
              '    if (baseline) {\n'
              '      point_delta.current_count = current_points.size();\n'
              '      point_delta.new_count = current_points.size();\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      point_delta = aether::sfm::SummarizeLiveCloudSameIdPointDeltasV2(\n'
              '          s->live_cloud_diag_previous_points_v2, current_points);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const double point_compute_ms = NowMs() - point_start_ms;\n'
              '    char point_line[2304];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(\n'
              '        point_line, sizeof(point_line),\n'
              '        "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"live_cloud_same_id_point_delta_v2\\","\n'
              '        "\\"contract\\":\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_SNAPSHOT_DIAG_V2_20260810\\","\n'
              '        "\\"stage\\":\\"%s\\",\\"snapshot_seq\\":%llu,"\n'
              '        "\\"previous_snapshot_seq\\":%llu,\\"baseline\\":%s,"\n'
              '        "\\"valid\\":%s,\\"previous_count\\":%zu,\\"current_count\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"common_count\\":%zu,\\"valid_delta_count\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"new_count\\":%zu,\\"dropped_count\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"median_dx_m\\":%.12g,\\"median_dy_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"median_dz_m\\":%.12g,\\"coherent_median_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"p50_m\\":%.12g,\\"p90_m\\":%.12g,\\"p99_m\\":%.12g,"\n'
              '        "\\"max_m\\":%.12g,\\"gte_5cm_count\\":%zu,"\n'
              '        "\\"gte_10cm_count\\":%zu,\\"worst_point_id\\":\\"%llu\\","\n'
              '        "\\"compute_ms\\":%.3f,\\"observation_only\\":true}",\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), stage,\n'
              '        static_cast<unsigned long long>(snapshot_seq),\n'
              '        static_cast<unsigned long long>(\n'
              '            baseline ? 0 : s->live_cloud_diag_previous_snapshot_seq_v2),\n'
              '        baseline ? "true" : "false", point_delta.valid ? "true" : "false",\n'
              '        point_delta.previous_count, point_delta.current_count,\n'
              '        point_delta.common_count, point_delta.valid_delta_count,\n'
              '        point_delta.new_count, point_delta.dropped_count,\n'
              '        point_delta.median_delta_m[0], point_delta.median_delta_m[1],\n'
