              '        point_delta.median_delta_m[2], point_delta.coherent_median_norm_m,\n'
              '        point_delta.p50_norm_m, point_delta.p90_norm_m,\n'
              '        point_delta.p99_norm_m, point_delta.max_norm_m,\n'
              '        point_delta.warning_5cm_count, point_delta.severe_10cm_count,\n'
              '        static_cast<unsigned long long>(point_delta.worst_point_id),\n'
              '        point_compute_ms);\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, point_line);\n'
              '\n'
              '    s->live_cloud_diag_previous_points_v2 = std::move(current_points);\n'
              '    s->live_cloud_diag_has_previous_points_v2 = true;\n'
              '    s->live_cloud_diag_previous_snapshot_seq_v2 = snapshot_seq;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    // Diagnostics are fail-open by contract. Do not change model or return code.\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'struct LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1 {\n'
              '  int raw_calls = 0;\n'
              '  int calls = 0;\n'
              '  std::array<uint32_t, 16> query{};\n'
              '  std::array<int, 16> neighbor_count{};\n'
              '  std::array<int, 16> neighbor_total{};\n'
              '  std::array<std::array<uint32_t, 15>, 16> neighbors{};\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'void ResetLocalBundleTraceV1() {\n'
              '  aether_ilr_bundle_trace_calls = 0;\n'
              '  std::memset(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_query, 0,\n'
              '              sizeof(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_query));\n'
              '  std::memset(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_count, 0,\n'
              '              sizeof(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_count));\n'
              '  std::memset(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_total, 0,\n'
              '              sizeof(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_total));\n'
              '  std::memset(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbors, 0,\n'
              '              sizeof(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbors));\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1 CaptureLocalBundleTraceV1() {\n'
              '  LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1 snapshot;\n'
              '  snapshot.raw_calls = std::max(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_calls, 0);\n'
              '  snapshot.calls = std::min(snapshot.raw_calls, 16);\n'
              '  for (int call = 0; call < snapshot.calls; ++call) {\n'
              '    snapshot.query[call] = aether_ilr_bundle_trace_query[call];\n'
              '    snapshot.neighbor_count[call] = std::min(\n'
              '        std::max(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_count[call], 0), 15);\n'
              '    snapshot.neighbor_total[call] =\n'
              '        std::max(aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbor_total[call], 0);\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < snapshot.neighbor_count[call]; ++i) {\n'
              '      snapshot.neighbors[call][i] =\n'
              '          aether_ilr_bundle_trace_neighbors[call][i];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return snapshot;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool LocalBundleTraceExactV1(const LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1& fresh,\n'
              '                             const LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1& mutable_cache) {\n'
              '  if (fresh.raw_calls != mutable_cache.raw_calls ||\n'
              '      fresh.calls != mutable_cache.calls) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  for (int call = 0; call < fresh.calls; ++call) {\n'
              '    if (fresh.query[call] != mutable_cache.query[call] ||\n'
              '        fresh.neighbor_count[call] != mutable_cache.neighbor_count[call] ||\n'
              '        fresh.neighbor_total[call] != mutable_cache.neighbor_total[call]) {\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < fresh.neighbor_count[call]; ++i) {\n'
              '      if (fresh.neighbors[call][i] != mutable_cache.neighbors[call][i]) {\n'
              '        return false;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendTailLocalBundleTraceV1(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '    int frame_id,\n'
              '    int cache_reused,\n'
              '    uint64_t cache_generation,\n'
              '    const char* source,\n'
              '    const LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1& trace) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheTraceEnabled()) return;\n'
              '  const char* mode =\n'
              '      TailCacheMode() == TailCacheModeV1::kOn\n'
              '          ? "on"\n'
              '          : (TailCacheMode() == TailCacheModeV1::kShadow ? "shadow" : "off");\n'
              '  for (int call = 0; call < trace.calls; ++call) {\n'
              '    const int count = trace.neighbor_count[call];\n'
              '    const int total = trace.neighbor_total[call];\n'
              '    std::string line =\n'
              '        "{\\"t\\":" + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '        ",\\"type\\":\\"tail_local_bundle_v1\\",\\"fid\\":" +\n'
              '        std::to_string(frame_id) + ",\\"cache\\":\\"" + mode +\n'
              '        "\\",\\"source\\":\\"" + source +\n'
              '        "\\",\\"cache_reused\\":" + std::to_string(cache_reused) +\n'
              '        ",\\"tail_gen\\":" + std::to_string(cache_generation) +\n'
              '        ",\\"call\\":" + std::to_string(call) + ",\\"query\\":" +\n'
              '        std::to_string(trace.query[call]) +\n'
              '        ",\\"neighbors\\":[";\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n'
              '      if (i != 0) line += \',\';\n'
              '      line += std::to_string(trace.neighbors[call][i]);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    line += "],\\"ba_images\\":[" +\n'
              '            std::to_string(trace.query[call]);\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n'
              '      line += \',\';\n'
              '      line += std::to_string(trace.neighbors[call][i]);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    line += "],\\"neighbor_total\\":" + std::to_string(total) +\n'
              '            ",\\"truncated\\":" +\n'
              '            (trace.raw_calls > trace.calls || total > count ? "true" : "false") +\n'
              '            "}";\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendTailMatchCandidatesV1(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                                 int frame_id,\n'
              '                                 int configured_k,\n'
              '                                 const std::vector<int>& candidates) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheTraceEnabled()) return;\n'
              '  std::string line =\n'
              '      "{\\"t\\":" + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '      ",\\"type\\":\\"tail_match_candidates_v1\\",\\"fid\\":" +\n'
              '      std::to_string(frame_id) + ",\\"configured_k\\":" +\n'
              '      std::to_string(configured_k) + ",\\"match_candidate_ids\\":[";\n'
              '  bool first = true;\n'
              '  for (const int index : candidates) {\n'
              '    if (index < 0 || index >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size())) continue;\n'
              '    if (!first) line += \',\';\n'
              '    first = false;\n'
              '    line += std::to_string(s->frames[static_cast<size_t>(index)].frame_id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  line += "],\\"image_ids\\":[";\n'
              '  first = true;\n'
              '  for (const int index : candidates) {\n'
              '    if (index < 0 || index >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size())) continue;\n'
              '    if (!first) line += \',\';\n'
              '    first = false;\n'
              '    line += std::to_string(s->frames[static_cast<size_t>(index)].image_id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  line += "]}";\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'uint64_t NewGpuTimestampRunId() {\n'
              '  static std::atomic<uint64_t> sequence{1};\n'
              '  constexpr uint64_t kSequenceBits = 20;\n'
              '  constexpr uint64_t kSequenceMask = (uint64_t{1} << kSequenceBits) - 1;\n'
              '  const uint64_t epoch_ms =\n'
              '      static_cast<uint64_t>(std::max<int64_t>(EpochMs(), 1));\n'
              '  const uint64_t suffix =\n'
              '      sequence.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed) & kSequenceMask;\n'
              '  return (epoch_ms << kSequenceBits) | suffix;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendGpuTimestampProbeRecord(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (s == nullptr || s->gpu_timestamp_probe_written ||\n'
              '      aether_sed_gpu_timestamp_probe_v1 == nullptr) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  AetherGpuTimestampProbeV1 probe{};\n'
              '  probe.struct_size = sizeof(probe);\n'
              '  if (aether_sed_gpu_timestamp_probe_v1(&probe) !=\n'
              '      AETHER_GPU_TIMESTAMP_PULL_OK_V1) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  const aether::official::gpu_timestamp_writer_v1::RecordIdentityV1 identity{\n'
              '      .run_id = s->gpu_timestamp_run_id,\n'
              '      .frame_id = -1,\n'
              '      .frame_ordinal = 0,\n'
              '      .probe_record_id = s->gpu_timestamp_next_record_id,\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  std::string json;\n'
              '  if (!aether::official::gpu_timestamp_writer_v1::SerializeProbeRecordV1(\n'
              '          probe, identity, EpochMs(), &json)) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, json);\n'
              '  ++s->gpu_timestamp_next_record_id;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_probe_instance_id = probe.probe_instance_id;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_probe_written = true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void PullGpuTimestampAfterExtraction(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (s == nullptr) return;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = false;\n'
              '  ++s->gpu_timestamp_frame_ordinal;\n'
              '  AppendGpuTimestampProbeRecord(s);\n'
              '  if (!s->gpu_timestamp_probe_written ||\n'
              '      aether_dsp_sift_take_last_gpu_timestamp_frame_v1 == nullptr) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  AetherGpuTimestampFrameV1 frame{};\n'
              '  frame.struct_size = sizeof(frame);\n'
              '  if (aether_dsp_sift_take_last_gpu_timestamp_frame_v1(&frame) !=\n'
              '          AETHER_GPU_TIMESTAMP_PULL_OK_V1 ||\n'
              '      frame.probe_instance_id != s->gpu_timestamp_probe_instance_id) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame = frame;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ordinal =\n'
              '      s->gpu_timestamp_frame_ordinal;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendGpuTimestampFrameRecord(aether_sfm_session* s, int frame_id) {\n'
              '  if (s == nullptr || !s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready ||\n'
              '      !s->gpu_timestamp_probe_written) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const AetherGpuTimestampFrameV1 frame =\n'
              '      s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame;\n'
              '  const uint64_t frame_ordinal =\n'
              '      s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ordinal;\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const aether::official::gpu_timestamp_writer_v1::RecordIdentityV1 identity{\n'
              '      .run_id = s->gpu_timestamp_run_id,\n'
              '      .frame_id = frame_id,\n'
              '      .frame_ordinal = frame_ordinal,\n'
              '      .probe_record_id = s->gpu_timestamp_next_record_id,\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  std::string json;\n'
              '  if (!aether::official::gpu_timestamp_writer_v1::SerializeFrameRecordV1(\n'
              '          frame, identity, EpochMs(), &json)) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, json);\n'
              '  ++s->gpu_timestamp_next_record_id;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [AETHER BA-RING 2026-07-26, signed] Drain the per-solve ceres ring into a\n'
              '// pullable ba_rounds record. Settles the stage-1 "thread tax vs\n'
              '// round-position iteration count" confound and prices ftol early-stop for\n'
              '// the round-allocation sign-off. Pure observation.\n'
              'void AppendBaRingJsonl(aether_sfm_session* s, int stage) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const int n = aether_ba_ring_count();\n'
              '    if (n <= 0) return;\n'
              '    std::string line = "{\\"t\\":" + std::to_string(EpochMs()) +\n'
              '                       ",\\"type\\":\\"ba_rounds\\",\\"stage\\":" +\n'
              '                       std::to_string(stage) + ",\\"solves\\":[";\n'
              '    char buf[256];  // [BA-OTHER-SPLIT] +3 字段留头\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < n && i < 12; ++i) {\n'
              '      double total_s, jac_s, lin_s, res_s;\n'
              '      int iters, term, threads;\n'
              '      if (!aether_ba_ring_get(i, &total_s, &jac_s, &lin_s, &res_s, &iters,\n'
              '                              &term, &threads)) {\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [BA-OTHER-SPLIT 2026-08-13] total-(jac+lin+res) 的 ~20% 黑盒:\n'
              '      // pre=问题构建/排序/Schur 结构探测,min=minimizer 总时(减去\n'
              '      // jac+lin+res 即信赖域步长逻辑与参数更新),post=收尾。取不到就写 0。\n'
              '      double pre_s = 0, min_s = 0, post_s = 0;\n'
              '      aether_ba_ring_get_split(i, &pre_s, &min_s, &post_s);\n'
              '      std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf),\n'
              '                    "%s{\\"total_s\\":%.2f,\\"jac_s\\":%.2f,\\"lin_s\\":%.2f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"res_s\\":%.2f,\\"pre_s\\":%.2f,\\"min_s\\":%.2f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"post_s\\":%.2f,\\"iters\\":%d,\\"term\\":%d,\\"thr\\":%d}",\n'
              '                    i ? "," : "", total_s, jac_s, lin_s, res_s, pre_s, min_s,\n'
              '                    post_s, iters, term, threads);\n'
              '      line += buf;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    line += "]}";\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    // telemetry only\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// JSON string sanitizer for the Metal error text (quotes/backslashes/control\n'
              '// chars → space; keeps the line machine-parseable without a JSON library).\n'
              'std::string JsonSafe(const char* text, size_t max_len) {\n'
              '  std::string out;\n'
              '  if (!text) return out;\n'
              '  out.reserve(max_len);\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; text[i] != \'\\0\' && i < max_len; ++i) {\n'
              '    const char c = text[i];\n'
              '    out.push_back((c == \'"\' || c == \'\\\\\' || (c >= 0 && c < 0x20)) ? \' \' : c);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return out;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void NoteGpuMatchFailure(aether_sfm_session* s, int rc, int prev_frame_id,\n'
              '                         int new_frame_id) {\n'
              '  ++s->stat_gpu_match_fail_total;\n'
              '  const int bucket = (rc >= 1 && rc <= 7) ? rc : 0;\n'
              '  ++s->stat_gpu_match_fail_by_rc[bucket];\n'
              '  // [P1-REPAY-THERMAL2] rc=7 (thermal/GPU-pressure kill) dirties the clean\n'
              '  // history the conditional repay gate requires.\n'
              '  if (rc == 7) s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7 = 0;\n'
              '  ++s->gpu_match_fail_streak;\n'
              '  if (s->gpu_match_fail_streak > s->stat_gpu_match_fail_max_streak) {\n'
              '    s->stat_gpu_match_fail_max_streak = s->gpu_match_fail_streak;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // [RC7-FILELOG 2026-07-11] Every capture-time matcher failure lands one\n'
              '  // timestamped jsonl line WITH the pair id — 21 rc=7 events per capture is\n'
              '  // the observed worst case order of magnitude (cap45), a full thermal\n'
              '  // collapse (cap43, ~600) is still <100 KB. rc=7 lines carry the Metal\n'
              '  // error description when the platform TU (pwofficial_gpu_match.mm) recorded one.\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    char head[192];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(head, sizeof(head),\n'
              '                  "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"gpu_match_fail\\",\\"rc\\":%d,"\n'
              '                  "\\"pair\\":[%d,%d],\\"streak\\":%lld,\\"total\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                  "\\"thermal\\":%d",\n'
              '                  static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), rc, prev_frame_id,\n'
              '                  new_frame_id, static_cast<long long>(s->gpu_match_fail_streak),\n'
              '                  static_cast<long long>(s->stat_gpu_match_fail_total),\n'
              '                  s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));\n'
              '    std::string line(head);\n'
              '    if (rc == 7 && aether_gpu_match_last_error != nullptr) {\n'
              '      char err[192] = {0};\n'
              '      if (aether_gpu_match_last_error(err, sizeof(err)) > 0) {\n'
              '        line += ",\\"metal_err\\":\\"";\n'
              '        line += JsonSafe(err, sizeof(err));\n'
              '        line += "\\"";\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    line += "}";\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (s->gpu_match_fail_streak == kGpuMatchFailStreakWarn ||\n'
              '      (s->gpu_match_fail_streak > kGpuMatchFailStreakWarn &&\n'
              '       s->gpu_match_fail_streak % 64 == 0)) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] GPU matcher failing in a segment: "\n'
              '                 << s->gpu_match_fail_streak\n'
              '                 << " consecutive pair failures (last rc=" << rc\n'
              '                 << ", total=" << s->stat_gpu_match_fail_total\n'
              '                 << "). Pairs are skipped fail-closed; finalize re-matches "\n'
              '                    "starved frames.";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [P1-RC7-RETRY 2026-07-11] Backoff retry around the Metal GEMM matcher for\n'
              '// rc=7 ONLY (MTLCommandBufferStatusError — the transient "command buffer\n'
              '// killed under thermal/GPU pressure" failure). cap46 forensics: 49 rc=7\n'
              '// events during capture and 93 more during finalize re-match; descriptors\n'
              '// were intact throughout, i.e. the loss was recoverable by simply asking\n'
              '// again once the GPU had a breather. rc=1/2/5/6 are deterministic (bad args /\n'
              '// pipeline unavailable / MTLBuffer alloc) and are never retried. Two retries\n'
              '// with 50 ms then 100 ms backoff bound the extra latency of a persistently\n'
              '// dead pair at ~150 ms + two matcher calls. Default ON: the retry only\n'
              '// changes behavior on pairs that are currently dropped fail-closed (pure\n'
              '// recovery), host CPU-matcher builds never reach this path, and\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_GPU_MATCH_RETRY=0 is the same-binary kill switch (N>0 overrides the\n'
              '// retry count).\n'
              'int GpuMatchRetryLimit() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_GPU_MATCH_RETRY")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 2;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-07] Probe pre-scoring threshold.\n'
              '// PROVENANCE: this gate is a replication of Changchang Wu, "Towards\n'
              '// Linear-time Incremental Structure from Motion" (ICCV 2013) §3 "Preemptive\n'
              '// Feature Matching" — match a small subset of each image\'s features first\n'
              '// (Wu: the top-100 largest-SCALE features) and skip the whole pair when the\n'
              '// subset match count is below a threshold (Wu: t_h = 4). Our working point\n'
              '// comes from the 08-03 recon (_host_experiments/probe-gate-recon-20260803):\n'
              '// a 512-row subsample of the NEW frame\'s descriptors matched against the\n'
              '// candidate\'s full descriptor set predicts whether the full pair survives\n'
              '// TVG with AUC 0.9527 (corr 0.9862 with the full match count); by the\n'
              '// inlier-WEIGHTED cost ledger, probe512 < 3 skips 42.6% of pairs at 1.75%\n'
              '// weighted-inlier loss (inside the quality-lane "obs >= 0.98x" gate).\n'
              '// The probe stays a REAL small match, exactly as in the paper: same matcher\n'
              '// entry, same Lowe ratio, absolute-distance gate and mutual cross-check as\n'
              '// the full pair — no new approximate scoring was invented.\n'
              '// Subset order: default = deterministic even integer stride over the row\n'
              '// index (cross-platform, samples every octave). The shipped canonical row\n'
              '// order is octave↓/scale↓/response↓ (canonical_feature_selector_v1), so\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_TOP=1 selects the FIRST kProbeGateRows rows\n'
              '// instead — the literal Wu "top-scale h" subset — as a measurable A/B arm\n'
              '// (do not flip the default without an A/B verdict).\n'
              '// DEFAULT 3 (ON since 2026-08-08); 0 reproduces the pre-08-08 candidate loop\n'
              '// byte-for-byte. N>0 arms the gate: candidates whose probe score < N never\n'
              '// enter the full GEMM/TVG/db path — and are REGISTERED in the probe-debt\n'
              '// ledger ([PROBE-DEBT], delivery-lossless hard line) so idle repay/finalize\n'
              '// re-match attempt every one of them later. Skipped pairs are counted per\n'
              '// frame (probe_gate jsonl) and per capture (finalize segments json).\n'
              'int ProbeGateMin() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_MIN")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [2026-08-08 SHIPPED ON, then ⚰️ REVERTED THE SAME DAY — user-signed\n'
              '    // "保证 live 云和交付云都无损"]\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // The gate shipped in the morning on a DELIVERED-cloud definition of\n'
              '    // lossless, and on that definition it holds: the two_view_geometries pair\n'
              '    // set is a strict SUPERSET of the gate-off arm (onlyA == 0 on three\n'
              '    // independent runs), the probe-debt ledger closes (registered 658 ==\n'
              '    // repaid 658, left == 0), and delivered points move +0.041% — inside noise.\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // The user then clarified the requirement: the AR LIVE cloud must be\n'
              '    // lossless too, because watching coverage grow shot by shot IS the product.\n'
              '    // Measured against that bar the gate fails: it defers 25.3% of candidate\n'
              '    // pairs out of the capture phase, so cap201\'s live model ends at 139,7xx\n'
              '    // instead of 141,048 — a systematic -1,256 points (-0.89%); the gate-off arm\n'
              '    // reproduced 141,048 exactly on all 9 runs.\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // [LIVE-GROW-NOW] repays that debt into the live model during capture idle\n'
              '    // and even overshoots the ceiling (141,637). But it only runs after 2 s of\n'
              '    // frame-idle, so a fast shooting cadence never triggers it and the live\n'
              '    // cloud is back to -0.89%. A guarantee cannot be conditional on how the user\n'
              '    // paces the shutter — so the gate goes back OFF and its -4.0% stream win is\n'
              '    // given back. OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_MIN=3 re-arms it (all the\n'
              '    // machinery — batch probe, debt ledger, live/finalize repay — stays wired).\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'constexpr int kProbeGateRowsDefault = 512;  // recon-fixed probe height\n'
              '// [PROBE-ROWS 2026-08-08] The probe\'s OWN cost is measurable: cap201 spends\n'
              '// 3.3s probing to save 6.0s of full GEMM (net -2.7s of a ~50s stream). Halving\n'
              '// the probe height should halve its cost while keeping the gate\'s purpose.\n'
              '// This is safe to tune because the gate is delivery-lossless BY CONSTRUCTION,\n'
              '// not by tuning: every skipped pair is registered in the probe-debt ledger and\n'
              '// re-matched at finalize (cap201: registered 658 == repaid 658, left == 0). A\n'
              '// worse probe therefore costs skip ACCURACY (fewer pairs deferred, or the wrong\n'
              '// ones), never delivered geometry. ⚠️ The score threshold ProbeGateMin() was\n'
              '// recon-calibrated at 512 rows — fewer rows shift the score distribution, so an\n'
              '// A/B must re-report skip rate together with the wall-clock.\n'
              'int ProbeGateRows() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_ROWS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 32 && v <= 8192) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return kProbeGateRowsDefault;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// [PROBE-BATCH 2026-08-08] Batched probe submission (default ON when the gate\n'
              '// is armed and the batch symbol is linked; =0 forces the 08-07 per-pair probe\n'
              '// form — kept as the A/B control arm).\n'
              'bool ProbeGateBatchEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_BATCH");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-08] Wu-faithful top-rows subset (see provenance note\n'
              '// above). DEFAULT ON since 2026-08-08: this is the literal paper subset (Wu\n'
              '// scores the top-scale features, not a stride sample), and it carried every\n'
              '// phase-2 arm that passed the lossless gates. =0 restores the 08-03 recon\'s\n'
              '// even-stride subset as the A/B control.\n'
              'bool ProbeGateTopRows() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_TOP");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(aether_sfm_session* s, int frame1,\n'
              '                           const uint8_t* d1, int n1, int frame2,\n'
              '                           const uint8_t* d2, int n2, double max_ratio,\n'
              '                           uint32_t* out_pairs, int max_pairs,\n'
              '                           int* out_num_matches) {\n'
              '  const auto match_once = [&] {\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '    if (s && s->descriptor_residency_nonce != 0 && frame1 >= 0 && frame2 >= 0 &&\n'
              '        aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_resident != nullptr) {\n'
              '      constexpr uint32_t kImmutableDescriptorGenerationV1 = 1;\n'
              '      return aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_resident(\n'
              '          s->descriptor_residency_nonce, static_cast<uint32_t>(frame1),\n'
              '          kImmutableDescriptorGenerationV1, d1, n1,\n'
              '          static_cast<uint32_t>(frame2), kImmutableDescriptorGenerationV1, d2,\n'
              '          n2, max_ratio, out_pairs, max_pairs, out_num_matches);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '    return aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs(d1, n1, d2, n2, max_ratio, out_pairs,\n'
              '                                       max_pairs, out_num_matches);\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  int rc = match_once();\n'
              '  for (int attempt = 0; rc == 7 && attempt < GpuMatchRetryLimit(); ++attempt) {\n'
              '    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(50 * (attempt + 1)));\n'
              '    if (s) ++s->stat_gpu_retry_attempts;\n'
              '    rc = match_once();\n'
              '    if (rc == 0 && s) ++s->stat_gpu_retry_recovered;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return rc;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendDescriptorResidencyStats(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '  if (!s || s->descriptor_residency_nonce == 0 ||\n'
              '      aether_gpu_match_descriptor_residency_stats == nullptr) {\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  uint64_t hits = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t misses = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t evictions = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t stale_replacements = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t upload_bytes = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t resident_bytes = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t resident_entries = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t allocation_failures = 0;\n'
              '  uint64_t device_resets = 0;\n'
              '  const int present = aether_gpu_match_descriptor_residency_stats(\n'
              '      s->descriptor_residency_nonce, &hits, &misses, &evictions,\n'
              '      &stale_replacements, &upload_bytes, &resident_bytes, &resident_entries,\n'
              '      &allocation_failures, &device_resets);\n'
              '  const int enabled = DescriptorResidencyEnabledCore() ? 1 : 0;\n'
              '  char line[640];\n'
              '  std::snprintf(\n'
              '      line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '      "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"descriptor_residency_v1\\",\\"schema\\":1,"\n'
              '      "\\"enabled\\":%d,\\"state_present\\":%d,\\"hits\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"misses\\":%llu,\\"evictions\\":%llu,\\"stale_replacements\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"upload_bytes\\":%llu,\\"resident_bytes\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"resident_entries\\":%llu,\\"allocation_failures\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"device_resets\\":%llu}",\n'
              '      static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), enabled, present,\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(hits),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(misses),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(evictions),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(stale_replacements),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(upload_bytes),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(resident_bytes),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(resident_entries),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(allocation_failures),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(device_resets));\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '#else\n'
              '  (void)s;\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AppendTailCacheStats(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheTraceEnabled()) return;\n'
              '  const TailCacheModeV1 mode = TailCacheMode();\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '  char line[512];\n'
              '  std::snprintf(\n'
              '      line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '      "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"tail_cache_v1\\",\\"schema\\":1,"\n'
              '      "\\"mode\\":\\"%s\\",\\"state\\":%d,\\"generation\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"invalidated_generation\\":%llu,\\"rebuild_parent_generation\\":%llu,"\n'
              '      "\\"dirty_reason\\":\\"%s\\",\\"images\\":%zu,\\"pairs\\":%zu}",\n'
              '      static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '      mode == TailCacheModeV1::kOn\n'
              '          ? "on"\n'
              '          : (mode == TailCacheModeV1::kShadow ? "shadow" : "off"),\n'
              '      static_cast<int>(s->tail_cache_epoch.state()),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(s->tail_cache_epoch.generation()),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(\n'
              '          s->tail_cache_epoch.invalidated_generation()),\n'
              '      static_cast<unsigned long long>(\n'
              '          s->tail_cache_epoch.rebuild_parent_generation()),\n'
              '      TailCacheDirtyReasonName(s->tail_cache_epoch.dirty_reason()),\n'
              '      s->tail_cache_epoch.image_count(), s->tail_cache_epoch.pair_count());\n'
              '  AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Guided-matcher sibling (same rc semantics; used by the enrichment verify\n'
              '// chain, where a transient rc=7 previously failed the whole spatial pair).\n'
              'int GpuMatchGuidedRetry(aether_sfm_session* s, const uint8_t* d1, int n1,\n'
              '                        const float* xy1, const uint8_t* d2, int n2,\n'
              '                        const float* xy2, double max_ratio,\n'
              '                        const float* matrix12, const float* matrix21,\n'
              '                        int guide_mode, float max_residual, uint32_t* out_pairs,\n'
              '                        int max_pairs, int* out_num_matches) {\n'
              '  int rc = aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_guided(\n'
              '      d1, n1, xy1, d2, n2, xy2, max_ratio, matrix12, matrix21, guide_mode,\n'
              '      max_residual, out_pairs, max_pairs, out_num_matches);\n'
              '  for (int attempt = 0; rc == 7 && attempt < GpuMatchRetryLimit(); ++attempt) {\n'
              '    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(50 * (attempt + 1)));\n'
              '    if (s) ++s->stat_gpu_retry_attempts;\n'
              '    rc = aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_guided(\n'
              '        d1, n1, xy1, d2, n2, xy2, max_ratio, matrix12, matrix21, guide_mode,\n'
              '        max_residual, out_pairs, max_pairs, out_num_matches);\n'
              '    if (rc == 0 && s) ++s->stat_gpu_retry_recovered;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return rc;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialCandidatePool = 12;\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialAnchorsPerFrame = 3;\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialPreliminaryInliers = 15;\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialFinalInliers = 30;\n'
              'constexpr double kSpatialPreliminaryMinInlierRatio = 0.10;\n'
              'constexpr double kSpatialFinalMinInlierRatio = 0.25;\n'
              'constexpr double kSpatialMatchRatio = 0.8;\n'
              'constexpr double kGuidedMaxErrorPixels = 4.0;\n'
              '// [POSE-DIRECT-E 2026-08-11] 自举带宽。实测(cap_1786414194441541,86 个相邻帧对、\n'
              '// 44,361 个已验证 TVG 内点):由 ARKit 相对位姿直接导出的 E,其 Sampson 残差\n'
              '// 中位 2.79px / p99 52px / max 93.8px(尾巴几乎全部来自第 0 帧,ARKit 刚初始化)。\n'
              '// 4px 精带只能容纳 63.3% 的真对应,128px 带容纳 100.000% —— 用户铁律"真对应一个\n'
              '// 都不能漏"要求后者。宽带只用于**自举**:先在宽带内取候选、由 RANSAC 反解出一个\n'
              '// 数据自证的 E,再用标准 4px 精带做最终重配,消歧能力不打折。\n'
              'constexpr double kPoseDirectBootstrapErrorPixels = 128.0;\n'
              '// [POSE-DIRECT-DIAG] 仅诊断:允许 env 覆盖自举带宽,用于定位单位/尺度问题。\n'
              'inline double PoseDirectBandPixels() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_POSE_DIRECT_BAND_PX");\n'
              '    if (!e || !*e) return kPoseDirectBootstrapErrorPixels;\n'
              '    const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '    return v > 0.0 ? v : kPoseDirectBootstrapErrorPixels;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// 纯旋转时 E 退化(t≈0),低于此基线不做 pose-direct。\n'
              'constexpr double kPoseDirectMinBaselineMeters = 0.02;\n'
              'constexpr double kSpatialFinalMaxErrorPixels = 1.0;\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialMaxTotalPairs = 1200;\n'
              '// Reserve finish-time budget for 2-of-3 confirmation, neighborhood expansion,\n'
              '// and the quadratic fallback. At 300-400 frames this still gives every frame\n'
              '// its best anchor before any frame consumes all three.\n'
              'constexpr int kSpatialInitialAnchorBudget = 720;\n'
              '\n'
              '// [SCAN-MATRIX ENV 2026-07-11] Threshold-scan hooks for the host gate matrix.\n'
              '// Same pattern as TriMinAngleDeg / FinalizeBaThreads: getenv once (static\n'
              '// cache), default == the shipped constant, so an UNSET environment is\n'
              '// bit-identical to the shipped binary (the tri-angle defaults return the exact\n'
              '// radian literal, not a fresh degree→radian conversion). Context: every one of\n'
              '// these gates was calibrated in the pre-DSP-fix era; the hooks let the scan\n'
              '// matrix re-price them per-arm without rebuilding. The matrix\'s first verdict\n'
              '// (T20 2026-07-11) re-priced all four tri-angle defaults 3.0°→2.0°; env\n'
              '// overrides remain live for future re-pricing.\n'
              'double EnvGateDouble(const char* name, double fallback) {\n'
              '  const char* e = std::getenv(name);\n'
              '  if (e && e[0]) {\n'
              '    const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0.0) return v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return fallback;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ① OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_TRI_MIN_ANGLE — live add_frame 2-view CREATION parallax gate,\n'
              '//    degrees in the env, radians out. Default = shipped 2.0°.\n'
              '//    [T20 2026-07-11] 3.0°→2.0° per the tri-angle scan matrix (cap44/cap45\n'
              '//    db-replay, all four gates moved together): T20 recovered the point count\n'
              '//    lost to the DSP-fix descriptors (+10-20%) with reproj/thickness inside\n'
              '//    the judge gates; T15 re-admitted low-parallax fuzz, T25 kept losing\n'
              '//    points. Combined-verified with the 6-scale DSP config before shipping.\n'
              'double LiveCreateTriMinAngleRad() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_TRI_MIN_ANGLE");\n'
              '    if (e && e[0]) {\n'
              '      const double deg = std::atof(e);\n'
              '      if (deg > 0.0) return deg * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0.03490658503988659;  // shipped 2.0° literal (T20)\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ② OFFICIAL_AETHER_GROW_REPROJ_PX — live TVG-inlier grow reprojection gate. Default 14.\n'
              'double LiveGrowMaxReprojPx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_GROW_REPROJ_PX", 14.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ②b OFFICIAL_AETHER_CREATE_REPROJ_PX — live NEW-POINT reprojection gate.\n'
              '// Default 10 (unchanged shipped value; unset == byte-identical behaviour).\n'
              '// Exists so the create gate can be swept the way the grow gate already can:\n'
              '// a 2026-07-26 provenance run showed 86.6% of the delivered cloud is authored\n'
              '// by this live triangulator and that 27.7% of what it creates is then thrown\n'
              '// away by official filtering (filter_max_reproj_error 4.0 / filter_min_tri_angle\n'
              '// 1.5deg). Tightening the GROW gate made that worse, not better — grow and\n'
              '// create compete, so an observation refused by grow spawns a fresh low-parallax\n'
              '// point instead. This knob tests the other half of that hypothesis.\n'
              'double LiveCreateMaxReprojPx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached =\n'
              '      EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_CREATE_REPROJ_PX", 10.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ②c OFFICIAL_AETHER_OFFICIAL_TRIANGULATE — call upstream\n'
              '// IncrementalMapper::TriangulateImage on each accepted frame, the way\n'
              '// controllers/incremental_pipeline.cc:817 does after registering an image.\n'
              '// DEFAULT OFF: unset leaves the shipped path byte-identical. The point of the\n'
              '// knob is to measure what the official triangulator finds on ARKit-registered\n'
              '// poses that the hand-written create/grow/merge misses.\n'
              'bool OfficialTriangulateImageEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_OFFICIAL_TRIANGULATE");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ②d OFFICIAL_AETHER_SELFDEV_TRIANGULATE=0 — turn OFF the hand-written live\n'
              '// create/grow/merge and leave point authoring entirely to upstream\n'
              '// IncrementalMapper::TriangulateImage (②c). DEFAULT ON: unset keeps the\n'
              '// shipped behaviour. Matching and the db writes are NOT affected — the\n'
              '// official triangulator consumes exactly those correspondences — only the\n'
              '// three live_recon mutation sites (AddPoint3D / AddObservation /\n'
              '// MergePoints3D) are skipped. Set ②c and clear this to get the pure\n'
              '// upstream-triangulation arm.\n'
              'bool SelfDevLiveTriangulationEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_SELFDEV_TRIANGULATE");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ②e OFFICIAL_AETHER_PAIR_DRAFT — display-layer temporary pair cloud.\n'
              '// [PAIR-DRAFT 2026-08-09 用户签"第2张要出云"] 第 2 张注册后在 live_recon 的\n'
              '// 克隆上跑官方 TriangulateImage,点只存 session 草稿区、只经 previewTracked\n'
              '// 兜底供 AR 预览;真模型零触碰、克隆三角化跑在专属线程(隔离 thread_local\n'
              '// PRNG,同 tail-shadow 的既有手法)⇒ 第 3 帧起交付/live 逐位=旧行为。\n'
              '// 直接拆门入模型的形态已判死(交付 -1.78%/live -3.1%,四跑零散布,违反双重\n'

              '\n'
              '// ②e OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_IGNORE_2VIEW=0 — let the OFFICIAL triangulator keep\n'
              '// two-view feature tracks. Upstream default is true ("By default, COLMAP\n'
              '// ignores two-view feature tracks in triangulation, resulting in fewer 3D\n'
              '// points than possible" — official FAQ), which confounded the arm-E\n'
              '// self-vs-official comparison: the official triangulator never even TRIED the\n'
              '// 2-view geometry that makes up most of the delta. Unset keeps upstream\'s\n'
              '// default, so shipped behaviour is byte-identical.\n'
              '// [TRI-2VIEW 2026-08-12 用户签决转正] 默认从 true 翻成 **false = 保留 2-view 观测**。\n'
              '// 官方选项帮助原文自陈 ignore_two_view_tracks=true "resulting in fewer 3D points\n'
              '// than possible"。两个真机 cap 用新尺子(Sim3 对齐 + 观测保留率 + ETH3D 体素归一化\n'
              '// + 米制核对)复核,方向与幅度一致、无一项变差:\n'
              '//   cap101: 点 +12.8% 观测 +9.2% reproj −1.7% 覆盖R@2cm 98.33% 精度P@2cm 95.09% 米制 0.972\n'
              '//   cap60 : 点 +9.5%  观测 +5.5% reproj −1.4% 覆盖R@2cm 98.00% 精度P@2cm 94.91% 米制 0.955\n'
              '//   浮点(密度归一)14.03%→13.78% 略降;track≥3 −1.4% 是分母效应(新增点多为 2-view)\n'
              '// ⚠️ 此前曾被旧的"逐点最近邻 churn"尺子以 45% 好点消失误判死刑 —— 那把尺子量的是\n'
              '// gauge 不是覆盖(见 [[project-pocketworld-pose-direct-e-verdict]])。\n'
              '// 逃生阀:OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_IGNORE_2VIEW=1 恢复旧行为。\n'
              'bool TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_IGNORE_2VIEW");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07] OFFICIAL_AETHER_BIRTH_MIN_TRI_ANGLE — host A/B\n'
              '// 专用"出生门"旋钮:一个 env(度)同时覆盖两处\n'
              '//   ① 点出生:triangulation.min_angle(官方 IncrementalTriangulator 的\n'
              '//      CREATION 视差角门,上游默认 1.5°;finalize phase1/phase2 已被\n'
              '//      OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_MIN_ANGLE(_P2) 抬到 2.0°,而流式 TriangulateImage\n'
              '//      的 official_options 构造点一直吃上游默认 1.5°);\n'
              '//   ② BA 后过滤:mapper.filter_min_tri_angle(FilterPoints/FilterAllPoints3D\n'
              '//      的存留视差角门,上游默认 1.5°,本文件此前从未改过)。\n'
              '// 教条依据=鬼层终审"根治只在 stage-1":交付层 3° 视差角过滤\n'
              '// (DeliverMinTriAngleDeg)是事后删,本实验把同一把刀前移到出生/BA 存留时。\n'
              '// 风险=低视差场景注册/增长变脆(MP-SfM 论文警告),故仅 host A/B。\n'
              '// DEFAULT UNSET(或 <=0)⇒ 两处全部保持现值,逐字节复现出货行为。\n'
              'double BirthMinTriAngleDeg() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BIRTH_MIN_TRI_ANGLE");\n'
              '    if (e && e[0]) {\n'
              '      const double d = std::atof(e);\n'
              '      if (d > 0.0) return d;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0.0;  // unset ⇒ 不覆盖\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07] 统一施加点:本文件每个\n'
              '// colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions 构造点在配置完成后调用一次。env 未设时\n'
              '// 严格 no-op(单变量纪律)。\n'
              'void ApplyBirthGateAB(colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions* o) {\n'
              '  const double b = BirthMinTriAngleDeg();\n'
              '  if (b > 0.0) {\n'
              '    o->triangulation.min_angle = b;        // ① 出生门\n'
              '    o->mapper.filter_min_tri_angle = b;    // ② BA 后 filter 门\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [A1B-V5 2026-07-27] Two controlled-comparison knobs for the async\n'
              '// preview-BA arm (defined here because the finalize apvba_summary emit needs\n'
              '// them before the arm\'s own code). The v4 matrix cleared every engineering\n'
              '// gate but left a reproducible point delta whose cause it could NOT isolate,\n'
              '// because two things changed at once.\n'
              '//   _PROPAGATE=0  — turn the ORB-SLAM-style correction propagation OFF while\n'
              '//     leaving everything else identical (= v3 semantics). The only way to\n'
              '//     price propagation on its own; v4 shipped it together with v3\'s\n'
              '//     zero-sqlite + backoff, which also moved every snapshot boundary.\n'
              '//   _MATCH_CADENCE=1 — kick at most ONE background pass per successful\n'
              '//     publish. Without it the arms are not comparable at all: a harvest-less\n'
              '//     tick returns ERR_NOT_REGISTERED, the Dart publish policy therefore does\n'
              '//     NOT reset its growth baselines, so it re-triggers every frame and the\n'
              '//     async arm kicks back-to-back — i.e. it runs strictly MORE global BA\n'
              '//     than the sync arm, and more BA means more FilterPoints/FilterFrames\n'
              '//     deletions. That confound is the leading explanation for the v4 delta,\n'
              '//     and this knob is what tests it.\n'
              'bool AsyncPreviewBaPropagate() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA_PROPAGATE");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'bool AsyncPreviewBaMatchCadence() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e =\n'
              '        std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA_MATCH_CADENCE");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// [A1B-V6 2026-07-27] _PARAM_ONLY=1 — the background pass solves PARAMETERS\n'
              '// ONLY (a single official AdjustGlobalBundle) and performs NO structural\n'
              '// mutation: no CompleteAndMergeTracks, no FilterPoints, no FilterFrames, no\n'
              '// retriangulation. Provenance: the v5 controlled 2x2 isolated the async\n'
              '// arm\'s entire point delta to a structural residual — the fingerprint was\n'
              '// 2-view deletions + track-merge differences, i.e. STRUCTURE decided on a\n'
              '// stale snapshot — while the cadence and propagation factors measured\n'
              '// +0.005pp/+0.008pp. Parameters perturbed a few frames late wash out in the\n'
              '// finalize\'s own stage-1/stage-2 convergence; structural divergence does\n'
              '// not. So v6 defers every structural decision to the synchronous finalize\n'
              '// (which redoes them from scratch over the full db anyway) and lets the\n'
              '// background thread touch only pose/point values.\n'
              'bool AsyncPreviewBaParamOnly() {\n'

              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ③ OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_TRI_ANGLE / OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_REPROJ_PX — RestoreTemporalDetail gates\n'
              '//    (tri-angle degrees in / radians out, reproj px). Defaults 2.0° / 3 px.\n'
              '//    [T20 2026-07-11] 3.0°→2.0°, moved in lock-step with the other three\n'

              'double TemporalDetailTriMinAngleRad() {\n'

              '    }\n'
              '    return 0.03490658503988659;  // shipped 2.0° literal (T20)\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'double TemporalDetailMaxReprojPx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_REPROJ_PX", 3.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ④ OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_PAIR_CAP — spatial-revisit enrichment budget override.\n'
              '//    UNSET (returns 0) = legacy behavior exactly: anchor budget 720, hard cap\n'
              '//    1200, quadratic fallback only when no spatial region confirmed, and the\n'
              '//    ACTUAL attempted-pair count is whatever the anchor funnel yields (cap45\n'
              '//    device run: 7). SET to N: N replaces the hard cap AND acts as a floor —\n'
              '//    AddSpatialRevisitMatches appends a top-up pass over the remaining\n'
              '//    beyond-K pairs (closest camera centers first) until N pairs were actually\n'
              '//    attempted. Every top-up pair still passes the unchanged verification\n'
              '//    chain (raw match ≥15 → TVG RANSAC ≥15 @10% → guided re-match → strict\n'
              '//    TVG ≥30 inliers @1px, ratio ≥0.25) before it reaches sqlite.\n'
              'int EnrichPairCapOverride() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_PAIR_CAP")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'int SpatialTotalPairBudget() {\n'
              '  const int cap = EnrichPairCapOverride();\n'
              '  // [KNIFE-A ③ 2026-07-11] max(default, N) semantics: OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_PAIR_CAP=N\n'
              '  // is a top-up FLOOR and must never REDUCE the legacy 1200 hard cap. The\n'
              '  // joint scan hit exactly this trap: N=300 silently shrank the anchor budget\n'
              '  // (min(720, 300)) and the total budget, so the "extra enrichment" arm was\n'
              '  // simultaneously STARVING the anchor funnel it was supposed to top up.\n'
              '  // UNSET stays bit-identical to the shipped binary.\n'
              '  return cap > 0 ? std::max(cap, kSpatialMaxTotalPairs) : kSpatialMaxTotalPairs;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'

              '// [P1-STAGE1-RECIPE 2026-07-11] Stage-1 refinement round/ftol overrides for\n'

              '// burned 5 rounds × thermal-slowed BA = 82.5 s and — through the AUTO enrich\n'

              '// accepting new attempts for the whole 83.7 s. Capping stage-1 rounds ends\n'

              '// 由本矩阵重验,默认零漂移。)\n'
              'int Stage1RoundsCap() {\n'

              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'double Stage1FtolOverride() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STAGE1_FTOL", 0.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [BA-STAGE1-FULL 2026-07-26, signed] The cap43-era stage-1 protections\n'
              '// (UTILITY QoS + halved ceres threads) assumed the enrichment was the\n'
              '// finalize critical path. cap_1785078141726265 proved that inverted after\n'
              '// the v2 matcher: enrich 35.6s < stage-1 46.7s, gate_wait=0 — the\n'
              '// protections were taxing the true critical path 2x to yield to the side\n'
              '// with 11s of slack. Defaults flip to full speed; the legacy posture stays\n'
              '// one env away for the heavy-revisit shape (cap46: enrich 137.9s vs window\n'
              '// 58.1s) where the old reasoning still holds.\n'
              'bool Stage1HalfThreadsLegacy() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STAGE1_HALF_THREADS");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'bool Stage1UtilityQosLegacy() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STAGE1_UTILITY_QOS");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [QUAD-PIPELINE 2026-07-26, signed] Order-preserving matcher prefetch for\n'
              '// the quadratic enrichment pass. Provenance: upstream COLMAP\'s own matching\n'
              '// architecture IS a matcher→verifier pipeline (vendored\n'
              '// colmap/controllers/feature_matching_utils.h:103-106, JobQueue chain with\n'
              '// concurrent matcher and verifier workers) — the serial per-pair\n'
              '// match→TVG loop in AddOfficialQuadraticPairs was our own simplification.\n'
              '// This variant keeps the EXACT bar the full-pipeline investigation\'s A5\n'
              '// clause demands: the GPU matcher (deterministic Metal + mutual\n'
              '// cross-check, no PRNG) runs on a prefetch thread, while TVG estimation and\n'
              '// all db writes stay on the enrichment thread in todo order — the RANSAC\n'
              '// thread-local PRNG stream is byte-identical to the serial loop.\n'
              '// cap_1785082211938612 measured the serial loop at 61.2s (444 pairs,\n'
              '// ~70-90ms GPU + ~40-60ms CPU each, fully serialized) with stage-1 waiting\n'
              '// 29s on it; overlapped, the pass approaches max(GPU_total, CPU_total).\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUAD_PIPELINE=0 restores the serial loop (also the\n'
              '// automatic fallback for resume sessions, whose db-load LRU cache must not\n'
              '// be read across threads).\n'
              '// [MATCH-TVG-OVERLAP 2026-08-08] Overlap the live candidate loop\'s GPU match\n'
              '// with its CPU two-view-geometry verification, the same way the finalize-side\n'
              '// AddOfficialQuadraticPairs already ships (see QuadPipelineEnabled below).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// The live loop is strictly serial today: match candidate i on the GPU, then\n'
              '// verify candidate i on the CPU, then move on — so each processor idles while\n'
              '// the other works. cap201 measures gpu 17.0 s and tvg 11.1 s of a 52.0 s\n'
              '// stream; overlapped, the pair costs max(17.0, 11.1) instead of their sum.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Why this is lossless on BOTH clouds (the standing requirement): nothing is\n'
              '// skipped, deferred or reordered. Every candidate is still matched and still\n'
              '// verified, and the consumer runs the TVG/db/grow section on ONE thread in the\n'
              '// unchanged candidate order — so the RANSAC thread_local PRNG stream is\n'
              '// byte-identical to the serial loop, exactly the argument the quadratic\n'
              '// pipeline was accepted on. The GPU matcher itself is deterministic (fused\n'
              '// kernel + mutual cross-check, no PRNG) and is serialised internally by\n'
              '// gMatchCallLock, so producer/consumer interleaving cannot change a result.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Restricted to the configuration where the loop makes NO extra matcher calls\n'
              '// of its own: the probe gate must be off (its per-pair route matches inside the\n'
              '// loop) and the epipolar-prior arm must be off. Both are the shipped defaults;\n'
              '// re-arming either falls back to the serial path automatically.\n'
              '// SHIPPED ON 2026-08-08 (user-signed). cap201, 12 interleaved pairs:\n'
              '//   live cloud  141,048 on ALL 24 runs — exactly equal, not "within noise"\n'
              '//   stream      paired-diff median -15.3% (min-vs-min -13.1%)\n'
              '//   delivered   see below\n'
              '// ⚠️ The delivered cloud looked -0.100% worse until the cause was found: this\n'
              '// pipeline\'s finalize is BIMODAL. stage-1 BA either runs the long path\n'
              '// (~7,300 ms) and stage 2 then needs 3 rounds, or the short path (~4,100 ms) and\n'
              '// stage 2 needs 4 — and the 4-round mode delivers ~250 fewer points at ~0.005\n'
              '// higher reproj. BOTH arms visit BOTH modes on identical input (serial 8:4,\n'
              '// overlap 3:9), so the mode is finalize\'s own nondeterminism (multi-threaded\n'
              '// Ceres reduction order flipping a convergence test), not something this change\n'
              '// introduced. CONDITIONED ON THE MODE the two arms are equivalent: 117,093 vs\n'
              '// 117,091 at 3 rounds, 116,842 vs 116,826 at 4 (0.002% / 0.014%).\n'
              '//   ⇒ any future delivered-cloud A/B on this pipeline must stratify by\n'
              '//     s2_rounds (or report the mode mix); an unstratified 0.1% is a mirage.\n'
              'bool MatchTvgOverlapEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_MATCH_TVG_OVERLAP");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool QuadPipelineEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUAD_PIPELINE");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ── [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] ARKit epipolar-prior guided matching — EXPERIMENT\n'
              '// ARM, DEFAULT OFF (unset env ⇒ byte-identical shipped behaviour). The\n'
              '// sign-off dossier knife: synthesize E = EssentialMatrixFromPose(\n'
              '// cam_b_from_cam_a) from the FED ARKit poses (FrameRecord.cam_from_world is\n'
              '// the raw prior — BA never writes it back), then run the EXISTING\n'
              '// COLMAP-parity guided kernel (guideMode=1, PrepareGuidedGeometry calibrated\n'
              '// path) so only candidates inside the epipolar band pay the descriptor\n'
              '// distance. Provenance: [官方] colmap MatchGuided semantics (the kernel is\n'
              '// already bit-parity with it); [调查 Track4] COLMAP guided accepts external\n'
              '// TwoViewGeometry / ORB-SLAM3-style widen-then-fallback / Sensors-2016\n'
              '// prior-noise sensitivity (95%→60% at 3σ ⇒ band must be gap-adaptive and\n'
              '// fallback mandatory). Classification: APPROXIMATE (restricting candidates\n'
              '// changes the Lowe-ratio denominator) — ships ONLY through the noise-band\n'
              '// quality gates + user sign-off; cap7 sized the prize (~300s of the 500s\n'
              '// wait is thermally-throttled GPU matching; band keep-fraction ~1-3% cuts\n'
              '// that FLOP nearly proportionally).\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_PRIOR_MATCH=1     master switch (default OFF)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_BASE_PX      band at gap 0 (default 30)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_PER_GAP_PX   widening per frame of gap (default 5)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_MAX_PX       band ceiling (default 150)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_FALLBACK_MIN      guided matches below this → redo\n'
              '//                                         the pair with the full GEMM (150)\n'
              'bool EpiPriorMatchEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_PRIOR_MATCH");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'double EpiBandBasePx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_BASE_PX", 30.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'double EpiBandPerGapPx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached =\n'
              '      EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_PER_GAP_PX", 5.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'double EpiBandMaxPx() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = EnvGateDouble("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_BAND_MAX_PX", 150.0);\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'int EpiFallbackMinMatches() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EPI_FALLBACK_MIN");\n'
              '    const int v = e ? std::atoi(e) : 150;\n'
              '    return v > 0 ? v : 150;\n'
              '  }();\n'

              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [INCREMENTAL-GLOBAL-BA 2026-07-13] Rolling capture-time global BA over\n'
              '// live_recon (DEFAULT OFF). See the session fields + MaybeIncrementalGlobalRefine\n'
              '// + the finalize collapse in RefineGlobalBA. All four knobs are env-gated so the\n'
              '// cap51 host A/B can sweep cadence/window/finalize-rounds without a rebuild.\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA=1        enable (default OFF → zero ship impact)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA_EVERY_N  refine cadence, registered frames (25)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA_WINDOW   free-pose sliding-window size (40)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_FINALIZE_ROUNDS    finalize global-BA round cap once the\n'
              '//                                         rolling refine has run (2 = "1-2 轮收尾")\n'
              '// [E3 2026-08-04] Streaming local-BA scheduling knobs. EXPERIMENT ONLY: with\n'
              '// neither variable set these reproduce the shipped behaviour exactly (enabled,\n'
              '// every frame), so installing them is a no-op for production.\n'
              '// Why they exist: the windowed local refinement measures 56.5% of stream cost,\n'
              '// and the founders\' own published architecture runs NO bundle adjustment inside\n'
              '// the incremental loop — RC exposes only a "Final model optimization", which is\n'
              '// itself user-disableable (sfmFinalModelOptimizationDraftMode). That made the\n'
              '// comparison purely theoretical until now.\n'
              '// ⚠️ Changing reconstruction output is a semantic change: host runs are\n'
              '// diagnostic only and can never select a production winner.\n'
              'bool StreamingLocalBaEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LOCAL_BA");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');  // default ON; only an explicit "0" disables\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int StreamingLocalBaPeriod(int session_value) {\n'
              '  static const int env_period = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_EVERY_N")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0;  // 0 = not overridden; the session field decides\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  if (env_period > 0) return env_period;\n'
              '  return session_value > 0 ? session_value : 1;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool IncrementalGlobalBaEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'

              '}\n'
              'int IncrementalGlobalBaEveryN() {\n'

              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 25;  // ~one bounded refine per 25 registered frames\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'int IncrementalGlobalBaWindow() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA_WINDOW")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 40;  // most-recent 40 frames get FREE poses; older = fixed anchors\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'int IncrementalFinalizeRounds() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_FINALIZE_ROUNDS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 2;  // finalize global BA rounds once the rolling refine pre-converged\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET 2026-07-11] Finalize-enrichment TIME gate. Target: the\n'
              '// cap46 device finalize, where the GPU db enrichment (dominated by 336\n'
              '// starved-frame re-match pairs at ~410 ms each on a still-hot GPU) ran\n'
              '// 137.9 s against a 58.1 s stage-1 window — enrichment became the finalize\n'
              '// critical path by +80 s. The gate stops STARTING new matcher attempts once\n'
              '// the budget is exhausted; whatever pair is in flight completes and is kept.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TIME_BUDGET_MS:\n'
              '//   unset      → AUTO: the budget is the ACTUAL stage-1 refinement window —\n'
              '//                enrichment stops accepting new attempts once the refine\n'
              '//                worker\'s stage-1 block has finished AND at least\n'
              '//                kEnrichAutoFloorMs have elapsed (the floor protects healthy\n'
              '//                captures from an early-converging stage 1; host D arms run\n'
              '//                their whole enrichment in 10-23 s, well under it). Where no\n'
              '//                stage 1 runs (OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_NO_OVERLAP=1, snapshot\n'
              '//                failure, resume/sync serial paths) AUTO never truncates —\n'
              '//                those paths keep legacy unlimited semantics.\n'
              '//   N > 0      → fixed wall budget of N ms for the enrichment passes (any\n'
              '//                path), for the host truncation-cost curve.\n'
              '//   N <= 0     → OFF: legacy unlimited (same-binary revert).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Debt priority under an armed gate: the enrichment thread runs the starved-\n'
              '// frame re-match FIRST (registration-critical repair pairs, gap-ascending —\n'
              '// the most valuable debt) and the spatial-revisit pass second (anchor funnel\n'
              '// rank-first, then the KNIFE-A ③ targeted top-up). With the gate off the\n'
              '// legacy order is preserved bit-identically.\n'
              'enum class EnrichBudgetMode { kOff = 0, kAuto = 1, kFixed = 2 };\n'
              'constexpr double kEnrichAutoFloorMs = 30000.0;\n'
              '\n'
              'EnrichBudgetMode EnrichBudgetModeOf() {\n'
              '  static const EnrichBudgetMode cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TIME_BUDGET_MS");\n'
              '    if (!e || !e[0]) return EnrichBudgetMode::kAuto;\n'
              '    const long v = std::atol(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0) return EnrichBudgetMode::kFixed;\n'
              '    return EnrichBudgetMode::kOff;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'double EnrichBudgetFixedMs() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TIME_BUDGET_MS");\n'
              '    const long v = e && e[0] ? std::atol(e) : 0;\n'
              '    return v > 0 ? static_cast<double>(v) : 0.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// True once the enrichment passes must stop STARTING new matcher attempts.\n'
              '// Reads enrich_start_ms (written by the enriching thread itself before the\n'
              '// passes start) and the stage1_done atomic (written by the refine worker).\n'
              'bool EnrichBudgetExhausted(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s || s->enrich_start_ms <= 0.0) return false;\n'
              '  switch (EnrichBudgetModeOf()) {\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kOff:\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kFixed:\n'
              '      return NowMs() - s->enrich_start_ms > EnrichBudgetFixedMs();\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kAuto:\n'
              '      return s->enrich_stage1_done.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&\n'
              '             NowMs() - s->enrich_start_ms > kEnrichAutoFloorMs;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return false;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Whether the gate can truncate THIS run (decides the debt-first order flip;\n'
              '// stage1_planned = the refine worker created a stage-1 cache snapshot).\n'
              'bool EnrichBudgetArmed(bool stage1_planned) {\n'
              '  switch (EnrichBudgetModeOf()) {\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kOff:\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kFixed:\n'
              '      return true;\n'
              '    case EnrichBudgetMode::kAuto:\n'
              '      return stage1_planned;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return false;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'enum class SpatialPairPurpose {\n'
              '  kAnchor,\n'
              '  kConfirm,\n'
              '  kExpansion,\n'
              '  kQuadraticAnchor,\n'
              '  kQuadraticConfirm,\n'
              '  kQuadraticExpansion,\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'struct VerifiedSpatialPair {\n'
              '  bool valid = false;\n'
              '  bool existing = false;\n'
              '  bool written = false;\n'
              '  int raw_matches = 0;\n'
              '  int initial_inliers = 0;\n'
              '  int final_inliers = 0;\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches matches;\n'
              '  colmap::TwoViewGeometry geometry;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'using SpatialPairCache = std::unordered_map<uint64_t, VerifiedSpatialPair>;\n'
              '\n'
              'uint64_t FramePairKey(int frame_idx1, int frame_idx2) {\n'
              '  const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::max(frame_idx1, frame_idx2));\n'
              '  const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::min(frame_idx1, frame_idx2));\n'
              '  return (static_cast<uint64_t>(hi) << 32) | lo;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-GROW-EXTRACT 2026-08-08] One side of a pair, as the live\n'
              '// create/grow/merge authoring needs it. Extracted from add_frame so the SAME\n'
              '// gates can be replayed later on a repaid probe-debt pair (see\n'
              '// ApplyProbeDebtGrowth) — the alternative was a second triangulator with its\n'
              '// own drifting gates, which is exactly what must not exist.\n'
              'struct LiveGrowView {\n'
              '  colmap::image_t image_id = 0;\n'
              '  const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>* points = nullptr;  // == Image::Points2D\n'
              '  const colmap::Camera* camera = nullptr;\n'
              '  colmap::Rigid3d cam_from_world;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              '// ── Incremental track growth into a live Reconstruction ──\n'
              '// Consult the RECON\'s Point2D->Point3D links (the single source of truth)\n'
              '// for track membership: create a new 2-view point or grow an existing\n'
              '// track by one observation. Querying the recon directly — not a side map —\n'
              '// means the per-frame floater filter can freely DeletePoint3D /\n'
              '// DeleteObservation (which reset those links) with NO dangling-id\n'
              '// bookkeeping. The windowed BA after the caller\'s loop refines these\n'
              '// poses+points.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Seed tracks from the GEOMETRICALLY-VERIFIED matches — the TwoViewGeometry\n'
              '// RANSAC (E/F/H) inliers, not the raw cross-checked pairs. A raw pair that\n'
              '// survives cross-check + Lowe ratio can still be geometrically inconsistent\n'
              '// (repetitive texture, specular highlights) → it seeds a floater the\n'
              '// creation gates don\'t always catch (the halo of scattered points around\n'
              '// the surface). Inliers remove those at the source.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// PURE MOVE (2026-08-08): the body below was lifted out of add_frame\'s\n'
              '// candidate loop unchanged; `prev`/`rec` became v1/v2, `s->live_recon` became\n'
              '// `recon`, and the four gate constants became parameters. The caller keeps\n'
              '// the kSelfDevTri opt-out (②d) and the stat_tvg_inlier_pairs accounting, so\n'
              '// the shipped live path stays bit-identical.\n'
              'void GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, colmap::Reconstruction* recon,\n'
              '    const LiveGrowView& v1, const LiveGrowView& v2,\n'
              '    const colmap::FeatureMatches& inliers, double min_tri_angle_rad,\n'
              '    double max_create_reproj_px, double max_grow_reproj_px,\n'
              '    double max_merge_reproj_px, bool merge_require_disjoint_images,\n'
              '    bool allow_merge, bool allow_grow,\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<PointIdPair, PointIdPairHash>* merge_trials,\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t>* touched) {\n'
              '  const Eigen::Matrix3x4d P_prev = v1.cam_from_world.ToMatrix();\n'
              '  const Eigen::Matrix3x4d P_cur = v2.cam_from_world.ToMatrix();\n'
              '  const Eigen::Vector3d c_prev = v1.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '  const Eigen::Vector3d c_cur = v2.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
