              '  const int grow_n = static_cast<int>(inliers.size());\n'
              '  for (int mm = 0; mm < grow_n; ++mm) {\n'
              '    const uint32_t i1 = inliers[mm].point2D_idx1;\n'
              '    const uint32_t i2 = inliers[mm].point2D_idx2;\n'
              '    if (i1 >= v1.points->size() || i2 >= v2.points->size()) continue;\n'
              '    const colmap::Point2D& o1 = recon->Image(v1.image_id).Point2D(i1);\n'
              '    const colmap::Point2D& o2 = recon->Image(v2.image_id).Point2D(i2);\n'
              '    const bool has1 = o1.HasPoint3D();\n'
              '    const bool has2 = o2.HasPoint3D();\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Both assigned: same track = nothing; different tracks = try a\n'
              '    // conservative live MergePoints3D. This directly attacks duplicate\n'
              '    // fragmented tracks without a finish-time global BA. The loop\'s\n'
              '    // input is now unconditionally the mandatory upright-TVG inlier set;\n'
              '    // there is no raw-match fallback that could glue unrelated texture.\n'
              '    if (has1 && has2) {\n'
              '      if (o1.point3D_id == o2.point3D_id) ++s->stat_already_assigned;\n'
              '      else {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_merge_needed;\n'
              '        // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] The live merge branch is a stand-in\n'
              '        // for the finish-time track merge we do not have DURING capture\n'
              '        // ("attacks duplicate fragmented tracks without a finish-time global\n'
              '        // BA"). A finalize-time replay HAS that finish-time pass — stage 2\'s\n'
              '        // CompleteAndMergeTracks — so replaying live merges there is not a\n'
              '        // recovery, it is a second, blinder merge on a mature model, where\n'
              '        // nearly every inlier already has both endpoints in tracks. Measured\n'
              '        // on cap201: replaying with merges enabled NET DELETED 1426 points\n'
              '        // (delivery -1.54% vs the -0.15% it was meant to close). Creation and\n'
              '        // observation growth — the two operations that actually restore what\n'
              '        // the skipped pair never got to author — stay on.\n'
              '        if (!allow_merge) continue;\n'
              '        const PointIdPair key =\n'
              '            CanonicalPointPair(o1.point3D_id, o2.point3D_id);\n'
              '        if (!merge_trials->insert(key).second) continue;\n'
              '        Eigen::Vector3d refit_xyz;\n'
              '        const MergeReject verdict =\n'
              '            CanMergeLivePoints(*recon, key.a, key.b, max_merge_reproj_px,\n'
              '                               merge_require_disjoint_images, &refit_xyz);\n'
              '        if (verdict == MergeReject::kNone) {\n'
              '          const colmap::point3D_t merged = recon->MergePoints3D(key.a, key.b);\n'
              '          // Install the union refit (the position the gate validated),\n'
              '          // not MergePoints3D\'s length-weighted average of two noisy\n'
              '          // low-parallax estimates. The windowed BA below refines it\n'
              '          // further (merged is in `touched`), and the post-BA reproj/\n'
              '          // tri-angle filters police it like any other point.\n'
              '          recon->Point3D(merged).xyz = refit_xyz;\n'
              '          touched->erase(key.a);\n'
              '          touched->erase(key.b);\n'
              '          touched->insert(merged);\n'
              '          ++s->stat_merge_accepted;\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_merge_rejected;\n'
              '          switch (verdict) {\n'
              '            case MergeReject::kSharedImage:\n'
              '              ++s->stat_merge_reject_shared_image;\n'
              '              break;\n'
              '            case MergeReject::kReproj:\n'
              '              ++s->stat_merge_reject_reproj;\n'
              '              break;\n'
              '            default:\n'
              '              ++s->stat_merge_reject_missing;\n'
              '              break;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // One side assigned: grow that Point3D by the unassigned observation.\n'
              '    // GATE the growth the same way creation is gated: the new observation\n'
              '    // must be in front of the growing camera AND reproject near the\n'
              '    // existing point. An UNGATED AddObservation lets a geometrically-wrong\n'
              '    // match (one that survived cross-check + Lowe ratio but is still a\n'
              '    // mismatch) attach a foreign-surface observation to a good point —\n'
              '    // geometry barely moves (the point isn\'t re-triangulated) but the\n'
              '    // track-based colorizer averages that stray pixel in → color bleed (a\n'
              '    // red object\'s pixel pulled into a brown floor point). Rejecting it\n'
              '    // fixes the color with no point-count loss (the keypoint stays free to\n'
              '    // seed its own point).\n'
              '    if (has1 || has2) {\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] The grow gate (14 px) is a CAPTURE-time\n'
              '      // allowance sized for ARKit drift, and during capture the local BA\n'
              '      // immediately re-solves the track it lands on. A finalize replay has\n'
              '      // neither excuse: the model is BA-converged, so a 14-px-off observation\n'
              '      // pushes its track past stage 2\'s filter_max_reproj_error and costs the\n'
              '      // whole point. Measured on cap201: replaying 1264 grown observations\n'
              '      // moved delivery from -0.151% to -0.196% and DROPPED n_obs by 222 below\n'
              '      // the no-replay arm. Off by default at replay time.\n'
              '      if (!allow_grow) continue;\n'
              '      const colmap::point3D_t pid = has1 ? o1.point3D_id : o2.point3D_id;\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t gimg = has1 ? v2.image_id : v1.image_id;\n'
              '      const colmap::point2D_t gidx = has1 ? i2 : i1;\n'
              '      const colmap::Rigid3d& gcfw =\n'
              '          has1 ? v2.cam_from_world : v1.cam_from_world;\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera& gcamera = has1 ? *v2.camera : *v1.camera;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector2d& gkp = has1 ? (*v2.points)[i2] : (*v1.points)[i1];\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d Xg = gcfw * recon->Point3D(pid).xyz;\n'
              '      if (Xg.z() <= 0.0) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_grow_rejected;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_grow_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> pg = gcamera.ImgFromCam(Xg);\n'
              '      const double grow_gate = max_grow_reproj_px;\n'
              '      if (!pg || (*pg - gkp).norm() > grow_gate) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_grow_rejected;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_grow_reject_reproj;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      recon->AddObservation(pid, colmap::TrackElement(gimg, gidx));\n'
              '      touched->insert(pid);\n'
              '      ++s->stat_grow_accepted;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Neither assigned: brand-new 2-view track. Triangulate + gate.\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nn1 =\n'
              '        v1.camera->CamFromImg((*v1.points)[i1]);\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nn2 =\n'
              '        v2.camera->CamFromImg((*v2.points)[i2]);\n'
              '    if (!nn1 || !nn2) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_reproj;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Eigen::Vector3d X_world;\n'
              '    if (!colmap::TriangulatePoint(P_prev, P_cur, *nn1, *nn2, &X_world)) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d X_prev = v1.cam_from_world * X_world;\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d X_cur = v2.cam_from_world * X_world;\n'
              '    if (X_prev.z() <= 0.0 || X_cur.z() <= 0.0) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(c_prev, c_cur, X_world) <\n'
              '        min_tri_angle_rad) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> rr1 = v1.camera->ImgFromCam(X_prev);\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> rr2 = v2.camera->ImgFromCam(X_cur);\n'
              '    if (!rr1 || !rr2) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_reproj;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if ((*rr1 - (*v1.points)[i1]).norm() > max_create_reproj_px ||\n'
              '        (*rr2 - (*v2.points)[i2]).norm() > max_create_reproj_px) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_create_reject_reproj;\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::Track track;\n'
              '    track.AddElement(v1.image_id, i1);\n'
              '    track.AddElement(v2.image_id, i2);\n'
              '    const colmap::point3D_t pid =\n'
              '        recon->AddPoint3D(X_world, std::move(track), Eigen::Vector3ub::Zero());\n'
              '    touched->insert(pid);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [E1 2026-08-04] Active connected-component count over the live pair graph.\n'
              '// A single capture session must resolve to exactly ONE component; two or more\n'
              '// means the pair topology fragmented, which is a stage-1 defect rather than a\n'
              '// quality tradeoff. Havlena\'s Table 8.1 reports this next to wall clock and\n'
              '// registered count, and it was the one column the frame_split record never\n'
              '// carried — so a fragmented session was indistinguishable from a healthy one in\n'
              '// telemetry. Union-find over the resolved-pair set costs microseconds against a\n'
              '// ~1 s frame; this is pure observation and changes no behaviour.\n'
              'int CountActiveComponentsV1(const aether_sfm_session& s) {\n'
              '  const int n = static_cast<int>(s.frames.size());\n'
              '  if (n <= 0) return 0;\n'
              '  std::vector<int> parent(static_cast<size_t>(n));\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) parent[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = i;\n'
              '  const auto find = [&parent](int x) {\n'
              '    while (parent[static_cast<size_t>(x)] != x) {\n'
              '      parent[static_cast<size_t>(x)] =\n'
              '          parent[static_cast<size_t>(parent[static_cast<size_t>(x)])];\n'
              '      x = parent[static_cast<size_t>(x)];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return x;\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  for (const uint64_t key : s.live_pairs_done) {\n'
              '    const int a = static_cast<int>(key >> 32);\n'
              '    const int b = static_cast<int>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);\n'
              '    if (a < 0 || a >= n || b < 0 || b >= n) continue;\n'
              '    const int ra = find(a);\n'
              '    const int rb = find(b);\n'
              '    if (ra != rb) parent[static_cast<size_t>(ra)] = rb;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // Count distinct roots reached from ACTIVE frames only. Withdrawn frames\n'
              '  // (image_id == 0) and featureless frames are not vertices of the graph, but a\n'
              '  // withdrawn frame can still be the union-find root of a live set, so the\n'
              '  // roots must be collected rather than tested with find(i) == i.\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<int> roots;\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& frame = s.frames[static_cast<size_t>(i)];\n'
              '    if (frame.image_id == 0 || frame.n_keypoints <= 0) continue;\n'
              '    roots.insert(find(i));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return static_cast<int>(roots.size());\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool IsQuadraticPurpose(SpatialPairPurpose purpose) {\n'
              '  return purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticAnchor ||\n'
              '         purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticConfirm ||\n'
              '         purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticExpansion;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool IsExpansionPurpose(SpatialPairPurpose purpose) {\n'
              '  return purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kExpansion ||\n'
              '         purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticExpansion;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void CopyMatrixRowMajor(const Eigen::Matrix3d& matrix,\n'
              '                        std::array<float, 9>* out) {\n'
              '  for (int row = 0; row < 3; ++row) {\n'
              '    for (int col = 0; col < 3; ++col) {\n'
              '      (*out)[row * 3 + col] = static_cast<float>(matrix(row, col));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void CopyPixelPoints(const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points,\n'
              '                     std::vector<float>* out) {\n'
              '  out->resize(points.size() * 2);\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i) {\n'
              '    (*out)[2 * i] = static_cast<float>(points[i].x());\n'
              '    (*out)[2 * i + 1] = static_cast<float>(points[i].y());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void CopyNormalizedPoints(const colmap::Camera& camera,\n'
              '                          const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& points,\n'
              '                          std::vector<float>* out) {\n'
              '  out->resize(points.size() * 2);\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i) {\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> normalized = camera.CamFromImg(points[i]);\n'
              '    (*out)[2 * i] = normalized ? static_cast<float>(normalized->x()) : 1e6f;\n'
              '    (*out)[2 * i + 1] =\n'
              '        normalized ? static_cast<float>(normalized->y()) : 1e6f;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void SetAllCameraIntrinsicsConstant(\n'
              '    const colmap::Reconstruction& reconstruction,\n'
              '    const std::vector<colmap::image_t>& image_ids,\n'
              '    colmap::BundleAdjustmentConfig& config) {\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::camera_t> camera_ids;\n'
              '  camera_ids.reserve(image_ids.size());\n'
              '  for (const colmap::image_t image_id : image_ids) {\n'
              '    if (!reconstruction.ExistsImage(image_id)) continue;\n'
              '    const colmap::Image& image = reconstruction.Image(image_id);\n'
              '    if (camera_ids.insert(image.CameraId()).second) {\n'
              '      config.SetConstantCamIntrinsics(image.CameraId());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool PrepareGuidedGeometry(const aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                           const FrameRecord& a,\n'
              '                           const FrameRecord& b,\n'
              '                           const colmap::TwoViewGeometry& geometry,\n'
              '                           std::vector<float>* xy_a,\n'
              '                           std::vector<float>* xy_b,\n'
              '                           std::array<float, 9>* matrix_ab,\n'
              '                           std::array<float, 9>* matrix_ba,\n'
              '                           int* guide_mode,\n'
              '                           float* max_residual,\n'
              '                           // [POSE-DIRECT-E 2026-08-11] 自举级要更宽的带;默认值\n'
              '                           // 保持出货口径,既有两个调用点逐字节不变。\n'
              '                           double max_error_pixels = kGuidedMaxErrorPixels) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !xy_a || !xy_b || !matrix_ab || !matrix_ba || !guide_mode ||\n'
              '      !max_residual) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  const bool calibrated =\n'
              '      (geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::CALIBRATED ||\n'
              '       geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::CALIBRATED_RIG) &&\n'
              '      geometry.E.has_value();\n'
              '  const bool uncalibrated =\n'
              '      geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::UNCALIBRATED &&\n'
              '      geometry.F.has_value();\n'
              '  const bool homography =\n'
              '      (geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::PLANAR ||\n'
              '       geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::PANORAMIC ||\n'
              '       geometry.config == colmap::TwoViewGeometry::PLANAR_OR_PANORAMIC) &&\n'
              '      geometry.H.has_value();\n'
              '\n'
              '  if (calibrated) {\n'
              '    CopyNormalizedPoints(a.camera, a.points, xy_a);\n'
              '    CopyNormalizedPoints(b.camera, b.points, xy_b);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(*geometry.E, matrix_ab);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(geometry.E->transpose(), matrix_ba);\n'
              '    // Match COLMAP\'s native two-view convention for unequal cameras: average\n'
              '    // each camera\'s pixel-to-normalized threshold.\n'
              '    const double normalized_error =\n'
              '        0.5 * (a.camera.CamFromImgThreshold(max_error_pixels) +\n'
              '               b.camera.CamFromImgThreshold(max_error_pixels));\n'
              '    *max_residual = static_cast<float>(normalized_error * normalized_error);\n'
              '    *guide_mode = 1;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (uncalibrated) {\n'
              '    CopyPixelPoints(a.points, xy_a);\n'
              '    CopyPixelPoints(b.points, xy_b);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(*geometry.F, matrix_ab);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(geometry.F->transpose(), matrix_ba);\n'
              '    *max_residual = static_cast<float>(max_error_pixels * max_error_pixels);\n'
              '    *guide_mode = 1;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (homography) {\n'
              '    if (!geometry.H->allFinite() || std::abs(geometry.H->determinant()) < 1e-12) {\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const Eigen::Matrix3d inverse = geometry.H->inverse();\n'
              '    if (!inverse.allFinite()) return false;\n'
              '    CopyPixelPoints(a.points, xy_a);\n'
              '    CopyPixelPoints(b.points, xy_b);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(*geometry.H, matrix_ab);\n'
              '    CopyMatrixRowMajor(inverse, matrix_ba);\n'
              '    *max_residual = static_cast<float>(max_error_pixels * max_error_pixels);\n'
              '    *guide_mode = 2;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return false;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [POSE-DIRECT-E 2026-08-11] 用两帧的 ARKit CamFromWorld 直接构造引导用的\n'
              '// CALIBRATED 几何(E = [t]_x R,cam_b_from_cam_a)。只做"候选搜索区域"的种子,\n'
              '// 绝不作为最终几何落库 —— 最终仍由 mandatory-gravity TVG RANSAC 从真实对应重估。\n'
              'bool BuildPoseDirectGuideGeometry(const FrameRecord& a,\n'
              '                                  const FrameRecord& b,\n'
              '                                  colmap::TwoViewGeometry* out) {\n'
              '  if (!out || !a.has_pose || !b.has_pose) return false;\n'
              '  const colmap::Rigid3d b_from_a =\n'
              '      b.cam_from_world * colmap::Inverse(a.cam_from_world);\n'
              '  // ⚠️ COLMAP 4.x 的 Rigid3d 访问器是成员函数(重力 RA 那次已踩过一次)。\n'
              '  if (!b_from_a.translation().allFinite()) return false;\n'
              '  // 纯旋转 ⇒ E 退化,交给既有路径(此时也没有视差可三角化)。\n'
              '  if (b_from_a.translation().norm() < kPoseDirectMinBaselineMeters) return false;\n'
              '  const Eigen::Matrix3d essential = colmap::EssentialMatrixFromPose(b_from_a);\n'
              '  if (!essential.allFinite()) return false;\n'
              '  out->config = colmap::TwoViewGeometry::CALIBRATED;\n'
              '  out->E = essential;\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Geometry-guided re-match core. Shared by the spatial-enrichment verify chain\n'
              '// (VerifySpatialPair) and the live temporal add_frame path\n'
              '// (AddFrameFeaturesImpl, OFFICIAL_AETHER_GUIDED_TEMPORAL=1). Pure w.r.t. session state\n'
              '// except for the rc=7 retry counters inside GpuMatchGuidedRetry — the\n'
              '// guided-pair/inlier telemetry is booked by each caller so spatial and temporal\n'
              '// totals stay separable.\n'
              'bool RunGuidedMatch(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                    const FrameRecord& a,\n'
              '                    const FrameRecord& b,\n'
              '                    const colmap::TwoViewGeometry& geometry,\n'
              '                    colmap::FeatureMatches* guided_matches,\n'
              '                    double max_error_pixels = kGuidedMaxErrorPixels) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !guided_matches || !s->options.use_gpu_match ||\n'
              '      aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_guided == nullptr) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::vector<float> xy_a;\n'
              '  std::vector<float> xy_b;\n'
              '  std::array<float, 9> matrix_ab{};\n'
              '  std::array<float, 9> matrix_ba{};\n'
              '  int guide_mode = 0;\n'
              '  float max_residual = 0.0f;\n'
              '  if (!PrepareGuidedGeometry(s, a, b, geometry, &xy_a, &xy_b, &matrix_ab,\n'
              '                             &matrix_ba, &guide_mode, &max_residual,\n'
              '                             max_error_pixels)) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  const int cap = std::min(a.n_keypoints, b.n_keypoints);\n'
              '  if (cap < kSpatialFinalInliers) return false;\n'
              '  std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '  int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '  // [P1-RC7-RETRY] transient rc=7 gets two backoff retries before the pair\n'
              '  // fails closed (see GpuMatchGuidedRetry).\n'
              '  const int rc = GpuMatchGuidedRetry(\n'
              '      s, a.descriptors.data(), a.n_keypoints, xy_a.data(),\n'
              '      b.descriptors.data(), b.n_keypoints, xy_b.data(), kSpatialMatchRatio,\n'
              '      matrix_ab.data(), matrix_ba.data(), guide_mode, max_residual,\n'
              '      pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '  if (rc != 0) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  guided_matches->resize(num_matches);\n'
              '  for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '    (*guided_matches)[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '    (*guided_matches)[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] ARKit-prior guided match for one pair (see the env\n'
              '// block for provenance and classification). Returns true with pair_buf/\n'
              '// num_matches filled when the guided path succeeded AND cleared the\n'
              '// fallback gate; false ⇒ caller runs the unchanged full GEMM (missing\n'
              '// poses / resume frames / rc failure / match-count collapse all land here —\n'
              '// the ORB-SLAM3-style safety net).\n'
              'bool ArkitGuidedMatchPair(aether_sfm_session* s, const FrameRecord& a,\n'
              '                          const FrameRecord& b, int gap, double ratio,\n'
              '                          uint32_t* pair_buf, int cap, int* num_matches) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !pair_buf || !num_matches || cap <= 0) return false;\n'
              '  if (!a.has_pose || !b.has_pose) return false;\n'
              '  if (a.descriptors.empty() || b.descriptors.empty()) return false;\n'
              '  if (!s->options.use_gpu_match ||\n'
              '      aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_guided == nullptr) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  colmap::TwoViewGeometry geo;\n'
              '  geo.config = colmap::TwoViewGeometry::CALIBRATED;\n'
              '  geo.E = colmap::EssentialMatrixFromPose(b.cam_from_world *\n'
              '                                          colmap::Inverse(a.cam_from_world));\n'
              '  std::vector<float> xy_a;\n'
              '  std::vector<float> xy_b;\n'
              '  std::array<float, 9> matrix_ab{};\n'
              '  std::array<float, 9> matrix_ba{};\n'
              '  int guide_mode = 0;\n'
              '  float max_residual = 0.0f;\n'
              '  if (!PrepareGuidedGeometry(s, a, b, geo, &xy_a, &xy_b, &matrix_ab,\n'
              '                             &matrix_ba, &guide_mode, &max_residual)) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // Gap-adaptive band (Sensors-2016: fixed narrow bands break when the\n'
              '  // prior degrades; ARKit relative-pose error grows with the frame gap).\n'
              '  const double band_px =\n'
              '      std::min(EpiBandMaxPx(), EpiBandBasePx() + EpiBandPerGapPx() * gap);\n'
              '  const double normalized =\n'
              '      0.5 * (a.camera.CamFromImgThreshold(band_px) +\n'
              '             b.camera.CamFromImgThreshold(band_px));\n'
              '  max_residual = static_cast<float>(normalized * normalized);\n'
              '  ++s->stat_epi_attempted;\n'
              '  const int rc = GpuMatchGuidedRetry(\n'
              '      s, a.descriptors.data(), a.n_keypoints, xy_a.data(),\n'
              '      b.descriptors.data(), b.n_keypoints, xy_b.data(), ratio,\n'
              '      matrix_ab.data(), matrix_ba.data(), guide_mode, max_residual, pair_buf,\n'
              '      cap, num_matches);\n'
              '  if (rc != 0) {\n'
              '    ++s->stat_epi_fallback;\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (*num_matches < EpiFallbackMinMatches()) {\n'
              '    ++s->stat_epi_fallback;\n'
              '    *num_matches = 0;\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool VerifySpatialPair(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                       int frame_idx1,\n'
              '                       int frame_idx2,\n'
              '                       SpatialPairPurpose purpose,\n'
              '                       SpatialPairCache* cache,\n'
              '                       int* attempted_total) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || !cache || !attempted_total || frame_idx1 == frame_idx2) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const int later = std::max(frame_idx1, frame_idx2);\n'
              '  const int earlier = std::min(frame_idx1, frame_idx2);\n'
              '  if (earlier < 0 || later >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size())) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const uint64_t key = FramePairKey(later, earlier);\n'
              '  const auto cached = cache->find(key);\n'
              '  if (cached != cache->end()) return cached->second.valid;\n'
              '  auto [it, inserted] = cache->try_emplace(key);\n'
              '  (void)inserted;\n'
              '  VerifiedSpatialPair& result = it->second;\n'
              '  const FrameRecord& a = s->frames[earlier];\n'
              '  const FrameRecord& b = s->frames[later];\n'
              '  if (a.n_keypoints <= 0 || b.n_keypoints <= 0) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  if (s->db->ExistsMatches(a.image_id, b.image_id)) {\n'
              '    result.existing = true;\n'
              '    if (!s->db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(a.image_id, b.image_id)) return false;\n'
              '    result.matches = s->db->ReadMatches(a.image_id, b.image_id);\n'
              '    result.geometry = s->db->ReadTwoViewGeometry(a.image_id, b.image_id);\n'
              '    result.raw_matches = static_cast<int>(result.matches.size());\n'
              '    result.initial_inliers =\n'
              '        static_cast<int>(result.geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '    result.final_inliers = result.initial_inliers;\n'
              '    result.valid = result.raw_matches >= kSpatialPreliminaryInliers &&\n'
              '                   result.final_inliers >= kSpatialFinalInliers &&\n'
              '                   static_cast<double>(result.initial_inliers) >=\n'
              '                       kSpatialFinalMinInlierRatio * result.raw_matches;\n'
              '    return result.valid;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [RESUME 2026-07-10, CORRECTED 2026-08-04] This used to state that\n'
              '  // resume-rebuilt FrameRecords carry NO in-memory descriptors/keypoints. That\n'
              '  // stopped being true when the mandatory-ARKit recovery contract made\n'
              '  // RebuildFrameRecordsForResume read keypoints+descriptors back in order to\n'
              '  // recompute each frame\'s identity digest: ACTIVE resumed frames now arrive\n'
              '  // fully featured, so resumed pairs really do reach the matchers here instead\n'
              '  // of being confined to the existing-matches fast path. Withdrawn frames are\n'
              '  // the only ones still guaranteed empty (RebuildFrameRecordsForResume mirrors\n'
              '  // remove_frame and clears them). The guard stays as fail-closed defense\n'
              '  // against a null descriptor pointer, but it is no longer the resume gate it\n'
              '  // was written to be — a behaviour change that is NOT covered by the\n'
              '  // host-verification evidence for mandatory-arkit-gravity-v1.\n'
              '  if (a.descriptors.empty() || b.descriptors.empty()) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  if (*attempted_total >= SpatialTotalPairBudget()) {\n'
              '    ++s->stat_spatial_budget_skipped;\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET] Time gate on FRESH matcher attempts only — the\n'
              '  // existing-matches db fast path above stays free, and an in-flight pair is\n'
              '  // never aborted. No-op unless a budget is armed (see EnrichBudgetExhausted).\n'
              '  if (EnrichBudgetExhausted(s)) {\n'
              '    ++s->stat_enrich_budget_stopped;\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  ++*attempted_total;\n'
              '  ++s->stat_spatial_pairs_attempted;\n'
              '  if (purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kAnchor ||\n'
              '      purpose == SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticAnchor) {\n'
              '    ++s->stat_spatial_anchor_attempted;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (IsExpansionPurpose(purpose)) ++s->stat_spatial_expanded_attempted;\n'
              '  if (IsQuadraticPurpose(purpose)) ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_attempted;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const int cap = std::min(a.n_keypoints, b.n_keypoints);\n'
              '  if (cap < kSpatialPreliminaryInliers) return false;\n'
              '  std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '  int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '  int match_rc = 1;\n'
              '  if (s->options.use_gpu_match) {\n'
              '    // Device finish-time policy is fail-closed: missing/erroring Metal never\n'
              '    // falls into O(N^2) CPU matching and turns a short finalize into minutes.\n'
              '    // [P1-RC7-RETRY] transient rc=7 gets two backoff retries first.\n'
              '    if (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs == nullptr) return false;\n'
              '    match_rc = GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '        s, a.frame_id, a.descriptors.data(), a.n_keypoints, b.frame_id,\n'
              '        b.descriptors.data(), b.n_keypoints, kSpatialMatchRatio,\n'
              '        pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '    if (match_rc != 0) return false;\n'
              '  } else {\n'
              '    match_rc = aether_sift_match_pairs(\n'
              '        a.descriptors.data(), a.n_keypoints, b.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '        b.n_keypoints, kSpatialMatchRatio, pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (match_rc != 0 || num_matches < kSpatialPreliminaryInliers) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '  for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '    matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '    matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches matches_for_tvg = matches;\n'
              '  const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;\n'
              '  const auto initial_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '      a, a.points, b, b.points, matches_for_tvg, tvg_options);\n'
              '  if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(initial_tvg)) return false;\n'
              '  colmap::TwoViewGeometry geometry = initial_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '  const int initial_inliers =\n'
              '      static_cast<int>(geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '  if (initial_inliers < kSpatialPreliminaryInliers ||\n'
              '      static_cast<double>(initial_inliers) <\n'
              '          kSpatialPreliminaryMinInlierRatio * num_matches) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Guided matching only proposes additional correspondences inside the\n'
              '  // initial geometry\'s 4px band. It does NOT prove they are inliers. COLMAP\'s\n'
              '  // global pipeline explicitly warns that writing guided candidates directly\n'
              '  // to two_view_geometries regresses reconstruction quality. Re-estimate TVG\n'
              '  // over the guided candidate set with the global mapper\'s strict gate before\n'
              '  // any correspondence reaches sqlite.\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches guided_matches;\n'
              '  if (!RunGuidedMatch(s, a, b, geometry, &guided_matches)) return false;\n'
              '  // [GUIDED-TEMPORAL 2026-07-12] Stat bookkeeping moved out of RunGuidedMatch;\n'
              '  // the spatial path books exactly the same guided-pair/inlier totals as before.\n'
              '  ++s->stat_spatial_guided_pairs;\n'
              '  s->stat_spatial_guided_inliers += static_cast<int64_t>(guided_matches.size());\n'
              '  colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions final_tvg_options;\n'
              '  final_tvg_options.ransac_options.max_error = kSpatialFinalMaxErrorPixels;\n'
              '  final_tvg_options.min_num_inliers = kSpatialFinalInliers;\n'
              '  final_tvg_options.min_inlier_ratio = kSpatialFinalMinInlierRatio;\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches guided_for_tvg = guided_matches;\n'
              '  const auto final_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '      a, a.points, b, b.points, guided_for_tvg, final_tvg_options);\n'
              '  if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(final_tvg)) return false;\n'
              '  colmap::TwoViewGeometry final_geometry = final_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '  const int final_inliers =\n'
              '      static_cast<int>(final_geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '  if (final_inliers < kSpatialFinalInliers ||\n'
              '      static_cast<double>(final_inliers) <\n'
              '          kSpatialFinalMinInlierRatio * guided_matches.size()) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  result.raw_matches = static_cast<int>(guided_matches.size());\n'
              '  result.initial_inliers = initial_inliers;\n'
              '  result.final_inliers = final_inliers;\n'
              '  result.matches = std::move(guided_matches);\n'
              '  result.geometry = std::move(final_geometry);\n'
              '  result.valid = true;\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool WriteVerifiedSpatialPair(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                              int frame_idx1,\n'
              '                              int frame_idx2,\n'
              '                              bool quadratic,\n'
              '                              SpatialPairCache* cache) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || !cache || frame_idx1 == frame_idx2) return false;\n'
              '  const int later = std::max(frame_idx1, frame_idx2);\n'
              '  const int earlier = std::min(frame_idx1, frame_idx2);\n'
              '  const auto it = cache->find(FramePairKey(later, earlier));\n'
              '  if (it == cache->end() || !it->second.valid) return false;\n'
              '  VerifiedSpatialPair& result = it->second;\n'
              '  if (result.existing || result.written) return true;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const FrameRecord& a = s->frames[earlier];\n'
              '  const FrameRecord& b = s->frames[later];\n'
              '  if (s->db->ExistsMatches(a.image_id, b.image_id) ||\n'
              '      s->db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(a.image_id, b.image_id)) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  s->db->WriteMatches(a.image_id, b.image_id, result.matches);\n'
              '  s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(a.image_id, b.image_id, result.geometry);\n'
              '  result.written = true;\n'
              '  ++s->stat_spatial_pairs_written;\n'
              '  s->stat_spatial_inliers += result.final_inliers;\n'
              '  if (quadratic) ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_written;\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> SelectDiverseAnchors(\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> pool, int temporal_k) {\n'
              '  std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> selected;\n'
              '  selected.reserve(kSpatialAnchorsPerFrame);\n'
              '  const int min_separation = std::max(3, temporal_k / 3);\n'
              '  for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : pool) {\n'
              '    bool separated = true;\n'
              '    for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& prior : selected) {\n'
              '      if (std::abs(candidate.j - prior.j) < min_separation) {\n'
              '        separated = false;\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (separated) selected.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '    if (static_cast<int>(selected.size()) == kSpatialAnchorsPerFrame) break;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : pool) {\n'
              '    if (static_cast<int>(selected.size()) == kSpatialAnchorsPerFrame) break;\n'
              '    const bool duplicate = std::any_of(\n'
              '        selected.begin(), selected.end(), [&](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& x) {\n'
              '          return x.i == candidate.i && x.j == candidate.j;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '    if (!duplicate) selected.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  for (int rank = 0; rank < static_cast<int>(selected.size()); ++rank) {\n'
              '    selected[rank].anchor_rank = rank;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return selected;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> BuildSpatialAnchors(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, int temporal_k) {\n'
              '  constexpr double kPrimaryDistanceMeters = 1.0;\n'
              '  constexpr double kFallbackDistanceMeters = 1.5;\n'
              '  constexpr double kPrimaryAngleRadians = 45.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '  constexpr double kFallbackAngleRadians = 60.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '  const double primary_min_dot = std::cos(kPrimaryAngleRadians);\n'
              '  const double fallback_min_dot = std::cos(kFallbackAngleRadians);\n'
              '  std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> anchors;\n'
              '\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()); ++i) {\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& current = s->frames[i];\n'
              '    if (!current.has_pose) continue;\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d center = current.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d forward = CameraForwardWorld(current.cam_from_world);\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> primary;\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> fallback;\n'
              '    for (int j = 0; j + temporal_k < i; ++j) {\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& previous = s->frames[j];\n'
              '      if (!previous.has_pose) continue;\n'
              '      const double distance =\n'
              '          (center - previous.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc()).norm();\n'
              '      if (distance > kFallbackDistanceMeters) continue;\n'
              '      const double dot = std::max(\n'
              '          -1.0, std::min(1.0, forward.dot(CameraForwardWorld(previous.cam_from_world))));\n'
              '      if (dot < fallback_min_dot) continue;\n'
              '      const double angle = std::acos(dot);\n'
              '      SpatialRevisitCandidate candidate;\n'
              '      candidate.i = i;\n'
              '      candidate.j = j;\n'
              '      candidate.distance_m = distance;\n'
              '      candidate.angle_rad = angle;\n'
              '      if (distance <= kPrimaryDistanceMeters && dot >= primary_min_dot) {\n'
              '        candidate.score = distance / kPrimaryDistanceMeters +\n'
              '                          angle / kPrimaryAngleRadians;\n'
              '        primary.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        candidate.score = 2.0 + distance / kFallbackDistanceMeters +\n'
              '                          angle / kFallbackAngleRadians;\n'
              '        fallback.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const auto quality_order = [](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& a,\n'
              '                                  const SpatialRevisitCandidate& b) {\n'
              '      if (a.score != b.score) return a.score < b.score;\n'
              '      return std::abs(a.i - a.j) > std::abs(b.i - b.j);\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    std::sort(primary.begin(), primary.end(), quality_order);\n'
              '    std::sort(fallback.begin(), fallback.end(), quality_order);\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> pool;\n'
              '    pool.reserve(kSpatialCandidatePool);\n'
              '    for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : primary) {\n'
              '      if (static_cast<int>(pool.size()) == kSpatialCandidatePool) break;\n'
              '      pool.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : fallback) {\n'
              '      if (static_cast<int>(pool.size()) == kSpatialCandidatePool) break;\n'
              '      pool.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->stat_spatial_pairs_considered += pool.size();\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> selected =\n'
              '        SelectDiverseAnchors(std::move(pool), temporal_k);\n'
              '    anchors.insert(anchors.end(), selected.begin(), selected.end());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return anchors;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> BuildQuadraticAnchors(\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_session* s, int temporal_k) {\n'
              '  std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> anchors;\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()); ++i) {\n'
              '    int rank = 0;\n'
              '    for (int offset = 1; offset <= i; offset *= 2) {\n'
              '      if (offset > temporal_k) {\n'
              '        SpatialRevisitCandidate candidate;\n'
              '        candidate.i = i;\n'
              '        candidate.j = i - offset;\n'
              '        candidate.score = static_cast<double>(rank);\n'
              '        candidate.anchor_rank = rank;\n'
              '        candidate.quadratic = true;\n'
              '        anchors.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '        if (++rank == kSpatialAnchorsPerFrame) break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (offset > i / 2) break;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return anchors;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int ProcessRevisitAnchors(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                          std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> anchors,\n'
              '                          bool quadratic,\n'
              '                          int anchor_attempt_limit,\n'
              '                          SpatialPairCache* cache,\n'
              '                          int* attempted_total) {\n'
              '  std::sort(anchors.begin(), anchors.end(),\n'
              '            [](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& a,\n'
              '               const SpatialRevisitCandidate& b) {\n'
              '              // Rank-first scheduling gives every frame its best candidate\n'
              '              // before any frame consumes candidate two or three. Quality then\n'
              '              // decides globally, so a chronological early return cannot starve\n'
              '              // the tail of a long capture.\n'
              '              if (a.anchor_rank != b.anchor_rank) {\n'
              '                return a.anchor_rank < b.anchor_rank;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              if (a.score != b.score) return a.score < b.score;\n'
              '              if (a.i != b.i) return a.i < b.i;\n'
              '              return a.j < b.j;\n'
              '            });\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> successful;\n'
              '  size_t anchor_index = 0;\n'
              '  for (; anchor_index < anchors.size(); ++anchor_index) {\n'
              '    if (*attempted_total >= anchor_attempt_limit) break;\n'
              '    const SpatialPairPurpose purpose =\n'
              '        quadratic ? SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticAnchor\n'
              '                  : SpatialPairPurpose::kAnchor;\n'
              '    if (VerifySpatialPair(s, anchors[anchor_index].i, anchors[anchor_index].j,\n'
              '                          purpose, cache, attempted_total)) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_spatial_anchor_passed;\n'
              '      successful.push_back(anchors[anchor_index]);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (anchor_index < anchors.size()) {\n'
              '    s->stat_spatial_budget_skipped += anchors.size() - anchor_index;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::sort(successful.begin(), successful.end(),\n'
              '            [&](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& a,\n'
              '                const SpatialRevisitCandidate& b) {\n'
              '              const int a_inliers = cache->at(FramePairKey(a.i, a.j)).final_inliers;\n'
              '              const int b_inliers = cache->at(FramePairKey(b.i, b.j)).final_inliers;\n'
              '              if (a_inliers != b_inliers) return a_inliers > b_inliers;\n'
              '              return a.score < b.score;\n'
              '            });\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Non-max suppression avoids proving and expanding the same physical revisit\n'
              '  // dozens of times when adjacent frames all selected the same loop closure.\n'
              '  std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> seeds;\n'
              '  for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : successful) {\n'
              '    const bool overlaps = std::any_of(\n'
              '        seeds.begin(), seeds.end(), [&](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& seed) {\n'
              '          return std::abs(candidate.i - seed.i) <= 4 &&\n'
              '                 std::abs(candidate.j - seed.j) <= 4;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '    if (!overlaps) seeds.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int confirmed_regions = 0;\n'
              '  for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& seed : seeds) {\n'
              '    int available = 0;\n'
              '    int passed = 0;\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> support_pairs;\n'
              '    for (int delta = -1; delta <= 1; ++delta) {\n'
              '      const int i = seed.i + delta;\n'
              '      const int j = seed.j + delta;\n'
              '      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) ||\n'
              '          j >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) || i == j) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++available;\n'
              '      const SpatialPairPurpose purpose =\n'
              '          quadratic ? SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticConfirm\n'
              '                    : SpatialPairPurpose::kConfirm;\n'
              '      if (VerifySpatialPair(s, i, j, purpose, cache, attempted_total)) {\n'
              '        ++passed;\n'
              '        support_pairs.emplace_back(i, j);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (available < 2 || passed < 2) continue;\n'
              '\n'
              '    ++confirmed_regions;\n'
              '    ++s->stat_spatial_regions_confirmed;\n'
              '    for (const auto& pair : support_pairs) {\n'
              '      WriteVerifiedSpatialPair(s, pair.first, pair.second, quadratic, cache);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // A proven anchor seeds the complete local covisibility neighborhood. Each\n'
              '    // expanded pair still has to independently pass raw TVG, guided matching,\n'
              '    // >=20 inliers, and >=10% initial inlier ratio before it reaches sqlite.\n'
              '    for (int di = -2; di <= 2; ++di) {\n'
              '      for (int dj = -2; dj <= 2; ++dj) {\n'
              '        const int i = seed.i + di;\n'
              '        const int j = seed.j + dj;\n'
              '        if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) ||\n'
              '            j >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) || i == j) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const SpatialPairPurpose purpose =\n'
              '            quadratic ? SpatialPairPurpose::kQuadraticExpansion\n'
              '                      : SpatialPairPurpose::kExpansion;\n'
              '        if (VerifySpatialPair(s, i, j, purpose, cache, attempted_total)) {\n'
              '          WriteVerifiedSpatialPair(s, i, j, quadratic, cache);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return confirmed_regions;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A ③ 2026-07-11] Snapshot the delivered model\'s 2-view / low-parallax\n'
              '// tracks into the enrichment-targeting hint (see EnrichTargetHint). Called by\n'
              '// the refine worker on the live-reuse path BEFORE the enrichment thread spawns\n'
              '// (the model is mutated concurrently afterwards). Track positions are the\n'
              '// pre-refine live estimates — noisy in depth, but the targeting predicate only\n'
              '// needs coarse visibility + parallax direction, both of which survive that\n'
              '// noise.\n'
              'constexpr double kEnrichTargetLowParallaxRad = 3.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              'constexpr double kEnrichTargetGainRad = 5.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '\n'
              'std::shared_ptr<const EnrichTargetHint> BuildEnrichTargetHint(\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_session& s, const colmap::Reconstruction& recon) {\n'
              '  auto hint = std::make_shared<EnrichTargetHint>();\n'
              '  std::unordered_map<colmap::image_t, int> idx_of;\n'
              '  idx_of.reserve(s.frames.size());\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(s.frames.size()); ++i) {\n'
              '    idx_of[s.frames[i].image_id] = i;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  hint->by_frame.assign(s.frames.size(), {});\n'
              '  std::vector<int> fidx;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [pid, pt] : recon.Points3D()) {\n'
              '    const auto& els = pt.track.Elements();\n'
              '    if (els.size() < 2) continue;\n'
              '    fidx.clear();\n'
              '    for (const auto& el : els) {\n'
              '      const auto it = idx_of.find(el.image_id);\n'
              '      if (it == idx_of.end() || !s.frames[it->second].has_pose) continue;\n'
              '      if (std::find(fidx.begin(), fidx.end(), it->second) == fidx.end()) {\n'
              '        fidx.push_back(it->second);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (fidx.size() < 2) continue;\n'
              '    double max_ang = 0.0;\n'
              '    for (size_t a = 0; a < fidx.size(); ++a) {\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d ca =\n'
              '          s.frames[fidx[a]].cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '      for (size_t b = a + 1; b < fidx.size(); ++b) {\n'
              '        max_ang = std::max(\n'
              '            max_ang,\n'
              '            colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                ca, s.frames[fidx[b]].cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc(),\n'
              '                pt.xyz));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (els.size() != 2 && max_ang >= kEnrichTargetLowParallaxRad) continue;\n'
              '    const int32_t tid = static_cast<int32_t>(hint->tracks.size());\n'
              '    hint->tracks.push_back({pt.xyz, fidx});\n'
              '    for (const int f : fidx) hint->by_frame[f].push_back(tid);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return hint;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A ③] How many hint tracks can pair (i, j) upgrade: a track observed\n'
              '// by ONE side while the OTHER side (a) does not already observe it, (b) sees\n'
              '// its position inside the frame with positive depth, and (c) adds ≥5° parallax\n'
              '// against at least one existing observation. Pure poses + current positions —\n'
              '// no descriptors touched.\n'
              'int ScoreEnrichPair(const aether_sfm_session& s, const EnrichTargetHint& hint,\n'
              '                    int i, int j) {\n'
              '  const auto sees = [&](const FrameRecord& fr, const Eigen::Vector3d& X) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d xc = fr.cam_from_world * X;\n'
              '    if (xc.z() <= 0.0) return false;\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> px = fr.camera.ImgFromCam(xc);\n'
              '    return px && px->x() >= 0.0 && px->y() >= 0.0 &&\n'
              '           px->x() < static_cast<double>(fr.camera.width) &&\n'
              '           px->y() < static_cast<double>(fr.camera.height);\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  const auto count_dir = [&](int obs_f, int new_f) {\n'
              '    if (obs_f < 0 || obs_f >= static_cast<int>(hint.by_frame.size())) return 0;\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& nf = s.frames[new_f];\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d cn = nf.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '    int n = 0;\n'
              '    for (const int32_t tid : hint.by_frame[obs_f]) {\n'
              '      const EnrichTargetHint::LowTrack& t = hint.tracks[tid];\n'
              '      if (std::find(t.frame_idxs.begin(), t.frame_idxs.end(), new_f) !=\n'
              '          t.frame_idxs.end()) {\n'
              '        continue;  // both sides already observe it — no third view to gain\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!sees(nf, t.xyz)) continue;\n'
              '      for (const int f : t.frame_idxs) {\n'
              '        if (colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                cn, s.frames[f].cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc(), t.xyz) >=\n'
              '            kEnrichTargetGainRad) {\n'
              '          ++n;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return n;\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  return count_dir(i, j) + count_dir(j, i);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AddSpatialRevisitMatches(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly) return;\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || s->frames.size() < 3 || s->camera_id == 0) return;\n'
              '  const int temporal_k = s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12;\n'
              '  SpatialPairCache cache;\n'
              '  cache.reserve(kSpatialMaxTotalPairs * 2);\n'
              '  int attempted_total = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> spatial_anchors =\n'
              '      BuildSpatialAnchors(s, temporal_k);\n'
              '  const int spatial_regions = ProcessRevisitAnchors(\n'
              '      s, spatial_anchors, false,\n'
              '      std::min(kSpatialInitialAnchorBudget, SpatialTotalPairBudget()), &cache,\n'
              '      &attempted_total);\n'
              '\n'
              '  // COLMAP-style quadratic overlap is a sparse safety net, not parallel blind\n'
              '  // work: only use powers-of-two temporal gaps when ARKit produced no confirmed\n'
              '  // multi-frame revisit region at all.\n'
              '  if (spatial_regions == 0 && attempted_total < SpatialTotalPairBudget()) {\n'
              '    const std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> quadratic_anchors =\n'
              '        BuildQuadraticAnchors(s, temporal_k);\n'
              '    ProcessRevisitAnchors(s, quadratic_anchors, true, SpatialTotalPairBudget(),\n'
              '                          &cache, &attempted_total);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [SCAN-MATRIX ENV ④ 2026-07-11] Enrichment top-up: with\n'
              '  // OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_PAIR_CAP=N set, N is a floor as well as the cap. The anchor\n'
              '  // funnel alone can starve enrichment (cap45 device run attempted only 7\n'
              '  // pairs — a short orbit barely produces pose-gated revisit candidates), so\n'
              '  // the matrix could never price "what do 200/300 extra finalize matches\n'
              '  // buy". Enumerate every beyond-K pair, closest camera centers first, and\n'
              '  // keep attempting until N pairs were actually matched. Verification is the\n'
              '  // unchanged strict chain; anchor-pass pairs that verified but were left\n'
              '  // unwritten pending 2-of-3 region confirmation are flushed from the cache\n'
              '  // for free. UNSET env skips this block entirely — default behavior is\n'
              '  // bit-identical to the pre-hook binary.\n'
              '  const int enrich_cap = EnrichPairCapOverride();\n'
              '  if (enrich_cap > 0) {\n'
              '    // [KNIFE-A ③ 2026-07-11] Targeted ordering: with OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TARGETED=1\n'
              '    // and a hint snapshot available (live-reuse finalize), rank the top-up\n'
              '    // pairs by how many 2-view/low-parallax tracks each pair can hand a\n'
              '    // ≥5°-parallax third observation — the observation TrackUpgrade then\n'
              '    // banks — instead of by camera-center proximity, which is blind to the\n'
              '    // upgrade demand. Zero-score pairs keep the legacy distance order behind\n'
              '    // the scored ones, so the floor semantics still fills to N either way.\n'
              '    const std::shared_ptr<const EnrichTargetHint> hint =\n'
              '        EnrichTargetedEnabled() ? s->enrich_hint : nullptr;\n'
              '    if (hint) {\n'
              '      s->stat_enrich_targeted_tracks =\n'
              '          static_cast<int64_t>(hint->tracks.size());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::vector<SpatialRevisitCandidate> topup;\n'
              '    const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < num_frames; ++i) {\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& frame_i = s->frames[i];\n'
              '      for (int j = 0; j + temporal_k < i; ++j) {\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& frame_j = s->frames[j];\n'
              '        SpatialRevisitCandidate candidate;\n'
              '        candidate.i = i;\n'
              '        candidate.j = j;\n'
              '        if (frame_i.has_pose && frame_j.has_pose) {\n'
              '          candidate.distance_m =\n'
              '              (frame_i.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc() -\n'
              '               frame_j.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc())\n'
              '                  .norm();\n'
              '          candidate.score = candidate.distance_m;\n'
              '          if (hint) {\n'
              '            candidate.target_score = ScoreEnrichPair(*s, *hint, i, j);\n'
              '            if (candidate.target_score > 0) ++s->stat_enrich_targeted_scored;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          // Pose-less frames sort last, smaller temporal gaps first.\n'
              '          candidate.score = 1.0e9 + static_cast<double>(i - j);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        topup.push_back(candidate);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::sort(topup.begin(), topup.end(),\n'
              '              [](const SpatialRevisitCandidate& a,\n'
              '                 const SpatialRevisitCandidate& b) {\n'
              '                if (a.target_score != b.target_score) {\n'
              '                  return a.target_score > b.target_score;  // upgrades first\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                if (a.score != b.score) return a.score < b.score;\n'
              '                if (a.i != b.i) return a.i < b.i;\n'
              '                return a.j < b.j;\n'
              '              });\n'
              '    if (hint) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] enrich top-up targeted: low-parallax "\n'
              '                      "tracks="\n'
              '                   << s->stat_enrich_targeted_tracks\n'
              '                   << " scored_pairs=" << s->stat_enrich_targeted_scored\n'
              '                   << " of " << topup.size() << " beyond-K pairs";\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    for (const SpatialRevisitCandidate& candidate : topup) {\n'
              '      if (attempted_total >= enrich_cap) break;\n'
              '      if (VerifySpatialPair(s, candidate.i, candidate.j,\n'
              '                            SpatialPairPurpose::kExpansion, &cache,\n'
              '                            &attempted_total)) {\n'
              '        WriteVerifiedSpatialPair(s, candidate.i, candidate.j, false, &cache);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A 2026-07-11] Forward declaration (defined after RestoreTemporalDetail\n'
              '// next to its main consumer UpgradeLowParallaxTracks): pose-fixed point-only\n'
              '// Gauss-Newton polish used by both the ① grow refit and the ② track upgrade.\n'
              'void PolishPointGN(const std::vector<const colmap::Image*>& images,\n'
              '                   const std::vector<const colmap::Camera*>& cameras,\n'
              '                   const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& obs_px,\n'
              '                   Eigen::Vector3d* xyz);\n'
              '\n'
              '// Rebuild the dense, locally stable detail layer after the global camera solve.\n'
              '// Spatial/guided pairs have already done their job by constraining loop closure;\n'
              '// this pass consumes only the original temporal K-neighbor TVG inliers. Camera\n'
              '// poses and intrinsics stay fixed: the pass may create points or add a clean\n'
              '// observation, but never merges existing points and never runs another BA.\n'
              '// Processing larger temporal gaps first gives each new point the strongest\n'
              '// available baseline inside K before adjacent frames try to grow its track.\n'
              'void RestoreTemporalDetail(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                           colmap::Reconstruction* reconstruction) {\n'
              '  if (kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly) return;\n'
              '  if (!s || !reconstruction || s->frames.size() < 2) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [SCAN-MATRIX ENV ③] defaults = shipped 2.0° (T20) / 3 px; OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_TRI_ANGLE /\n'
              '  // OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_REPROJ_PX override for the host gate matrix.\n'
              '  const double kMinTriAngleRad = TemporalDetailTriMinAngleRad();\n'
              '  // 39-capture replay: 4 px kept one 2.8x-q99 ray; 3 px retained ~62.7k\n'
              '  // delivered points while reducing all added points below 1.18x-q99.\n'
              '  const double kMaxReprojPx = TemporalDetailMaxReprojPx();\n'
              '  // [KNIFE-A ① 2026-07-11] OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_GROW_REFIT=1: grow acceptance becomes a\n'
              '  // union refit (CanMergeLivePoints recipe) instead of "≤3 px at the CURRENT,\n'
              '  // never-refined position". The delivered 2-view thick points are born of\n'
              '  // low-parallax DLT depth noise; a wide-baseline observation of the SAME\n'
              '  // surface point reprojects ~3.6 px off that noisy position (p90: 5.3 px) and\n'
              '  // the current-position gate rejects exactly the observation that could fix\n'
              '  // it. The refit triangulates over track+candidate (full union baseline),\n'
              '  // accepts only if EVERY union observation is in front of its camera and\n'
              '  // within kMaxReprojPx of the REFIT position (+ the same stable-baseline\n'
              '  // rule, evaluated at the refit), and installs the refit position on accept.\n'
              '  // A failed refit falls through to the legacy gates, so acceptance is a\n'
              '  // strict superset of the default arm. Default OFF: unset env is\n'
              '  // bit-identical to the shipped behavior.\n'
              '  const bool kGrowRefit = TdGrowRefitEnabled();\n'
              '  const int temporal_k =\n'
              '      std::max(1, s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12);\n'
              '  const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '  auto db = colmap::Database::Open(s->db_path);\n'
              '\n'
              '  const auto reprojects_cleanly = [&](const colmap::Image& image,\n'
              '                                      const colmap::Camera& camera,\n'
              '                                      colmap::point2D_t point2D_idx,\n'
              '                                      const Eigen::Vector3d& xyz) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam = image.CamFromWorld() * xyz;\n'
              '    if (x_cam.z() <= 0.0) return false;\n'
              '    const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> projected = camera.ImgFromCam(x_cam);\n'
              '    return projected &&\n'
              '           (*projected - image.Point2D(point2D_idx).xy).norm() <=\n'
              '               kMaxReprojPx;\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  const auto has_image_in_track = [](const colmap::Track& track,\n'
              '                                     colmap::image_t image_id) {\n'
              '    return std::any_of(track.Elements().begin(), track.Elements().end(),\n'
              '                       [image_id](const colmap::TrackElement& element) {\n'
              '                         return element.image_id == image_id;\n'
              '                       });\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  const auto has_stable_baseline = [&](const colmap::Track& track,\n'
              '                                       const colmap::Image& candidate,\n'
              '                                       const Eigen::Vector3d& xyz) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d candidate_center =\n'
              '        candidate.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '    for (const colmap::TrackElement& element : track.Elements()) {\n'
              '      if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(element.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& observed = reconstruction->Image(element.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!observed.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      if (colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '              candidate_center, observed.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(), xyz) >=\n'
              '          kMinTriAngleRad) {\n'
              '        return true;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  for (int gap = std::min(temporal_k, num_frames - 1); gap >= 1; --gap) {\n'
              '    for (int right = gap; right < num_frames; ++right) {\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& frame1 = s->frames[right - gap];\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& frame2 = s->frames[right];\n'
              '      if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(frame1.image_id) ||\n'
              '          !reconstruction->ExistsImage(frame2.image_id)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      colmap::Image& image1 = reconstruction->Image(frame1.image_id);\n'
              '      colmap::Image& image2 = reconstruction->Image(frame2.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!image1.HasPose() || !image2.HasPose() ||\n'
              '          !db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(frame1.image_id, frame2.image_id)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      const colmap::TwoViewGeometry geometry =\n'
              '          db->ReadTwoViewGeometry(frame1.image_id, frame2.image_id);\n'
              '      if (geometry.inlier_matches.empty()) continue;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_temporal_detail_pairs;\n'
              '      s->stat_temporal_detail_matches += geometry.inlier_matches.size();\n'
              '\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera& camera1 =\n'
              '          reconstruction->Camera(image1.CameraId());\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera& camera2 =\n'
              '          reconstruction->Camera(image2.CameraId());\n'
              '      for (const colmap::FeatureMatch& match : geometry.inlier_matches) {\n'
              '        if (match.point2D_idx1 >= image1.NumPoints2D() ||\n'
              '            match.point2D_idx2 >= image2.NumPoints2D()) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_conflicts;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        const colmap::Point2D& point1 =\n'
              '            image1.Point2D(match.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '        const colmap::Point2D& point2 =\n'
              '            image2.Point2D(match.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '        const bool has1 = point1.HasPoint3D();\n'
              '        const bool has2 = point2.HasPoint3D();\n'
              '\n'
              '        if (has1 && has2) {\n'
              '          if (point1.point3D_id != point2.point3D_id) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_temporal_detail_conflicts;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        if (has1 || has2) {\n'
              '          const colmap::point3D_t point3D_id =\n'
              '              has1 ? point1.point3D_id : point2.point3D_id;\n'
              '          const colmap::image_t grow_image_id =\n'
              '              has1 ? frame2.image_id : frame1.image_id;\n'
              '          const colmap::point2D_t grow_point2D_idx =\n'
              '              has1 ? match.point2D_idx2 : match.point2D_idx1;\n'
              '          colmap::Image& grow_image = has1 ? image2 : image1;\n'
              '          const colmap::Camera& grow_camera = has1 ? camera2 : camera1;\n'
              '          const colmap::Point3D& point3D =\n'
              '              reconstruction->Point3D(point3D_id);\n'
              '          if (has_image_in_track(point3D.track, grow_image_id)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_temporal_detail_conflicts;\n'
              '            continue;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          // [KNIFE-A ①] union-refit grow acceptance (env-gated; see above).\n'
              '          if (kGrowRefit) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_td_grow_refit_attempted;\n'
              '            // Legacy-gate verdict, attribution only: how many accepts the\n'
              '            // refit SAVED relative to the 3px@current gate.\n'
              '            const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam_cur =\n'
              '                grow_image.CamFromWorld() * point3D.xyz;\n'
              '            const bool legacy_ok =\n'
              '                x_cam_cur.z() > 0.0 &&\n'
              '                reprojects_cleanly(grow_image, grow_camera, grow_point2D_idx,\n'
              '                                   point3D.xyz) &&\n'
              '                has_stable_baseline(point3D.track, grow_image, point3D.xyz);\n'
              '            bool usable = true;\n'
              '            std::vector<Eigen::Matrix3x4d> cams_from_world;\n'
              '            std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_points;\n'
              '            std::vector<const colmap::Image*> union_imgs;\n'
              '            std::vector<const colmap::Camera*> union_cams;\n'
              '            std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> union_px;\n'
              '            cams_from_world.reserve(point3D.track.Length() + 1);\n'
              '            cam_points.reserve(point3D.track.Length() + 1);\n'
              '            for (const colmap::TrackElement& el : point3D.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '              if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(el.image_id)) {\n'
              '                usable = false;\n'
              '                break;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              const colmap::Image& im = reconstruction->Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '              if (!im.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= im.NumPoints2D()) {\n'
              '                usable = false;\n'
              '                break;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              const colmap::Camera& cm =\n'
              '                  reconstruction->Camera(im.CameraId());\n'
              '              const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nc =\n'
              '                  cm.CamFromImg(im.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy);\n'
              '              if (!nc) {\n'
              '                usable = false;\n'
              '                break;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              cams_from_world.push_back(im.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix());\n'
              '              cam_points.push_back(*nc);\n'
              '              union_imgs.push_back(&im);\n'
              '              union_cams.push_back(&cm);\n'
              '              union_px.push_back(im.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy);\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nc_cand =\n'
              '                grow_camera.CamFromImg(\n'
              '                    grow_image.Point2D(grow_point2D_idx).xy);\n'
              '            if (usable && nc_cand) {\n'
              '              cams_from_world.push_back(grow_image.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix());\n'
              '              cam_points.push_back(*nc_cand);\n'
              '              union_imgs.push_back(&grow_image);\n'
              '              union_cams.push_back(&grow_camera);\n'
              '              union_px.push_back(grow_image.Point2D(grow_point2D_idx).xy);\n'
              '              Eigen::Vector3d refit_xyz;\n'
              '              if (colmap::TriangulateMultiViewPoint(\n'
