              '                      colmap::span<const Eigen::Matrix3x4d>(\n'
              '                          cams_from_world.data(), cams_from_world.size()),\n'
              '                      colmap::span<const Eigen::Vector2d>(cam_points.data(),\n'
              '                                                          cam_points.size()),\n'
              '                      &refit_xyz)) {\n'
              '                // Pose-fixed GN polish: the DLT minimizes an ALGEBRAIC\n'
              '                // residual; converge to the pixel-reproj optimum the\n'
              '                // acceptance gate below actually measures (raises rescue\n'
              '                // rate AND lowers the residual the added observation carries\n'
              '                // into the delivered model\'s mean reproj).\n'
              '                PolishPointGN(union_imgs, union_cams, union_px, &refit_xyz);\n'
              '                bool refit_ok = reprojects_cleanly(\n'
              '                    grow_image, grow_camera, grow_point2D_idx, refit_xyz);\n'
              '                if (refit_ok) {\n'
              '                  for (const colmap::TrackElement& el :\n'
              '                       point3D.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '                    const colmap::Image& im =\n'
              '                        reconstruction->Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '                    if (!reprojects_cleanly(\n'
              '                            im, reconstruction->Camera(im.CameraId()),\n'
              '                            el.point2D_idx, refit_xyz)) {\n'
              '                      refit_ok = false;\n'
              '                      break;\n'
              '                    }\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                // [P2-FRAG-MERGE-REPRICE] Optional absolute parallax floor\n'
              '                // on the refit ACCEPT: the union\'s θ_max at the refit must\n'
              '                // reach OFFICIAL_AETHER_TD_GROW_REFIT_MIN_THETA_DEG. A union still\n'
              '                // inside the low-parallax ambiguity band keeps the noisy\n'
              '                // depth the ghost layer is made of (cap47: GROW_REFIT arm\n'
              '                // ghost 4.3%→5.5%); refusing the refit here drops the\n'
              '                // candidate to the legacy gates below (superset preserved).\n'
              '                static const double kGrowMinThetaRad =\n'
              '                    TdGrowRefitMinThetaDeg() * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '                if (refit_ok && kGrowMinThetaRad > 0.0) {\n'
              '                  double th_union = 0.0;\n'
              '                  for (size_t i2 = 0; i2 < union_imgs.size(); ++i2) {\n'
              '                    const Eigen::Vector3d ci =\n'
              '                        union_imgs[i2]->CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '                    for (size_t j2 = i2 + 1; j2 < union_imgs.size(); ++j2) {\n'
              '                      th_union = std::max(\n'
              '                          th_union,\n'
              '                          colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                              ci,\n'
              '                              union_imgs[j2]->CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(),\n'
              '                              refit_xyz));\n'
              '                    }\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                  if (th_union < kGrowMinThetaRad) {\n'
              '                    ++s->stat_td_grow_refit_min_theta_rej;\n'
              '                    refit_ok = false;\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                if (refit_ok &&\n'
              '                    has_stable_baseline(point3D.track, grow_image,\n'
              '                                        refit_xyz)) {\n'
              '                  reconstruction->Point3D(point3D_id).xyz = refit_xyz;\n'
              '                  reconstruction->AddObservation(\n'
              '                      point3D_id,\n'
              '                      colmap::TrackElement(grow_image_id, grow_point2D_idx));\n'
              '                  ++s->stat_temporal_detail_grown;\n'
              '                  ++s->stat_td_grow_refit_accepted;\n'
              '                  if (!legacy_ok) ++s->stat_td_grow_refit_saved;\n'
              '                  continue;\n'
              '                }\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            // Refit unusable/rejected: fall through to the legacy gates so\n'
              '            // this arm accepts a strict superset of the default arm.\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam =\n'
              '              grow_image.CamFromWorld() * point3D.xyz;\n'
              '          if (x_cam.z() <= 0.0) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '            continue;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          if (!reprojects_cleanly(grow_image, grow_camera,\n'
              '                                  grow_point2D_idx, point3D.xyz)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_reproj;\n'
              '            continue;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          if (!has_stable_baseline(point3D.track, grow_image, point3D.xyz)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '            continue;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          reconstruction->AddObservation(\n'
              '              point3D_id,\n'
              '              colmap::TrackElement(grow_image_id, grow_point2D_idx));\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_grown;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_point1 =\n'
              '            camera1.CamFromImg(point1.xy);\n'
              '        const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_point2 =\n'
              '            camera2.CamFromImg(point2.xy);\n'
              '        if (!cam_point1 || !cam_point2) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_reproj;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        Eigen::Vector3d xyz;\n'
              '        if (!colmap::TriangulatePoint(image1.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix(),\n'
              '                                     image2.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix(),\n'
              '                                     *cam_point1, *cam_point2, &xyz)) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam1 = image1.CamFromWorld() * xyz;\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam2 = image2.CamFromWorld() * xyz;\n'
              '        if (x_cam1.z() <= 0.0 || x_cam2.z() <= 0.0) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                image1.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(),\n'
              '                image2.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(), xyz) <\n'
              '            kMinTriAngleRad) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!reprojects_cleanly(image1, camera1, match.point2D_idx1, xyz) ||\n'
              '            !reprojects_cleanly(image2, camera2, match.point2D_idx2, xyz)) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_reproj;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        colmap::Track track;\n'
              '        track.AddElement(frame1.image_id, match.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '        track.AddElement(frame2.image_id, match.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '        reconstruction->AddPoint3D(xyz, std::move(track),\n'
              '                                   Eigen::Vector3ub::Zero());\n'
              '        ++s->stat_temporal_detail_created;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  db->Close();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A ② 2026-07-11] Pose-fixed point-only Gauss-Newton polish (analytic\n'
              '// pinhole Jacobian, ≤4 iterations). The union DLT is algebraic (minimizes an\n'
              '// algebraic residual, not pixels); this polish converges the refit position to\n'
              '// the pixel-reproj optimum the acceptance gate actually measures. Poses and\n'
              '// intrinsics stay constant — this is the "每点位姿固定微 LM". Non-pinhole\n'
              '// cameras (never produced by this pipeline) skip the polish and keep the DLT\n'
              '// result; any degeneracy (behind-camera, singular H) also returns early with\n'
              '// the input position unchanged.\n'
              'void PolishPointGN(const std::vector<const colmap::Image*>& images,\n'
              '                   const std::vector<const colmap::Camera*>& cameras,\n'
              '                   const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>& obs_px,\n'
              '                   Eigen::Vector3d* xyz) {\n'
              '  for (int iter = 0; iter < 4; ++iter) {\n'
              '    Eigen::Matrix3d H = Eigen::Matrix3d::Zero();\n'
              '    Eigen::Vector3d b = Eigen::Vector3d::Zero();\n'
              '    int n_used = 0;\n'
              '    for (size_t i = 0; i < images.size(); ++i) {\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera& cam = *cameras[i];\n'
              '      if (cam.model_id != colmap::SimplePinholeCameraModel::model_id &&\n'
              '          cam.model_id != colmap::PinholeCameraModel::model_id) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::Rigid3d& cfw = images[i]->CamFromWorld();\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d xc = cfw * (*xyz);\n'
              '      if (xc.z() <= 1e-9) return;\n'
              '      const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> px = cam.ImgFromCam(xc);\n'
              '      if (!px) return;\n'
              '      const double fx = cam.FocalLengthX();\n'
              '      const double fy = cam.FocalLengthY();\n'
              '      const double iz = 1.0 / xc.z();\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector2d r = *px - obs_px[i];\n'
              '      Eigen::Matrix<double, 2, 3> jc;\n'
              '      jc << fx * iz, 0.0, -fx * xc.x() * iz * iz,  //\n'
              '          0.0, fy * iz, -fy * xc.y() * iz * iz;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Matrix<double, 2, 3> J =\n'
              '          jc * cfw.rotation().toRotationMatrix();\n'
              '      H += J.transpose() * J;\n'
              '      b += J.transpose() * r;\n'
              '      ++n_used;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (n_used < 2) return;\n'
              '    H.diagonal().array() += 1e-8 * H.trace() + 1e-12;\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d dx = H.ldlt().solve(b);\n'
              '    if (!dx.allFinite()) return;\n'
              '    *xyz -= dx;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [KNIFE-A ② 2026-07-11] TrackUpgrade — finalize-tail 2-view upgrade pass\n'
              '// (env OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRACK_UPGRADE=1, default OFF; runs AFTER RestoreTemporalDetail\n'
              '// so the detail layer\'s fresh 2-view points are upgrade candidates too).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHY: the delivered cloud\'s thickness lives in its 2-view / low-parallax\n'
              '// (θ_max < 3°) points — live 2° creations plus RestoreTemporalDetail\n'
              '// creations, all triangulated OUTSIDE the BA\'s reach (pure DLT after stage 2,\n'
              '// never refined). The observations that could fix them usually EXIST in the\n'
              '// db (temporal TVGs RestoreTemporalDetail\'s gap≤K loop never pairs with them,\n'
              '// enrichment spatial TVGs it never consumes at all) but are unreachable:\n'
              '// the vendored mapper\'s Complete() gate rejects candidates >4 px from the\n'
              '// CURRENT noisy position — for a point with 0.0067 thickness, a 10°-baseline\n'
              '// observation sits ~3.6 px off (p90 points: 5.3 px), exactly the observation\n'
              '// that could repair it. This pass does refit-semantics itself instead of\n'
              '// touching the shared vendored mapper path:\n'
              '//   1. index EVERY db TVG inlier that touches an eligible track\'s keypoints;\n'
              '//   2. per eligible point, collect free (unassigned) partner keypoints from\n'
              '//      images outside the track — one per image;\n'
              '//   3. trimmed union refit: DLT over track+candidates, pose-fixed GN polish,\n'
              '//      accept only if EVERY union observation is in front of its camera and\n'
              '//      within the RestoreTemporalDetail reproj gate of the refit position;\n'
              '//      violating candidates are dropped (≤3 rounds) rather than failing the\n'
              '//      whole point;\n'
              '//   4. on accept: install the refit position + AddObservation the survivors.\n'
              '// Never deletes a point or an observation (点数只增不减); thickness improves\n'
              '// because the upgraded points move to their full-baseline positions.\n'
              'void UpgradeLowParallaxTracks(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                              colmap::Reconstruction* reconstruction) {\n'
              '  if (kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly) return;\n'
              '  if (!TrackUpgradeEnabled() || !s || !reconstruction) return;\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const double kMaxReprojPx = TemporalDetailMaxReprojPx();\n'
              '    constexpr double kLowParallaxRad = 3.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // θ_max (max pairwise triangulation angle) over a track\'s posed obs.\n'
              '    const auto theta_max = [&](const colmap::Track& track,\n'
              '                               const Eigen::Vector3d& xyz) {\n'
              '      double best = 0.0;\n'
              '      const auto& els = track.Elements();\n'
              '      for (size_t a = 0; a < els.size(); ++a) {\n'
              '        if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(els[a].image_id)) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Image& ia = reconstruction->Image(els[a].image_id);\n'
              '        if (!ia.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d ca = ia.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '        for (size_t b = a + 1; b < els.size(); ++b) {\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(els[b].image_id)) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& ib = reconstruction->Image(els[b].image_id);\n'
              '          if (!ib.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '          best = std::max(best,\n'
              '                          colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                              ca, ib.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(), xyz));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return best;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    // Whole-model distribution summary (the 归因插桩): n / 2-view / θ<3° and\n'
              '    // the θ_max p50. Fills the stat triple passed in.\n'
              '    const auto summarize = [&](int64_t* n_out, int64_t* n2_out,\n'
              '                               int64_t* lt3_out, double* p50_out) {\n'
              '      std::vector<double> thetas;\n'
              '      thetas.reserve(reconstruction->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '      int64_t n2 = 0, lt3 = 0;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, pt] : reconstruction->Points3D()) {\n'
              '        const double th = theta_max(pt.track, pt.xyz);\n'
              '        thetas.push_back(th);\n'
              '        if (pt.track.Length() == 2) ++n2;\n'
              '        if (th < kLowParallaxRad) ++lt3;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      *n_out = static_cast<int64_t>(thetas.size());\n'
              '      *n2_out = n2;\n'
              '      *lt3_out = lt3;\n'
              '      if (!thetas.empty()) {\n'
              '        std::nth_element(thetas.begin(), thetas.begin() + thetas.size() / 2,\n'
              '                         thetas.end());\n'
              '        *p50_out = thetas[thetas.size() / 2] * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    };\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 1) Eligible set = 2-view tracks + low-parallax multi-view tracks, and\n'
              '    //    the pre-pass distribution.\n'
              '    std::vector<colmap::point3D_t> eligible;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::vector<double> thetas;\n'
              '      thetas.reserve(reconstruction->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '      int64_t n2 = 0, lt3 = 0;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, pt] : reconstruction->Points3D()) {\n'
              '        const double th = theta_max(pt.track, pt.xyz);\n'
              '        thetas.push_back(th);\n'
              '        const bool two_view = pt.track.Length() == 2;\n'
              '        if (two_view) ++n2;\n'
              '        if (th < kLowParallaxRad) ++lt3;\n'
              '        if (two_view || th < kLowParallaxRad) eligible.push_back(pid);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->stat_upgrade_pre_npts = static_cast<int64_t>(thetas.size());\n'
              '      s->stat_upgrade_pre_2view = n2;\n'
              '      s->stat_upgrade_pre_lt3 = lt3;\n'
              '      if (!thetas.empty()) {\n'
              '        std::nth_element(thetas.begin(), thetas.begin() + thetas.size() / 2,\n'
              '                         thetas.end());\n'
              '        s->upgrade_theta_p50_pre_deg =\n'
              '            thetas[thetas.size() / 2] * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->stat_upgrade_eligible = static_cast<int64_t>(eligible.size());\n'
              '    if (eligible.empty()) {\n'
              '      s->upgrade_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 2) Correspondence index over ALL db TVG inliers (temporal, enrichment\n'
              '    //    spatial, finalize re-match — everything), restricted to the eligible\n'
              '    //    tracks\' keypoints so memory stays bounded.\n'
              '    const auto key_of = [](colmap::image_t img, uint32_t idx) {\n'
              '      return (static_cast<uint64_t>(img) << 32) | idx;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<uint64_t> needed;\n'
              '    needed.reserve(eligible.size() * 3);\n'
              '    for (const colmap::point3D_t pid : eligible) {\n'
              '      for (const colmap::TrackElement& el :\n'
              '           reconstruction->Point3D(pid).track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        needed.insert(key_of(el.image_id, el.point2D_idx));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<uint64_t,\n'
              '                       std::vector<std::pair<colmap::image_t, uint32_t>>>\n'
              '        corr;\n'
              '    corr.reserve(needed.size());\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      auto db = colmap::Database::Open(s->db_path);\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pair_id, n_inliers] :\n'
              '           db->ReadTwoViewGeometryNumInliers()) {\n'
              '        if (n_inliers <= 0) continue;\n'
              '        const auto [id1, id2] = colmap::PairIdToImagePair(pair_id);\n'
              '        const colmap::TwoViewGeometry g = db->ReadTwoViewGeometry(id1, id2);\n'
              '        for (const colmap::FeatureMatch& m : g.inlier_matches) {\n'
              '          const uint64_t k1 = key_of(id1, m.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '          const uint64_t k2 = key_of(id2, m.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '          if (needed.count(k1)) corr[k1].emplace_back(id2, m.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '          if (needed.count(k2)) corr[k2].emplace_back(id1, m.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      db->Close();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 3) Per-point trimmed union refit. Serial by design: acceptance claims\n'
              '    //    free keypoints, and first-come-first-served needs a total order.\n'
              '    for (const colmap::point3D_t pid : eligible) {\n'
              '      if (!reconstruction->ExistsPoint3D(pid)) continue;  // defensive\n'
              '      const colmap::Point3D& point = reconstruction->Point3D(pid);\n'
              '\n'
              '      std::unordered_set<colmap::image_t> excluded;  // track ∪ picked images\n'
              '      for (const colmap::TrackElement& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        excluded.insert(el.image_id);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      std::vector<std::pair<colmap::image_t, uint32_t>> kept;\n'
              '      for (const colmap::TrackElement& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        const auto it = corr.find(key_of(el.image_id, el.point2D_idx));\n'
              '        if (it == corr.end()) continue;\n'
              '        for (const auto& [img2, idx2] : it->second) {\n'
              '          if (excluded.count(img2)) continue;\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(img2)) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& im2 = reconstruction->Image(img2);\n'
              '          if (!im2.HasPose() || idx2 >= im2.NumPoints2D()) continue;\n'
              '          if (im2.Point2D(idx2).HasPoint3D()) continue;  // taken — no merge here\n'
              '          excluded.insert(img2);  // one candidate per image\n'
              '          kept.emplace_back(img2, idx2);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (kept.empty()) continue;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_upgrade_attempted;\n'
              '\n'
              '      Eigen::Vector3d accepted_xyz = Eigen::Vector3d::Zero();\n'
              '      bool accepted = false;\n'
              '      for (int round = 0; round < 3 && !kept.empty(); ++round) {\n'
              '        std::vector<Eigen::Matrix3x4d> cams_from_world;\n'
              '        std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_points;\n'
              '        std::vector<const colmap::Image*> imgs;\n'
              '        std::vector<const colmap::Camera*> cams;\n'
              '        std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> px;\n'
              '        bool bad = false;\n'
              '        const auto push_obs = [&](colmap::image_t img_id, uint32_t idx) {\n'
              '          if (bad) return;\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(img_id)) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& im = reconstruction->Image(img_id);\n'
              '          if (!im.HasPose() || idx >= im.NumPoints2D()) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const colmap::Camera& cm = reconstruction->Camera(im.CameraId());\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector2d p = im.Point2D(idx).xy;\n'
              '          const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nc = cm.CamFromImg(p);\n'
              '          if (!nc) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          cams_from_world.push_back(im.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix());\n'
              '          cam_points.push_back(*nc);\n'
              '          imgs.push_back(&im);\n'
              '          cams.push_back(&cm);\n'
              '          px.push_back(p);\n'
              '        };\n'
              '        for (const colmap::TrackElement& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '          push_obs(el.image_id, el.point2D_idx);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const size_t n_track = imgs.size();\n'
              '        for (const auto& [img2, idx2] : kept) push_obs(img2, idx2);\n'
              '        if (bad) break;\n'
              '\n'
              '        Eigen::Vector3d X;\n'
              '        if (!colmap::TriangulateMultiViewPoint(\n'
              '                colmap::span<const Eigen::Matrix3x4d>(cams_from_world.data(),\n'
              '                                                      cams_from_world.size()),\n'
              '                colmap::span<const Eigen::Vector2d>(cam_points.data(),\n'
              '                                                    cam_points.size()),\n'
              '                &X)) {\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        PolishPointGN(imgs, cams, px, &X);\n'
              '\n'
              '        bool track_bad = false;\n'
              '        std::vector<size_t> drop;  // indices into kept, ascending\n'
              '        double worst_res = -1.0;\n'
              '        size_t worst_cand = static_cast<size_t>(-1);\n'
              '        for (size_t o = 0; o < imgs.size(); ++o) {\n'
              '          double res = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector3d xc = imgs[o]->CamFromWorld() * X;\n'
              '          if (xc.z() > 0.0) {\n'
              '            const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> pp = cams[o]->ImgFromCam(xc);\n'
              '            if (pp) res = (*pp - px[o]).norm();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const bool ok = res <= kMaxReprojPx;\n'
              '          if (o < n_track) {\n'
              '            if (!ok) track_bad = true;\n'
              '          } else {\n'
              '            const size_t ci = o - n_track;\n'
              '            if (!ok) drop.push_back(ci);\n'
              '            if (res > worst_res) {\n'
              '              worst_res = res;\n'
              '              worst_cand = ci;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!track_bad && drop.empty()) {\n'
              '          accepted = true;\n'
              '          accepted_xyz = X;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!drop.empty()) {\n'
              '          for (auto it = drop.rbegin(); it != drop.rend(); ++it) {\n'
              '            kept.erase(kept.begin() + *it);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          // Track violated while every candidate fits: the candidate set is\n'
              '          // self-consistent but pulls the point off its own track — shed the\n'
              '          // worst-residual candidate and retry.\n'
              '          if (worst_cand == static_cast<size_t>(-1)) break;\n'
              '          kept.erase(kept.begin() + worst_cand);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      if (accepted && !kept.empty()) {\n'
              '        reconstruction->Point3D(pid).xyz = accepted_xyz;\n'
              '        for (const auto& [img2, idx2] : kept) {\n'
              '          reconstruction->AddObservation(\n'
              '              pid, colmap::TrackElement(img2, idx2));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ++s->stat_upgrade_accepted;\n'
              '        s->stat_upgrade_obs_added += static_cast<int64_t>(kept.size());\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 4) Post-pass distribution.\n'
              '    summarize(&s->stat_upgrade_post_npts, &s->stat_upgrade_post_2view,\n'
              '              &s->stat_upgrade_post_lt3, &s->upgrade_theta_p50_post_deg);\n'
              '    s->upgrade_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] track-upgrade: eligible="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_eligible\n'
              '                 << " attempted=" << s->stat_upgrade_attempted\n'
              '                 << " accepted=" << s->stat_upgrade_accepted\n'
              '                 << " obs_added=" << s->stat_upgrade_obs_added << " pre{n="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_pre_npts << " 2view="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_pre_2view << " lt3="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_pre_lt3 << " theta_p50="\n'
              '                 << s->upgrade_theta_p50_pre_deg << "deg} post{n="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_post_npts << " 2view="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_post_2view << " lt3="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_upgrade_post_lt3 << " theta_p50="\n'
              '                 << s->upgrade_theta_p50_post_deg << "deg} in "\n'
              '                 << static_cast<int64_t>(s->upgrade_ms) << "ms";\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    // Enhancement pass only — never take the finalize down.\n'
              '    s->upgrade_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] track-upgrade aborted: " << e.what();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [P2-FRAG-MERGE 2026-07-11] FragmentMerge — finalize-tail duplicate-track\n'
              '// merge pass (env OFFICIAL_AETHER_FRAG_MERGE=1, default OFF; runs AFTER\n'
              '// UpgradeLowParallaxTracks so upgraded tracks participate with their full\n'
              '// baselines).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHY: the remaining delivered thickness lives in REPEATED fragments of the\n'
              '// same physical surface point — DSP-SIFT emits near-identical keypoints at\n'
              '// several scales, each seeding its own low-parallax 2-view track (KNIFE-A\n'
              '// verdict: ~18k upgrade-eligible 2-view points whose partner observations\n'
              '// already sit on OTHER tracks; cap45 cross-track conflicts = 39,187).\n'
              '// TrackUpgrade deliberately skips partner keypoints that already carry a\n'
              '// Point3D ("taken — no merge here"); this pass handles exactly that case:\n'
              '// when the db\'s TVG inlier graph CONNECTS two tracks (a keypoint of track A\n'
              '// matched a keypoint of track B), they are fragment candidates of ONE point.\n'
              '// A union refit over ALL observations of BOTH tracks (DLT + pose-fixed GN —\n'
              '// the CanMergeLivePoints / KNIFE-A recipe) decides:\n'
              '//   accept ⇔ EVERY union observation is in front of its camera AND within\n'
              '//   the RestoreTemporalDetail reproj gate of the refit position AND the\n'
              '//   union\'s θ_max at the refit STRICTLY exceeds both fragments\' current\n'
              '//   θ_max. 防误合并: two physically distinct points cannot both fit one 3D\n'
              '//   position within the all-observation reproj gate, and a merge that does\n'
              '//   not widen the effective baseline has no thickness value to bank.\n'
              '// Unlike TrackUpgrade there is NO candidate trimming: every union\n'
              '// observation belongs to one of the two DELIVERED tracks, and dropping one\n'
              '// would delete delivered data — a violating observation therefore rejects\n'
              '// the merge instead. Chains (A–B, B–C) merge fully within the single pass\n'
              '// via redirect chasing. Accounting (物理点守恒): each accepted merge removes\n'
              '// exactly one point id (two fragments → one multi-view point), so\n'
              '// frag_post_npts + frag_accepted == frag_pre_npts must hold; observations\n'
              '// are conserved exactly (MergePoints3D concatenates the tracks).\n'
              'void MergeFragmentTracks(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                         colmap::Reconstruction* reconstruction) {\n'
              '  if (kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly) return;\n'
              '  if (!FragMergeEnabled() || !s || !reconstruction) return;\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const double kMaxReprojPx = FragMergeMaxReprojPx() > 0.0\n'
              '                                    ? FragMergeMaxReprojPx()\n'
              '                                    : TemporalDetailMaxReprojPx();\n'
              '    constexpr double kLowParallaxRad = 3.0 * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '    // [P2-FRAG-MERGE-REPRICE] absolute post-merge parallax floor (0 = off).\n'
              '    const double kMinThetaRad = FragMergeMinThetaDeg() * M_PI / 180.0;\n'
              '\n'
              '    const auto theta_max = [&](const colmap::Track& track,\n'
              '                               const Eigen::Vector3d& xyz) {\n'
              '      double best = 0.0;\n'
              '      const auto& els = track.Elements();\n'
              '      for (size_t a = 0; a < els.size(); ++a) {\n'
              '        if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(els[a].image_id)) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Image& ia = reconstruction->Image(els[a].image_id);\n'
              '        if (!ia.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d ca = ia.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '        for (size_t b = a + 1; b < els.size(); ++b) {\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(els[b].image_id)) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& ib = reconstruction->Image(els[b].image_id);\n'
              '          if (!ib.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '          best = std::max(best,\n'
              '                          colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                              ca, ib.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(), xyz));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return best;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    const auto summarize = [&](int64_t* n_out, int64_t* n2_out,\n'
              '                               int64_t* lt3_out, double* p50_out) {\n'
              '      std::vector<double> thetas;\n'
              '      thetas.reserve(reconstruction->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '      int64_t n2 = 0, lt3 = 0;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, pt] : reconstruction->Points3D()) {\n'
              '        const double th = theta_max(pt.track, pt.xyz);\n'
              '        thetas.push_back(th);\n'
              '        if (pt.track.Length() == 2) ++n2;\n'
              '        if (th < kLowParallaxRad) ++lt3;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      *n_out = static_cast<int64_t>(thetas.size());\n'
              '      *n2_out = n2;\n'
              '      *lt3_out = lt3;\n'
              '      if (!thetas.empty()) {\n'
              '        std::nth_element(thetas.begin(), thetas.begin() + thetas.size() / 2,\n'
              '                         thetas.end());\n'
              '        *p50_out = thetas[thetas.size() / 2] * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    };\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 1) Pre-pass distribution + eligible set: the 2-view / low-parallax\n'
              '    //    fragments the thickness lives in. The merge PARTNER may be any\n'
              '    //    track (folding a 2-view fragment into a healthy multi-view track is\n'
              '    //    the most valuable dedup of all).\n'
              '    std::vector<colmap::point3D_t> eligible;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::vector<double> thetas;\n'
              '      thetas.reserve(reconstruction->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '      int64_t n2 = 0, lt3 = 0;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, pt] : reconstruction->Points3D()) {\n'
              '        const double th = theta_max(pt.track, pt.xyz);\n'
              '        thetas.push_back(th);\n'
              '        const bool two_view = pt.track.Length() == 2;\n'
              '        if (two_view) ++n2;\n'
              '        if (th < kLowParallaxRad) ++lt3;\n'
              '        if (two_view || th < kLowParallaxRad) eligible.push_back(pid);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->stat_frag_pre_npts = static_cast<int64_t>(thetas.size());\n'
              '      s->stat_frag_pre_2view = n2;\n'
              '      s->stat_frag_pre_lt3 = lt3;\n'
              '      if (!thetas.empty()) {\n'
              '        std::nth_element(thetas.begin(), thetas.begin() + thetas.size() / 2,\n'
              '                         thetas.end());\n'
              '        s->frag_theta_p50_pre_deg = thetas[thetas.size() / 2] * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (eligible.empty()) {\n'
              '      s->frag_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 2) TVG-inlier correspondence index restricted to the eligible tracks\'\n'
              '    //    keypoints (same recipe/memory bound as TrackUpgrade step 2).\n'
              '    const auto key_of = [](colmap::image_t img, uint32_t idx) {\n'
              '      return (static_cast<uint64_t>(img) << 32) | idx;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<uint64_t> needed;\n'
              '    needed.reserve(eligible.size() * 3);\n'
              '    for (const colmap::point3D_t pid : eligible) {\n'
              '      for (const colmap::TrackElement& el :\n'
              '           reconstruction->Point3D(pid).track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        needed.insert(key_of(el.image_id, el.point2D_idx));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<uint64_t,\n'
              '                       std::vector<std::pair<colmap::image_t, uint32_t>>>\n'
              '        corr;\n'
              '    corr.reserve(needed.size());\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      auto db = colmap::Database::Open(s->db_path);\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pair_id, n_inliers] :\n'
              '           db->ReadTwoViewGeometryNumInliers()) {\n'
              '        if (n_inliers <= 0) continue;\n'
              '        const auto [id1, id2] = colmap::PairIdToImagePair(pair_id);\n'
              '        const colmap::TwoViewGeometry g = db->ReadTwoViewGeometry(id1, id2);\n'
              '        for (const colmap::FeatureMatch& m : g.inlier_matches) {\n'
              '          const uint64_t k1 = key_of(id1, m.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '          const uint64_t k2 = key_of(id2, m.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '          if (needed.count(k1)) corr[k1].emplace_back(id2, m.point2D_idx2);\n'
              '          if (needed.count(k2)) corr[k2].emplace_back(id1, m.point2D_idx1);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      db->Close();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 3) Candidate track pairs: an eligible track\'s keypoint matched (TVG\n'
              '    //    inlier) against a keypoint ASSIGNED to a different track = one\n'
              '    //    link; multiplicity = correspondence evidence strength.\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<PointIdPair, int, PointIdPairHash> links;\n'
              '    for (const colmap::point3D_t pid : eligible) {\n'
              '      if (!reconstruction->ExistsPoint3D(pid)) continue;  // defensive\n'
              '      for (const colmap::TrackElement& el :\n'
              '           reconstruction->Point3D(pid).track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        const auto it = corr.find(key_of(el.image_id, el.point2D_idx));\n'
              '        if (it == corr.end()) continue;\n'
              '        for (const auto& [img2, idx2] : it->second) {\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(img2)) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& im2 = reconstruction->Image(img2);\n'
              '          if (!im2.HasPose() || idx2 >= im2.NumPoints2D()) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::Point2D& p2 = im2.Point2D(idx2);\n'
              '          if (!p2.HasPoint3D() || p2.point3D_id == pid) continue;\n'
              '          ++links[CanonicalPointPair(pid, p2.point3D_id)];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->stat_frag_links = static_cast<int64_t>(links.size());\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 4) Deterministic strongest-evidence-first order.\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<PointIdPair, int>> ranked(links.begin(),\n'
              '                                                    links.end());\n'
              '    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n'
              '              [](const std::pair<PointIdPair, int>& a,\n'
              '                 const std::pair<PointIdPair, int>& b) {\n'
              '                if (a.second != b.second) return a.second > b.second;\n'
              '                if (a.first.a != b.first.a) return a.first.a < b.first.a;\n'
              '                return a.first.b < b.first.b;\n'
              '              });\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 5) Serial merge with redirect chasing (fragment CHAINS A–B, B–C fold\n'
              '    //    fully within this one pass).\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<colmap::point3D_t, colmap::point3D_t> redirect;\n'
              '    const auto resolve = [&](colmap::point3D_t pid) {\n'
              '      auto it = redirect.find(pid);\n'
              '      while (it != redirect.end()) {\n'
              '        pid = it->second;\n'
              '        it = redirect.find(pid);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return pid;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pair, n_links] : ranked) {\n'
              '      const colmap::point3D_t a = resolve(pair.a);\n'
              '      const colmap::point3D_t b = resolve(pair.b);\n'
              '      if (a == b) continue;  // already folded into the same point\n'
              '      if (!reconstruction->ExistsPoint3D(a) ||\n'
              '          !reconstruction->ExistsPoint3D(b)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++s->stat_frag_attempted;\n'
              '      const colmap::Point3D& pa = reconstruction->Point3D(a);\n'
              '      const colmap::Point3D& pb = reconstruction->Point3D(b);\n'
              '\n'
              '      // Union refit: DLT over every observation of both tracks + pose-fixed\n'
              '      // GN polish (the KNIFE-A recipe).\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Matrix3x4d> cams_from_world;\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> cam_points;\n'
              '      std::vector<const colmap::Image*> imgs;\n'
              '      std::vector<const colmap::Camera*> cams;\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> px;\n'
              '      bool bad = false;\n'
              '      const auto push_track = [&](const colmap::Track& track) {\n'
              '        for (const colmap::TrackElement& el : track.Elements()) {\n'
              '          if (bad) return;\n'
              '          if (!reconstruction->ExistsImage(el.image_id)) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& im = reconstruction->Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '          if (!im.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= im.NumPoints2D()) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const colmap::Camera& cm = reconstruction->Camera(im.CameraId());\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector2d p = im.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy;\n'
              '          const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> nc = cm.CamFromImg(p);\n'
              '          if (!nc) {\n'
              '            bad = true;\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          cams_from_world.push_back(im.CamFromWorld().ToMatrix());\n'
              '          cam_points.push_back(*nc);\n'
              '          imgs.push_back(&im);\n'
              '          cams.push_back(&cm);\n'
              '          px.push_back(p);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      };\n'
              '      push_track(pa.track);\n'
              '      push_track(pb.track);\n'
              '      if (bad || cam_points.size() < 3) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_frag_reject_degenerate;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      Eigen::Vector3d X;\n'
              '      if (!colmap::TriangulateMultiViewPoint(\n'
              '              colmap::span<const Eigen::Matrix3x4d>(cams_from_world.data(),\n'
              '                                                    cams_from_world.size()),\n'
              '              colmap::span<const Eigen::Vector2d>(cam_points.data(),\n'
              '                                                  cam_points.size()),\n'
              '              &X)) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_frag_reject_degenerate;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      PolishPointGN(imgs, cams, px, &X);\n'
              '\n'
              '      // Acceptance 1: EVERY union observation in front of its camera and\n'
              '      // within the reproj gate of the refit position.\n'
              '      bool ok = true;\n'
              '      for (size_t o = 0; o < imgs.size(); ++o) {\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d xc = imgs[o]->CamFromWorld() * X;\n'
              '        if (xc.z() <= 0.0) {\n'
              '          ok = false;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> pp = cams[o]->ImgFromCam(xc);\n'
              '        if (!pp || (*pp - px[o]).norm() > kMaxReprojPx) {\n'
              '          ok = false;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!ok) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_frag_reject_reproj;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Acceptance 2: the union\'s θ_max at the refit must STRICTLY beat both\n'
              '      // fragments\' current θ_max.\n'
              '      const double th_a = theta_max(pa.track, pa.xyz);\n'
              '      const double th_b = theta_max(pb.track, pb.xyz);\n'
              '      double th_union = 0.0;\n'
              '      for (size_t i2 = 0; i2 < imgs.size(); ++i2) {\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d ci =\n'
              '            imgs[i2]->CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '        for (size_t j2 = i2 + 1; j2 < imgs.size(); ++j2) {\n'
              '          th_union = std::max(\n'
              '              th_union,\n'
              '              colmap::CalculateTriangulationAngle(\n'
              '                  ci, imgs[j2]->CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc(), X));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (th_union <= std::max(th_a, th_b)) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_frag_reject_theta;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [P2-FRAG-MERGE-REPRICE] Acceptance 3: the union must LEAVE the\n'
              '      // low-parallax ambiguity band, not merely rise inside it — a merged\n'
              '      // point still below the floor keeps the depth-noise position that\n'
              '      // builds the coherent ghost layer (cap47 verdict). Off by default.\n'
              '      if (kMinThetaRad > 0.0 && th_union < kMinThetaRad) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_frag_reject_min_theta;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      const size_t union_len = imgs.size();\n'
              '      const colmap::point3D_t merged = reconstruction->MergePoints3D(a, b);\n'
              '      // Install the refit position (the one the gates validated), not\n'
              '      // MergePoints3D\'s length-weighted average of two noisy fragments.\n'
              '      reconstruction->Point3D(merged).xyz = X;\n'
              '      redirect[a] = merged;\n'
              '      redirect[b] = merged;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_frag_accepted;\n'
              '      s->stat_frag_obs_merged += static_cast<int64_t>(union_len);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 6) Post-pass distribution + the conservation invariant.\n'
              '    summarize(&s->stat_frag_post_npts, &s->stat_frag_post_2view,\n'
              '              &s->stat_frag_post_lt3, &s->frag_theta_p50_post_deg);\n'
              '    s->frag_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    const bool conserved =\n'
              '        s->stat_frag_post_npts + s->stat_frag_accepted ==\n'
              '        s->stat_frag_pre_npts;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] frag-merge: links=" << s->stat_frag_links\n'
              '                 << " attempted=" << s->stat_frag_attempted\n'
              '                 << " accepted=" << s->stat_frag_accepted\n'
              '                 << " obs_merged=" << s->stat_frag_obs_merged\n'
              '                 << " rej{reproj=" << s->stat_frag_reject_reproj\n'
              '                 << " theta=" << s->stat_frag_reject_theta\n'
              '                 << " min_theta=" << s->stat_frag_reject_min_theta\n'
              '                 << " degen=" << s->stat_frag_reject_degenerate << "} pre{n="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_frag_pre_npts << " 2view="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_frag_pre_2view << " lt3=" << s->stat_frag_pre_lt3\n'
              '                 << " theta_p50=" << s->frag_theta_p50_pre_deg\n'
              '                 << "deg} post{n=" << s->stat_frag_post_npts << " 2view="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_frag_post_2view << " lt3="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_frag_post_lt3 << " theta_p50="\n'
              '                 << s->frag_theta_p50_post_deg << "deg} conservation="\n'
              '                 << (conserved ? "OK" : "VIOLATED") << " in "\n'
              '                 << static_cast<int64_t>(s->frag_ms) << "ms";\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    // Enhancement pass only — never take the finalize down.\n'
              '    s->frag_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] frag-merge aborted: " << e.what();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'std::string ArkitPoseStorePath(const aether_sfm_session& session) {\n'
              '  return session.db_path + ".arkit_pose_v1";\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1 ArkitPoseRecordFromFrame(\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& frame) {\n'
              '  aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1 record;\n'
              '  record.frame_id = frame.frame_id;\n'
              '  record.image_id = static_cast<uint32_t>(frame.persisted_image_id);\n'
              '  record.active = frame.image_id != 0;\n'
              '  record.frame_identity_digest = frame.frame_identity_digest;\n'
              '  record.cam_from_world_q_xyzw = {\n'
              '      frame.cam_from_world.rotation().x(), frame.cam_from_world.rotation().y(),\n'
              '      frame.cam_from_world.rotation().z(), frame.cam_from_world.rotation().w()};\n'
              '  record.cam_from_world_t_xyz = {frame.cam_from_world.translation().x(),\n'
              '                                 frame.cam_from_world.translation().y(),\n'
              '                                 frame.cam_from_world.translation().z()};\n'
              '  record.gravity_cam_xyz = {frame.gravity_cam.x(), frame.gravity_cam.y(),\n'
              '                            frame.gravity_cam.z()};\n'
              '  return record;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool PersistArkitPoseSnapshot(const aether_sfm_session& session,\n'
              '                              const FrameRecord& pending) {\n'
              '  std::vector<aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1> records;\n'
              '  records.reserve(session.frames.size() + 1);\n'
              '  for (const FrameRecord& frame : session.frames) {\n'
              '    records.push_back(ArkitPoseRecordFromFrame(frame));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  records.push_back(ArkitPoseRecordFromFrame(pending));\n'
              '  return aether::sfm::WriteArkitPoseStoreV1(ArkitPoseStorePath(session),\n'
              '                                            records) ==\n'
              '         aether::sfm::ArkitPoseStoreStatusV1::kOk;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Write-ahead variant: persists the snapshot with `withdrawn_frame_id` already\n'
              '// marked inactive WITHOUT mutating the session. remove_frame calls this before\n'
              '// it destroys the frame\'s db matches, so a crash or io error in the destructive\n'
              '// half can never leave the pair "sidecar still says active / db rows already\n'
              '// gone" — which on resume restores the frame as a live starved frame and\n'
              '// re-matches a photo the user explicitly deleted.\n'
              'bool PersistArkitPoseSnapshotWithWithdrawal(const aether_sfm_session& session,\n'
              '                                            int withdrawn_frame_id) {\n'
              '  if (withdrawn_frame_id < 0 ||\n'
              '      withdrawn_frame_id >= static_cast<int>(session.frames.size())) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  std::vector<aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1> records;\n'
              '  records.reserve(session.frames.size());\n'
              '  for (const FrameRecord& frame : session.frames) {\n'
              '    records.push_back(ArkitPoseRecordFromFrame(frame));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  records[static_cast<size_t>(withdrawn_frame_id)].active = false;\n'
              '  return aether::sfm::WriteArkitPoseStoreV1(ArkitPoseStorePath(session),\n'
              '                                            records) ==\n'
              '         aether::sfm::ArkitPoseStoreStatusV1::kOk;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool PersistCurrentArkitPoseSnapshot(const aether_sfm_session& session) {\n'
              '  std::vector<aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1> records;\n'
              '  records.reserve(session.frames.size());\n'
              '  for (const FrameRecord& frame : session.frames) {\n'
              '    records.push_back(ArkitPoseRecordFromFrame(frame));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return aether::sfm::WriteArkitPoseStoreV1(ArkitPoseStorePath(session),\n'
              '                                            records) ==\n'
              '         aether::sfm::ArkitPoseStoreStatusV1::kOk;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ── Mandatory ARKit recovery ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '// A recovery session is allowed to exist only when the atomic sidecar has one\n'
              '// valid ARKit pose and camera-frame gravity vector for every DB image. The old\n'
              '// path rebuilt unposed frames and silently entered COLMAP registration (P3P).\n'
              '// That path is intentionally gone: any missing/corrupt/count-mismatched sidecar\n'
              '// blocks recovery. A valid snapshot recreates the registered reconstruction at\n'
              '// the persisted ARKit poses and triangulates only from already-verified TVGs.\n'
              'bool RebuildFrameRecordsForResume(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db) return false;\n'
              '  if (!s->frames.empty()) return true;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    std::vector<aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1> pose_records;\n'
              '    if (aether::sfm::ReadArkitPoseStoreV1(ArkitPoseStorePath(*s),\n'
              '                                         &pose_records) !=\n'
              '        aether::sfm::ArkitPoseStoreStatusV1::kOk) {\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::vector<colmap::Image> images = s->db->ReadAllImages();\n'
              '    if (images.empty() || images.size() != pose_records.size()) return false;\n'
              '    std::sort(images.begin(), images.end(),\n'
              '              [](const colmap::Image& a, const colmap::Image& b) {\n'
              '                return a.ImageId() < b.ImageId();\n'
              '              });\n'
              '    auto restored = std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>();\n'
              '    std::vector<FrameRecord> frames;\n'
              '    frames.reserve(images.size());\n'
              '    std::vector<colmap::image_t> image_ids;\n'
              '    image_ids.reserve(images.size());\n'
              '    aether_ba_clear_gravity_priors();\n'
              '    for (size_t i = 0; i < images.size(); ++i) {\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& image = images[i];\n'
              '      const aether::sfm::ArkitPoseRecordV1& pose = pose_records[i];\n'
              '      if (pose.frame_id != static_cast<int32_t>(i) ||\n'
              '          pose.image_id != static_cast<uint32_t>(image.ImageId())) {\n'
              '        return false;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      char expected_name[64];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(expected_name, sizeof(expected_name), "frame_%06zu.jpg", i);\n'
              '      if (image.Name() != expected_name) return false;\n'
              '\n'
              '      FrameRecord rec;\n'
              '      rec.image_id = image.ImageId();\n'
              '      rec.persisted_image_id = image.ImageId();\n'
              '      rec.camera_id = image.CameraId();\n'
              '      rec.camera = s->db->ReadCamera(image.CameraId());\n'
              '      rec.frame_id = pose.frame_id;\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureKeypoints keypoints =\n'
              '          s->db->ReadKeypoints(image.ImageId());\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureDescriptors descriptors =\n'
              '          s->db->ReadDescriptors(image.ImageId());\n'
              '      rec.n_keypoints = static_cast<int>(keypoints.size());\n'
              '      rec.points = colmap::FeatureKeypointsToPointsVector(keypoints);\n'
              '      rec.descriptors.assign(descriptors.data.data(),\n'
              '                             descriptors.data.data() + descriptors.data.size());\n'
              '      rec.frame_identity_digest = FrameIdentityDigestV1(\n'
              '          image.Name(), rec.camera, rec.points, rec.descriptors);\n'
              '      if (rec.frame_identity_digest != pose.frame_identity_digest) return false;\n'
              '      rec.cam_from_world = colmap::Rigid3d(\n'
              '          Eigen::Quaterniond(pose.cam_from_world_q_xyzw[3],\n'
              '                             pose.cam_from_world_q_xyzw[0],\n'
              '                             pose.cam_from_world_q_xyzw[1],\n'
              '                             pose.cam_from_world_q_xyzw[2]),\n'
              '          Eigen::Vector3d(pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[0],\n'
              '                          pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[1],\n'
              '                          pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[2]));\n'
              '      rec.gravity_cam = Eigen::Vector3d(pose.gravity_cam_xyz[0],\n'
              '                                        pose.gravity_cam_xyz[1],\n'
              '                                        pose.gravity_cam_xyz[2]);\n'
              '      rec.has_pose = true;\n'
              '\n'
              '      if (!pose.active) {\n'
              '        // [WITHDRAWN-PARITY 2026-08-04] Reproduce EVERY mutation that live\n'
              '        // aether_sfm_remove_frame applies to a withdrawn frame, not just\n'
              '        // image_id. remove_frame also drops descriptors/points and zeroes\n'
              '        // n_keypoints; restoring image_id=0 alone left the frame looking fully\n'
              '        // featured, and FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames gates candidates on\n'
              '        // n_keypoints only. The withdrawn frame was therefore re-admitted to\n'
              '        // rematching and its pairs written as WriteMatches(0, other) /\n'
              '        // WriteTwoViewGeometry(0, other). Those rows insert silently (no\n'
              '        // foreign keys, and ImagePairToPairId accepts 0), then abort stage-2 in\n'
              '        // DatabaseCache::Load -> image_to_frame_id.at(0) with std::out_of_range,\n'
              '        // which RefineGlobalBA turns into RefineFailClosed. The poisoned rows\n'
              '        // persist, so every later finalize AND resume dies at the same place and\n'
              '        // the capture can never produce a point cloud again.\n'
              '        rec.image_id = 0;\n'
              '        rec.descriptors.clear();\n'
              '        rec.descriptors.shrink_to_fit();\n'
              '        rec.points.clear();\n'
              '        rec.points.shrink_to_fit();\n'
              '        rec.n_keypoints = 0;\n'
              '        frames.push_back(std::move(rec));\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!restored->ExistsCamera(rec.camera_id)) {\n'
              '        restored->AddCameraWithTrivialRig(rec.camera);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      colmap::Image restored_image;\n'
              '      restored_image.SetImageId(rec.image_id);\n'
              '      restored_image.SetName(image.Name());\n'
              '      restored_image.SetCameraId(rec.camera_id);\n'
              '      restored_image.SetPoints2D(rec.points);\n'
              '      restored->AddImageWithTrivialFrame(std::move(restored_image),\n'
              '                                         rec.cam_from_world);\n'
              '      const double gravity[3] = {rec.gravity_cam.x(), rec.gravity_cam.y(),\n'
              '                                 rec.gravity_cam.z()};\n'
              '      // ⚠️ Same unvalidated 0.5 deg as the live ingest path — see the\n'
              '      // PROVENANCE GAP note at the other kGravityBaSigmaRad definition. These\n'
              '      // two constants must be changed together or resume and live capture will\n'
              '      // anchor at different stiffnesses for the same project.\n'
              '      constexpr double kGravityBaSigmaRad =\n'
              '          0.5 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0;\n'
              '      if (!aether_ba_set_gravity_prior(image.Name().c_str(), gravity,\n'
              '                                       kGravityBaSigmaRad)) {\n'
              '        return false;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      image_ids.push_back(rec.image_id);\n'
              '      frames.push_back(std::move(rec));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (image_ids.empty()) return false;\n'
              '    colmap::DatabaseCache::Options cache_options;\n'
              '    cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '    auto cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, cache_options);\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions options;\n'
              '    options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '    options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks = TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '    ApplyBirthGateAB(&options);  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07]\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache);\n'
              '    mapper.BeginReconstruction(restored);\n'
              '    for (const colmap::image_t image_id : image_ids) {\n'
              '      mapper.TriangulateImage(options.Triangulation(), image_id);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    mapper.CompleteAndMergeTracks(options.Triangulation());\n'
              '    mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '\n'
              '    s->camera_id = frames.front().camera_id;\n'
              '    s->frames = std::move(frames);\n'
              '    s->reg_order = std::move(image_ids);\n'
              '    s->live_recon = std::move(restored);\n'
              '    s->live_recon_ready = true;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(ERROR) << "[aether_sfm] mandatory ARKit recovery blocked: "\n'
              '               << e.what();\n'
              '    s->frames.clear();\n'
              '    s->reg_order.clear();\n'
              '    s->live_recon.reset();\n'
              '    s->live_recon_ready = false;\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ── Finalize-time starved-frame re-match ────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '// [FINALIZE-REMATCH 2026-07-11] Repairs the db after a capture-time GPU\n'
              '// matcher collapse. Device evidence (capture 43, iPhone 14 Pro): thermal=\n'
              '// serious from ~f52 → aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs failed in whole segments,\n'
              '// add_frame skipped those pairs fail-closed, so the db simply LACKS the\n'
              '// matches for a contiguous block → 37/118 frames unregistered, delivery\n'
              '// collapsed to ~26k points. Host replay proved the block\'s DESCRIPTORS are\n'
              '// intact (features were full 8192/frame; re-matching registers the block), so\n'
              '// finalize — which runs after capture, typically cooler, with no 2 s/frame\n'
              '// budget — re-runs the MISSING temporal-window pairs through the SAME matcher\n'
              '// route before RunIncremental consumes the db.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Starved trigger (calibrated on the capture-43 db): a frame is starved when\n'
              '// it participates in < kRematchMinValidWindowPairs db pairs with\n'
              '// >= kRematchValidInlierGate TVG inliers inside the temporal K-window.\n'
              '// cap43 separation is clean: healthy frames sit at 4-21 valid window pairs,\n'
              '// the collapsed block at 0-3.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Candidates = temporal pairs (j, f) with gap <= K, ABSENT from `matches`,\n'
              '// where EITHER side is starved. Either-side bridges the block boundary (the\n'
              '// first healthy frame after a collapse still re-matches against the starved\n'
              '// tail behind it). Missing-only keeps the pass idempotent (add_frame never\n'
              '// writes a failed pair, so absence == never-succeeded) and write-once — the\n'
              '// exact WriteMatches → EstimateTwoViewGeometry → WriteTwoViewGeometry\n'
              '// sequence add_frame uses; RunIncremental\'s min_num_matches=15 then filters\n'
              '// weak pairs identically to capture-time pairs.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Second trigger — near-adjacent chain holes: a missing (f-1, f) or (f-2, f)\n'
              '// pair is ALWAYS re-matched, starved or not. The capture-42 db showed the\n'
              '// milder failure shape: frames keep 5-14 valid window pairs (above the\n'
              '// starved gate) while the CONSECUTIVE chain has whole runs of never-attempted\n'
              '// pairs (f105-f128) — partly a spatial-first side effect (revisit segments\n'
              '// match across passes instead of adjacent frames) amplified by the thermal\n'
              '// failures. The incremental mapper leans on chain continuity; these holes are\n'
              '// cheap to close (cap42 +25 pairs, cap43 +2, healthy captures ~0).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Budget: kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs bounds the extreme case (cap43\'s fully\n'
              '// collapsed tail needs 686 pairs; the GPU GEMM matcher is ~0.1 s/pair at\n'
              '// 8192 kp → ~1 min worst case, paid only by a capture that would otherwise\n'
              '// lose a whole registration block). Gap-ascending round-robin spends the\n'
              '// budget on the nearest (most registrable) neighbours of every starved frame\n'
              '// first.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Matcher policy = capture parity (HANDOFF §0:12): use_gpu_match=1 routes to\n'
              '// the Metal GEMM matcher and a per-pair failure SKIPS that pair — CPU\n'
              '// brute-force is never a device fallback. use_gpu_match=0 (host replay /\n'
              '// no-Metal builds) uses the CPU matcher, exactly as capture did then. Resume\n'
              '// sessions (FrameRecords rebuilt without descriptors) load keypoints +\n'
              '// descriptors per frame from the db into a window-bounded cache (~K+1 frames\n'
              '// ≈ 16 MB at 8192 kp — transient, freed on return).\n'
              'constexpr int kRematchMinValidWindowPairs = 4;  // cap43-calibrated (see above)\n'
              'constexpr int kRematchValidInlierGate = 15;     // == pipeline min_num_matches\n'
              'constexpr int kRematchNearGap = 2;              // chain holes always re-matched\n'
              'constexpr int kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs = 800;   // > cap43 worst case (688)\n'
              '\n'
              '// [ENRICH-TVG-FARM 2026-08-11] Multi-worker TVG verification for the two db\n'
              '// enrichment passes (starved-frame re-match + quadratic overlap). The passes\n'
              '// are matcher→TVG→persist chains whose CPU bulk is the per-pair RANSAC inside\n'
              '// EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry (pure function of camera/points/\n'
              '// gravity/matches — no session, no db, no GPU). This farm runs ONLY that\n'
              '// estimation on N workers; the caller keeps the GPU matcher serialized and\n'
              '// commits results (stats + WriteMatches/WriteTwoViewGeometry) strictly in\n'
              '// submission order on its own thread — the sqlite handle is never touched off\n'
              '// the enriching thread (OOM-campaign hard rule).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Determinism: every job re-seeds COLMAP\'s thread_local PRNG with a seed\n'
              '// derived from the image-id pair, so the RANSAC stream is a function of the\n'
              '// pair alone — independent of worker count or scheduling. N=1 and N=3 are\n'
              '// bit-identical, reruns are bit-identical. (The serial env-off path keeps the\n'
              '// legacy carried-over PRNG stream and stays byte-identical to before.)\n'
              '//\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TVG_THREADS: 0/unset = off (legacy serial TVG).\n'
              'int EnrichTvgThreads() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ENRICH_TVG_THREADS");\n'
              '    const int v = e && e[0] ? std::atoi(e) : 0;\n'
              '    if (v <= 0) return 0;\n'
              '    return std::min(v, 8);\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'uint32_t EnrichTvgPairSeed(colmap::image_t id1, colmap::image_t id2) {\n'
              '  uint64_t h = (static_cast<uint64_t>(id1) << 32) | id2;\n'
              '  h ^= h >> 33;\n'
              '  h *= 0xff51afd7ed558ccdULL;\n'
              '  h ^= h >> 33;\n'
              '  h *= 0xc4ceb9fe1a85ec53ULL;\n'
              '  h ^= h >> 33;\n'
              '  // Never 0/-1: SetPRNGSeed(-1) means "seed from time" upstream.\n'
              '  const uint32_t seed = static_cast<uint32_t>(h) | 1u;\n'
              '  return seed == static_cast<uint32_t>(-1) ? 1u : seed;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'struct EnrichTvgJob {\n'
              '  // Inputs — pts_* must stay valid until the job is committed; the callers\n'
              '  // gate the farm to in-memory (live borrowed) feature storage.\n'
              '  const FrameRecord* frame_a = nullptr;\n'
              '  const FrameRecord* frame_b = nullptr;\n'
              '  const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>* pts_a = nullptr;\n'
              '  const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>* pts_b = nullptr;\n'
              '  int a_idx = -1, b_idx = -1;  // frame indices (probe-debt growth bookkeeping)\n'
              '  colmap::FeatureMatches matches;\n'
              '  const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions* tvg_options = nullptr;\n'
              '  bool was_probe_debt = false;  // re-match bookkeeping rides along\n'
              '  // Output\n'
              '  aether::sfm::MandatoryGravityTwoViewResultV1 tvg;\n'
              '  std::atomic<bool> ready{false};\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'class EnrichTvgFarm {\n'
              ' public:\n'
              '  explicit EnrichTvgFarm(int n_workers) {\n'
              '    workers_.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(n_workers));\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < n_workers; ++i) {\n'
              '      workers_.emplace_back([this] {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        for (;;) {\n'
              '          EnrichTvgJob* job = nullptr;\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(mu_);\n'
              '            cv_.wait(lk, [this] { return stop_ || !queue_.empty(); });\n'
              '            if (stop_ && queue_.empty()) return;\n'
              '            job = queue_.front();\n'
              '            queue_.pop_front();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          colmap::SetPRNGSeed(EnrichTvgPairSeed(job->frame_a->image_id,\n'
              '                                                job->frame_b->image_id));\n'
              '          job->tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '              *job->frame_a, *job->pts_a, *job->frame_b, *job->pts_b,\n'
              '              job->matches, *job->tvg_options);\n'
              '          job->ready.store(true, std::memory_order_release);\n'
              '          done_cv_.notify_all();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  ~EnrichTvgFarm() {\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mu_);\n'
              '      stop_ = true;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    cv_.notify_all();\n'
              '    for (auto& w : workers_) w.join();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void Submit(EnrichTvgJob* job) {\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mu_);\n'
              '      queue_.push_back(job);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    cv_.notify_one();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Blocks until the given job (the caller\'s in-order front) is estimated.\n'
              '  void Wait(EnrichTvgJob* job) {\n'
              '    if (job->ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return;\n'
              '    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(mu_);\n'
              '    done_cv_.wait(\n'
              '        lk, [&] { return job->ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire); });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              ' private:\n'
              '  std::vector<std::thread> workers_;\n'
              '  std::mutex mu_;\n'
              '  std::condition_variable cv_;       // workers wait for jobs\n'
              '  std::condition_variable done_cv_;  // caller waits for completions\n'
              '  std::deque<EnrichTvgJob*> queue_;\n'
              '  bool stop_ = false;\n'
              '};\n'
              '\n'
              'void FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  // [SIGNED 2026-07-26] Un-gated from kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly. This\n'
              '  // pass is pair GENERATION + matching under colmap-DEFAULT\n'
              '  // TwoViewGeometryOptions — it only WriteMatches/WriteTwoViewGeometry, never\n'
              '  // touches a Point3D — i.e. the same official-semantics classification that\n'
              '  // re-enabled the quadratic pass. It exists to repay the debt the thermal\n'
              '  // throttle creates: the shipped plugin opts into OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K_HOT=6\n'
              '  // whose "delivery-lossless by construction" promise depends on THIS\n'
              '  // function re-matching the throttled frames\' missing temporal-window pairs\n'
              '  // at finish time. With the gate in place that promise was silently dead:\n'
              '  // a 149-frame capture with 118 thermal-serious frames delivered ~20% fewer\n'
              '  // points on device than the same db replayed with full matching. All 3D\n'
              '  // geometry remains with the official mapper/triangulator/BA downstream.\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || s->frames.size() < 3 || s->camera_id == 0) return;\n'
              '  const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '      s->options.use_gpu_match && (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs != nullptr);\n'
              '  // Fail-closed like VerifySpatialPair: GPU requested but symbol absent →\n'
