              '  // skip the whole pass rather than degrade into O(N²) CPU matching.\n'
              '  if (s->options.use_gpu_match && !gpu_avail) return;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    const int K = s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12;\n'
              '\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<colmap::image_t, int> idx_of;\n'
              '    idx_of.reserve(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    // Withdrawn frames all carry image_id == 0, so indexing them here would\n'
              '    // collapse every one of them onto the same key and let the last writer win.\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < num_frames; ++i) {\n'
              '      if (s->frames[i].image_id == 0) continue;\n'
              '      idx_of[s->frames[i].image_id] = i;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 1) Per-frame valid-pair count inside the temporal K-window, from the\n'
              '    //    TVG table (counts capture-time pairs AND anything the spatial\n'
              '    //    revisit pass just wrote).\n'
              '    std::vector<int> win_valid(num_frames, 0);\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pair_id, n_inliers] :\n'
              '         s->db->ReadTwoViewGeometryNumInliers()) {\n'
              '      if (n_inliers < kRematchValidInlierGate) continue;\n'
              '      const auto [id1, id2] = colmap::PairIdToImagePair(pair_id);\n'
              '      const auto a = idx_of.find(id1);\n'
              '      const auto b = idx_of.find(id2);\n'
              '      if (a == idx_of.end() || b == idx_of.end()) continue;\n'
              '      if (std::abs(a->second - b->second) > K) continue;\n'
              '      ++win_valid[a->second];\n'
              '      ++win_valid[b->second];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::vector<char> starved(num_frames, 0);\n'
              '    int64_t n_starved = 0;\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < num_frames; ++i) {\n'
              '      // [THERMAL-THROTTLE 2026-07-11] Frames fed with a reduced live K\n'
              '      // (thermal serious → 12→6) are ALWAYS treated as starved: they may sit\n'
              '      // above the valid-pair gate (6 healthy pairs > 4) yet still miss half\n'
              '      // their temporal window. Re-matching restores the full K topology at\n'
              '      // finish time, which is what keeps the throttle delivery-lossless.\n'
              '      if (s->frames[i].n_keypoints > 0 &&\n'
              '          (win_valid[i] < kRematchMinValidWindowPairs ||\n'
              '           s->frames[i].fed_throttled)) {\n'
              '        starved[i] = 1;\n'
              '        ++n_starved;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->stat_finalize_rematch_starved_frames += n_starved;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 2) Missing pairs, gap-ascending round-robin so every frame gets its\n'
              '    //    nearest neighbours before the budget can clip anything:\n'
              '    //      gap <= kRematchNearGap  → always (chain-hole trigger),\n'
              '    //      gap <= K                → when either side is starved.\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> todo;  // (j, f), j < f, frame indices\n'
              '    todo.reserve(kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs);\n'
              '    for (int gap = 1; gap <= K; ++gap) {\n'
              '      for (int f = gap; f < num_frames; ++f) {\n'
              '        const int j = f - gap;\n'
              '        if (gap > kRematchNearGap && !starved[f] && !starved[j]) continue;\n'
              '        // A withdrawn frame is never a rematch candidate. AddOfficialQuadraticPairs\n'
              '        // already gates on this; the gate is repeated here so the two pair-writing\n'
              '        // paths cannot drift apart again.\n'
              '        if (s->frames[j].image_id == 0 || s->frames[f].image_id == 0) continue;\n'
              '        if (s->frames[j].n_keypoints <= 0 || s->frames[f].n_keypoints <= 0) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (s->db->ExistsMatches(s->frames[j].image_id,\n'
              '                                 s->frames[f].image_id)) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ++s->stat_finalize_rematch_candidates;\n'
              '        if (static_cast<int>(todo.size()) < kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs) {\n'
              '          todo.emplace_back(j, f);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] Append every remaining probe-skipped pair (Wu\n'
              '    // ICCV 2013 preemptive-matching gate, [PROBE-GATE]) that idle repay has\n'
              '    // not settled yet. The starved-window rule above cannot see skipped pairs\n'
              '    // on healthy frames, nor skipped spatial/loop pairs whose index gap\n'
              '    // exceeds K — the explicit ledger closes exactly that hole and is what\n'
              '    // makes the gate delivery-LOSSLESS (user hard line): finalize attempts\n'
              '    // every entry with the same matcher/TVG/persistence as a gate-off run.\n'
              '    // Debt pairs are deliberately appended BEYOND kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs:\n'
              '    // the ledger is already bounded by the capture\'s skip count, and clipping\n'
              '    // it would silently break the lossless promise. Empty ledger (gate off)\n'
              '    // → this block is a no-op and the pass is byte-identical to before.\n'
              '    if (!s->probe_skipped_pairs.empty()) {\n'
              '      std::unordered_set<uint64_t> in_todo;\n'
              '      in_todo.reserve(todo.size());\n'
              '      for (const auto& [tj, tf] : todo) in_todo.insert(FramePairKey(tj, tf));\n'
              '      std::vector<uint64_t> debt(s->probe_skipped_pairs.begin(),\n'
              '                                 s->probe_skipped_pairs.end());\n'
              '      std::sort(debt.begin(), debt.end());  // deterministic enqueue order\n'
              '      for (const uint64_t key : debt) {\n'
              '        const int f = static_cast<int>(key >> 32);\n'
              '        const int j = static_cast<int>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);\n'
              '        if (j < 0 || f <= j || f >= num_frames) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // malformed — void the entry\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (s->frames[j].image_id == 0 || s->frames[f].image_id == 0) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // withdrawn frame → debt void\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (s->frames[j].n_keypoints <= 0 || s->frames[f].n_keypoints <= 0) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (in_todo.count(key)) continue;  // already a starved-window entry\n'
              '        if (s->db->ExistsMatches(s->frames[j].image_id,\n'
              '                                 s->frames[f].image_id)) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // resolved by an earlier pass\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        todo.emplace_back(j, f);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (todo.empty()) return;\n'
              '    // Cache locality for the resume-path db loads: process by later frame\n'
              '    // ascending; every needed partner then lives within the last K indices.\n'
              '    std::sort(todo.begin(), todo.end(),\n'
              '              [](const std::pair<int, int>& a, const std::pair<int, int>& b) {\n'
              '                if (a.second != b.second) return a.second < b.second;\n'
              '                return a.first < b.first;\n'
              '              });\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Feature access: borrow the in-memory FrameRecord (live sessions) or\n'
              '    // load keypoints+descriptors from the db (resume sessions), cached and\n'
              '    // evicted outside the sliding window.\n'
              '    struct RematchFeat {\n'
              '      const uint8_t* desc = nullptr;\n'
              '      const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>* pts = nullptr;\n'
              '      int n = 0;\n'
              '      std::vector<uint8_t> desc_store;\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> pts_store;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<int, RematchFeat> cache;\n'
              '    const auto get_feat = [&](int i) -> const RematchFeat* {\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& fr = s->frames[i];\n'
              '      auto it = cache.find(i);\n'
              '      if (it != cache.end()) return it->second.n > 0 ? &it->second : nullptr;\n'
              '      RematchFeat& feat = cache[i];\n'
              '      if (!fr.descriptors.empty() && !fr.points.empty()) {\n'
              '        feat.desc = fr.descriptors.data();\n'
              '        feat.pts = &fr.points;\n'
              '        feat.n = fr.n_keypoints;\n'
              '        return &feat;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureKeypoints kps = s->db->ReadKeypoints(fr.image_id);\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureDescriptors d = s->db->ReadDescriptors(fr.image_id);\n'
              '      const int n = static_cast<int>(kps.size());\n'
              '      if (n <= 0 || d.data.rows() != n || d.data.cols() != 128) {\n'
              '        feat.n = 0;  // negative-cache the malformed frame\n'
              '        return nullptr;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      feat.desc_store.assign(d.data.data(),\n'
              '                             d.data.data() + static_cast<size_t>(n) * 128);\n'
              '      feat.pts_store = colmap::FeatureKeypointsToPointsVector(kps);\n'
              '      feat.desc = feat.desc_store.data();\n'
              '      feat.pts = &feat.pts_store;\n'
              '      feat.n = n;\n'
              '      return &feat;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 3) Match + persist, mirroring add_frame\'s sequence and gates exactly.\n'
              '    const double ratio =\n'
              '        s->options.match_max_ratio > 0 ? s->options.match_max_ratio : 0.8;\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;  // colmap defaults\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf;\n'
              '    size_t done = 0;\n'
              '    // [ENRICH-TVG-FARM 2026-08-11] Optional N-worker TVG verification. The\n'
              '    // matcher stays serialized on THIS thread; estimated pairs are committed\n'
              '    // (stats + db writes) strictly in todo order below, so sqlite stays\n'
              '    // single-threaded and counters keep their serial meaning.\n'
              '    const int tvg_threads = EnrichTvgThreads();\n'
              '    std::unique_ptr<EnrichTvgFarm> farm;\n'
              '    if (tvg_threads > 0) farm = std::make_unique<EnrichTvgFarm>(tvg_threads);\n'
              '    const size_t max_inflight = static_cast<size_t>(tvg_threads) * 2;\n'
              '    std::deque<std::unique_ptr<EnrichTvgJob>> inflight;\n'
              '    const auto commit_front = [&] {\n'
              '      EnrichTvgJob* job = inflight.front().get();\n'
              '      farm->Wait(job);\n'
              '      if (MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(job->tvg)) {\n'
              '        // Mirrors the serial block below: write + stats + probe-debt growth.\n'
              '        s->db->WriteMatches(job->frame_a->image_id, job->frame_b->image_id,\n'
              '                            job->matches);\n'
              '        const colmap::TwoViewGeometry& geometry = job->tvg.geometry;\n'
              '        s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(job->frame_a->image_id,\n'
              '                                    job->frame_b->image_id, geometry);\n'
              '        ++s->stat_finalize_rematch_written;\n'
              '        s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers +=\n'
              '            static_cast<int64_t>(geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '        if (job->was_probe_debt && !geometry.inlier_matches.empty()) {\n'
              '          s->probe_debt_grow.push_back(ProbeDebtGrowPair{\n'
              '              job->a_idx, job->b_idx, geometry.inlier_matches});\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      inflight.pop_front();\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    const auto drain_all = [&] {\n'
              '      while (!inflight.empty()) commit_front();\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    for (const auto& [j, f] : todo) {\n'
              '      // [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET] Same time gate as VerifySpatialPair: stop STARTING\n'
              '      // new re-match attempts once the budget is exhausted; the remaining\n'
              '      // (least-valuable — todo is gap-ascending) pairs are counted and left\n'
              '      // for a future finalize retry. No-op unless a budget is armed.\n'
              '      if (EnrichBudgetExhausted(s)) {\n'
              '        const int64_t remaining = static_cast<int64_t>(todo.size() - done);\n'
              '        s->stat_enrich_budget_stopped += remaining;\n'
              '        LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize re-match stopped by the "\n'
              '                        "enrichment time budget: "\n'
              '                     << remaining << " of " << todo.size()\n'
              '                     << " candidate pairs left unattempted";\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++done;\n'
              '      // Evict cache entries that fell out of the sliding window (memory\n'
              '      // bound for resume-path loads; borrowed live entries are cheap).\n'
              '      for (auto it = cache.begin(); it != cache.end();) {\n'
              '        if (it->first < f - K) it = cache.erase(it);\n'
              '        else ++it;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const RematchFeat* fa = get_feat(j);\n'
              '      const RematchFeat* fb = get_feat(f);\n'
              '      if (!fa || !fb) continue;\n'
              '      const int cap = fa->n < fb->n ? fa->n : fb->n;\n'
              '      pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '      int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_finalize_rematch_attempted;\n'
              '      // [P1-RC7-RETRY] rc=7 gets two backoff retries (cap46: 93/336 finalize\n'
              '      // re-match pairs failed rc=7 on the still-hot GPU and were lost).\n'
              '      const int mrc =\n'
              '          gpu_avail\n'
              '              ? GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(s, s->frames[j].frame_id, fa->desc,\n'
              '                                       fa->n, s->frames[f].frame_id, fb->desc,\n'
              '                                       fb->n, ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap,\n'
              '                                       &num_matches)\n'
              '              : aether_sift_match_pairs(fa->desc, fa->n, fb->desc, fb->n,\n'
              '                                        ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap,\n'
              '                                        &num_matches);\n'
              '      if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '        // Still failing (device still hot / Metal unavailable): keep the\n'
              '        // capture-time semantics — skip the pair, never CPU brute-force.\n'
              '        // (A probe-debt pair keeps its ledger entry: a later finalize retry\n'
              '        // can still attempt it.)\n'
              '        ++s->stat_finalize_rematch_failed;\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] One completed (rc==0) full-match attempt\n'
              '      // settles a probe-debt pair regardless of outcome — exactly the single\n'
              '      // live attempt a gate-off run gave it.\n'
              '      bool was_probe_debt = false;\n'
              '      if (!s->probe_skipped_pairs.empty() &&\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(FramePairKey(j, f)) > 0) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_probe_debt_repaid_finalize;\n'
              '        was_probe_debt = true;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (num_matches <= 0) continue;  // legitimate zero-match pair\n'
              '\n'
              '      colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '      for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [ENRICH-TVG-FARM 2026-08-11] Farm path: only when both frames\' points\n'
              '      // are the live in-memory borrow (&fr.points, stable for the whole\n'
              '      // finalize). db-loaded cache entries slide-evict, so those pairs (and\n'
              '      // the env-off default) keep the inline estimation.\n'
              '      const bool farm_eligible =\n'
              '          farm && !s->frames[j].descriptors.empty() &&\n'
              '          !s->frames[f].descriptors.empty();\n'
              '      if (farm_eligible) {\n'
              '        auto job = std::make_unique<EnrichTvgJob>();\n'
              '        job->frame_a = &s->frames[j];\n'
              '        job->frame_b = &s->frames[f];\n'
              '        job->pts_a = fa->pts;\n'
              '        job->pts_b = fb->pts;\n'
              '        job->a_idx = j;\n'
              '        job->b_idx = f;\n'
              '        job->matches = std::move(matches);\n'
              '        job->tvg_options = &tvg_options;\n'
              '        job->was_probe_debt = was_probe_debt;\n'
              '        farm->Submit(job.get());\n'
              '        inflight.push_back(std::move(job));\n'
              '        while (inflight.size() >= max_inflight) commit_front();\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (farm) drain_all();  // keep todo-order commits before inline work\n'
              '      const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '          s->frames[j], *fa->pts, s->frames[f], *fb->pts, matches,\n'
              '          tvg_options);\n'
              '      if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) {\n'
              '        // Do not create an ExistsMatches tombstone: the pair remains debt and\n'
              '        // may be retried after more correspondences become available.\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->db->WriteMatches(s->frames[j].image_id, s->frames[f].image_id,\n'
              '                          matches);\n'
              '      const colmap::TwoViewGeometry& geometry = tvg.geometry;\n'
              '      s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(s->frames[j].image_id,\n'
              '                                  s->frames[f].image_id, geometry);\n'
              '      ++s->stat_finalize_rematch_written;\n'
              '      s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers +=\n'
              '          static_cast<int64_t>(geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] The DB now carries this pair, but the\n'
              '      // DELIVERED cloud inherits the live model, which never saw it — the\n'
              '      // pair missed its capture-time create/grow/merge window and official\n'
              '      // Retriangulate only recovers image pairs whose triangulated ratio is\n'
              '      // still under re_min_ratio. Park the verified inliers; the finalize\n'
              '      // worker replays them through the live gates after joining this\n'
              '      // thread (ApplyProbeDebtGrowth). This thread must NOT touch the model:\n'
              '      // stage-1 refinement owns it right now.\n'
              '      if (was_probe_debt && !geometry.inlier_matches.empty()) {\n'
              '        s->probe_debt_grow.push_back(\n'
              '            ProbeDebtGrowPair{j, f, geometry.inlier_matches});\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [ENRICH-TVG-FARM] In-flight pairs complete even on a budget stop — the\n'
              '    // same "started work finishes" semantics as the serial gate.\n'
              '    if (farm) drain_all();\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize re-match: starved_frames="\n'
              '                 << n_starved\n'
              '                 << " candidates=" << s->stat_finalize_rematch_candidates\n'
              '                 << " attempted=" << s->stat_finalize_rematch_attempted\n'
              '                 << " written=" << s->stat_finalize_rematch_written\n'
              '                 << " inliers=" << s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers\n'
              '                 << " failed=" << s->stat_finalize_rematch_failed\n'
              '                 << " gpu_fail_capture=" << s->stat_gpu_match_fail_total\n'
              '                 << " probe_debt_repaid="\n'
              '                 << s->stat_probe_debt_repaid_finalize\n'
              '                 << " probe_debt_left=" << s->probe_skipped_pairs.size();\n'
              '    // [ENRICH-JSONL 2026-07-26, signed] Pullable summary — glog is invisible\n'
              '    // in release builds, and the K6 lossless promise must be verifiable from\n'
              '    // the sidecar alone (written > 0 on a throttled capture, unattempted 0).\n'
              '    // [PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] probe_debt_* fields: the delivery-lossless\n'
              '    // promise of the probe gate is verifiable the same way (debt_left == 0\n'
              '    // and unattempted == 0 on a completed finalize).\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      char rline[384];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(rline, sizeof(rline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"finalize_rematch_summary\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"starved_frames\\":%lld,\\"candidates\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"attempted\\":%lld,\\"written\\":%lld,\\"inliers\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"failed\\":%lld,\\"unattempted\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"probe_debt_registered\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"probe_debt_repaid_live\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"probe_debt_repaid_finalize\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"probe_debt_left\\":%lld}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '                    (long long)n_starved,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_finalize_rematch_candidates,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_finalize_rematch_attempted,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_finalize_rematch_written,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_finalize_rematch_failed,\n'
              '                    (long long)(todo.size() - done),\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_probe_debt_registered,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_probe_debt_repaid_live,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_probe_debt_repaid_finalize,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->probe_skipped_pairs.size());\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, rline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    // Enhancement pass only — a failure here must never take finalize down.\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize re-match aborted: " << e.what();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [OFFICIAL-QUADRATIC 2026-07-25] Faithful port of COLMAP\'s sequential\n'
              '// quadratic-overlap pair generation.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Upstream: SequentialPairingOptions{overlap = 10, quadratic_overlap = true}\n'
              '// (colmap/controllers/pairing.h:88-91) and SequentialPairGenerator::Next\n'
              '// (pairing.cc:519-527), which emits, for every image i, the pairs\n'
              '// (i, i + 2^k) for k = 0 .. overlap-1 — i.e. gaps 1,2,4,...,512.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHY: the capture-time K-window (SelectStreamCandidates, K=12) covers only\n'
              '// near gaps, and the ARKit spatial-revisit pass is disabled at the official\n'
              '// endpoint, so the shipped binary produces NO long-range edges at all. Those\n'
              '// edges (i+128 / i+256 / i+512) are exactly what keeps a several-hundred-frame\n'
              '// sequence from drifting, which upstream enables by default.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// This is pair SELECTION only — as upstream, candidates are matched with the\n'
              '// shipped matcher, verified by colmap::EstimateTwoViewGeometry under COLMAP\n'
              '// DEFAULT TwoViewGeometryOptions, and appended to the matches /\n'
              '// two_view_geometries tables. No Point3D or observation is touched: all\n'
              '// geometry is left to the official mapper / triangulator / global BA that\n'
              '// consume the db next, so the delivered model is still the official endpoint.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Deliberately NOT gated by kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly: that constant\n'
              '// disables the SELF-DEVELOPED passes, one of which (RestoreTemporalDetail)\n'
              '// runs AFTER the final global BA. This pass is upstream COLMAP behaviour and\n'
              '// runs strictly BEFORE it. Kill switch: OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUADRATIC_OVERLAP=0.\n'
              'int OfficialQuadraticOverlap() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUADRATIC_OVERLAP")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 10;  // colmap SequentialPairingOptions::overlap default\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void AddOfficialQuadraticPairs(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  const int overlap = OfficialQuadraticOverlap();\n'
              '  if (overlap <= 0) return;\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || s->frames.size() < 2 || s->camera_id == 0) return;\n'
              '  const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '      s->options.use_gpu_match && (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs != nullptr);\n'
              '  // Same fail-closed policy as the rest of finalize: a requested-but-missing\n'
              '  // Metal matcher never degrades into O(N^2) CPU matching.\n'
              '  if (s->options.use_gpu_match && !gpu_avail) return;\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 1) Upstream pair set: (i, i + 2^k), k = 0..overlap-1. Pairs already in\n'
              '    //    the db (capture-time K-window) are skipped — re-matching identical\n'
              '    //    descriptors at the same ratio would only rewrite the same rows.\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<int, int>> todo;\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < num_frames; ++i) {\n'
              '      for (int k = 0; k < overlap; ++k) {\n'
              '        const int64_t j64 = static_cast<int64_t>(i) + (1ll << k);\n'
              '        if (j64 >= num_frames) break;\n'
              '        const int j = static_cast<int>(j64);\n'
              '        const colmap::image_t img1 = s->frames[i].image_id;\n'
              '        const colmap::image_t img2 = s->frames[j].image_id;\n'
              '        if (img1 == 0 || img2 == 0 || img1 == img2) continue;\n'
              '        if (s->db->ExistsMatches(img1, img2) ||\n'
              '            s->db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(img1, img2)) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        todo.emplace_back(i, j);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [QUAD-PREPAY 2026-07-26] No early return on an empty todo: the\n'
              '    // capture-idle prepay may have paid the whole debt, and the pullable\n'
              '    // summary below must land either way so a device run stays verifiable\n'
              '    // from the sidecar alone.\n'
              '    // [ENRICH-ORDER 2026-07-26] Gap-ascending so a budget stop sheds the\n'
              '    // LEAST valuable tail first: measured on cap_1785066707194992, gap-16\n'
              '    // pairs average ~130 inliers while gap-128 pairs average 0.7 — the\n'
              '    // original i-major order interleaves them and a mid-pass stop loses\n'
              '    // high-value near pairs while having paid for far garbage.\n'
              '    std::stable_sort(todo.begin(), todo.end(),\n'
              '                     [](const std::pair<int, int>& a,\n'
              '                        const std::pair<int, int>& b) {\n'
              '                       return (a.second - a.first) < (b.second - b.first);\n'
              '                     });\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 2) Feature access: borrow the in-memory FrameRecord (live sessions) or\n'
              '    //    load keypoints+descriptors from the db (resume sessions). Unlike the\n'
              '    //    K-windowed re-match, the reach here is up to 512 frames, so db loads\n'
              '    //    use a bounded LRU instead of a sliding window. Borrowed entries own\n'
              '    //    no memory and are never evicted; `pin` protects the partner frame of\n'
              '    //    the pair currently being matched.\n'
              '    struct QuadFeat {\n'
              '      const uint8_t* desc = nullptr;\n'
              '      const std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d>* pts = nullptr;\n'
              '      int n = 0;\n'
              '      bool loaded = false;  // owns desc_store/pts_store\n'
              '      uint64_t last_use = 0;\n'
              '      std::vector<uint8_t> desc_store;\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Vector2d> pts_store;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    constexpr int kQuadMaxLoadedFrames = 64;  // ~1 MB/frame at 8192 features\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<int, QuadFeat> cache;\n'
              '    uint64_t use_clock = 0;\n'
              '    int loaded_count = 0;\n'
              '    const auto get_feat = [&](int i, int pin) -> const QuadFeat* {\n'
              '      auto it = cache.find(i);\n'
              '      if (it != cache.end()) {\n'
              '        it->second.last_use = ++use_clock;\n'
              '        return it->second.n > 0 ? &it->second : nullptr;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& fr = s->frames[i];\n'
              '      if (!fr.descriptors.empty() && !fr.points.empty()) {\n'
              '        QuadFeat& feat = cache[i];\n'
              '        feat.desc = fr.descriptors.data();\n'
              '        feat.pts = &fr.points;\n'
              '        feat.n = fr.n_keypoints;\n'
              '        feat.last_use = ++use_clock;\n'
              '        return feat.n > 0 ? &feat : nullptr;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (loaded_count >= kQuadMaxLoadedFrames) {\n'
              '        int victim = -1;\n'
              '        uint64_t oldest = UINT64_MAX;\n'
              '        for (const auto& [idx, f] : cache) {\n'
              '          if (f.loaded && idx != pin && f.last_use < oldest) {\n'
              '            oldest = f.last_use;\n'
              '            victim = idx;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // unordered_map erase only invalidates references to the erased\n'
              '        // element, and `pin` is excluded above, so the caller\'s held pointer\n'
              '        // stays valid.\n'
              '        if (victim >= 0) {\n'
              '          cache.erase(victim);\n'
              '          --loaded_count;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      QuadFeat& feat = cache[i];\n'
              '      feat.last_use = ++use_clock;\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureKeypoints kps = s->db->ReadKeypoints(fr.image_id);\n'
              '      const colmap::FeatureDescriptors d = s->db->ReadDescriptors(fr.image_id);\n'
              '      const int n = static_cast<int>(kps.size());\n'
              '      if (n <= 0 || d.data.rows() != n || d.data.cols() != 128) {\n'
              '        feat.n = 0;  // negative-cache the malformed frame\n'
              '        return nullptr;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      feat.desc_store.assign(d.data.data(),\n'
              '                             d.data.data() + static_cast<size_t>(n) * 128);\n'
              '      feat.pts_store = colmap::FeatureKeypointsToPointsVector(kps);\n'
              '      feat.desc = feat.desc_store.data();\n'
              '      feat.pts = &feat.pts_store;\n'
              '      feat.n = n;\n'
              '      feat.loaded = true;\n'
              '      ++loaded_count;\n'
              '      return &feat;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 3) Match + verify + persist. Same matcher chain and ratio as add_frame;\n'
              '    //    COLMAP-DEFAULT two-view geometry options (no guided re-match, no\n'
              '    //    tightened inlier gates) so what lands in sqlite is what upstream\n'
              '    //    would have written for the same pair.\n'
              '    const double ratio =\n'
              '        s->options.match_max_ratio > 0 ? s->options.match_max_ratio : 0.8;\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;  // colmap defaults\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf;\n'
              '    int64_t written = 0;\n'
              '    int64_t budget_stopped = 0;\n'
              '    size_t done = 0;\n'
              '    // [QUAD-PIPELINE 2026-07-26, signed] Order-preserving prefetch (see\n'
              '    // QuadPipelineEnabled for provenance). Eligibility: GPU matcher present\n'
              '    // and every todo frame\'s features in memory (live sessions always;\n'
              '    // resume sessions fall back to the serial loop below because the\n'
              '    // db-load LRU cache must not be read across threads).\n'
              '    bool quad_pipeline = QuadPipelineEnabled() && gpu_avail;\n'
              '    if (quad_pipeline) {\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pi, pj] : todo) {\n'
              '        if (s->frames[pi].descriptors.empty() ||\n'
              '            s->frames[pj].descriptors.empty()) {\n'
              '          quad_pipeline = false;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (quad_pipeline) {\n'
              '      struct QuadMatchResult {\n'
              '        int i = 0, j = 0;\n'
              '        bool ran_matcher = false;\n'
              '        int mrc = 1;\n'
              '        int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '        std::vector<uint32_t> pairs;\n'
              '      };\n'
              '      std::mutex q_mu;\n'
              '      std::condition_variable q_cv;\n'
              '      std::deque<QuadMatchResult> q;\n'
              '      bool producer_done = false;\n'
              '      bool consumer_abort = false;\n'
              '      constexpr size_t kQuadPipelineDepth = 3;  // ≤3×64KB result lookahead\n'
              '      std::thread producer([&] {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        for (const auto& [pi, pj] : todo) {\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(q_mu);\n'
              '            q_cv.wait(lk, [&] {\n'
              '              return q.size() < kQuadPipelineDepth || consumer_abort;\n'
              '            });\n'
              '            if (consumer_abort) break;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          QuadMatchResult r;\n'
              '          r.i = pi;\n'
              '          r.j = pj;\n'
              '          const FrameRecord& fa = s->frames[pi];\n'
              '          const FrameRecord& fb = s->frames[pj];\n'
              '          const int cap =\n'
              '              fa.n_keypoints < fb.n_keypoints ? fa.n_keypoints : fb.n_keypoints;\n'
              '          if (cap > 0) {\n'
              '            r.pairs.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '            r.ran_matcher = true;\n'
              '            // Deterministic Metal matcher + mutual cross-check — no PRNG on\n'
              '            // this thread; the RANSAC stream stays on the consumer.\n'
              '            // [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] experiment arm first (default OFF).\n'
              '            if (EpiPriorMatchEnabled() &&\n'
              '                ArkitGuidedMatchPair(s, fa, fb, pj - pi, ratio,\n'
              '                                     r.pairs.data(), cap, &r.num_matches)) {\n'
              '              r.mrc = 0;\n'
              '            } else {\n'
              '              r.mrc = GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                  s, fa.frame_id, fa.descriptors.data(), fa.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                  fb.frame_id, fb.descriptors.data(), fb.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                  r.pairs.data(), cap, &r.num_matches);\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(q_mu);\n'
              '          q.push_back(std::move(r));\n'
              '          q_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(q_mu);\n'
              '        producer_done = true;\n'
              '        q_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // Consumer = THIS thread: budget gate, stats, TVG, and db writes in\n'
              '      // exact todo order — the same sequence the serial loop produces.\n'
              '      // [ENRICH-TVG-FARM 2026-08-11] Optional N-worker TVG stage between the\n'
              '      // matcher producer and the (single-threaded) db commit. The pipeline is\n'
              '      // live-only by eligibility, so &frames[].points is stable for every job.\n'
              '      const int q_tvg_threads = EnrichTvgThreads();\n'
              '      std::unique_ptr<EnrichTvgFarm> q_farm;\n'
              '      if (q_tvg_threads > 0)\n'
              '        q_farm = std::make_unique<EnrichTvgFarm>(q_tvg_threads);\n'
              '      const size_t q_max_inflight = static_cast<size_t>(q_tvg_threads) * 2;\n'
              '      std::deque<std::unique_ptr<EnrichTvgJob>> q_inflight;\n'
              '      const auto q_commit_front = [&] {\n'
              '        EnrichTvgJob* job = q_inflight.front().get();\n'
              '        q_farm->Wait(job);\n'
              '        if (MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(job->tvg)) {\n'
              '          s->db->WriteMatches(job->frame_a->image_id, job->frame_b->image_id,\n'
              '                              job->matches);\n'
              '          s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(job->frame_a->image_id,\n'
              '                                      job->frame_b->image_id,\n'
              '                                      job->tvg.geometry);\n'
              '          ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_written;\n'
              '          ++written;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        q_inflight.pop_front();\n'
              '      };\n'
              '      const auto q_drain_all = [&] {\n'
              '        while (!q_inflight.empty()) q_commit_front();\n'
              '      };\n'
              '      for (size_t idx = 0; idx < todo.size(); ++idx) {\n'
              '        QuadMatchResult r;\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(q_mu);\n'
              '          q_cv.wait(lk, [&] { return !q.empty() || producer_done; });\n'
              '          if (q.empty()) break;  // producer ended early\n'
              '          r = std::move(q.front());\n'
              '          q.pop_front();\n'
              '          q_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (EnrichBudgetExhausted(s)) {\n'
              '          budget_stopped = static_cast<int64_t>(todo.size() - done);\n'
              '          s->stat_enrich_budget_stopped += budget_stopped;\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] official quadratic overlap stopped by "\n'
              '                          "the enrichment time budget: "\n'
              '                       << budget_stopped << " of " << todo.size()\n'
              '                       << " candidate pairs left unattempted";\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ++done;\n'
              '        if (!r.ran_matcher) continue;  // cap<=0 (serial: skipped pre-attempt)\n'
              '        ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_attempted;\n'
              '        if (r.mrc != 0) continue;\n'
              '        if (r.num_matches <= 0) continue;\n'
              '        colmap::FeatureMatches matches(r.num_matches);\n'
              '        for (int m = 0; m < r.num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx1 = r.pairs[2 * m];\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx2 = r.pairs[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (q_farm) {\n'
              '          auto job = std::make_unique<EnrichTvgJob>();\n'
              '          job->frame_a = &s->frames[r.i];\n'
              '          job->frame_b = &s->frames[r.j];\n'
              '          job->pts_a = &s->frames[r.i].points;\n'
              '          job->pts_b = &s->frames[r.j].points;\n'
              '          job->a_idx = r.i;\n'
              '          job->b_idx = r.j;\n'
              '          job->matches = std::move(matches);\n'
              '          job->tvg_options = &tvg_options;\n'
              '          q_farm->Submit(job.get());\n'
              '          q_inflight.push_back(std::move(job));\n'
              '          while (q_inflight.size() >= q_max_inflight) q_commit_front();\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const colmap::image_t img1 = s->frames[r.i].image_id;\n'
              '        const colmap::image_t img2 = s->frames[r.j].image_id;\n'
              '        const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '            s->frames[r.i], s->frames[r.i].points, s->frames[r.j],\n'
              '            s->frames[r.j].points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '        if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '        s->db->WriteMatches(img1, img2, matches);\n'
              '        s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(img1, img2, tvg.geometry);\n'
              '        ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_written;\n'
              '        ++written;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [ENRICH-TVG-FARM] In-flight pairs complete on every exit path\n'
              '      // (natural end, budget stop, early producer end).\n'
              '      if (q_farm) q_drain_all();\n'
              '      {\n'
              '        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(q_mu);\n'
              '        consumer_abort = true;\n'
              '        q_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      producer.join();\n'
              '    } else\n'
              '    for (const auto& [i, j] : todo) {\n'
              '      // [ENRICH-ORDER 2026-07-26] Same per-pair budget gate as the starved-\n'
              '      // frame re-match: stop STARTING new pairs once the enrichment budget is\n'
              '      // exhausted (no-op unless armed). With the gap-ascending order above,\n'
              '      // the unattempted remainder is the least-valuable far tail.\n'
              '      if (EnrichBudgetExhausted(s)) {\n'
              '        budget_stopped = static_cast<int64_t>(todo.size() - done);\n'
              '        s->stat_enrich_budget_stopped += budget_stopped;\n'
              '        LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] official quadratic overlap stopped by "\n'
              '                        "the enrichment time budget: "\n'
              '                     << budget_stopped << " of " << todo.size()\n'
              '                     << " candidate pairs left unattempted";\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++done;\n'
              '      const QuadFeat* fa = get_feat(i, /*pin=*/-1);\n'
              '      if (!fa) continue;\n'
              '      const QuadFeat* fb = get_feat(j, /*pin=*/i);\n'
              '      if (!fb) continue;\n'
              '      const int cap = fa->n < fb->n ? fa->n : fb->n;\n'
              '      if (cap <= 0) continue;\n'
              '      pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '      int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_attempted;\n'
              '      // [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] experiment arm first (default OFF); any\n'
              '      // failure falls through to the unchanged full-GEMM path.\n'
              '      int mrc = 1;\n'
              '      if (EpiPriorMatchEnabled() && gpu_avail &&\n'
              '          ArkitGuidedMatchPair(s, s->frames[i], s->frames[j], j - i, ratio,\n'
              '                               pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches)) {\n'
              '        mrc = 0;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [P1-RC7-RETRY] transient rc=7 on a hot GPU gets two backoff retries.\n'
              '      if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '        mrc =\n'
              '            gpu_avail ? GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                            s, s->frames[i].frame_id, fa->desc, fa->n,\n'
              '                            s->frames[j].frame_id, fb->desc, fb->n, ratio,\n'
              '                            pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches)\n'
              '                      : aether_sift_match_pairs(fa->desc, fa->n, fb->desc,\n'
              '                                                fb->n, ratio, pair_buf.data(),\n'
              '                                                cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (mrc != 0) continue;          // still hot / unavailable: skip the pair\n'
              '      if (num_matches <= 0) continue;  // legitimate zero-match pair\n'
              '\n'
              '      colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '      for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t img1 = s->frames[i].image_id;\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t img2 = s->frames[j].image_id;\n'
              '      const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '          s->frames[i], *fa->pts, s->frames[j], *fb->pts, matches,\n'
              '          tvg_options);\n'
              '      if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '      s->db->WriteMatches(img1, img2, matches);\n'
              '      s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(img1, img2, tvg.geometry);\n'
              '      ++s->stat_spatial_quadratic_written;\n'
              '      ++written;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] official quadratic overlap: overlap="\n'
              '                 << overlap << " candidates=" << todo.size()\n'
              '                 << " written=" << written << " in " << (NowMs() - t0)\n'
              '                 << " ms";\n'
              '    // [ENRICH-JSONL 2026-07-26, signed] glog is invisible in release builds\n'
              '    // (DeviceLog only carries Dart lines), so the pass summary goes to the\n'
              '    // pullable sidecar — verifying a device run must not require pulling the\n'
              '    // 200 MB db to count pair gaps.\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      char qline[288];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(qline, sizeof(qline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"quadratic_summary\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"candidates\\":%zu,\\"written\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"budget_stopped\\":%lld,\\"ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"prepaid_attempted\\":%lld,\\"prepaid_written\\":%lld}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), todo.size(),\n'
              '                    (long long)written, (long long)budget_stopped,\n'
              '                    NowMs() - t0, (long long)s->stat_prepay_attempted,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_prepay_written);\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, qline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] experiment-arm accounting (absent when off).\n'
              '    if (EpiPriorMatchEnabled()) {\n'
              '      char eline[224];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(eline, sizeof(eline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"epi_summary\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"attempted\\":%lld,\\"fallback\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"band_base\\":%.0f,\\"band_per_gap\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"band_max\\":%.0f,\\"fallback_min\\":%d}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_epi_attempted,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_epi_fallback, EpiBandBasePx(),\n'
              '                    EpiBandPerGapPx(), EpiBandMaxPx(), EpiFallbackMinMatches());\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, eline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] official quadratic overlap aborted: "\n'
              '                 << e.what();\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] official quadratic overlap aborted";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Pick the largest reconstruction in the manager and write the JSON summary.\n'
              'std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> PickBestAndReport(\n'
              '    const std::shared_ptr<colmap::ReconstructionManager>& manager,\n'
              '    double solve_ms, char* out_json, int out_cap) {\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> best;\n'
              '  size_t best_reg = 0, best_pts = 0;\n'
              '  double best_reproj = 0.0, best_track = 0.0;\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; i < manager->Size(); ++i) {\n'
              '    const auto& recon = manager->Get(i);\n'
              '    if (recon->NumRegImages() >= best_reg) {\n'
              '      best_reg = recon->NumRegImages();\n'
              '      best_pts = recon->NumPoints3D();\n'
              '      best_reproj = recon->ComputeMeanReprojectionError();\n'
              '      best_track = recon->ComputeMeanTrackLength();\n'
              '      best = recon;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '    std::snprintf(out_json, out_cap,\n'
              '                  "{\\"solve_ms\\":%.1f,\\"n_models\\":%zu,\\"n_registered\\":%zu,"\n'
              '                  "\\"n_points3d\\":%zu,\\"reproj_px\\":%.4f,\\"track_len\\":%.3f}",\n'
              '                  solve_ms, manager->Size(), best_reg, best_pts, best_reproj,\n'
              '                  best_track);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return best;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [TRI-ANGLE A/B 2026-07-11] Finalize triangulation CREATION gate, env-tunable\n'
              '// for host replay A/B (cap43 registration-rate investigation: device 81/118\n'
              '// registered on the first 3.0° build vs 75% on the 1.5° build). Phase 1\n'
              '// (RunIncremental / mapper registration) and phase 2 (RefineGlobalBA) read\n'
              '// SEPARATE env names so "registration-wide, refine-strict" configs can be\n'
              '// tested. Unset -> the shipped 2.0° (T20 2026-07-11, scan-matrix verdict —\n'
              '// moved in lock-step with the two streaming gates, see\n'
              '// LiveCreateTriMinAngleRad).\n'
              'double TriMinAngleDeg(const char* env_name) {\n'
              '  const char* e = std::getenv(env_name);\n'
              '  if (e && e[0]) {\n'
              '    const double d = std::atof(e);\n'
              '    if (d > 0.0) return d;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return 2.0;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [TRI-TRANSITIVITY 2026-08-05] IncrementalTriangulator::Options::\n'
              '// max_transitivity —— 搜索对应关系时的**传递深度**。上游默认 1(只看直接匹配\n'
              '// 过的帧对);抬到 2 会多走一层传递:A↔B、B↔C 已匹配但 A↔C 未建立时,也把\n'
              '// A、C 的观测纳入同一 track 考察。\n'
              '//\n'
              '// 为什么打这个靶:我方 146 帧真机采集实测 track 长度 **61.3% 是 2-view**\n'
              '// (p50=2 / p90=5)。分层实测显示 2-view 的代价是真的:σ_depth p50 5.89mm vs\n'
              '// 7+ 视 1.14mm(5.2×),且 31.7% 的 2-view 视差不足 5°。而我方全部采集都是\n'
              '// out-and-back(净绕行 ≤0.37 圈、无一回到起点),三元组"缺边"正是短 track 的\n'
              '// 结构性来源 —— 传递搜索直接补这类缺口,且**零 GPU、零新增匹配、零热**\n'
              '// (只在已匹配的对应图上多走一层,不跑 matcher)。\n'
              '//\n'
              '// ⚠️ 上游同结构体里 complete_max_transitivity 默认已是 5(Complete 步早就走\n'
              '// 多层),所以把 Triangulate/Retriangulate 的深度从 1 抬到 2 是与上游自身设计\n'
              '// 一致的方向,不是发明新机制。\n'
              '// ⚠️ 代价未知:传递搜索的对应集会变大 → 三角化候选更多 → CPU 上升幅度需实测。\n'
              '// 因此**默认关**(unset ⇒ 返回上游默认 1,逐字节复现现状),仅\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_TRANSITIVITY=2 时生效,便于单变量 A/B。\n'
              'int TriMaxTransitivity() {\n'
              '  const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_TRANSITIVITY");\n'
              '  if (e && e[0]) {\n'
              '    const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '    if (v >= 1 && v <= 5) return v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return 1;  // 上游默认\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [AETHER BA-MIXED 2026-07-11] Finalize global BA mixed-precision solves\n'
              '// (fp32 factorize/solve of the reduced camera system + fp64 iterative-\n'
              '// refinement steps). ⚠️ DEFAULT OFF — host A/B (cap42, spatial_ab streaming\n'
              '// driver, GPU matcher, thread-count controlled at 12) VETOED the default-on\n'
              '// plan: the mixed arm shifted the DELIVERED cloud far outside the noise band\n'
              '// (points 66752→72897 = +9.2%, mean reproj 1.0156→1.0430 = +0.027, 9-gate\n'
              '// 4/9: reproj_median +5.2%, tri_angle x0.943, weak_track +5.4%, arkit_pos\n'
              '// +15.7%). Attribution was exact: the threads-only arm was equal to baseline\n'
              '// to 4 decimals on every metric, the mixed-only arm reproduced the full\n'
              '// drift. Mechanism: the CAUCHY-reweighted Schur complement is near-singular\n'
              '// (the same conditioning that crashes Accelerate\'s fp64 sparse Cholesky);\n'
              '// fp32 factorization error scales with the condition number, iterative\n'
              '// refinement stalls on it, and the LM trajectory converges to a visibly\n'
              '// different (worse) state — NOT run-to-run noise. Opt-in for future\n'
              '// experiments: OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_MIXED=1 (+OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_MIXED_REFINE=N, default 3).\n'
              '// Capture-time LOCAL BA is never touched either way.\n'
              'bool BaMixedEnabled() {\n'
              '  const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_MIXED");\n'
              '  return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [AETHER BA-THREADS 2026-07-11] Finalize BA ceres thread budget. Previous\n'
              '// behavior was num_threads=-1 -> hardware_concurrency (host M3 Pro 12, A16 6)\n'
              '// for every solve above the 6000-residual floor. New default = min(6, hw-2):\n'
              '// leaves headroom for the rest of the process (GPU-matcher CPU side, db I/O,\n'
              '// Dart/UI on device) instead of saturating every core with Schur workers —\n'
              '// A16: 4 threads (2P+2E stay free), host M3 Pro: 6. Env OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_THREADS\n'
              '// overrides (any positive integer). The stage-1 overlap window still halves\n'
              '// whatever this returns (enrichment is the critical path there); stage 2 and\n'
              '// the full re-run run the full budget.\n'
              'int FinalizeBaThreads() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_THREADS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [BA-THREADS-POST 2026-07-28] min(6, hw-2) → min(6, hw). The finalize\n'
              '    // runs strictly AFTER capture: the camera is off, so the two-core camera\n'
              '    // reserve is dead weight — the same rationale as the matcher sprint mode\n'
              '    // and the signed stage-1 full-thread knife. Quality: thread count was\n'
              '    // proven bit-equal 07-11 (signed, min(6,hw-2) knife) and re-proven\n'
              '    // 2026-07-28 on host (_host_fixtures/prepay_threads_exact: T4/T6 ×2\n'
              '    // delivered PLYs byte-identical, iteration counts identical; finalize\n'
              '    // wall −16.1%/−12.8%). Device: A16 4 → 6. The capture-period windowed\n'
              '    // incremental BA keeps its own reserve via LiveBaThreads() below.\n'
              '    const int hw = static_cast<int>(std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n'
              '    return std::max(1, std::min(6, hw));\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Capture-period thread budget (windowed incremental global BA): the camera,\n'
              '// GPU-matcher CPU side, and Dart UI are live — keep the two-core reserve.\n'
              'int LiveBaThreads() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_BA_THREADS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const int hw = static_cast<int>(std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n'
              '    return std::max(1, std::min(6, hw - 2));\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-LBA-THREADS 2026-07-29] Capture-time LOCAL BA thread knobs.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// The streaming path builds its OWN IncrementalPipelineOptions (see the\n'
              '// add_frame body) and never touched either of these, so it silently inherited\n'
              '// the two upstream defaults:\n'
              '//   num_threads = -1                                   → every core\n'
              '//   ba_min_num_residuals_for_cpu_multi_threading = 50000\n'
              '// and bundle_adjustment_ceres.cc forces num_threads=1 whenever the problem is\n'
              '// UNDER that floor. Our 6-image window carries ~39k residuals, i.e. under it —\n'
              '// measured on cap7_day_A: 240 solves at threads=1 vs 102 at threads=12. So the\n'
              '// hot path oscillates between fully serial and fully saturating, and never sits\n'
              '// at the deliberate LiveBaThreads() reserve that exists precisely to keep the\n'
              '// camera/UI alive mid-capture (the reserve is only wired to the periodic\n'
              '// incremental GLOBAL BA).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// [AETHER-T2 profile] the Ceres minimizer is 92.6% of the solve and the solve is\n'
              '// 81.5% of local BA, so thread count is the largest LOSSLESS lever available.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Both knobs default to "leave the upstream value alone" so an unset\n'
              '// environment is byte-identical to the shipped binary — the arm stays a clean\n'
              '// single variable.\n'
              'int LiveLocalBaThreadsOverride() {  // <=0 → don\'t touch\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_LBA_THREADS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LOCAL-FTOL-AB 2026-08-08] Ceres function_tolerance for the LIVE per-frame\n'
              '// local BA. DEFAULT 0.0 == the shipped COLMAP value, so an unset env leaves the\n'
              '// solve byte-identical. See the wiring comment at the add_frame call site for\n'
              '// why zero is suspected to be a defect rather than a choice.\n'
              'double LiveLocalBaFtol() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_LBA_FTOL")) {\n'
              '      const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0.0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-LBA-LOSS-AB 2026-08-10 用户签决] 流式 local BA 的 loss 从未被覆盖,\n'
              '// 一直吃上游 SOFT_L1@1.0;finalize/批量早已是 CAUCHY@1.0(见 :6336)。\n'
              '// bench50 上 CAUCHY 0.8319px vs 默认 0.9996px,但那是整条重建口径 ——\n'
              '// 6 帧小窗口的 local BA 是否同样受益必须单变量重放定罪。\n'
              '// 未设或 <0 == 逐字节等于出货路径(不碰字段)。1=SOFT_L1 2=CAUCHY。\n'
              'int LiveLocalBaLossType() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_LBA_LOSS")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return -1;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-LBA-NATURAL-STOP 2026-08-10 用户签决"收敛做到自然停止,不设上限"]\n'
              '// 现状:ftol=0(永不因收敛提前停)+ 上游 max_iter=25 ⇒ cap201 有 49.7% 的\n'
              '// live solve 撞上限被打断(NO_CONVERGENCE)。自然停止 = ftol 给正常值\n'
              '// (LIVE_LBA_FTOL=1e-6)+ 本上限放大到碰不到(Ceres 必须给整数)。\n'
              '// 未设或 <=0 == 逐字节等于出货路径(吃上游默认 25)。\n'
              'int LiveLocalBaMaxIter() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_LBA_MAX_ITER")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [LIVE-TRI-MINANGLE-AB 2026-08-10 用户签决] 流式 TriangulateImage 的\n'
              '// min_angle 从未被设置,吃上游 1.5°;T20(2026-07-11)已把其余四处统一到\n'
              '// 2.0°,这里是唯一漏网。裁决标准(用户原话):"可以减少错误和漂移的点,\n'
              '// 但是正确的点一个都不能减少" —— 正确覆盖掉一格即出局,且须先过 E9-C\n'
              '// 位姿翘曲门。未设或 <=0 == 逐字节等于出货路径(不碰字段)。单位:度。\n'
              'double LiveTriMinAngleDegOverride() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_TRI_MIN_ANGLE_DEG")) {\n'
              '      const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '      if (v > 0.0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 0.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [GRAVITY-RA 2026-08-10 用户签决"抄官方/不自研"] finalize 重力对齐旋转平均\n'
              '// pass(ECCV24 arXiv:2410.12763,vendored COLMAP 4.1.0 官方实现)。默认关 =\n'
              '// 逐字节出货;env 开启后插在 stage-1 与 stage-2 之间:用 db 的 TVG 相对旋转 +\n'
              '// 每帧 ARKit 重力(1-DoF 圆回归分层求解)重解全局旋转,平移按"相机中心不变"\n'
              '// 重组(t_new = -R_new·C_old),再交 stage-2 全局 BA 收回平移与点。\n'
              'bool FinalizeGravityRaEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_GRAVITY_RA");\n'
              '    return e != nullptr && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// 返回 RA 应用前的位姿快照(gauge 重锚用;空 = pass 没跑/没应用)。\n'
              'std::unordered_map<colmap::frame_t, colmap::Rigid3d> MaybeRunFinalizeGravityRa(\n'
              '    const std::string& db_path, colmap::Reconstruction* refined) {\n'
              '  if (!FinalizeGravityRaEnabled() || refined == nullptr) return {};\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // 快照旧位姿:RA 写回会把平移抹成 NaN(官方 controller 是从零建图的\n'
              '    // 用法),我们要保相机中心。\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<colmap::frame_t, colmap::Rigid3d> old_poses;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [frame_id, frame] : refined->Frames()) {\n'
              '      if (frame.HasPose()) old_poses.emplace(frame_id, frame.RigFromWorld());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (old_poses.size() < 3) return {};\n'
              '\n'
              '    // pose graph:enriched db 的 TVG 相对旋转(官方分解入口)。\n'
              '    auto database = colmap::Database::Open(db_path);\n'
              '    colmap::DatabaseCache::Options cache_options;\n'
              '    cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    auto cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*database, cache_options);\n'
              '    colmap::MaybeDecomposeRelativePoses(cache.get());\n'
              '    colmap::PoseGraph pose_graph;\n'
              '    pose_graph.Load(*cache->CorrespondenceGraph());\n'
              '    if (pose_graph.Empty()) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] empty pose graph — skipped";\n'
              '      return {};\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 每帧重力:registry 的 gravity_cam 是相机系"物理向下",直接作\n'
              '    // PosePrior::gravity(COLMAP 的 gravity_dir 约定 = 世界 +Y 即"重力轴",\n'
              '    // 实测:取反会让 RA 世界整体倒置 —— gauge 轴 [-0.66,0.69,-0.29]@176.5°;\n'
              '    // 不取反 gauge 应为纯 yaw。规范对齐无论哪种都能拉回,但符号必须写对,\n'
              '    // 免得后人拿 gauge 轴当诊断时被误导)。\n'
              '    std::vector<colmap::PosePrior> pose_priors;\n'
              '    pose_priors.reserve(refined->NumImages());\n'
              '    for (const auto& [image_id, image] : refined->Images()) {\n'
              '      double g[3];\n'
              '      if (!aether_ba_get_gravity_prior(image.Name().c_str(), g)) continue;\n'
              '      Eigen::Vector3d gravity(g[0], g[1], g[2]);\n'
              '      const double n = gravity.norm();\n'
              '      if (!std::isfinite(n) || n < 1e-9) continue;\n'
              '      colmap::PosePrior prior;\n'
              '      prior.pose_prior_id = image_id;\n'
              '      prior.gravity = gravity / n;\n'
              '      pose_priors.push_back(prior);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::RotationEstimatorOptions ra_options;\n'
              '    ra_options.use_gravity = !pose_priors.empty();\n'
              '    ra_options.use_stratified = true;\n'
              '    // skip_initialization 保持 false:PR #4225 —— gravity 模式的 MST 初始化\n'
              '    // 是防 180° 翻转的关键(修复前 5/1000 随机翻转)。\n'
              '    ra_options.skip_initialization = false;\n'
              '    // refinement pass 语义(官方注释:"Set to true for refinement passes")。\n'
              '    ra_options.filter_unregistered = true;\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (!colmap::RunRotationAveraging(\n'
              '            ra_options, pose_graph, *refined, pose_priors)) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] solve failed — old poses restored";\n'
              '      for (const auto& [frame_id, pose] : old_poses) {\n'
              '        refined->Frame(frame_id).SetRigFromWorld(pose);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return {};\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 规范对齐:RA 解带任意全局规范(重力钉住竖轴后仍余全局 yaw;首测\n'
              '    // 不对齐时全帧统一偏 ~176.5°,stage-2 因点云仍在旧规范而屠到 42/110)。\n'
              '    // 求单个全局旋转 S = polar(Σ R_ra_iᵀ·R_old_i),把 RA 解拉回旧模型规范;\n'
              '    // 重力符号若不一致,S 会带出非 yaw 分量 —— 遥测里记 S 的轴向供核对。\n'
              '    Eigen::Matrix3d gauge_accum = Eigen::Matrix3d::Zero();\n'
              '    int gauge_n = 0;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [frame_id, old_pose] : old_poses) {\n'
              '      const auto& frame = refined->Frame(frame_id);\n'
              '      if (!frame.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& ra_q = frame.RigFromWorld().rotation();\n'
              '      if (ra_q.coeffs().hasNaN()) continue;\n'
              '      gauge_accum += ra_q.toRotationMatrix().transpose() *\n'
              '                     old_pose.rotation().toRotationMatrix();\n'
              '      ++gauge_n;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Eigen::Matrix3d gauge = Eigen::Matrix3d::Identity();\n'
              '    if (gauge_n >= 3) {\n'
              '      Eigen::JacobiSVD<Eigen::Matrix3d> svd(\n'
              '          gauge_accum, Eigen::ComputeFullU | Eigen::ComputeFullV);\n'
              '      gauge = svd.matrixU() * svd.matrixV().transpose();\n'
              '      if (gauge.determinant() < 0) {\n'
              '        Eigen::Matrix3d flip = Eigen::Matrix3d::Identity();\n'
              '        flip(2, 2) = -1;\n'
              '        gauge = svd.matrixU() * flip * svd.matrixV().transpose();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const Eigen::AngleAxisd gauge_aa(gauge);\n'
              '    const Eigen::Quaterniond gauge_q(gauge);\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 写回:相机中心不变,旋转取"规范对齐后的 RA 结果";没解到的帧恢复旧位姿。\n'
              '    int applied = 0;\n'
              '    double max_delta_deg = 0.0, sum_delta_deg = 0.0;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [frame_id, old_pose] : old_poses) {\n'
              '      auto& frame = refined->Frame(frame_id);\n'
              '      const colmap::Rigid3d ra_pose =\n'
              '          frame.HasPose() ? frame.RigFromWorld() : old_pose;\n'
              '      const bool ra_valid = frame.HasPose() &&\n'
              '                            !ra_pose.rotation().coeffs().hasNaN();\n'
              '      if (!ra_valid) {\n'
              '        frame.SetRigFromWorld(old_pose);\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const Eigen::Quaterniond aligned_q = ra_pose.rotation() * gauge_q;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d center_old =\n'
              '          -(old_pose.rotation().inverse() * old_pose.translation());\n'
              '      frame.SetRigFromWorld(\n'
              '          colmap::Rigid3d(aligned_q, -(aligned_q * center_old)));\n'
              '      const double delta_deg =\n'
              '          old_pose.rotation().angularDistance(aligned_q) * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '      max_delta_deg = std::max(max_delta_deg, delta_deg);\n'
              '      sum_delta_deg += delta_deg;\n'
              '      ++applied;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    LOG(INFO) << "[gravity-ra] applied=" << applied << "/" << old_poses.size()\n'
              '              << " priors=" << pose_priors.size()\n'
              '              << " gauge_deg=" << gauge_aa.angle() * 180.0 / M_PI\n'
              '              << " gauge_axis=[" << gauge_aa.axis().transpose() << "]"\n'
              '              << " mean_delta=" << (applied ? sum_delta_deg / applied : 0.0)\n'
              '              << "deg max_delta=" << max_delta_deg\n'
              '              << "deg ms=" << (NowMs() - t0);\n'
              '    return old_poses;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] exception: " << e.what() << " — skipped";\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] unknown exception — skipped";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return {};\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [GRAVITY-RA GAUGE-REANCHOR 2026-08-10] stage-2 是自由规范求解:RA 扰动初值\n'
              '// 后整个模型的 gauge 会被 BA 带走(实测 vs 基线整体转 1.18°、尺度漂 0.86%,\n'
              '// 刚体对齐后翘曲仍 7.5mm 爆 E9-C 门)。交付铁律 = 同 gauge 直出 ⇒ 这里用\n'
              '// Umeyama(含尺度)把 stage-2 之后的模型整体锚回 RA 前的位姿快照 —— 与\n'
              '// 位置先验 BA"每 pass 对当前重建重估 Sim3"同一先例。快照 gauge 即\n'
              '// live_reuse 贴住 ARKit 的原 gauge,基线路径不跑此函数(快照为空)。\n'
              'void MaybeReanchorAfterGravityRa(\n'
              '    const std::unordered_map<colmap::frame_t, colmap::Rigid3d>& snapshot,\n'
              '    colmap::Reconstruction* refined) {\n'
              '  if (snapshot.empty() || refined == nullptr) return;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> src, dst;\n'
              '    src.reserve(snapshot.size());\n'
              '    dst.reserve(snapshot.size());\n'
              '    for (const auto& [frame_id, old_pose] : snapshot) {\n'
              '      if (!refined->ExistsFrame(frame_id)) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& frame = refined->Frame(frame_id);\n'
              '      if (!frame.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& cur = frame.RigFromWorld();\n'
              '      if (cur.rotation().coeffs().hasNaN() || cur.translation().hasNaN()) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      src.push_back(-(cur.rotation().inverse() * cur.translation()));\n'
              '      dst.push_back(\n'
              '          -(old_pose.rotation().inverse() * old_pose.translation()));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (src.size() < 3) return;\n'
              '    Eigen::Matrix<double, 3, Eigen::Dynamic> S(3, src.size()), D(3, src.size());\n'
              '    for (size_t i = 0; i < src.size(); ++i) {\n'
              '      S.col(i) = src[i];\n'
              '      D.col(i) = dst[i];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const Eigen::Matrix4d T = Eigen::umeyama(S, D, /*with_scaling=*/true);\n'
              '    const Eigen::Matrix3d sR = T.topLeftCorner<3, 3>();\n'
              '    const double scale = std::cbrt(sR.determinant());\n'
              '    if (!std::isfinite(scale) || scale < 0.5 || scale > 2.0) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] reanchor scale " << scale\n'
              '                   << " out of sane band — skipped";\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const Eigen::Matrix3d R = sR / scale;\n'
              '    const colmap::Sim3d new_from_old(\n'
              '        scale, Eigen::Quaterniond(R), T.topRightCorner<3, 1>());\n'
              '    refined->Transform(new_from_old);\n'
              '    const Eigen::AngleAxisd aa(R);\n'
              '    LOG(INFO) << "[gravity-ra] reanchored: rot="\n'
              '              << aa.angle() * 180.0 / M_PI\n'
              '              << "deg scale=" << scale\n'
              '              << " t=" << T.topRightCorner<3, 1>().norm();\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] reanchor exception: " << e.what();\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[gravity-ra] reanchor unknown exception";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int LiveLocalBaMtFloorOverride() {  // <0 → don\'t touch\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_LBA_MT_FLOOR")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return -1;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Run the validated IncrementalPipeline over (db_path, image_path) into a fresh\n'
              '// reconstruction manager, fill *out_recon with the best model + write JSON.\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t RunIncremental(\n'
              '    const std::string& db_path, const std::string& image_path,\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_options_t& opts,\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<colmap::ReconstructionManager>* out_manager,\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction>* out_recon, char* out_json,\n'
              '    int out_cap, bool local_only = false) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    auto pipeline_opts = std::make_shared<colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions>();\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks = TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    // No images on device => no point colors to read; skip color extraction\n'
