              '    // (avoids per-image "could not read image" warnings + the file I/O).\n'
              '    if (image_path.empty()) pipeline_opts->extract_colors = false;\n'
              '    // [A] Defer ALL in-loop global BA (periodic + recovery) to the single\n'
              '    // finalize solve. Grounded on the real-res bench (396 frames, 9555 kp/img,\n'
              '    // 231k pts, host ceres): per-frame registration becomes LOCAL-BA-ONLY ->\n'
              '    // worst single frame 16122ms (periodic, 19 frames >2s) collapses to 511ms,\n'
              '    // ZERO frames >2s. The periodic global BA is the only O(N)-growing term and\n'
              '    // the per-frame SLA breaker; the EARLIER periodic caps (gref1/giter15) were\n'
              '    // NOT enough at real resolution (in-loop + recovery solves still spiked to\n'
              '    // 17s). Deferring takes every O(N) global solve off the per-frame critical\n'
              '    // path so capture-time UI latency stays local-only regardless of N.\n'
              '    // Bonus: reproj IMPROVES 1.1651 -> 1.1455 — the single finalize global BA\n'
              '    // over the complete model converges cleaner than incremental periodic refines.\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->defer_global_ba = true;\n'
              '    // [ASYNC] local_only => also skip the finalize global BA, so Run() returns a\n'
              '    // LOCAL-only reconstruction (instant). The async-finalize worker then runs\n'
              '    // the global BA off the critical path. Batch/sync callers pass false and get\n'
              '    // the validated full-finalize result (reproj 1.1455).\n'
              '    if (local_only) pipeline_opts->skip_finalize_global_ba = true;\n'
              '    // Local BA caps = the per-frame UI cost (the ONLY thing on the critical path\n'
              '    // now). liter15 + mt6000 (multi-thread above 6k residuals). max 511ms desktop.\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_local_max_num_iterations = 15;\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_min_num_residuals_for_cpu_multi_threading = 6000;\n'
              '    // [AETHER ship config 2026-06-24] Wire the grounded BA optimizations (were only\n'
              '    // in the bench before; production was using default SOFT_L1 local / TRIVIAL\n'
              '    // global = the useless ~local-floor finalize):\n'
              '    //  - CAUCHY local+global: the finalize\'s ENTIRE value. A1 experiment: TRIVIAL\n'
              '    //    finalize reproj 0.938 ~= local floor; CAUCHY -> 0.84. NOT droppable.\n'
              '    //  - global CAUCHY routes to DENSE_SCHUR (override in incremental_pipeline.cc):\n'
              '    //    Eigen dense Cholesky (Accelerate sparse fails on CAUCHY). Device finalize\n'
              '    //    192s(ITERATIVE) -> 53s(DENSE) -> 43s(+gftol). Full quality.\n'
              '    //  - gftol=1e-6: each solve stops on convergence instead of burning the giter\n'
              '    //    cap (function_tolerance was 0). -30% time, quality within +-0.003 noise.\n'
              '    //  - keep-CAUCHY local pass-2 (incremental_mapper.cc) + lnum=6: better preview\n'
              '    //    (0.936->0.86) + lower drift (2.08%/7.12% -> 1.42%/4.87%). Device 2s-gate\n'
              '    //    1695ms (the per-frame SLA only binds the FUTURE streaming path; current\n'
              '    //    production runs pipeline.Run() as ONE post-capture batch -> no per-frame gate).\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_local_loss_type = 2;     // CAUCHY\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_local_loss_scale = 1.0;\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_global_loss_type = 2;    // CAUCHY (-> DENSE_SCHUR via override)\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_global_loss_scale = 1.0;\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_global_function_tolerance = 1e-6;  // converge-stop\n'
              '    // [AETHER BA-MIXED/THREADS 2026-07-11] This is the db-driven batch /\n'
              '    // full-re-run finalize: global BA gets the finalize thread budget\n'
              '    // (OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_THREADS override; default min(6, hw-2), was -1 -> all\n'
              '    // cores; quality equal to baseline to 4 decimals on every 9-gate metric,\n'
              '    // host A/B). Mixed precision stays OFF unless OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_MIXED=1\n'
              '    // (A/B-vetoed default, see BaMixedEnabled). num_threads also feeds the\n'
              '    // local-BA solves of this batch path — intended: same headroom\n'
              '    // rationale, and the live capture-time local BA does NOT go through here\n'
              '    // (hand-built options in the streaming path).\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_global_mixed_precision = BaMixedEnabled();\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->num_threads = FinalizeBaThreads();\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->mapper.ba_local_num_images = 6;\n'
              '    // Each image already carries the calibrated intrinsics of its exact ARFrame.\n'
              '    // Keep those values constant throughout mapper registration and every BA.\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_refine_focal_length = false;\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->ba_refine_extra_params = false;\n'
              '    // [TRI-ANGLE 2026-07-11] Creation parallax gate 1.5°(colmap default)→2.0°\n'
              '    // (first shipped at 3.0°, re-priced to 2.0° by the T20 scan-matrix verdict),\n'
              '    // aligned with BOTH streaming creation gates (add_frame kMinTriAngleRad and\n'
              '    // RestoreTemporalDetail — 2.0° each). The delivered cloud\'s low-parallax\n'
              '    // tail was born HERE: the mapper\'s IncrementalTriangulator created tracks\n'
              '    // down to 1.5° pairwise parallax (cap47 attribution: 25.9% of native\n'
              '    // 2-view delivered points sat below 3° — depth-ambiguous fuzz around thin\n'
              '    // structures). Creation-only: filter_min_tri_angle stays at its default,\n'
              '    // so BA may still keep an existing point that drifts into [1.5°, 3°) —\n'
              '    // same semantics as the live path. init_min_tri_angle (initial pair) is\n'
              '    // far above both and unaffected (incremental_pipeline.cc:459 overrides\n'
              '    // min_angle for the init pair only).\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->triangulation.min_angle =\n'
              '        TriMinAngleDeg("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_MIN_ANGLE");\n'
              '    // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07] env 设置时覆盖上面的出生门 + filter 门。\n'
              '    ApplyBirthGateAB(pipeline_opts.get());\n'
              '    // [AETHER] NOTE: ignore_redundant_points3D + freeze-intrinsics were tried (RAM\n'
              '    // 2.36->1.45GB, 4x faster) but cost reproj 0.955->0.9952 (~4%) -> REVERTED per the\n'
              '    // zero-quality-loss requirement. Full intrinsic refinement + all points stay.\n'
              '    // Quality-neutral memory wins only: ITERATIVE routing (device-safe, same optimum)\n'
              '    // + (GLOMAP) tracks_full release. Full-scene fit relies on moderate (non-exhaustive)\n'
              '    // match density, not on dropping points/intrinsics.\n'
              '    // [B] Per-frame margin knob, GATED on the iPhone BA-factor measurement:\n'
              '    // ba_local_num_images 6->4 cuts per-frame max 511->389ms but costs reproj\n'
              '    // 1.1455->1.1574. Default keeps 6 (best reproj); drop to 4 ONLY if the device\n'
              '    // cannot hold a 511ms local BA under the 2s SLA. (Memory-bound BA likely runs\n'
              '    // ~3-5x desktop, not the ~6x of compute-bound extraction -> 511ms*4 ~ 2s.)\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // Finalize global BA is now the ONLY global solve -> it MUST run FULL\n'
              '    // (default gref5/giter50) to converge; the old E4 periodic caps under-converge\n'
              '    // it (reproj 1.1847). Cost ~103s desktop (~5-8min device) is a one-time\n'
              '    // POST-capture price, async-able onto a worker thread later. Left at defaults.\n'
              '    auto manager = std::make_shared<colmap::ReconstructionManager>();\n'
              '\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    pipeline_opts->image_path = image_path;  // [4.0.4] image_path moved into options\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalPipeline pipeline(\n'
              '        pipeline_opts, colmap::Database::Open(db_path), manager);\n'
              '    pipeline.Run();\n'
              '    const double solve_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '\n'
              '    *out_manager = manager;\n'
              '    *out_recon = PickBestAndReport(manager, solve_ms, out_json, out_cap);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '      std::snprintf(out_json, out_cap, "{\\"error\\":\\"%s\\"}", e.what());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Final refinement is fail-closed. A db-driven full rerun would register\n'
              '// cameras through unconstrained absolute pose (P3P), discarding the mandatory\n'
              '// ARKit gravity identity. That historical fallback is intentionally removed:\n'
              '// an exception is reported as ERROR and the immutable db + pose sidecar remain\n'
              '// available for an exact known-pose retry.\n'
              '// [GHOST-MASK 2026-07-12] defined after WriteFinalizeSegments below.\n'
              'void MaybeWriteGhostMask(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                         const colmap::Reconstruction& recon);\n'
              '\n'
              'void RefineFailClosed(aether_sfm_session* s, const char* why) {\n'
              '  LOG(ERROR) << "[aether_sfm] mandatory-ARKit refine blocked (" << why\n'
              '             << "); no P3P/full-rerun fallback is permitted";\n'
              '  s->finalize_status.store(3);  // AETHER_SFM_FINALIZE_ERROR\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Shared phase-2 pipeline options (the validated Cauchy global config).\n'
              '// [2026-07-10] COMPLETE the phase-2 Cauchy config. This block previously set only\n'
              '// min_num_matches + residuals, so ba_global_loss_type defaulted to 0 (TRIVIAL) —\n'
              '// phase 2 was NOT the full Cauchy it was described as. Set the SAME validated global\n'
              '// config the synchronous RunPipeline uses (RunIncremental above): Cauchy@1.0,\n'
              '// gftol 1e-6, gref5/giter50 (defaults, set explicit so they hit the log).\n'
              '// RefineReconstruction runs iterative global refinement, which reads\n'
              '// ba_global_* for its loss. NOTE: this sets ONLY the loss config; the\n'
              '// RefineReconstruction-vs-TriangulateReconstruction choice is a SEPARATE algorithmic\n'
              '// decision, intentionally left unchanged here for a controlled A/B.\n'
              'std::shared_ptr<colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions> MakePhase2Options(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  auto popts = std::make_shared<colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions>();\n'
              '  popts->triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks = TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '  popts->min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '  popts->ba_min_num_residuals_for_cpu_multi_threading = 6000;\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_loss_type = 2;              // CAUCHY (was defaulting to 0=TRIVIAL)\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_loss_scale = 1.0;\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_function_tolerance = 1e-6;  // converge-stop\n'
              '  // [BA-FTOL 2026-08-11 研究旋钮,默认不改行为] 全局 BA 相对代价下降阈值。\n'
              '  // 与硬迭代帽的区别:ftol 仍按**收敛判据**停,只是把"已经不影响交付的\n'
              '  // 末尾迭代"划进收敛;硬帽则可能在真收敛前截断(真机 b32 三 solve 均\n'
              '  // term=0 收敛于 49/50/30 迭代 ⇒ 帽<30 必截真活)。无损交付线优先验这把。\n'
              '  if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_GLOBAL_FTOL")) {\n'
              '    const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0.0) popts->ba_global_function_tolerance = v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // [FTOL-AB 2026-08-12 用户签] 逐场交替臂。AB_PERIOD(每 N 帧翻相位)是**匹配\n'
              '  // 路径**的臂,且注释明写只对无状态旋钮有效 —— ftol 是 finalize 一次性旋钮,\n'
              '  // 逐帧翻相位对它无意义。这里按 capture 定臂:同一作品全程同臂,不同作品交替。\n'
              '  // 选臂用 db 路径(含 cap_<μs>)的 FNV-1a 奇偶 —— 无状态、可复现、免落盘计数\n'
              '  // 器;拍摄时刻本身随机,长期约 50/50。臂号进 segments,由 Dart 透传遥测。\n'
              '  // 与上面的固定 FTOL 旋钮互斥:固定旋钮已设值时不参与交替(臂=-1)。\n'
              '  if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_GLOBAL_FTOL_AB")) {\n'
              '    const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0.0 && !std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_GLOBAL_FTOL")) {\n'
              '      uint64_t h = 1469598103934665603ULL;\n'
              '      for (const char c : s->db_path) {\n'
              '        h ^= static_cast<uint8_t>(c);\n'
              '        h *= 1099511628211ULL;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const int arm = static_cast<int>(h & 1ULL);\n'
              '      if (arm == 1) popts->ba_global_function_tolerance = v;\n'
              '      s->stat_phase2_ftol_ab_arm = arm;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // [FINALIZE-TOTAL-ROUNDS 2026-08-10 用户签] 总轮预算 5→3。\n'
              '  // 轮预算自平衡:砍单段(STAGE1_ROUNDS_CAP=1)的轮经余额公式流给 stage2,\n'
              '  // host 矩阵实测净 +0.5s 且点 −0.3% = 判死;砍**总**预算才减真工作量。\n'
              '  // 两场真机 DB host 复放(每臂多次,同臂输出逐次相同):\n'
              '  //   85帧: finalize 8.2→5.5s(−33%) 点 46,835→47,297(+1.0%) reproj +0.011px\n'
              '  //   132帧: 14.8→9.9s(−33%)      点 81,601→82,225(+0.76%) reproj +0.011px\n'
              '  // 机理:后两轮 BA+复筛在"再抛光"——多筛一批点换 0.01px。用户签收\n'
              '  // reproj +1% 的质量带权衡(点数正向)。回滚:env 推 5(ENV-FILE 免重装)。\n'
              '  //\n'
              '  // 🔴 [ROUNDS-REVERT 2026-08-13 用户签] 上面的 5→3 已**撤回**,默认回到上游\n'
              '  // COLMAP 的 5(incremental_pipeline.h:135)。撤回依据不是新的聚合数字——\n'
              '  // 今天的 host 矩阵(b34/s4 各 3 发,同臂逐位相同)与 08-10 的方向一致:\n'
              '  //   3→5 轮:点数 −0.6~0.8%,reproj −0.012~0.015px,finalize +52~69%\n'
              '  // 而是**用户肉眼判据**:5 轮的作品"浮点少了很多"。机理是外层每轮多跑一次\n'
              '  // FilterPoints,被删掉的那 0.6~0.8% 不是随机点,是不干净的点——"点数"与\n'
              '  // "平均 reproj"两个聚合指标把这件事稀释掉了,肉眼没有。\n'
              '  // ⚠️ 教训:局部质量(浮点/鬼层)不能只看聚合指标,肉眼判据优先。\n'
              '  // 时间代价真机实测:80帧/46.6k点 finalize 14.2s(30s 预算内);大场景仍需盯。\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_max_refinements = 5;\n'
              '  if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_TOTAL_ROUNDS")) {\n'
              '    const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0) popts->ba_global_max_refinements = v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_max_num_iterations = 50;\n'
              '  // [BA-ITER-CAP 2026-08-11 研究旋钮,默认不改行为] 全局 BA 单 solve 迭代\n'
              '  // 上限覆盖(两段生效)。b31 真机账:stage1 两 solve 各 51 iters 打满\n'
              '  // (term=NO_CONVERGENCE,上游刻意语义),51 iters ≈ 10.4s/solve = 拍完\n'
              '  // 等待的最大单项。COLMAP FAQ 把"减少 LM 迭代"列为标准提速手段;质量\n'
              '  // 必须过五场 host 矩阵 + 真机质量带(点数≥0.98×,允许正向超出)。\n'
              '  if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_GLOBAL_MAX_ITERS")) {\n'
              '    const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '    if (v > 0) popts->ba_global_max_num_iterations = v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  popts->ba_refine_focal_length = false;\n'
              '  popts->ba_refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '  popts->ba_refine_extra_params = false;\n'
              '  // [AETHER BA-MIXED/THREADS 2026-07-11] Finalize phase-2 (stage 1 + stage 2)\n'
              '  // global BA: the finalize thread budget (default min(6, hw-2),\n'
              '  // OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_THREADS override; quality equal to baseline to 4 decimals on\n'
              '  // every 9-gate metric, host A/B). Mixed precision stays OFF unless\n'
              '  // OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_MIXED=1 (A/B-vetoed default, see BaMixedEnabled). Stage 1\n'
              '  // additionally halves the budget during the enrichment overlap window (see\n'
              '  // RefineGlobalBA).\n'
              '  popts->ba_global_mixed_precision = BaMixedEnabled();\n'
              '  popts->num_threads = FinalizeBaThreads();\n'
              '  // [TRI-ANGLE 2026-07-11] Same 2.0° (T20) creation gate as RunIncremental:\n'
              '  // IterativeGlobalRefinement\'s CompleteAndMergeTracks/retriangulation reads\n'
              '  // Triangulation() too — keep phase 2 from re-admitting the <2° tail that\n'
              '  // phase 1 now refuses to create.\n'
              '  popts->triangulation.min_angle = TriMinAngleDeg("OFFICIAL_AETHER_TRI_MIN_ANGLE_P2");\n'
              '  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07] env 设置时覆盖上面的出生门 + filter 门。\n'
              '  ApplyBirthGateAB(popts.get());\n'
              '  // ⛔ [TRI-TRANSITIVITY 2026-08-05 已撤回] 这里曾设 triangulation.max_transitivity,\n'
              '  // **在 finalize 路径上完全无效**,host A/B 两臂逐位相同(n_points/track3plus/\n'
              '  // n_obs/reproj 全等)。根因:finalize 的 IterativeGlobalRefinement 只跑\n'
              '  // CompleteAndMergeTracks + Retriangulate,而\n'
              '  //   · Retriangulate 遍历 obs_manager_->ImagePairs(),只处理**已匹配过**的欠重建\n'
              '  //     帧对,源码里根本不读 max_transitivity;\n'
              '  //   · 读 max_transitivity 的是 IncrementalTriangulator::TriangulateImage\n'
              '  //     (incremental_triangulator.cc:133/213),那只在**增量注册新图**时跑。\n'
              '  // finalize 阶段所有图早已注册完 ⇒ 该旋钮在此处是死代码。\n'
              '  // 正确位置是拍摄期的 TriangulateImage 调用点(本文件 4659/7644/8615),\n'
              '  // 但那直接撞热预算,需先 host 量代价再定。\n'
              '  // [FINALIZE-OVERLAP 2026-07-11] Load ALL images into the DatabaseCache, not\n'
              '  // just match-connected ones. The live-reuse recon registers frames by ARKit\n'
              '  // pose; a frame whose every db pair fell below min_num_matches exists in the\n'
              '  // recon but — without this — NOT in the cache, and ObservationManager\'s\n'
              '  // bookkeeping throws std::out_of_range (previously only caught by the full\n'
              '  // re-run fallback, which dropped the live registrations). Loading the image\n'
              '  // with zero correspondences keeps it inert but resolvable.\n'
              '  popts->load_all_images = true;\n'
              '  popts->image_path = s->image_path;  // [4.0.4] image_path moved into options\n'
              '  // [PHASE2-CONFIG-ECHO 2026-08-12] 记下**最终生效**的三个值(在所有 env 覆盖\n'
              '  // 与 AB 选臂之后),供 WriteFinalizeSegments 落盘。Dart 遥测从此透传真值,\n'
              '  // 不再自己编。\n'
              '  s->stat_phase2_gftol = popts->ba_global_function_tolerance;\n'
              '  s->stat_phase2_gref = popts->ba_global_max_refinements;\n'
              '  s->stat_phase2_giter = popts->ba_global_max_num_iterations;\n'
              '  return popts;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [AETHER FINALIZE-SEGMENTS 2026-07-11] Persist the finalize worker\'s phase-2\n'
              '// internal segment timings + BA-solver observability as ONE JSON object at\n'
              '// <db_dir>/official_finalize_segments.json. Motivation (cap44): these numbers only ever\n'
              '// reached stderr, which a detached (unplugged) device run loses entirely —\n'
              '// the 105 s single-core finalize tail was unattributable. The Dart layer reads\n'
              '// this file after REFINED and forwards it into telemetry_dart.jsonl.\n'
              '// Telemetry-only: zero algorithm changes; best-effort (failures are logged and\n'
              '// swallowed). Written BEFORE finalize_status flips to REFINED so the Dart\n'
              '// reader never races a partial file (write-to-temp + atomic rename).\n'
              'void WriteFinalizeSegments(aether_sfm_session* s, bool live_reuse,\n'

              '                                  &threads, &dense_backend);\n'

              '        buf, sizeof(buf),\n'
              '        "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"live_reuse\\":%d,"\n'
              '        "\\"cache_pre_ms\\":%lld,\\"enrich_ms\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"stage1_ms\\":%lld,\\"stage1_rounds\\":%d,\\"stage1_state\\":\\"%s\\","\n'
              '        "\\"stage2_ms\\":%lld,\\"stage2_rounds_budget\\":%d,"\n'
              '        "\\"temporal_ms\\":%lld,\\"total_ms\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"rematch_starved_frames\\":%lld,\\"rematch_candidates\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"rematch_attempted\\":%lld,\\"rematch_written\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"rematch_inliers\\":%lld,\\"rematch_failed\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"rematch_budget\\":%d,"\n'
              '        // [P1 2026-07-11] finalize-speedup package attribution: idle repay /\n'
              '        // rc=7 retry / enrichment time-budget truncation.\n'
              '        "\\"repay_calls\\":%lld,\\"repay_attempted\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"repay_written\\":%lld,\\"repay_inliers\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"repay_failed\\":%lld,\\"repay_skipped_thermal\\":%lld,"\n'

              '        "\\"idle_prepay_ticks\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        // [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-07] live probe pre-scoring attribution (all\n'
              '        // zero unless OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_MIN > 0).\n'
              '        // [PROBE-BATCH/PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] batch submissions + the\n'
              '        // delivery-lossless debt ledger (registered == repaid_live +\n'
              '        // repaid_finalize + left on a completed finalize).\n'
              '        "\\"probe_attempted\\":%lld,\\"probe_skipped\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"probe_passed\\":%lld,\\"probe_fail_open\\":%lld,\\"probe_ms\\":%lld,"\n'

              '        "\\"probe_debt_repaid_finalize\\":%lld,\\"probe_debt_left\\":%lld,"\n'

              '        "\\"enrich_targeted_tracks\\":%lld,\\"enrich_targeted_scored\\":%lld,"\n'

              '        "\\"frag_post_lt3\\":%lld,\\"frag_post_p50_deg\\":%.3f,"\n'

              '        "\\"dense_backend\\":\\"%s\\","\n'
              '        // [PHASE2-CONFIG-ECHO 2026-08-12] phase-2 全局 BA 的**真实生效值**\n'
              '        // (env 覆盖与 AB 选臂之后)。ftol_ab_arm:-1=交替关闭,0=base,1=变体。\n'
              '        "\\"gftol_used\\":%.3e,\\"gref_used\\":%d,\\"giter_used\\":%d,"\n'
              '        "\\"ftol_ab_arm\\":%d,"\n'
              '        "\\"mixed\\":%d,\\"threads\\":%d}\\n",\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(epoch_ms), live_reuse ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(cache_pre_ms),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(enrich_ms), static_cast<long long>(stage1_ms),\n'
              '        stage1_rounds, stage1_state, static_cast<long long>(stage2_ms),\n'
              '        stage2_rounds_budget, static_cast<long long>(temporal_ms),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(total_ms),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_starved_frames),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_candidates),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_attempted),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_written),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_finalize_rematch_failed),\n'
              '        kFinalizeRematchMaxPairs,\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_repay_calls),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(s->stat_repay_attempted),\n'

              '        s->stat_phase2_gftol, s->stat_phase2_gref, s->stat_phase2_giter,\n'
              '        s->stat_phase2_ftol_ab_arm, mixed, threads);\n'
              '    if (n <= 0 || n >= static_cast<int>(sizeof(buf))) return;\n'
              '    FILE* f = std::fopen(tmp.string().c_str(), "w");\n'
              '    if (!f) return;\n'
              '    const size_t written = std::fwrite(buf, 1, static_cast<size_t>(n), f);\n'
              '    std::fclose(f);\n'
              '    if (written != static_cast<size_t>(n)) {\n'
              '      std::remove(tmp.string().c_str());\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::error_code ec;\n'
              '    std::filesystem::rename(tmp, dst, ec);  // atomic on the same volume\n'
              '    if (ec) std::remove(tmp.string().c_str());\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    // Telemetry only — never take the finalize down.\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [GHOST-MASK 2026-07-12] Env-gated (OFFICIAL_AETHER_GHOST_MASK=1, default OFF) ghost\n'
              '// layer display-mask sidecar. Runs the aether_ghost_mask.h pass (Phase0\n'
              '// D band @1.5cm + per-cell bimodal detector, parity-gated bit-identical to the\n'
              '// Python reference on the cap46/47 device clouds) over the finalize model and\n'
              '// writes per-point flag bytes to <db_dir>/ghost_mask.bin plus a stats JSON to\n'
              '// <db_dir>/ghost_mask.json. DISPLAY-ONLY metadata for the selection-view\n'
              '// render gate (L2) and the L1 CasDiffMVS arbitration: NO point is deleted or\n'
              '// moved, the delivered PLY/db stay byte-identical (全量交付铁律).\n'
              '//  - flag order == Points3D() iteration order == aether_sfm_get_points /\n'
              '//    get_points_tracked enumeration (the map is not mutated after REFINED, so\n'
              '//    a later getter sees the same order; the Dart PLY writer preserves it).\n'
              '//  - coordinates are float32-cast first (the delivered-PLY quantization) so\n'
              '//    the flags match what PLY-based tooling recomputes bit-for-bit.\n'
              '//  - best-effort: failures are logged and swallowed; never blocks finalize.\n'
              '// [L1-PLAN 2026-07-12] Alongside the mask sidecar, write the CasDiffMVS L1\n'
              '// arbitration inputs into the run_dir: arbitration_plan.json (Swift runner /\n'
              '// Mac harness), arbitration_plan.bin (C++ arbitration twin) and\n'
              '// arbitration_points.bin (per in-region point sd/local_off). "按面积挑" ref\n'
              '// scheduling: budgeted greedy set-cover over the band15-marked cells (env\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_L1_BUDGET_MS / 960ms-per-ref), sources via covis_select, dv from the\n'
              '// ref\'s own observed sparse depths — see aether_l1_plan.h. Best-effort like\n'
              '// the mask itself: failures are logged and swallowed.\n'
              'void WriteL1PlanSidecars(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                         const colmap::Reconstruction& recon,\n'
              '                         const std::vector<double>& cloud_xyz,\n'
              '                         const std::vector<uint8_t>& flags,\n'
              '                         const aether_ghost::GhostMaskStats& st,\n'
              '                         const aether_ghost::GhostMaskIntermediates& inter) {\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  const std::filesystem::path dir =\n'
              '      std::filesystem::path(s->db_path).parent_path();\n'
              '  // frameId -> saved color JPEG (Dart facade sidecar, one JSON line per fed\n'
              '  // frame). Hand-rolled field scan — the file is produced by our own\n'
              '  // _persistFedMeta with plain container paths (no escapes beyond \\/).\n'
              '  std::unordered_map<int, std::string> jpeg_of;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::ifstream jf(dir / "official_sfm_fed_frames.jsonl");\n'
              '    std::string line;\n'
              '    while (std::getline(jf, line)) {\n'
              '      const size_t fpos = line.find("\\"frameId\\":");\n'
              '      const size_t jpos = line.find("\\"jpegPath\\":\\"");\n'
              '      if (fpos == std::string::npos || jpos == std::string::npos) continue;\n'
              '      const int fid = std::atoi(line.c_str() + fpos + 10);\n'
              '      std::string path;\n'
              '      for (size_t i = jpos + 12; i < line.size() && line[i] != \'"\'; ++i) {\n'
              '        if (line[i] == \'\\\\\' && i + 1 < line.size()) ++i;  // \\/ or \\" escapes\n'
              '        path.push_back(line[i]);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (fid >= 0 && !path.empty()) jpeg_of[fid] = std::move(path);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (jpeg_of.empty()) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_plan: official_sfm_fed_frames.jsonl missing/empty "\n'
              '                    "— no JPEG mapping, plan skipped";\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // registered frames -> L1Frame (pose, model-res K, jpeg), ordered by\n'
              '  // frame_id for determinism.\n'
              '  std::vector<aether_l1::L1Frame> frames;\n'
              '  std::unordered_map<colmap::image_t, int> frame_of_image;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::vector<const colmap::Image*> imgs;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [iid, image] : recon.Images()) {\n'
              '      if (image.HasPose()) imgs.push_back(&image);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::sort(imgs.begin(), imgs.end(),\n'
              '              [](const colmap::Image* a, const colmap::Image* b) {\n'
              '                return a->ImageId() < b->ImageId();\n'
              '              });\n'
              '    for (const colmap::Image* image : imgs) {\n'
              '      int fid = -1;\n'
              '      if (std::sscanf(image->Name().c_str(), "frame_%d.jpg", &fid) != 1) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      aether_l1::L1Frame fr;\n'
              '      fr.frame_id = fid;\n'
              '      const auto jit = jpeg_of.find(fid);\n'
              '      if (jit != jpeg_of.end()) fr.jpeg = jit->second;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Matrix3x4d m = image->CamFromWorld().ToMatrix();\n'
              '      for (int r = 0; r < 3; ++r)\n'
              '        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) fr.w2c[r * 4 + c] = m(r, c);\n'
              '      fr.w2c[15] = 1.0;\n'
              '      const colmap::Camera& cam = *image->CameraPtr();\n'
              '      const double sx = static_cast<double>(aether_l1::kProcW) /\n'
              '                        static_cast<double>(cam.width);\n'
              '      const double sy = static_cast<double>(aether_l1::kProcH) /\n'
              '                        static_cast<double>(cam.height);\n'
              '      fr.k[0] = cam.FocalLengthX() * sx;\n'
              '      fr.k[1] = cam.FocalLengthY() * sy;\n'
              '      fr.k[2] = cam.PrincipalPointX() * sx;\n'
              '      fr.k[3] = cam.PrincipalPointY() * sy;\n'
              '      frame_of_image[image->ImageId()] = static_cast<int>(frames.size());\n'
              '      frames.push_back(std::move(fr));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // per-frame observed point rows + the full-precision track points, in the\n'
              '  // SAME Points3D iteration order the flags were computed from.\n'
              '  std::vector<double> pts;\n'
              '  pts.reserve(cloud_xyz.size());\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    int32_t row = 0;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : recon.Points3D()) {\n'
              '      pts.push_back(pt.xyz.x());\n'
              '      pts.push_back(pt.xyz.y());\n'
              '      pts.push_back(pt.xyz.z());\n'
              '      for (const auto& el : pt.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        const auto it = frame_of_image.find(el.image_id);\n'
              '        if (it != frame_of_image.end())\n'
              '          frames[static_cast<size_t>(it->second)].obs.push_back(row);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++row;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  int budget_ms = aether_l1::kDefaultBudgetMs;\n'
              '  if (const char* b = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_L1_BUDGET_MS")) {\n'
              '    const int v = std::atoi(b);\n'
              '    if (v > 0) budget_ms = v;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  aether_l1::L1Plan plan;\n'
              '  if (!aether_l1::BuildL1Plan(cloud_xyz.data(), flags.data(),\n'
              '                              cloud_xyz.size() / 3, st.plane_n, st.plane_d,\n'
              '                              pts.data(), pts.size() / 3, frames, budget_ms,\n'
              '                              &plan)) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_plan: BuildL1Plan failed";\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (plan.refs.empty()) {\n'
              '    // No marked cells / no eligible refs: leave no stale plan behind.\n'
              '    std::error_code ec;\n'
              '    std::filesystem::remove(dir / "arbitration_plan.json", ec);\n'
              '    std::filesystem::remove(dir / "arbitration_plan.bin", ec);\n'
              '    std::filesystem::remove(dir / "arbitration_points.bin", ec);\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_plan: no refs (marked_cells="\n'
              '                 << plan.n_marked_cells << ") — plan not written";\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const bool ok =\n'
              '      aether_l1::WriteL1PlanJson((dir / "arbitration_plan.json").string(),\n'
              '                                 plan, frames) &&\n'
              '      aether_l1::WriteL1PlanBin((dir / "arbitration_plan.bin").string(), plan,\n'
              '                                frames) &&\n'
              '      aether_l1::WriteL1PointsSidecar(\n'
              '          (dir / "arbitration_points.bin").string(), cloud_xyz.data(),\n'
              '          flags.data(), cloud_xyz.size() / 3, inter.sd, inter.local_off);\n'
              '  LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_plan: refs=" << plan.refs.size() << "/"\n'
              '               << plan.budget_refs << " marked_cells=" << plan.n_marked_cells\n'
              '               << " cov2=" << plan.n_cells_cov2\n'
              '               << " cov1=" << plan.n_cells_cov1\n'
              '               << " cov0=" << plan.n_cells_cov0\n'
              '               << " dropped_srcs=" << plan.n_refs_dropped_srcs\n'
              '               << " missing_jpeg=" << plan.n_frames_missing_jpeg\n'
              '               << " write=" << (ok ? "ok" : "FAILED") << " ("\n'
              '               << static_cast<int>(NowMs() - t0) << "ms)";\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void MaybeWriteGhostMask(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                         const colmap::Reconstruction& recon) {\n'
              '  const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_GHOST_MASK");\n'
              '  if (!e || e[0] != \'1\') return;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    const auto& pts = recon.Points3D();\n'
              '    std::vector<double> xyz;\n'
              '    xyz.reserve(pts.size() * 3);\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : pts) {\n'
              '      xyz.push_back(static_cast<double>(static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.x())));\n'
              '      xyz.push_back(static_cast<double>(static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.y())));\n'
              '      xyz.push_back(static_cast<double>(static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.z())));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::vector<uint8_t> flags;\n'
              '    aether_ghost::GhostMaskStats st;\n'
              '    aether_ghost::GhostMaskIntermediates inter;\n'
              '    if (!aether_ghost::ComputeGhostMask(xyz.data(), xyz.size() / 3, &flags,\n'
              '                                        &st, &inter)) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] ghost_mask: degenerate cloud ("\n'
              '                   << xyz.size() / 3 << " pts) — no sidecar";\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const std::filesystem::path dir =\n'
              '        std::filesystem::path(s->db_path).parent_path();\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      const std::filesystem::path tmp = dir / "ghost_mask.bin.tmp";\n'
              '      FILE* f = std::fopen(tmp.string().c_str(), "wb");\n'
              '      if (!f) return;\n'
              '      const size_t w = std::fwrite(flags.data(), 1, flags.size(), f);\n'
              '      std::fclose(f);\n'
              '      if (w != flags.size()) {\n'
              '        std::remove(tmp.string().c_str());\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      std::error_code ec;\n'
              '      std::filesystem::rename(tmp, dir / "ghost_mask.bin", ec);\n'
              '      if (ec) {\n'
              '        std::remove(tmp.string().c_str());\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    char buf[768];\n'
              '    const int n = std::snprintf(\n'
              '        buf, sizeof(buf),\n'
              '        "{\\"version\\":1,\\"n_points\\":%lld,\\"n_region\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"n_band15\\":%lld,\\"n_band10\\":%lld,\\"n_cell_ghost\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"n_clean\\":%lld,\\"n_bim_cells\\":%lld,\\"n_floor_cells\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"plane_n\\":[%.17g,%.17g,%.17g],\\"plane_d\\":%.17g,"\n'
              '        "\\"pass_ms\\":%lld,"\n'
              '        "\\"bits\\":\\"0:in_region,1:band15,2:cell_ghost,3:in_clean,"\n'
              '        "4:band10\\",\\"order\\":\\"points3d_iteration==get_points\\"}\\n",\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_points),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_region),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_band15),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_band10),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_cell_ghost),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_clean),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_bim_cells),\n'
              '        static_cast<long long>(st.n_floor_cells), st.plane_n[0], st.plane_n[1],\n'
              '        st.plane_n[2], st.plane_d, static_cast<long long>(NowMs() - t0));\n'
              '    if (n > 0 && n < static_cast<int>(sizeof(buf))) {\n'
              '      const std::filesystem::path tmp = dir / "ghost_mask.json.tmp";\n'
              '      FILE* f = std::fopen(tmp.string().c_str(), "w");\n'
              '      if (f) {\n'
              '        const size_t w = std::fwrite(buf, 1, static_cast<size_t>(n), f);\n'
              '        std::fclose(f);\n'
              '        std::error_code ec;\n'
              '        if (w == static_cast<size_t>(n)) {\n'
              '          std::filesystem::rename(tmp, dir / "ghost_mask.json", ec);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (w != static_cast<size_t>(n) || ec)\n'
              '          std::remove(tmp.string().c_str());\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] ghost_mask: n=" << st.n_points\n'
              '                 << " region=" << st.n_region << " band15=" << st.n_band15\n'
              '                 << " cell_ghost=" << st.n_cell_ghost\n'
              '                 << " bim_cells=" << st.n_bim_cells << "/"\n'
              '                 << st.n_floor_cells << " (" << static_cast<int>(NowMs() - t0)\n'
              '                 << "ms)";\n'
              '    // [L1-PLAN 2026-07-12] CasDiffMVS arbitration inputs (same env gate; the\n'
              '    // enclosing try swallows any failure so finalize is never blocked).\n'
              '    WriteL1PlanSidecars(s, recon, xyz, flags, st, inter);\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] ghost_mask failed (swallowed): "\n'
              '                 << ex.what();\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] ghost_mask failed (swallowed)";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Kill switch. Default ON, but the whole pass is\n'
              '// inert unless the probe gate armed and actually skipped something, so an\n'
              '// unset environment is byte-for-byte the shipped behaviour either way. =0\n'
              '// restores the 08-08 phase-1 shape (db-lossless, delivery -0.163%).\n'
              'bool ProbeDebtGrowEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Pose source for the replay. The live pass\n'
              '// triangulates with the frame\'s ARKit pose; by the time we replay, the model\n'
              '// has been through capture-time local BA and the stage-1 global rounds, so\n'
              '// its own poses are the ones every gate downstream (stage-2 FilterPoints,\n'
              '// the delivery parallax filter) will judge these points against. Default =\n'
              '// model poses; =1 replays with the raw ARKit pose instead (A/B arm).\n'
              'bool ProbeDebtGrowArkitPose() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW_ARKIT_POSE");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Replay the live MERGE branch too. DEFAULT OFF\n'
              '// on evidence, not on taste: cap201 with merges on net-deleted 1426 points\n'
              '// (delivery -1.54% vs A) because at replay time nearly every inlier already\n'
              '// has both endpoints in a track, so the pass degenerates into a second\n'
              '// track-merge over a mature model — a job stage 2\'s CompleteAndMergeTracks\n'
              '// already owns. =1 restores it as an A/B arm.\n'
              '// [LIVE-GROW-NOW 2026-08-08 SHIPPED ON — user-signed "空闲补账 + 当场长云作为\n'
              '// 基线"] Arm the idle repay to grow the live model in place instead of parking\n'
              '// the pair for the finalize replay.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Why this is the right default even though the host A/B looks bad: the host\n'
              '// replay bench feeds frames BACK-TO-BACK, so it has no idle at all. To exercise\n'
              '// this leg there I had to force it inline every frame\n'
              '// (AETHER_REPLAY_LIVE_REPAY + IDLE_PREPAY_IDLE_MS=0), which is the worst case —\n'
              '// the repay\'s whole cost lands on stream (49.7s -> 60.8s). In production the leg\n'
              '// only runs after IdlePrepayIdleMs() (2000 ms) of frame-idle and never at\n'
              '// thermal serious+, i.e. it spends time the capture was ALREADY wasting while\n'
              '// the user walks to the next viewpoint.\n'
              '//   Effect when it does run (cap201, forced): live cloud 139,7xx -> 141,637,\n'
              '//   i.e. ABOVE the gate-off ceiling of 141,048 (+0.42%), reproducible to the\n'
              '//   point across runs; delivered 116,856 -> 118,396 (+1.3%).\n'
              '//   Effect when there is no idle: the leg never fires and this flag is a no-op.\n'
              '// So arming it can only ever move the live cloud toward (or past) the gate-off\n'
              '// ceiling, never below it.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Declared cost: the delivered cloud becomes a function of how idle the capture\n'
              '// was. Replay determinism is knowingly traded for the live preview, which is\n'
              '// what the user actually watches.\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW_LIVE=0 restores the park-everything behaviour.\n'
              'bool ProbeDebtGrowLiveEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW_LIVE");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool ProbeDebtGrowObs() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW_OBS");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool ProbeDebtGrowMerge() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_DEBT_GROW_MERGE");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Replay every repaid probe-debt pair through\n'
              '// the live create/grow/merge gates, closing the last delivery gap the gate\n'
              '// opened.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHY THIS EXISTS: phase 1 made the probe gate lossless at the DB layer — the\n'
              '// ledger guarantees every skipped pair gets the same single full-match\n'
              '// attempt a gate-off run gave it, and a from-scratch rebuild off either DB\n'
              '// lands on the identical cloud. The DELIVERED cloud is not a from-scratch\n'
              '// rebuild: it inherits the live model. A skipped pair therefore still missed\n'
              '// its capture-time authoring window, and the official finalize\n'
              '// retriangulation does not fully cover for it (IncrementalTriangulator::\n'
              '// Retriangulate skips any image pair whose triangulated/total correspondence\n'
              '// ratio already exceeds re_min_ratio — precisely the case for a late pair\n'
              '// whose keypoints neighbouring pairs have already put in tracks).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHERE IT RUNS: after enrich.join(), where the finalize worker is the sole\n'
              '// owner of the model — the producing legs (capture worker / enrichment\n'
              '// thread) must never touch live_recon. Points authored here still face the\n'
              '// full stage-2 gauntlet (CompleteAndMergeTracks, Retriangulate, Cauchy BA,\n'
              '// FilterPoints/FilterFrames), exactly like a point born during capture.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// WHAT IT REPLAYS — CREATION ONLY, by measurement (cap201, A=probe off):\n'
              '//   replay off (phase-1 shape)     116664   -0.151%\n'
              '//   + creation + grow + merge      115042   -1.539%   (net -1426 points!)\n'
              '//   + creation + grow              116611   -0.196%\n'
              '//   + creation only                116858   +0.015%   ← default\n'
              '// The other two branches are capture-time devices that a finalize-time model\n'
              '// does not need and cannot absorb: merge substitutes for the finish-time\n'
              '// track merge that stage 2 actually performs, and the 14 px grow gate is\n'
              '// sized for ARKit drift that the converged model no longer has. Only\n'
              '// creation restores something that is genuinely missing — the 3D point a\n'
              '// skipped pair never got to author. See the two branch comments in\n'
              '// GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers for the per-branch evidence.\n'
              'void ApplyProbeDebtGrowth(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                          colmap::Reconstruction* recon) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (s->probe_debt_grow.empty()) return;\n'
              '  if (!recon || !ProbeDebtGrowEnabled() ||\n'
              '      !SelfDevLiveTriangulationEnabled()) {\n'
              '    s->probe_debt_grow.clear();\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // Same gate constants as the live authoring path, read through the same\n'
              '    // accessors — there is exactly one definition of "how a live point is\n'
              '    // allowed to be born".\n'
              '    const double min_tri_angle_rad = LiveCreateTriMinAngleRad();\n'
              '    const double max_create_reproj_px = LiveCreateMaxReprojPx();\n'
              '    const double max_grow_reproj_px = LiveGrowMaxReprojPx();\n'
              '    constexpr double kMaxMergeReprojPx = 8.0;\n'
              '    constexpr bool kMergeRequireDisjointImages = false;\n'
              '    const bool arkit_pose = ProbeDebtGrowArkitPose();\n'
              '    const bool allow_merge = ProbeDebtGrowMerge();\n'
              '    const bool allow_grow = ProbeDebtGrowObs();\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Deterministic replay order (later frame ascending, then earlier), the\n'
              '    // same ordering the finalize re-match uses for cache locality. The legs\n'
              '    // append in attempt order, which is already deterministic, but the two\n'
              '    // legs can interleave across runs on device.\n'
              '    std::sort(s->probe_debt_grow.begin(), s->probe_debt_grow.end(),\n'
              '              [](const ProbeDebtGrowPair& a, const ProbeDebtGrowPair& b) {\n'
              '                if (a.f != b.f) return a.f < b.f;\n'
              '                return a.j < b.j;\n'
              '              });\n'
              '\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<PointIdPair, PointIdPairHash> merge_trials;\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> touched;\n'
              '    const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    const int64_t points_before = static_cast<int64_t>(recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '    const int64_t grown_before = s->stat_grow_accepted;\n'
              '    for (const ProbeDebtGrowPair& e : s->probe_debt_grow) {\n'
              '      if (e.j < 0 || e.f < 0 || e.j >= num_frames || e.f >= num_frames) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& f1 = s->frames[e.j];\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& f2 = s->frames[e.f];\n'
              '      // A withdrawn frame carries image_id == 0 and is not in the model.\n'
              '      if (f1.image_id == 0 || f2.image_id == 0) continue;\n'
              '      if (!f1.has_pose || !f2.has_pose) continue;\n'
              '      if (f1.points.empty() || f2.points.empty()) continue;\n'
              '      if (!recon->ExistsImage(f1.image_id) ||\n'
              '          !recon->ExistsImage(f2.image_id)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& im1 = recon->Image(f1.image_id);\n'
              '      const colmap::Image& im2 = recon->Image(f2.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!im1.HasPose() || !im2.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      // The inlier indices address Point2D slots; a mismatch means this\n'
              '      // FrameRecord is not the one the model registered — skip, never guess.\n'
              '      if (im1.NumPoints2D() != f1.points.size() ||\n'
              '          im2.NumPoints2D() != f2.points.size()) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const colmap::Rigid3d pose1 =\n'
              '          arkit_pose ? f1.cam_from_world : im1.CamFromWorld();\n'
              '      const colmap::Rigid3d pose2 =\n'
              '          arkit_pose ? f2.cam_from_world : im2.CamFromWorld();\n'
              '      const LiveGrowView v1{f1.image_id, &f1.points, &f1.camera, pose1};\n'
              '      const LiveGrowView v2{f2.image_id, &f2.points, &f2.camera, pose2};\n'
              '      GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers(\n'
              '          s, recon, v1, v2, e.inliers, min_tri_angle_rad,\n'
              '          max_create_reproj_px, max_grow_reproj_px, kMaxMergeReprojPx,\n'
              '          kMergeRequireDisjointImages, allow_merge, allow_grow, &merge_trials,\n'
              '          &touched);\n'
              '      ++s->stat_probe_debt_grow_pairs;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->stat_probe_debt_grow_added =\n'
              '        static_cast<int64_t>(recon->NumPoints3D()) - points_before;\n'
              '    s->stat_probe_debt_grow_grown = s->stat_grow_accepted - grown_before;\n'
              '    s->stat_probe_debt_grow_touched = static_cast<int64_t>(touched.size());\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n'
              '    // Enhancement pass only — a failure here must never take finalize down.\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] probe-debt growth aborted: " << ex.what();\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] probe-debt growth aborted";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  s->stat_probe_debt_grow_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '  s->probe_debt_grow.clear();\n'
              '  LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] probe-debt growth: pairs="\n'
              '               << s->stat_probe_debt_grow_pairs\n'
              '               << " points_added=" << s->stat_probe_debt_grow_added\n'
              '               << " obs_grown=" << s->stat_probe_debt_grow_grown\n'
              '               << " touched=" << s->stat_probe_debt_grow_touched\n'
              '               << " ms=" << s->stat_probe_debt_grow_ms;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Async-finalize worker.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// live_reuse=true (normal completion, [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY + FINALIZE-OVERLAP\n'
              '// 2026-07-11]): `model` IS the moved-out live recon (sole owner — user signed\n'
              '// off that the LOCAL model is never displayed, so nothing is published until\n'
              '// REFINED and the refinement runs IN PLACE, zero deep copies; the old chain\n'
              '// held live_recon + a LOCAL copy + a worker copy = 3 models at peak). The\n'
              '// finish-time db enrichment (AddSpatialRevisitMatches +\n'
              '// FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames — GPU matcher, writes the db) runs on a helper\n'
              '// thread IN PARALLEL with a stage-1 refinement over a PRE-enrichment\n'
              '// DatabaseCache snapshot (CPU: the same IterativeGlobalRefinement rounds the\n'
              '// old chain ran serially AFTER the enrichment — CompleteAndMergeTracks /\n'
              '// Retriangulate / Cauchy global BA over the capture-time pairs). Once both\n'
              '// finish, stage 2 = the UNCHANGED RefineReconstruction over the ENRICHED db:\n'
              '// its leading CompleteAndMergeTracks + Retriangulate consume the new spatial /\n'
              '// re-match pairs, its BA rounds converge-stop early because stage 1 already\n'
              '// converged the capture-graph part. Trade-off vs the serial baseline: the\n'
              '// enrichment pairs join the refinement only in stage 2 (late) instead of from\n'
              '// round 1 — accepted after the host 42/43 A/B held the quality gates (points\n'
              '// ±2%, reproj ±0.02); OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_NO_OVERLAP=1 is the same-binary revert\n'
              '// (enrichment then a single full refinement, i.e. the exact serial order).\n'
              '// db concurrency: the stage-1 cache snapshot is taken from s->db BEFORE the\n'
              '// enrichment thread starts; during the overlap the enrichment thread is the\n'
              '// db\'s only user; stage 2 reopens the db after the join.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// live_reuse=false (resume / degenerate path): unchanged semantics — `model`\n'
              '// is the published LOCAL_READY model (db-driven RunIncremental output, still\n'
              '// readable through the getters), so refine a deep copy and swap on success.\n'
              '// Enrichment already ran synchronously in aether_sfm_finalize_async there.\n'
              'void RefineGlobalBA(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                    std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> model,\n'
              '                    bool live_reuse) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '    // [S3.5 RESTORE 2026-07-11] The refined model IS the user-visible result\n'
              '    // (no two-phase preview) and the user is waiting on the foreground waiting\n'
              '    // page — run the global BA at user-initiated QoS so a default/background\n'
              '    // QoS std::thread doesn\'t get E-core-throttled mid-wait.\n'
              '    pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] finalize worker 入口打点\n'
              '    // [AETHER FINALIZE-SEGMENTS 2026-07-11] Drop any segments file from a\n'
              '    // previous finalize of this run_dir (resume retries) so the Dart reader\n'
              '    // can never pick up a stale record if this attempt ends in the fallback.\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      std::filesystem::remove(std::filesystem::path(s->db_path).parent_path() /\n'
              '                              "official_finalize_segments.json");\n'
              '    } catch (...) {\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    auto popts = MakePhase2Options(s);\n'
              '    // [INCREMENTAL-GLOBAL-BA 2026-07-13] If the rolling capture-time global BA\n'
              '    // ran this session, live_recon\'s poses are already near the global optimum, so\n'
              '    // the finalize global solve COLLAPSES: cap the round budget (default 2 — the\n'
              '    // "1-2 轮收尾"). Both stage 1 and stage 2 still run CompleteAndMergeTracks +\n'
              '    // Retriangulate every round, so the ≥1-track-completion requirement holds and\n'
              '    // the finalize free-gauge solve still closes revisit pairs that only appear in\n'
              '    // the enrichment (which the capture-time sliding window could not — see\n'
              '    // MaybeIncrementalGlobalRefine\'s quality note). The converge-stop usually\n'
              '    // breaks after round 1 anyway; this cap is the belt-and-suspenders lever the\n'
              '    // cap51 A/B sweeps (OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_FINALIZE_ROUNDS). Default OFF is\n'
              '    // preserved: incremental_global_ba_ran is only set when the env-gated trigger\n'
              '    // actually fired, so shipped captures are byte-for-byte unchanged.\n'
              '    if (s->incremental_global_ba_ran) {\n'
              '      const int cap = std::max(1, IncrementalFinalizeRounds());\n'
              '      const int before = popts->ba_global_max_refinements;\n'
              '      popts->ba_global_max_refinements = std::min(before, cap);\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize collapse: incremental global BA ran ("\n'
              '                   << s->stat_incremental_refines << " refines) → global-BA rounds "\n'
              '                   << before << " → " << popts->ba_global_max_refinements;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> refined;\n'
              '    double enrich_ms = 0.0, cache_pre_ms = 0.0, stage1_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    int stage1_rounds = 0;\n'
              '    const char* stage1_state = "off";\n'
              '    // [PROVENANCE 2026-07-26] Diagnostic only — no geometry is touched.\n'
              '    // The delivered cloud inherits the live model (Reconstruction::Load never\n'
              '    // touches points3D_), so every point the hand-written live create/grow/\n'
              '    // merge admitted under its 10 px / 14 px / 8 px / 2.0 deg gates survives\n'
              '    // into the PLY unless official filtering drops it. Point3D ids are stable\n'
              '    // and monotonically assigned, so the id set at this instant IS the\n'
              '    // self-developed origin set; anything the official Retriangulate /\n'
              '    // CompleteAndMergeTracks adds afterwards carries a fresh id. Counting the\n'
              '    // two groups at publish time answers the question that decides whether\n'
              '    // swapping the live triangulator for colmap\'s is worth anything at all.\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> live_origin_ids;\n'
              '    if (live_reuse && model) {\n'
              '      live_origin_ids.reserve(model->NumPoints3D() * 2);\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, _] : model->Points3D()) live_origin_ids.insert(pid);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (live_reuse) {\n'
              '      refined = std::move(model);  // in place — nothing else holds this model\n'
              '      // [KNIFE-A ③ 2026-07-11] Snapshot the enrichment-targeting hint from\n'
              '      // the (still-untouched) live model BEFORE the enrichment thread spawns\n'
              '      // and before stage 1 starts mutating `refined` — the enrichment thread\n'
              '      // must never read the model itself. Env-gated; nullptr = legacy\n'
              '      // distance ordering in the top-up.\n'
              '      if (EnrichTargetedEnabled() && s->frames.size() >= 3) {\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          s->enrich_hint = BuildEnrichTargetHint(*s, *refined);\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '          s->enrich_hint.reset();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Kill switch: OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_NO_OVERLAP=1 restores the serial order\n'
              '      // (enrichment first, then one full refinement over the enriched db).\n'
              '      static const bool no_overlap = [] {\n'
              '        const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_FINALIZE_NO_OVERLAP");\n'
              '        return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '      }();\n'
              '      // Stage-1 graph snapshot BEFORE the enrichment thread writes the db.\n'
              '      std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> cache_pre;\n'
              '      if (!no_overlap && s->db && s->frames.size() >= 2) {\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          const double t_cache = NowMs();\n'
              '          colmap::DatabaseCache::Options copts;\n'
              '          copts.min_num_matches =\n'
              '              static_cast<size_t>(popts->min_num_matches);\n'
              '          copts.load_all_images = true;  // see MakePhase2Options\n'
              '          cache_pre = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, copts);\n'
              '          cache_pre_ms = NowMs() - t_cache;\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          cache_pre.reset();\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize stage-1 cache snapshot failed"\n'
              '                          " ("\n'
              '                       << e.what() << ") — no overlap, serial refinement";\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Enrichment thread: GPU matcher writes the db; the passes are already\n'
              '      // fail-soft internally, but an escaped exception here would terminate\n'
              '      // the process (thread boundary) — catch everything. enrich_done is the\n'
              '      // stage-1 window flag: stage-1 refinement rounds only run while the GPU\n'
              '      // enrichment is still working (its wall time is "free"), so the round\n'
              '      // budget adapts to the capture — a healthy capture whose enrichment is\n'
              '      // seconds runs ~0 stage-1 rounds (stage 2 then IS the serial baseline),\n'
              '      // a heavy revisit capture fills the whole window with useful rounds.\n'
              '      std::atomic<bool> enrich_done{false};\n'
              '      // [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET] Arm the time gate for this run: AUTO arms only\n'
              '      // when a stage-1 window exists (cache_pre); FIXED always. Armed runs\n'
              '      // pay the most valuable debt first (starved-frame re-match before the\n'
              '      // spatial pass); unarmed runs keep the legacy order bit-identically.\n'
              '      const bool budget_armed = EnrichBudgetArmed(cache_pre != nullptr);\n'
              '      s->enrich_stage1_done.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '      s->enrich_start_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '      const double t_enrich0 = NowMs();\n'
              '      std::thread enrich([s, &enrich_ms, &enrich_done, t_enrich0,\n'
              '                          budget_armed] {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        s->enrich_start_ms = NowMs();\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          // [ENRICH-ORDER 2026-07-26, signed] Armed (budgeted) runs pay the\n'
              '          // MOST VALUABLE debt first. cap_1785066707194992 (156 frames, 115\n'
              '          // thermal-serious) proved the old "quadratic first, unbudgeted"\n'
              '          // order inverts that: the quadratic pass spent the whole 30s kAuto\n'
              '          // budget on long-range pairs (gap-128 pairs averaged 0.7 inliers)\n'
              '          // and FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames — whose near-field pairs average\n'
              '          // ~190-270 inliers and are the other half of the K6 throttle\'s\n'
              '          // "delivery-lossless" promise — was budget-stopped at zero, leaving\n'
              '          // ~100 throttled frames without gaps 7-12 (551 pts/frame delivered).\n'
              '          // Armed order is therefore rematch → quadratic (now budget-gated,\n'
              '          // gap-ascending) → spatial. Unarmed runs keep the legacy order\n'
              '          // bit-identically.\n'
              '          // [GPU-HANG-B1] enrichment(GPU matcher)各 pass 边界打点。\n'
              '          // [ENRICH-SPLIT 2026-08-11] Per-pass wall clock — the 22.4 s device\n'
              '          // enrich window is the finalize critical-path pole once stage-1 BA\n'
              '          // shrinks, and the three passes were previously indistinguishable.\n'
              '          double rematch_ms = 0, quad_ms = 0, spatial_ms = 0;\n'
              '          if (budget_armed) {\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_r = NowMs();\n'
              '            FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames(s);\n'
              '            rematch_ms = NowMs() - t_r;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_q = NowMs();\n'
              '            AddOfficialQuadraticPairs(s);\n'
              '            quad_ms = NowMs() - t_q;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_sp = NowMs();\n'
              '            AddSpatialRevisitMatches(s);\n'
              '            spatial_ms = NowMs() - t_sp;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '          } else {\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_q = NowMs();\n'
              '            AddOfficialQuadraticPairs(s);\n'
              '            quad_ms = NowMs() - t_q;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_sp = NowMs();\n'
              '            AddSpatialRevisitMatches(s);\n'
              '            spatial_ms = NowMs() - t_sp;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '            const double t_r = NowMs();\n'
              '            FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames(s);\n'
              '            rematch_ms = NowMs() - t_r;\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            char eline[256];\n'
              '            std::snprintf(eline, sizeof(eline),\n'
              '                          "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"enrich_split\\","\n'
              '                          "\\"armed\\":%d,\\"rematch_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '                          "\\"quadratic_ms\\":%.0f,\\"spatial_ms\\":%.0f}",\n'
              '                          static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '                          budget_armed ? 1 : 0, rematch_ms, quad_ms,\n'
              '                          spatial_ms);\n'
              '            AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, eline);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize db enrichment aborted: "\n'
              '                       << e.what();\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize db enrichment aborted";\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          if (s->db) s->db->Close();  // flush before stage 2 reopens it\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        enrich_ms = NowMs() - t_enrich0;\n'
              '        enrich_done.store(true);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // Stage-1 refinement (CPU) over the capture-time graph, in parallel\n'
              '      // with the enrichment: the SAME per-round sequence as colmap\'s\n'
              '      // IterativeGlobalRefinement (CompleteAndMergeTracks + Retriangulate\n'
              '      // once, then rounds of AdjustGlobalBundle + CompleteAndMergeTracks +\n'
              '      // FilterPoints with the identical converge-stop), except the loop also\n'
              '      // stops at the first round boundary after the enrichment finishes —\n'
              '      // stage-1 never spends meaningfully past the free window. Failure is\n'
              '      // non-fatal: stage 2 then simply runs the full refinement alone (== the\n'
              '      // serial baseline).\n'
              '      if (cache_pre) {\n'
              '        const double t_s1 = NowMs();\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        // During the overlap the ENRICHMENT is the critical path (its\n'
              '        // completion gates stage 2) while stage 1 is opportunistic filler —\n'
              '        // measured on cap43: with both at USER_INITIATED the stage-1 BA\n'
              '        // starved the enrichment\'s CPU side (TVG RANSAC) 42.9 s → 54.6 s.\n'
              '        // [BA-STAGE1-FULL 2026-07-26, signed] That premise inverted (see\n'
              '        // Stage1UtilityQosLegacy) — stage-1 now stays USER_INITIATED unless\n'
              '        // the legacy env restores the cap43 posture.\n'
              '        if (Stage1UtilityQosLegacy()) {\n'
              '          pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_UTILITY, 0);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        aether_ba_ring_reset();  // [AETHER BA-RING] attribute stage-1 solves\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache_pre);\n'
              '          mapper.BeginReconstruction(refined);\n'
              '          const auto mapper_opts = popts->Mapper();\n'
              '          auto ba_opts = popts->GlobalBundleAdjustment();\n'
              '          const auto tri_opts = popts->Triangulation();\n'
              '          // Halve the ceres thread pool for stage-1 solves only: the QoS drop\n'
              '          // alone did not stop the BA from starving the enrichment (ceres\n'
              '          // spawns its own default-QoS workers and the Schur solves saturate\n'
              '          // memory bandwidth — cap43 enrichment 42.9 s serial → 54.9 s under\n'
              '          // an all-cores stage 1). Stage 1 is window-bound filler, so slower\n'
              '          // rounds cost nothing; stage 2 keeps the full thread pool.\n'
              '          // [AETHER BA-THREADS 2026-07-11] The base is now the finalize\n'
              '          // budget (min(6, hw-2) / OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_THREADS) instead of raw\n'
              '          // hardware_concurrency: overlap window = budget/2, exclusive\n'
              '          // stage 2 = full budget.\n'
              '          if (ba_opts.ceres) {\n'
              '            const int full =\n'
              '                ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.num_threads > 0\n'
              '                    ? ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.num_threads\n'
              '                    : static_cast<int>(std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n'
              '            // [BA-STAGE1-FULL 2026-07-26, signed] Full threads by default —\n'
              '            // thread count is signed bit-equal (Ceres 两刀定案), and cap5\n'
              '            // measured the halved rounds at 22.4s vs 10.8s full speed while\n'
              '            // the enrichment they were protecting had 11s of slack. Legacy\n'
              '            // halving one env away for the heavy-revisit shape.\n'
              '            ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.num_threads =\n'
              '                Stage1HalfThreadsLegacy() ? std::max(1, full / 2) : full;\n'
              '            // [P1-STAGE1-RECIPE] optional stage-1-only ftol override (stage 2\n'
              '            // keeps the shipped ba_global_function_tolerance untouched).\n'
              '            if (Stage1FtolOverride() > 0.0) {\n'
              '              ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.function_tolerance =\n'
              '                  Stage1FtolOverride();\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            // [BA-ITER-CAP 2026-08-11] stage-1-only iteration cap (stage 2\n'
              '            // keeps ba_global_max_num_iterations): stage 1 is window-bound\n'
              '            // filler whose solves burn the full 50-iteration budget twice on\n'
              '            // device (b31: 2×51 iters, 21.5 s); the enriched graph gets its\n'
              '            // converged polish in stage 2 either way.\n'
              '            if (const char* e =\n'
              '                    std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_BA_S1_MAX_ITERS")) {\n'
              '              const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '              if (v > 0) ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.max_num_iterations = v;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          // Window checks between the sub-steps too: a healthy capture whose\n'
              '          // enrichment finishes in seconds must not pay for a full merge +\n'
              '          // retriangulate pass it gains nothing from (stage 2 redoes both on\n'
              '          // the enriched graph).\n'
              '          if (!enrich_done.load()) mapper.CompleteAndMergeTracks(tri_opts);\n'
              '          if (!enrich_done.load()) mapper.Retriangulate(tri_opts);\n'
              '          // [P1-STAGE1-RECIPE] optional round cap: ends stage 1 — and, via\n'
              '          // the AUTO enrich gate keyed on enrich_stage1_done, the enrichment\n'
              '          // window — after N rounds. The stage-2 remainder formula below\n'
              '          // hands the unused rounds back to stage 2 unchanged.\n'
              '          const int s1_rounds_max =\n'
              '              Stage1RoundsCap() > 0\n'
              '                  ? std::min(popts->ba_global_max_refinements,\n'
              '                             Stage1RoundsCap())\n'
              '                  : popts->ba_global_max_refinements;\n'
              '          for (int i = 0; i < s1_rounds_max; ++i) {\n'
              '            if (enrich_done.load()) break;  // window closed\n'
              '            s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] stage-1 逐轮打点\n'
              '            const size_t num_obs = refined->ComputeNumObservations();\n'
              '            // [AETHER-T1] stage-1 per-part attribution (our own loop).\n'
              '            const double t_s1ba = NowMs();\n'
              '            mapper.AdjustGlobalBundle(mapper_opts, ba_opts);\n'
              '            s->t1_stage1_ba_ms += NowMs() - t_s1ba;\n'
              '            const double t_s1mg = NowMs();\n'
              '            size_t num_changed = mapper.CompleteAndMergeTracks(tri_opts);\n'
              '            num_changed += mapper.FilterPoints(mapper_opts);\n'
              '            s->t1_stage1_merge_ms += NowMs() - t_s1mg;\n'
              '            ++stage1_rounds;\n'
              '            const double changed =\n'
              '                num_obs == 0 ? 0\n'
              '                             : static_cast<double>(num_changed) / num_obs;\n'
              '            if (changed < popts->ba_global_max_refinement_change) break;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '          stage1_state = "ok";\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          stage1_state = "failed";\n'
              '          stage1_rounds = 0;\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize stage-1 refine failed ("\n'
              '                       << e.what()\n'
              '                       << ") — stage 2 runs the full refinement alone";\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '          stage1_state = "failed";\n'
