              '          stage1_rounds = 0;\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize stage-1 refine failed — "\n'
              '                          "stage 2 runs the full refinement alone";\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        stage1_ms = NowMs() - t_s1;\n'
              '        s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] stage-1 结束打点\n'
              '        AppendBaRingJsonl(s, 1);  // [AETHER BA-RING] stage-1 per-solve drain\n'
              '        // [P1-ENRICH-BUDGET] AUTO-mode window signal: stage 1 is over, so any\n'
              '        // further enrichment wall time is pure critical path. The enrichment\n'
              '        // thread stops STARTING new matcher attempts once it sees this (past\n'
              '        // the healthy-capture floor); its in-flight pair still completes.\n'
              '        s->enrich_stage1_done.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '        cache_pre.reset();  // free the snapshot before stage 2 loads its own\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [AETHER-T1] Time the join: with the quadratic prepaid this should be\n'
              '      // ~0; anything left is enrichment blocking the critical path.\n'
              '      const double t_join = NowMs();\n'
              '      enrich.join();\n'
              '      s->t1_enrich_gate_wait_ms = NowMs() - t_join;\n'
              '      s->enrich_hint.reset();  // [KNIFE-A ③] snapshot no longer needed\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] The enrichment thread is joined and\n'
              '      // stage 1 is over, so `refined` has exactly one owner again: replay the\n'
              '      // repaid probe-debt pairs through the live authoring gates BEFORE\n'
              '      // stage 2, so its CompleteAndMergeTracks / Retriangulate / BA /\n'
              '      // FilterPoints see (and police) the recovered points like any other.\n'
              '      // No-op with an empty ledger, i.e. whenever the probe gate is off.\n'
              '      ApplyProbeDebtGrowth(s, refined.get());\n'
              '      // Stage 2 completes the round budget: baseline runs gref(=5) rounds\n'
              '      // total; stage 2 runs the remainder (floor 1 — the enriched graph\'s new\n'
              '      // pairs always get at least Retriangulate/CompleteAndMergeTracks + one\n'
              '      // Cauchy BA round + FilterPoints/FilterFrames). Without this cap the\n'
              '      // per-round merge/filter churn re-burns all 5 rounds in stage 2 and the\n'
              '      // parallel window buys nothing (measured cap42: 48.4 s ≈ serial 48.5 s).\n'
              '      if (stage1_rounds > 0) {\n'
              '        popts->ba_global_max_refinements =\n'
              '            std::max(1, popts->ba_global_max_refinements - stage1_rounds);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      // Resume/degenerate path: the LOCAL model is published — refine a copy.\n'
              '      refined = std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>(*model);\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Nothing to replay here: this model was\n'
              '      // BUILT from the db by RunIncremental, so the repaid pairs are already\n'
              '      // in it by construction. Drop the ledger rather than double-author.\n'
              '      s->probe_debt_grow.clear();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Stage 2 / main refinement over the (enriched) db — unchanged semantics:\n'
              '    // IterativeGlobalRefinement + FilterFrames + UpdatePoint3DErrors.\n'
              '    const double t_s2 = NowMs();\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T1] Attribute stage-2\'s interior via the vendored-mapper hooks.\n'
              '    aether_igr_pre_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    aether_igr_ba_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    aether_igr_merge_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    aether_igr_rounds = 0;\n'
              '    aether_ba_ring_reset();  // [AETHER BA-RING] attribute stage-2 solves\n'
              '    // [GRAVITY-RA 2026-08-10] env 门控(默认关):重力对齐 RA 重解全局旋转,\n'
              '    // 平移保相机中心;随后 stage-2 全局 BA 收回平移与点。\n'
              '    const auto gravity_ra_snapshot =\n'
              '        MaybeRunFinalizeGravityRa(s->db_path, refined.get());\n'
              '    auto manager = std::make_shared<colmap::ReconstructionManager>();\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalPipeline pipeline(\n'
              '        popts, colmap::Database::Open(s->db_path), manager);\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] stage-2 入口打点\n'
              '    // [FRAME-LOSS-DIAG 2026-08-11 用户铁律"拍多少帧就注册多少帧"] stage-2 的\n'
              '    // RefineReconstruction 结尾会无条件 FilterFrames(判据两条:该帧 3D 观测\n'
              '    // 数 <1,或相机内参 bogus),随后 TearDown 把无位姿的帧连 image 一起 erase\n'
              '    // ⇒ 真机实测 101 帧喂入、live 注册 100+、finalize 只剩 97。这里做**只读**\n'
              '    // 分诊:记下进 stage-2 前每帧的观测数与内参健康度,出来后 diff 出被删的是\n'
              '    // 哪几帧、走的哪条支路。上游 #3271 官方定性"预期行为"、无豁免开关,所以\n'
              '    // 修法要靠分诊结果选(bogus → 放宽三参数有社区实证;0 观测 → 抄\n'
              '    // image_registrator 的补注册)。\n'
              '    if (s->stat_pose_direct_attempted > 0 ||\n'
              '        s->stat_guided_temporal_attempted > 0) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[guided-chain] rung1_attempted="\n'
              '                   << s->stat_guided_temporal_attempted\n'
              '                   << " rung1_upgraded=" << s->stat_guided_temporal_upgraded\n'
              '                   << " rung1_extra_inliers="\n'
              '                   << s->stat_guided_temporal_extra_inliers\n'
              '                   << " | rung2_attempted=" << s->stat_pose_direct_attempted\n'
              '                   << " rung2_rescued=" << s->stat_pose_direct_rescued\n'
              '                   << " rung2_inliers=" << s->stat_pose_direct_inliers;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::unordered_map<colmap::frame_t, std::pair<size_t, std::string>>\n'
              '        diag_before;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [frame_id, frame] : refined->Frames()) {\n'
              '      if (!frame.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      size_t obs = 0;\n'
              '      std::string img_name;\n'
              '      for (const auto& data_id : frame.ImageIds()) {\n'
              '        if (!refined->ExistsImage(data_id.id)) continue;\n'
              '        const auto& im = refined->Image(data_id.id);\n'
              '        obs += im.NumPoints3D();\n'
              '        if (img_name.empty()) img_name = im.Name();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      diag_before.emplace(frame_id, std::make_pair(obs, img_name));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    pipeline.RefineReconstruction(refined);\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      int lost_zero_obs = 0, lost_with_obs = 0;\n'
              '      std::string lost_detail;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [frame_id, before] : diag_before) {\n'
              '        const bool still =\n'
              '            refined->ExistsFrame(frame_id) && refined->Frame(frame_id).HasPose();\n'
              '        if (still) continue;\n'
              '        (before.first == 0 ? lost_zero_obs : lost_with_obs)++;\n'
              '        if (lost_detail.size() < 400) {\n'
              '          lost_detail += " " + before.second + "(obs=" +\n'
              '                         std::to_string(before.first) + ")";\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[frame-loss] before=" << diag_before.size()\n'
              '                   << " after=" << refined->NumRegFrames()\n'
              '                   << " lost_zero_obs=" << lost_zero_obs\n'
              '                   << " lost_with_obs=" << lost_with_obs << " |" << lost_detail;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [GRAVITY-RA GAUGE-REANCHOR] 自由规范 BA 之后把模型锚回原 gauge(见\n'
              '    // 函数注释;基线路径快照为空 = no-op)。\n'
              '    MaybeReanchorAfterGravityRa(gravity_ra_snapshot, refined.get());\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] stage-2 结束打点\n'
              '    const double stage2_ms = NowMs() - t_s2;\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T1] Pullable split record — the p1_done→refined stretch used to\n'
              '    // be a 78s zero-log black box (56% of the total wait).\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      char sline[320];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(\n'
              '          sline, sizeof(sline),\n'
              '          "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"finalize_split\\",\\"stage1_ba_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '          "\\"stage1_merge_ms\\":%.0f,\\"enrich_gate_wait_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '          "\\"s2_pre_ms\\":%.0f,\\"s2_ba_ms\\":%.0f,\\"s2_merge_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '          "\\"s2_rounds\\":%d,\\"s2_other_ms\\":%.0f}",\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), s->t1_stage1_ba_ms,\n'
              '          s->t1_stage1_merge_ms, s->t1_enrich_gate_wait_ms, aether_igr_pre_ms,\n'
              '          aether_igr_ba_ms, aether_igr_merge_ms, aether_igr_rounds,\n'
              '          stage2_ms - aether_igr_pre_ms - aether_igr_ba_ms -\n'
              '              aether_igr_merge_ms);\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, sline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    AppendBaRingJsonl(s, 2);  // [AETHER BA-RING] stage-2 per-solve drain\n'
              '    // [A1B-ASYNC-PVBA] experiment-arm accounting (absent when off/idle).\n'
              '    if (s->stat_apvba_kicks > 0) {\n'
              '      char aline[512];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(aline, sizeof(aline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"apvba_summary\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"kicks\\":%lld,\\"merges\\":%lld,\\"dropped\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"last_ba_ms\\":%.0f,\\"merge_ms_total\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"copy_ms_total\\":%.0f,\\"corr_mm\\":%.2f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"corr_deg\\":%.3f,\\"propagate\\":%d,\\"match_cadence\\":%d,"\n'
              '                    "\\"param_only\\":%d,\\"last_error\\":\\"%s\\"}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_apvba_kicks,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_apvba_merges,\n'
              '                    (long long)s->stat_apvba_dropped,\n'
              '                    s->stat_apvba_last_ba_ms, s->stat_apvba_merge_ms_total,\n'
              '                    s->stat_apvba_copy_ms_total, s->stat_apvba_last_corr_mm,\n'
              '                    s->stat_apvba_last_corr_deg,\n'
              '                    AsyncPreviewBaPropagate() ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '                    AsyncPreviewBaMatchCadence() ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '                    AsyncPreviewBaParamOnly() ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '                    JsonSafe(s->apvba_last_error.c_str(), 96).c_str());\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, aline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const double t_td = NowMs();\n'
              '    RestoreTemporalDetail(s, refined.get());\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] temporal-detail 结束打点\n'
              '    const double temporal_ms = NowMs() - t_td;\n'
              '    // [KNIFE-A ② 2026-07-11] Finalize-tail 2-view upgrade over the delivered\n'
              '    // model (env-gated no-op by default; sets s->upgrade_ms + stat_upgrade_*).\n'
              '    UpgradeLowParallaxTracks(s, refined.get());\n'
              '    // [P2-FRAG-MERGE 2026-07-11] Finalize-tail duplicate-fragment merge\n'
              '    // (env-gated no-op by default; sets s->frag_ms + stat_frag_*).\n'
              '    MergeFragmentTracks(s, refined.get());\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] finalize 尾部 pass 结束打点\n'
              '    // [GHOST-MASK 2026-07-12] Display-mask sidecar over the delivered model\n'
              '    // (env-gated no-op by default; runs AFTER every pass that can move/merge\n'
              '    // points so the flags describe exactly what the getters will serve).\n'
              '    MaybeWriteGhostMask(s, *refined);\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] finalize worker: cache_pre="\n'
              '                 << static_cast<int64_t>(cache_pre_ms)\n'
              '                 << "ms enrich=" << static_cast<int64_t>(enrich_ms)\n'
              '                 << "ms stage1=" << static_cast<int64_t>(stage1_ms) << "ms ("\n'
              '                 << stage1_state << ", rounds=" << stage1_rounds\n'
              '                 << ") stage2=" << static_cast<int64_t>(stage2_ms)\n'
              '                 << "ms (rounds<=" << popts->ba_global_max_refinements\n'
              '                 << ") temporal=" << static_cast<int64_t>(temporal_ms)\n'
              '                 << "ms upgrade=" << static_cast<int64_t>(s->upgrade_ms)\n'
              '                 << "ms total=" << static_cast<int64_t>(NowMs() - t0) << "ms";\n'
              '    // [AETHER FINALIZE-SEGMENTS 2026-07-11] Same numbers into the persistent\n'
              '    // run_dir JSON (stderr double-write stays above) — BEFORE the status flips\n'
              '    // to REFINED so the Dart forwarder always sees a complete file.\n'
              '    WriteFinalizeSegments(s, live_reuse, cache_pre_ms, enrich_ms, stage1_ms,\n'
              '                          stage1_rounds, stage1_state, stage2_ms,\n'
              '                          popts->ba_global_max_refinements, temporal_ms,\n'
              '                          NowMs() - t0);\n'
              '    // [PROVENANCE 2026-07-26] Split the delivered points by origin. Purely a\n'
              '    // measurement: it decides whether the hand-written live triangulator is\n'
              '    // actually authoring the delivered cloud or whether official\n'
              '    // Retriangulate has already replaced most of it.\n'
              '    if (live_reuse && refined) {\n'
              '      int64_t from_live = 0, from_official = 0;\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, _] : refined->Points3D()) {\n'
              '        if (live_origin_ids.count(pid)) ++from_live;\n'
              '        else ++from_official;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const int64_t live_dropped =\n'
              '          static_cast<int64_t>(live_origin_ids.size()) - from_live;\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] delivered-point provenance: live-created="\n'
              '                   << from_live << " official-retriangulated=" << from_official\n'
              '                   << " live-dropped-by-official-filtering=" << live_dropped\n'
              '                   << " (live model had " << live_origin_ids.size()\n'
              '                   << "; capture-time official TriangulateImage added "\n'
              '                   << s->stat_official_tri_added << " obs)";\n'
              '      char pline[256];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(pline, sizeof(pline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"point_provenance\\",\\"live\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    "\\"official\\":%lld,\\"live_dropped\\":%lld,\\"live_total\\":%zu}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), (long long)from_live,\n'
              '                    (long long)from_official, (long long)live_dropped,\n'
              '                    live_origin_ids.size());\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, pline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '      s->recon = refined;\n'
              '      s->refine_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->finalize_status.store(2);  // AETHER_SFM_FINALIZE_REFINED\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    RefineFailClosed(s, e.what());\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    RefineFailClosed(s, "unknown exception");\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '}  // namespace\n'
              '\n'
              'extern "C" {\n'
              '\n'
              'void aether_sfm_options_default(aether_sfm_options_t* out) {\n'
              '  if (!out) return;\n'
              '  out->max_features = 2048;\n'
              '  out->image_width = 0;\n'
              '  out->image_height = 0;\n'
              '  out->match_max_ratio = 0.8f;\n'
              '  out->use_gpu_match = 0;  // CPU brute-force by default; iOS shim can flip to 1\n'
              '  out->k_neighbors = 6;\n'
              '  out->use_gpu_extract = 0;  // CPU DSP-SIFT by default; iOS shim flips to 1\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'const char* aether_sfm_result_str(aether_sfm_result_t code) {\n'
              '  switch (code) {\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_OK: return "AETHER_SFM_OK";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_DB: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_DB";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_EXTRACT: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_EXTRACT";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_NO_INITIAL_PAIR: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_NO_INITIAL_PAIR";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL";\n'
              '    case AETHER_SFM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED: return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return "AETHER_SFM_ERR_UNKNOWN";\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── batch (validated v1) ───────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_run(const char* db_path, const char* image_path,\n'
              '                                   const aether_sfm_options_t* options,\n'
              '                                   aether_sfm_session_t** out_session,\n'
              '                                   char* out_json, int out_cap) {\n'
              '  if (!db_path || !image_path) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '\n'
              '  aether_sfm_options_t opts;\n'
              '  if (options) {\n'
              '    opts = *options;\n'
              '  } else {\n'
              '    aether_sfm_options_default(&opts);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<colmap::ReconstructionManager> manager;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  // Batch/reference inputs do not carry the mandatory per-frame ARKit gravity\n'
              '  // contract. Never let a previous streaming session\'s registry leak into a\n'
              '  // batch solve in the same process.\n'
              '  aether_ba_clear_gravity_priors();\n'
              '  const aether_sfm_result_t rc = RunIncremental(\n'
              '      db_path, image_path, opts, &manager, &recon, out_json, out_cap);\n'
              '  if (rc != AETHER_SFM_OK) return rc;\n'
              '\n'
              '  if (out_session) {\n'
              '    auto* s = new aether_sfm_session();\n'
              '    aether_preclamp_instr_v1::ResetOfficialSessionRecords(s);\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '    if (DescriptorResidencyEnabledCore()) {\n'
              '      s->descriptor_residency_nonce = NewDescriptorResidencySessionNonce();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '    s->options = opts;\n'
              '    s->db_path = db_path;\n'
              '    s->image_path = image_path;\n'
              '    s->owns_db_file = false;  // batch path consumes a caller-owned db\n'
              '    s->recon_manager = manager;\n'
              '    s->recon = recon;\n'
              '    *out_session = s;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_run_dir(const char* capture_dir,\n'
              '                                       const aether_sfm_options_t* options,\n'
              '                                       aether_sfm_session_t** out_session,\n'
              '                                       char* out_json, int out_cap) {\n'
              '  // NOTE (honest stub): the in-process extract+match+db-build path is the\n'
              '  // streaming surface composed together (create → add_frame×N → finalize) over\n'
              '  // a directory of JPEGs + poses.json. JPEG decode is a platform-side concern\n'
              '  // (CGImage on iOS); this convenience entry needs a portable JPEG decoder to\n'
              '  // be self-contained, which is not yet vendored into the device archive. Until\n'
              '  // then callers use aether_sfm_run with a prebuilt db, or drive the streaming\n'
              '  // API directly with platform-decoded grayscale buffers.\n'
              '  (void)capture_dir;\n'
              '  (void)options;\n'
              '  (void)out_session;\n'
              '  if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '    std::snprintf(out_json, out_cap,\n'
              '                  "{\\"error\\":\\"run_dir not implemented: drive create/"\n'
              '                  "add_frame/finalize with platform-decoded frames\\"}");\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── streaming ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_create(const char* db_path,\n'
              '                                      const aether_sfm_options_t* options,\n'
              '                                      aether_sfm_session_t** out_session) {\n'
              '  if (!db_path || !out_session) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    aether_ba_clear_gravity_priors();\n'
              '    auto* s = new aether_sfm_session();\n'
              '    aether_preclamp_instr_v1::ResetOfficialSessionRecords(s);\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '    if (DescriptorResidencyEnabledCore()) {\n'
              '      s->descriptor_residency_nonce = NewDescriptorResidencySessionNonce();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '    s->gpu_timestamp_run_id = NewGpuTimestampRunId();\n'
              '    if (options) {\n'
              '      s->options = *options;\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      aether_sfm_options_default(&s->options);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY 2026-07-11] The live recon lives behind a shared_ptr\n'
              '    // (see the session struct comment); allocate it up front so every gated\n'
              '    // use can rely on non-null while live_recon_ready flags actual readiness.\n'
              '    s->live_recon = std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>();\n'
              '    s->db_path = db_path;\n'
              '    // Streaming callers pass a capture-owned db path (`sfm_live.db`). Keep it\n'
              '    // after free so detached/background finalize or the launch-time recovery\n'
              '    // sweep can retry if the app is killed mid-solve.\n'
              '    s->owns_db_file = false;\n'
              '    s->db = colmap::Database::Open(db_path);\n'
              '    if (!s->db) {\n'
              '      delete s;\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_DB;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    TailCacheInitialize(s);\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T3 2026-07-26] Build stamp into the pullable sidecar: twice in\n'
              '    // one day a capture was analysed against code that was written AFTER it\n'
              '    // ("代码态被当成真机态" — cap3/cap4 both needed after-the-fact forensics\n'
              '    // to notice). One line per session pins which binary produced the run.\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      char bline[384];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(bline, sizeof(bline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"build_stamp\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"built\\":\\"%s %s\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"diag_contract\\":\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_DIAG_V1_20260810\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"snapshot_diag_contract\\":"\n'
              '                    "\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_SNAPSHOT_DIAG_V2_20260810\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"native_diag_build_id\\":"\n'
              '                    "\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_DIAG_NATIVE_V2_20260810_AETHER_0ab02a0_SNAPSHOT_01\\","\n'
              '                    "\\"observation_only\\":true}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), __DATE__, __TIME__);\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, bline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [GPU-HANG-B1] 启动巡逻线程。回调只读 s->db_path(create 后不变),\n'
              '    // AppendMatchFailJsonl 本身 open-append-close、异常自吞,巡逻线程安全。\n'
              '    // s 的生命期由 aether_sfm_free 先 Stop() 后 delete 保证。\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.Start([s](const std::string& line) {\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    *out_session = s;\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_DB;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [INCREMENTAL-GLOBAL-BA 2026-07-13] Rolling capture-time global BA over\n'
              '// live_recon. DEFAULT OFF (OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA=1). Called from the tail\n'
              '// of AddFrameFeaturesImpl on the capture-worker isolate — the SAME thread that\n'
              '// owns live_recon — so it takes no lock (mirrors aether_sfm_global_refine\'s\n'
              '// threading contract). Fires only every IncrementalGlobalBaEveryN() registered\n'
              '// frames; a single call must stay well under the ~2s per-frame SLA or it stalls\n'
              '// the next frame and drops the capture frame-rate (the device A/B measures this).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// LIGHT CONFIG (deliberately weaker than the finalize solve): ONE pose-correcting\n'
              '// global BA pass — no structure-only stage 2 (that is left to finalize) — CAUCHY,\n'
              '// iter 10, observation-capped to the longest (revisit-spanning) tracks so cost is\n'
              '// DECOUPLED from total point count. Focal/pp fixed (trust the ARKit-seeded shared\n'
              '// camera), exactly like aether_sfm_global_refine stage 1.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// LOCAL-GLOBAL HYBRID (the bounded-cost trick): only frames registered in the\n'
              '// most-recent IncrementalGlobalBaWindow() get FREE poses; every older frame is\n'
              '// added as a SetConstantRigFromWorldPose ANCHOR. Fixed anchors pin the gauge (no\n'
              '// FixGauge needed) AND — because a revisit-spanning long track links a fresh free\n'
              '// frame to an OLD fixed frame — the solve pulls the fresh pose onto the already-\n'
              '// corrected anchor, collapsing the accumulating double-wall drift a window at a\n'
              '// time. Free-pose count ≈ window and points are obs-capped, so cost stays bounded\n'
              '// regardless of N (frozen anchors add residuals but no parameters).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// ⚠️ QUALITY CONSEQUENCE — WHY THIS MUST BE HOST-VALIDATED BEFORE DEFAULT-ON:\n'
              '//  (1) Freezing old poses makes this an EXPANDING SLIDING-WINDOW BA, not a full\n'
              '//      free-gauge global solve. Drift baked into an anchor before it was frozen is\n'
              '//      not re-optimized here; the design bet (standard incremental SLAM) is that\n'
              '//      correcting drift while it is still local keeps cumulative live_recon ≈ the\n'
              '//      one-shot finalize global solve. Unproven until the cap51 A/B.\n'
              '//  (2) The double-wall only closes for tracks that EXIST during capture (temporal\n'
              '//      + live-spatial matches). Revisit pairs that materialize only in the finalize\n'
              '//      enrichment (AddSpatialRevisitMatches) never existed here, so finalize\'s\n'
              '//      free-gauge solve + track completion remain REQUIRED (see RefineGlobalBA).\n'
              '// THERMAL-GATED (safe-fail): thermal>=3 (critical) skips entirely to give the\n'
              '// camera/GPU the device; thermal>=2 (serious) doubles the effective cadence. A\n'
              '// cold device does the full cadence; a hot device degrades toward current behavior\n'
              '// and never crashes (every failure is caught → the windowed cloud is untouched).\n'
              'static void MaybeIncrementalGlobalRefine(aether_sfm_session* s) {\n'
              '  if (!IncrementalGlobalBaEnabled()) return;\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->live_recon_ready || !s->live_recon) return;\n'
              '  const int thermal_now = s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '  if (thermal_now >= 3) return;  // critical: hand the device to camera/GPU\n'
              '  const size_t n_reg = s->reg_order.size();\n'
              '  if (n_reg < 3) return;\n'
              '  const size_t every = static_cast<size_t>(IncrementalGlobalBaEveryN()) *\n'
              '                       (thermal_now >= 2 ? 2 : 1);  // serious → half the cadence\n'
              '  if (n_reg < s->last_global_refine_reg + every) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // ---- Free-pose sliding window: recent frames free, older frames frozen ----\n'
              '    const size_t window = static_cast<size_t>(IncrementalGlobalBaWindow());\n'
              '    const size_t free_from = n_reg > window ? n_reg - window : 0;\n'
              '    colmap::BundleAdjustmentConfig cfg;\n'
              '    size_t n_frozen = 0;\n'
              '    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_reg; ++i) {\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t img = s->reg_order[i];\n'
              '      cfg.AddImage(img);\n'
              '      if (i < free_from) {\n'
              '        // Frozen anchor: its observations of the variable points still constrain\n'
              '        // the free poses, but its own world pose does not move.\n'
              '        cfg.SetConstantRigFromWorldPose(s->live_recon->Image(img).FrameId());\n'
              '        ++n_frozen;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (free_from == 0)  // whole capture inside one window → pin the gauge\n'
              '      cfg.FixGauge(colmap::BundleAdjustmentGauge::THREE_POINTS);\n'
              '    SetAllCameraIntrinsicsConstant(*s->live_recon, s->reg_order, cfg);\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Observation cap: rank tracks longest-first (longest = revisit-spanning) and\n'
              '    // add as variable points until the budget is hit — same bound as\n'
              '    // aether_sfm_global_refine, so cost is decoupled from total point count.\n'
              '    const size_t kMaxObs =\n'
              '        std::min<size_t>(400000, std::max<size_t>(80000, n_reg * 120));\n'
              '    std::vector<std::pair<int, colmap::point3D_t>> ranked;\n'
              '    ranked.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D())\n'
              '      ranked.emplace_back(static_cast<int>(pt.track.Length()), pid);\n'
              '    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n'
              '              std::greater<std::pair<int, colmap::point3D_t>>());\n'
              '    size_t obs_budget = 0;\n'
              '    for (const auto& [len, pid] : ranked) {\n'
              '      if (obs_budget >= kMaxObs) break;\n'
              '      cfg.AddVariablePoint(pid);\n'
              '      obs_budget += static_cast<size_t>(len);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::BundleAdjustmentOptions opt;\n'
              '    opt.refine_rig_from_world = true;    // non-anchor poses redistribute drift\n'
              '    opt.refine_points3D = true;\n'
              '    opt.refine_focal_length = false;     // trust each image\'s ARKit calibration\n'
              '    opt.refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '    opt.print_summary = false;\n'
              '    opt.ceres->loss_function_type =\n'
              '        colmap::CeresBundleAdjustmentOptions::LossFunctionType::CAUCHY;\n'
              '    opt.ceres->loss_function_scale = 1.0;\n'
              '    opt.ceres->solver_options.max_num_iterations = 10;  // light (vs 25 finalize)\n'
              '    // Leave core headroom for the camera / GPU matcher / UI mid-capture; the exact\n'
              '    // thread count is a device-A/B tuning knob (A16 → 4, host M3 Pro → 6).\n'
              '    // [BA-THREADS-POST 2026-07-28] capture-period budget split off from the\n'
              '    // finalize budget (which no longer reserves cores — camera is off there).\n'
              '    opt.ceres->solver_options.num_threads = LiveBaThreads();\n'
              '    colmap::CreateDefaultBundleAdjuster(opt, cfg, *s->live_recon)->Solve();\n'
              '\n'
              '    s->last_global_refine_reg = n_reg;\n'
              '    s->incremental_global_ba_ran = true;\n'
              '    ++s->stat_incremental_refines;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] incremental global BA #"\n'
              '                 << s->stat_incremental_refines << " reg=" << n_reg\n'
              '                 << " free=" << (n_reg - n_frozen) << " frozen=" << n_frozen\n'
              '                 << " varpts_obs<=" << kMaxObs << " thermal=" << thermal_now\n'
              '                 << " took=" << static_cast<int64_t>(NowMs() - t0) << "ms";\n'
              '    // NOTE: no preview re-publish here — the unconditional preview snapshot right\n'
              '    // after the caller\'s windowed-BA block already reads this refined live_recon.\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    // Safe-fail: keep the windowed cloud; advance the counter so a persistent\n'
              '    // failure does not retry (and stall) every subsequent frame.\n'
              '    s->last_global_refine_reg = n_reg;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] incremental global BA skipped (" << e.what()\n'
              '                 << ") — windowed cloud kept";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Shared core of aether_sfm_add_frame (extraction upstream) and\n'
              '// aether_sfm_add_frame_features (features injected — host replay/verification\n'
              '// of the streaming path from a pulled sfm_live.db). Everything from the shared\n'
              '// camera write onward is identical between the two entries; extract_ms is the\n'
              '// caller-measured extraction time (0 for injected features).\n'
              '//\n'
              '// [SCALE-PERSIST 2026-08-06] `scales` (optional, may be NULL): n per-keypoint\n'
              '// detection scales from the _v2 extractor. When present, keypoints persist\n'
              '// with their true scale via the official 4-parameter\n'
              '// colmap::FeatureKeypoint(x, y, scale, orientation) constructor (orientation\n'
              '// 0 — scale is what Projection-Accuracy-style filtering consumes); when NULL,\n'
              '// the pre-change unit-affine FeatureKeypoint(x, y) write is reproduced\n'
              '// byte-for-byte. Keypoint AFFINE COLUMNS ONLY: xy, descriptors, matches, TVG,\n'
              '// rec.points and FrameIdentityDigestV1 (xy+desc-derived) are unaffected.\n'
              'static aether_sfm_result_t AddFrameFeaturesImpl(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t* s, const float* xy, const uint8_t* desc,\n'
              '    const float* scales, int n, int width, int height, float fx, float fy,\n'
              '    float cx, float cy, const double pose_qwxyz[4], const double pose_t[3],\n'
              '    double extract_ms, int* out_frame_id) {\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // Production capture is an ARKit `.gravity` route. Validate and convert\n'
              '    // the required pose before the first database or reconstruction mutation;\n'
              '    // missing/invalid gravity identity is not permission to create an unposed\n'
              '    // frame and later fall back to P3P.\n'
              '    aether::sfm::MandatoryArkitGravityPoseV1 gravity_pose;\n'
              '    const aether::sfm::MandatoryArkitGravityPoseStatusV1 gravity_pose_status =\n'
              '        aether::sfm::BuildMandatoryArkitGravityPoseV1(pose_qwxyz, pose_t,\n'
              '                                                      &gravity_pose);\n'
              '    if (gravity_pose_status !=\n'
              '        aether::sfm::MandatoryArkitGravityPoseStatusV1::kOk) {\n'
              '      // [VIO-TELEMETRY 2026-08-04] Deliberately distinguishable. A rejection\n'
              '      // here is a pose-supply/VIO failure, which is a completely different\n'
              '      // condition from the "not enough texture" outcome the capture UI reports\n'
              '      // — yet before this tag the two were indistinguishable in a device log,\n'
              '      // so a VIO dropout got diagnosed as a scene problem and the user was told\n'
              '      // to re-shoot. The status code says which validator rejected the frame.\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm][ARKIT-POSE-REJECT] frame rejected before "\n'
              '                      "any db write, status="\n'
              '                   << static_cast<int>(gravity_pose_status);\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 2) One official COLMAP PINHOLE camera per 12 MP image, populated from\n'
              '    //    the exact same high-resolution ARFrame as the JPEG. Autofocus stays\n'
              '    //    enabled; per-frame calibration preserves the resulting focal change.\n'
              '    if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || !std::isfinite(fx) ||\n'
              '        !std::isfinite(fy) || !std::isfinite(cx) || !std::isfinite(cy) ||\n'
              '        fx <= 0.0f || fy <= 0.0f || cx < 0.0f || cy < 0.0f ||\n'
              '        cx >= static_cast<float>(width) || cy >= static_cast<float>(height)) {\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    colmap::Camera camera = colmap::Camera::CreateFromModelId(\n'
              '        colmap::kInvalidCameraId, colmap::PinholeCameraModel::model_id,\n'
              '        /*focal_length=*/fx, width, height);\n'
              '    camera.SetFocalLengthX(fx);\n'
              '    camera.SetFocalLengthY(fy);\n'
              '    camera.SetPrincipalPointX(cx);\n'
              '    camera.SetPrincipalPointY(cy);\n'
              '    const colmap::camera_t camera_id = s->db->WriteCamera(camera);\n'
              '    camera.camera_id = camera_id;\n'
              '    if (s->camera_id == 0) s->camera_id = camera_id;\n'
              '    // Each camera needs its own trivial rig before its image enters the live\n'
              '    // reconstruction.\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon) {\n'
              '      s->live_recon->AddCameraWithTrivialRig(camera);\n'
              '      s->live_recon_ready = true;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 3) Write image + keypoints + descriptors.\n'
              '    const int frame_id = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    colmap::Image image;\n'
              '    char name[64];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "frame_%06d.jpg", frame_id);\n'
              '    image.SetName(name);\n'
              '    image.SetCameraId(camera_id);\n'
              '    const colmap::image_t image_id = s->db->WriteImage(image);\n'
              '\n'
              '    // [ORDINAL-GUARD 2026-08-04] The db row for this ordinal now exists and\n'
              '    // COLMAP exposes no DeleteImage to take it back. frame_id is derived from\n'
              '    // s->frames.size(), so if this call returns early OR throws before the\n'
              '    // record is pushed, the NEXT frame regenerates this exact\n'
              '    // "frame_%06d.jpg" and dies on the images.name UNIQUE constraint — and so\n'
              '    // does every frame after it, i.e. one transient fault bricks the whole live\n'
              '    // capture instead of costing a single frame.\n'
              '    // REPRODUCED ON HOST 2026-08-04 (E3 replay, cap7_day): a single transient\n'
              '    // "database is locked" at frame 7 killed frames 8..145 of a 146-frame\n'
              '    // capture. The earlier fix covered only the pose-snapshot failure path;\n'
              '    // this guard covers EVERY exit path, exceptions included.\n'
              '    struct OrdinalGuardV1 {\n'
              '      aether_sfm_session* session = nullptr;\n'
              '      int frame_id = -1;\n'
              '      colmap::image_t image_id = 0;\n'
              '      colmap::camera_t camera_id = 0;\n'
              '      bool committed = false;\n'
              '      ~OrdinalGuardV1() {\n'
              '        if (committed || session == nullptr) return;\n'
              '        // Runs during stack unwinding too, so it must never throw.\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          FrameRecord withdrawn;\n'
              '          withdrawn.frame_id = frame_id;\n'
              '          withdrawn.image_id = 0;  // withdrawn: excluded from every pair path\n'
              '          withdrawn.persisted_image_id = image_id;\n'
              '          withdrawn.camera_id = camera_id;\n'
              '          withdrawn.n_keypoints = 0;\n'
              '          session->frames.push_back(std::move(withdrawn));\n'
              '          LOG(ERROR) << "[aether_sfm][ORDINAL-GUARD] frame " << frame_id\n'
              '                     << " aborted after its db row was created; the ordinal is "\n'
              '                        "consumed so the capture continues on the next frame";\n'
              '          // Restore db/sidecar count parity that recovery checks. Best effort:\n'
              '          // if this also fails, recovery stays blocked but the LIVE capture\n'
              '          // survives, which is the whole point of the guard.\n'
              '          (void)PersistCurrentArkitPoseSnapshot(*session);\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } ordinal_guard{s, frame_id, image_id, camera_id, false};\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::FeatureKeypoints kps(n);\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n'
              '      // [SCALE-PERSIST 2026-08-06] official 4-param constructor\n'
              '      // FeatureKeypoint(x, y, scale, orientation) — colmap/feature/types.cc:43\n'
              '      // (it THROW_CHECKs scale >= 0, hence the finite/positive guard; a\n'
              '      // non-finite extractor value degrades that keypoint to the legacy unit\n'
              '      // affine instead of aborting the frame).\n'
              '      if (scales != nullptr && std::isfinite(scales[i]) && scales[i] > 0.0f) {\n'
              '        kps[i] =\n'
              '            colmap::FeatureKeypoint(xy[2 * i], xy[2 * i + 1], scales[i], 0);\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        kps[i] = colmap::FeatureKeypoint(xy[2 * i], xy[2 * i + 1]);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [MIGRATION 4.0.4 / STEP 5] FeatureDescriptors is now a struct\n'
              '    // {FeatureExtractorType type; FeatureDescriptorsData data;}. The raw\n'
              '    // uint8 matrix is the `.data` member; tag the type so WriteDescriptors\n'
              '    // serializes it consistently with ReadDescriptors.\n'
              '    colmap::FeatureDescriptors descriptors;\n'
              '    descriptors.type = colmap::FeatureExtractorType::SIFT;\n'
              '    descriptors.data.resize(n, 128);\n'
              '    std::memcpy(descriptors.data.data(), desc,\n'
              '                static_cast<size_t>(n) * 128);\n'
              '    s->db->WriteKeypoints(image_id, kps);\n'
              '    s->db->WriteDescriptors(image_id, descriptors);\n'
              '\n'
              '    FrameRecord rec;\n'
              '    rec.frame_id = frame_id;\n'
              '    rec.image_id = image_id;\n'
              '    rec.persisted_image_id = image_id;\n'
              '    rec.camera_id = camera_id;\n'
              '    rec.camera = camera;\n'
              '    rec.n_keypoints = n;\n'
              '    rec.descriptors.assign(desc, desc + static_cast<size_t>(n) * 128);\n'
              '    rec.points = colmap::FeatureKeypointsToPointsVector(kps);\n'
              '    rec.frame_identity_digest =\n'
              '        FrameIdentityDigestV1(name, camera, rec.points, rec.descriptors);\n'
              '    TailCacheMirrorImage(s, camera, image_id, name, rec.points);\n'
              '\n'
              '    rec.cam_from_world = colmap::Rigid3d(\n'
              '        Eigen::Quaterniond(gravity_pose.cam_from_world_qwxyz[0],\n'
              '                           gravity_pose.cam_from_world_qwxyz[1],\n'
              '                           gravity_pose.cam_from_world_qwxyz[2],\n'
              '                           gravity_pose.cam_from_world_qwxyz[3]),\n'
              '        Eigen::Vector3d(gravity_pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[0],\n'
              '                        gravity_pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[1],\n'
              '                        gravity_pose.cam_from_world_t_xyz[2]));\n'
              '    rec.gravity_cam = Eigen::Vector3d(gravity_pose.gravity_cam_xyz[0],\n'
              '                                      gravity_pose.gravity_cam_xyz[1],\n'
              '                                      gravity_pose.gravity_cam_xyz[2]);\n'
              '    rec.has_pose = true;\n'
              '\n'
              '    const double gravity_cam_xyz[3] = {rec.gravity_cam.x(),\n'
              '                                       rec.gravity_cam.y(),\n'
              '                                       rec.gravity_cam.z()};\n'
              '    // ⚠️ PROVENANCE GAP (2026-08-04): 0.5 deg has no cited source. It is the\n'
              '    // stddev of an attitude ANCHOR, not of a gravity measurement (see\n'
              '    // gravity_ba_prior_v1.h). Two facts bound it and neither has been reconciled\n'
              '    // with this number: our own reconstruction-vs-ARKit rotation drift measures\n'
              '    // under 1 deg, i.e. up to 2 sigma of coherent, same-signed error across all\n'
              '    // frames; and RealityCapture ships its comparable orientation prior\n'
              '    // (sfmCameraPriorAccuracyYaw/Pitch/Roll) at 10.0, twenty times looser. Do\n'
              '    // not treat 0.5 as validated. Changing it changes reconstruction output and\n'
              '    // therefore requires an on-device A/B, not a host run.\n'
              '    constexpr double kGravityBaSigmaRad =\n'
              '        0.5 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0;\n'
              '    if (!aether_ba_set_gravity_prior(name, gravity_cam_xyz,\n'
              '                                     kGravityBaSigmaRad)) {\n'
              '      // This path is constructionally unreachable after the mandatory ingest\n'
              '      // validator, but it remains fail-closed if the registry contract ever\n'
              '      // drifts. No BA is allowed to silently proceed without this frame\'s\n'
              '      // gravity observation.\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!PersistArkitPoseSnapshot(*s, rec)) {\n'
              '      // The DB image now exists but there is no complete matching pose\n'
              '      // snapshot. Stop immediately: on relaunch the count mismatch is an\n'
              '      // explicit blocked recovery, never permission to invoke P3P.\n'
              '      //\n'
              '      // The ordinal is consumed by OrdinalGuardV1 on the way out (it covers this\n'
              '      // return and every other exit path, including exceptions), so this branch\n'
              '      // only has to report.\n'
              '      LOG(ERROR) << "[aether_sfm][ARKIT-PERSIST-FAIL] frame " << rec.frame_id\n'
              '                 << " dropped from the reconstruction: pose snapshot write "\n'
              '                    "failed. Live capture continues on the next ordinal.";\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Register this frame into the live Reconstruction with its ARKit pose.\n'
              '    // Trivial rig/frame (frame_id == image_id); the 2-arg overload sets the\n'
              '    // pose AND RegisterFrame()s it in one call.\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon_ready) {\n'
              '      colmap::Image rimg;\n'
              '      rimg.SetImageId(image_id);\n'
              '      rimg.SetName(name);\n'
              '      rimg.SetCameraId(rec.camera_id);\n'
              '      rimg.SetPoints2D(rec.points);  // kp order == point2D_idx\n'
              '      s->live_recon->AddImageWithTrivialFrame(std::move(rimg), rec.cam_from_world);\n'
              '      s->reg_order.push_back(image_id);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 4) Match against k_neighbors candidate frames (CPU brute-force,\n'
              '    //    MUTUALLY cross-checked inside aether_sift_match_pairs) and PERSIST\n'
              '    //    the correspondences — the exact sequence colmap\'s own matching\n'
              '    //    pipeline uses (feature_matching.cc): WriteMatches with the raw\n'
              '    //    cross-checked pairs, EstimateTwoViewGeometry (E/F/H RANSAC at\n'
              '    //    colmap defaults) over the same pairs, WriteTwoViewGeometry with the\n'
              '    //    verified inliers. finalize()\'s IncrementalPipeline consumes the\n'
              '    //    two_view_geometries table as-is (min_num_matches=15 filters weak\n'
              '    //    pairs there). This closes the formerly-documented streaming gap\n'
              '    //    ("matcher returns count only") that left the matches table empty\n'
              '    //    and made every finalize return ERR_NOT_REGISTERED.\n'
              '    //    [SPATIAL-FIRST 2026-07-11] Candidates are now SPATIAL-first (ARKit\n'
              '    //    camera-center K-NN ∩ view-angle < 45°), temporal-filled to K, with a\n'
              '    //    pure-temporal fallback when the frame has no usable pose — see\n'
              '    //    SelectStreamCandidates. Budget unchanged: at most K pairs matched.\n'
              '    //    [THERMAL-THROTTLE 2026-07-11] Live candidate K is now RUNTIME-tunable:\n'
              '    //      • base K: env OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K overrides options.k_neighbors\n'
              '    //        (production 12);\n'
              '    //      • hot K: when the platform-pushed thermal state is serious/critical\n'
              '    //        (>= 2, aether_sfm_set_thermal_state) the window drops to\n'
              '    //        OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K_HOT — halving the per-frame Metal matcher load\n'
              '    //        to give the camera/system GPU room (cap45 freeze: thermal-serious\n'
              '    //        GPU saturation → MTLCommandBufferStatusError → ARSession frame\n'
              '    //        stall ~2 min).\n'
              '    //    Throttled frames are marked fed_throttled and\n'
              '    //    FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames re-matches their missing temporal-window\n'
              '    //    pairs at finish time.\n'
              '    //    ⚠️ DEFAULT OFF (2026-07-11 host A/B verdict): the quality gate was\n'
              '    //    final points ±2% / reproj ±0.02 / registered ==. cap44 (109f) passed\n'
              '    //    (−0.36% / +0.0024 / 109==109), but cap45 (47f) exceeded the points\n'
              '    //    band in the POSITIVE direction on both K6 (+2.36%) and the K8 retry\n'
              '    //    (+2.69%) — the finalize backfill (temporal window) adds pairs the\n'
              '    //    live spatial-first K12 baseline never attempts, so the throttled arm\n'
              '    //    delivers MORE points (reproj in band, registered equal, baseline\n'
              '    //    rerun bit-identical ⇒ systematic, not noise). More-not-fewer is not\n'
              '    //    a loss, but it is outside the pre-agreed band → per the sign-off\n'
              '    //    rule the throttle ships DISABLED; OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K_HOT=<k> (e.g. 6)\n'
              '    //    is the opt-in knob pending a user decision on the new band.\n'
              '    static const int env_base_k = [] {\n'
              '      if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K")) {\n'
              '        const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '        if (v > 0) return v;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return 0;  // 0 = use options.k_neighbors\n'
              '    }();\n'
              '    static const int hot_k = [] {\n'
              '      if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K_HOT")) {\n'
              '        return std::atoi(e);  // >0 enables the thermal throttle at that K\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return 0;  // DEFAULT OFF (A/B verdict above)\n'
              '    }();\n'
              '    const int base_k = env_base_k > 0\n'
              '                           ? env_base_k\n'
              '                           : (s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors\n'
              '                                                         : 6);\n'
              '    const int thermal_now = s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '    const bool throttled = thermal_now >= 2 && hot_k > 0 && hot_k < base_k;\n'
              '    const int k = throttled ? hot_k : base_k;\n'
              '    if (throttled) {\n'
              '      rec.fed_throttled = true;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_thermal_throttled_frames;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (throttled != s->throttle_active_logged) {\n'
              '      s->throttle_active_logged = throttled;\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] thermal throttle "\n'
              '                   << (throttled ? "ON" : "OFF") << " at frame " << frame_id\n'
              '                   << " (thermal=" << thermal_now << ", live K "\n'
              '                   << (throttled ? base_k : hot_k) << "→"\n'
              '                   << (throttled ? hot_k : base_k)\n'
              '                   << "); finalize re-match restores the pair topology.";\n'
              '      char tline[160];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(tline, sizeof(tline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"thermal_throttle\\",\\"on\\":%d,"\n'
              '                    "\\"frame\\":%d,\\"thermal\\":%d,\\"k\\":%d}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), throttled ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '                    frame_id, thermal_now, k);\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, tline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    int cand_spatial = 0, cand_temporal = 0;\n'
              '    std::vector<int> candidates =\n'
              '        SelectSpatialK20TemporalT2Candidates(\n'
              '            *s, rec, frame_id, throttled, k, &cand_spatial, &cand_temporal);\n'
              '    // [VISUAL-LOOP V1] Every tenth valid frame, retrieve at most four remote\n'
              '    // candidates after excluding S20/T2 and any pair already in sqlite. The\n'
              '    // index is integer-only and platform neutral; failures are fail-closed to\n'
              '    // the already selected S20/T2 graph.\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      const std::vector<aether::sfm::VisualLoopCandidateV1> loop_candidates =\n'
              '          s->visual_loop_index.QueryAndAdd(\n'
              '              frame_id, rec.descriptors.data(), rec.n_keypoints, candidates);\n'
              '      for (const aether::sfm::VisualLoopCandidateV1& loop : loop_candidates) {\n'
              '        if (loop.frame_id < 0 || loop.frame_id >= frame_id) continue;\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& previous = s->frames[loop.frame_id];\n'
              '        if (previous.image_id == 0 ||\n'
              '            s->db->ExistsMatches(previous.image_id, image_id) ||\n'
              '            s->db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(previous.image_id, image_id)) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        candidates.push_back(loop.frame_id);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      std::sort(candidates.begin(), candidates.end());\n'
              '      candidates.erase(std::unique(candidates.begin(), candidates.end()),\n'
              '                       candidates.end());\n'
              '    } catch (...) {\n'
              '      // Retrieval is an insurance source, never a capture blocker.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    AppendTailMatchCandidatesV1(\n'
              '        s, frame_id, static_cast<int>(candidates.size()), candidates);\n'
              '    s->stat_cand_spatial_first_pairs += cand_spatial;\n'
              '    s->stat_cand_temporal_fallback_pairs += cand_temporal;\n'
              '    const double ratio = s->options.match_max_ratio > 0\n'
              '                             ? s->options.match_max_ratio\n'
              '                             : 0.7;\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;  // colmap defaults\n'
              '    const double t_match0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T2 2026-07-26] Five-way split of the (historically monolithic)\n'
              '    // match= wall: GPU pairing / TVG+db writes / official triangulation /\n'
              '    // windowed local BA / everything else. Pure observation, emitted as one\n'
              '    // frame_split jsonl line per frame — the serious-state 3.6x match=\n'
              '    // inflation was unattributable without it.\n'
              '    double t2_gpu_ms = 0.0, t2_tvg_ms = 0.0, t2_tri_ms = 0.0, t2_lba_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    // [INTERLEAVED-AB 2026-08-08] 本帧属于哪一臂:帧号 / 周期 的奇偶。\n'
              '    const int ab_phase =\n'
              '        AbPeriod() > 0 ? ((frame_id / AbPeriod()) & 1) : -1;\n'
              '    aether_match_set_ab_phase(ab_phase);\n'
              '    // [TVG-SPLIT 2026-08-08] zero the per-frame TVG RANSAC split HERE — before\n'
              '    // the candidate loop that produces it, not next to the ilr_* reset (which\n'
              '    // sits after the TVG section and silently wiped these counters).\n'
              '    aether_match_gpu_ms = aether_match_sleep_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    aether_match_chunks = 0;\n'
              '    aether_tvg_upright_ms = aether_tvg_homography_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    aether_tvg_upright_calls = aether_tvg_homography_calls = 0;\n'
              '    const bool tail_cache_observed = TailCacheTraceEnabled();\n'
              '    double t2_cache_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    int tail_cache_reused = 0;\n'
              '    uint64_t tail_cache_generation = 0;\n'
              '    int n_cand = 0, gpu_matches = 0, cpu_matches = 0;\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf;\n'
              '    // Live-preview triangulation scratch. Triangulate each NEW-frame keypoint at\n'
              '    // most once per add_frame (cheap dedup: a keypoint matched across several\n'
              '    // prev frames would otherwise emit several near-duplicate 3D points).\n'
              '    // Points created or grown in the live Reconstruction this frame → the\n'
              '    // variable set for the windowed local BA below.\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> touched;\n'
              '    // [SCAN-MATRIX ENV ①②] creation parallax + grow reproj gates, env-tunable\n'
              '    // (OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_TRI_MIN_ANGLE degrees / OFFICIAL_AETHER_GROW_REPROJ_PX px);\n'
              '    // defaults = shipped 2.0° (T20) / 14 px.\n'
              '    const double kMinTriAngleRad = LiveCreateTriMinAngleRad();\n'
              '    const double kMaxCreateReprojPx = LiveCreateMaxReprojPx();  // new-point gate\n'
              '    const bool kSelfDevTri = SelfDevLiveTriangulationEnabled();\n'
              '    const double kMaxGrowReprojPx = LiveGrowMaxReprojPx();  // TVG-inlier grow absorbs ARKit drift\n'
              '    constexpr double kMaxMergeReprojPx = 8.0;    // stricter: irreversible track merge\n'
              '    // [MERGE-GATE 2026-07-11] OFF = COLMAP-parity merge gating (COLMAP\'s merge\n'
              '    // has no disjoint-images requirement; the union-refit reproj gate in\n'
              '    // CanMergeLivePoints subsumes the safety concern). Host attribution on the\n'
              '    // cap47 db replay: shared-image rejections 250 vs 12,892 midpoint-reproj\n'
              '    // rejections — the real starvation was the midpoint test, fixed by the\n'
              '    // union refit; disjoint-off recovers the remaining legitimate fragments.\n'
              '    constexpr bool kMergeRequireDisjointImages = false;\n'
              '    const bool gpu_match_avail =\n'
              '        s->options.use_gpu_match && (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs != nullptr);\n'
              '    // [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-07] Build the NEW frame\'s 512-row descriptor subset\n'
              '    // ONCE per add_frame (64 KB memcpy) — reused against every candidate\n'
              '    // below. Only armed when the gate is on AND the frame actually has more\n'
              '    // rows than the probe (otherwise probe == full match and the gate could\n'
              '    // only add cost). Subset order: even stride by default;\n'
              '    // OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_TOP=1 takes the first rows instead — the\n'
              '    // canonical row order is octave↓/scale↓/response↓, so "first 512" is the\n'
              '    // literal Wu (ICCV 2013) top-scale preemptive-matching subset (A/B arm).\n'
              '    const int probe_gate_min = ProbeGateMin();\n'
              '    std::vector<uint8_t> probe_desc_buf;\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> probe_pair_buf;\n'
              '    int probe_rows = 0;\n'
              '    if (probe_gate_min > 0 && rec.n_keypoints > ProbeGateRows()) {\n'
              '      probe_rows = ProbeGateRows();\n'
              '      probe_desc_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(probe_rows) * 128);\n'
              '      if (ProbeGateTopRows()) {\n'
              '        std::memcpy(probe_desc_buf.data(), rec.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '                    static_cast<size_t>(probe_rows) * 128);\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        const int probe_step = rec.n_keypoints / probe_rows;  // >= 1\n'
              '        for (int r = 0; r < probe_rows; ++r) {\n'
              '          std::memcpy(probe_desc_buf.data() + static_cast<size_t>(r) * 128,\n'
              '                      rec.descriptors.data() +\n'
              '                          static_cast<size_t>(r) * probe_step * 128,\n'
              '                      128);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      probe_pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(probe_rows) * 2);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    int frame_probe_attempted = 0, frame_probe_skipped = 0;\n'
              '    double frame_probe_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    // [PROBE-BATCH 2026-08-08] Score ALL candidates\' probes in shared GPU\n'
              '    // submissions BEFORE the match loop (the 08-07 per-pair form paid one\n'
              '    // command-buffer round trip per candidate — 3.7 ms/probe ≈ 40% of a full\n'
              '    // pair — which ate the entire gain; judged 缓 pending this rework). The\n'
              '    // scores are bit-identical to the per-pair probe calls (same kernels,\n'
              '    // same shapes — only the submission is batched), so the skip decisions\n'
              '    // match the 08-07 control arm exactly. Any batch failure fails OPEN:\n'
              '    // scores stay absent and every pair proceeds to the full match (the\n'
              '    // in-loop per-pair probe is NOT retried after a batch failure — a\n'
              '    // struggling GPU must not get K more probe dispatches).\n'
              '    std::vector<int> probe_batch_scores;  // -1 = unscored → gate inert\n'
              '    bool probe_batch_ready = false;\n'
              '    const bool probe_batch_route =\n'
              '        probe_rows > 0 && gpu_match_avail && ProbeGateBatchEnabled() &&\n'
              '        aether_gpu_match_probe_batch != nullptr;\n'
              '    if (probe_batch_route && !candidates.empty()) {\n'
              '      std::vector<const uint8_t*> pb_descs;\n'
              '      std::vector<int> pb_ns;\n'
              '      std::vector<size_t> pb_pos;\n'
              '      pb_descs.reserve(candidates.size());\n'
              '      pb_ns.reserve(candidates.size());\n'
              '      pb_pos.reserve(candidates.size());\n'
              '      for (size_t ci = 0; ci < candidates.size(); ++ci) {\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& prev = s->frames[candidates[ci]];\n'
              '        if (prev.n_keypoints > 0 && !prev.descriptors.empty()) {\n'
              '          pb_descs.push_back(prev.descriptors.data());\n'
              '          pb_ns.push_back(prev.n_keypoints);\n'
              '          pb_pos.push_back(ci);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!pb_descs.empty()) {\n'
              '        std::vector<int> pb_counts(pb_descs.size(), 0);\n'
              '        const double t_p0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        const int prc = aether_gpu_match_probe_batch(\n'
              '            probe_desc_buf.data(), probe_rows, pb_descs.data(), pb_ns.data(),\n'
              '            static_cast<int>(pb_descs.size()), ratio, pb_counts.data());\n'
              '        const double probe_ms = NowMs() - t_p0;\n'
              '        frame_probe_ms += probe_ms;\n'
              '        s->stat_probe_ms += probe_ms;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_probe_batch_calls;\n'
              '        s->stat_probe_attempted += static_cast<int64_t>(pb_descs.size());\n'
              '        frame_probe_attempted += static_cast<int>(pb_descs.size());\n'
              '        if (prc == 0) {\n'
              '          probe_batch_scores.assign(candidates.size(), -1);\n'
              '          for (size_t k = 0; k < pb_pos.size(); ++k) {\n'
              '            probe_batch_scores[pb_pos[k]] = pb_counts[k];\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          probe_batch_ready = true;\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          s->stat_probe_fail_open += static_cast<int64_t>(pb_descs.size());\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::unordered_set<PointIdPair, PointIdPairHash> merge_trials;\n'
              '    // [MATCH-TVG-OVERLAP 2026-08-08] Prefetch the candidates\' GPU matches on a\n'
              '    // producer thread so the consumer below can verify pair i while pair i+1 is\n'
              '    // still on the GPU. Bounded depth keeps the lookahead memory small and stops\n'
              '    // the producer from running arbitrarily far ahead of a stalled consumer.\n'
              '    // Disarmed (the default) nothing is spawned and the loop matches inline\n'
              '    // exactly as before — byte-identical by construction.\n'
              '    struct OvMatchResult {\n'
              '      int mrc = 1;\n'
              '      int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '      bool used_gpu = false;\n'
              '      std::vector<uint32_t> pairs;\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    const bool ov_active = MatchTvgOverlapEnabled() && gpu_match_avail &&\n'
              '                           probe_gate_min <= 0 && !EpiPriorMatchEnabled() &&\n'
              '                           candidates.size() > 1;\n'
              '    std::mutex ov_mu;\n'
              '    std::condition_variable ov_cv;\n'
              '    std::deque<OvMatchResult> ov_q;\n'
              '    bool ov_abort = false;\n'
              '    constexpr size_t kOvDepth = 3;\n'
              '    std::thread ov_producer;\n'
              '    if (ov_active) {\n'
              '      ov_producer = std::thread([&] {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        for (size_t k = 0; k < candidates.size(); ++k) {\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(ov_mu);\n'
              '            ov_cv.wait(lk, [&] { return ov_q.size() < kOvDepth || ov_abort; });\n'
              '            if (ov_abort) break;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const FrameRecord& pf = s->frames[candidates[k]];\n'
              '          OvMatchResult r;\n'
              '          const int pcap = pf.n_keypoints < rec.n_keypoints ? pf.n_keypoints\n'
              '                                                            : rec.n_keypoints;\n'
              '          if (pcap > 0 && !pf.descriptors.empty()) {\n'
              '            r.pairs.resize(static_cast<size_t>(pcap) * 2);\n'
              '            r.mrc = GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                s, pf.frame_id, pf.descriptors.data(), pf.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                rec.frame_id, rec.descriptors.data(), rec.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                r.pairs.data(), pcap, &r.num_matches);\n'
              '            r.used_gpu = (r.mrc == 0);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(ov_mu);\n'
              '            ov_q.push_back(std::move(r));\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          ov_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    struct OvJoin {\n'
              '      std::thread* th;\n'
              '      std::mutex* mu;\n'
              '      std::condition_variable* cv;\n'
              '      bool* abort_flag;\n'
              '      ~OvJoin() {\n'
              '        if (th && th->joinable()) {\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(*mu);\n'
              '            *abort_flag = true;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          cv->notify_all();\n'
              '          th->join();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } ov_join{ov_active ? &ov_producer : nullptr, &ov_mu, &ov_cv, &ov_abort};\n'
              '    for (size_t cand_i = 0; cand_i < candidates.size(); ++cand_i) {\n'
              '      const int j = candidates[cand_i];\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& prev = s->frames[j];\n'
              '      ++n_cand;\n'
              '      s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] match 调用前打点\n'
              '      // [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-07] Cheap 512×n probe first: score = mutual\n'
              '      // cross-checked match count of the subset at the production ratio. A\n'
              '      // score below the threshold predicts (AUC 0.95) the full pair would die\n'
              '      // in TVG anyway → skip the full GEMM + TVG + db write. Fail-open on any\n'
              '      // probe error: the pair proceeds exactly as without the gate.\n'
              '      // [PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] Skipped pairs are REGISTERED in the debt\n'
              '      // ledger (and stay unmarked in live_pairs_done): idle repay drains the\n'
              '      // ledger opportunistically and finalize re-match attempts every\n'
              '      // remaining entry, so delivery is lossless by construction (user hard\n'
              '      // line) — not merely "recoverable when a frame happens to look\n'
              '      // starved", which missed healthy-frame and spatial/loop skips.\n'
              '      if (probe_rows > 0 && prev.n_keypoints > 0 &&\n'
              '          !prev.descriptors.empty()) {\n'
              '        if (probe_batch_ready) {\n'
              '          const int probe_score = probe_batch_scores[cand_i];\n'
              '          if (probe_score >= 0) {\n'
              '            if (probe_score < probe_gate_min) {\n'
              '              ++s->stat_probe_skipped;\n'
              '              ++frame_probe_skipped;\n'
              '              if (s->probe_skipped_pairs\n'
              '                      .insert(FramePairKey(frame_id, j))\n'
              '                      .second) {\n'
              '                ++s->stat_probe_debt_registered;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              continue;  // predicted-dead pair: full match skipped\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            ++s->stat_probe_passed;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        } else if (!probe_batch_route) {\n'
              '          // [PROBE-BATCH 2026-08-08] Per-pair probe form — the 08-07\n'
              '          // shipped shape, kept verbatim as the A/B control arm\n'
              '          // (OFFICIAL_AETHER_PROBE_GATE_BATCH=0) and as the CPU-matcher\n'
              '          // route (batching only removes GPU dispatch overhead).\n'
              '          const double t_p0 = NowMs();\n'
              '          int probe_matches = 0;\n'
              '          // No rc=7 backoff for the probe (a hot-GPU transient must not add\n'
              '          // 150 ms to a gate whose whole point is saving time) and no\n'
              '          // descriptor-residency route (the resident matrix is the FULL\n'
              '          // frame, not this subset) — hence the direct entry, not\n'
              '          // GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry.\n'
              '          const int prc =\n'
              '              gpu_match_avail\n'
              '                  ? aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs(\n'
              '                        probe_desc_buf.data(), probe_rows,\n'
              '                        prev.descriptors.data(), prev.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                        probe_pair_buf.data(), probe_rows, &probe_matches)\n'
              '                  : aether_sift_match_pairs(\n'
              '                        probe_desc_buf.data(), probe_rows,\n'
              '                        prev.descriptors.data(), prev.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                        probe_pair_buf.data(), probe_rows, &probe_matches);\n'
              '          const double probe_ms = NowMs() - t_p0;\n'
              '          frame_probe_ms += probe_ms;\n'
              '          s->stat_probe_ms += probe_ms;\n'
              '          ++s->stat_probe_attempted;\n'
              '          ++frame_probe_attempted;\n'
              '          if (prc == 0) {\n'
              '            if (probe_matches < probe_gate_min) {\n'
              '              ++s->stat_probe_skipped;\n'
              '              ++frame_probe_skipped;\n'
              '              if (s->probe_skipped_pairs\n'
              '                      .insert(FramePairKey(frame_id, j))\n'
              '                      .second) {\n'
              '                ++s->stat_probe_debt_registered;\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              continue;  // predicted-dead pair: full match skipped\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            ++s->stat_probe_passed;\n'
              '          } else {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_probe_fail_open;  // probe unavailable → gate inert\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // probe_batch_route && !probe_batch_ready → batch failed: gate\n'
              '        // inert for this frame (fail-open, already counted above).\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Cross-checked matches are unique per left index → min(n1,n2) bounds.\n'
              '      const int cap = prev.n_keypoints < rec.n_keypoints ? prev.n_keypoints\n'
              '                                                         : rec.n_keypoints;\n'
              '      pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '      int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '      // GPU tiled-GEMM first when enabled+linked (mutual cross-check inside).\n'
              '      // On device, a Metal failure used to fall back to the CPU brute-force\n'
              '      // matcher and could stall the streaming queue for minutes. If the GPU\n'
              '      // symbol is present but this pair fails, skip the pair; CPU remains the\n'
              '      // host/no-Metal fallback when the weak GPU symbol is absent.\n'
              '      int mrc = 1;\n'
              '      bool used_gpu = false;\n'
              '      // [MATCH-TVG-OVERLAP 2026-08-08] Armed: take the prefetched result instead\n'
              '      // of matching inline. The queue is filled in candidate order, so popping\n'
