              '      // in order keeps every downstream side effect (fail-streak accounting,\n'
              '      // db writes, TVG PRNG stream) in exactly the serial sequence.\n'
              '      bool ov_consumed = false;\n'
              '      if (ov_active) {\n'
              '        OvMatchResult r;\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(ov_mu);\n'
              '          ov_cv.wait(lk, [&] { return !ov_q.empty(); });\n'
              '          r = std::move(ov_q.front());\n'
              '          ov_q.pop_front();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ov_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '        ov_consumed = true;\n'
              '        mrc = r.mrc;\n'
              '        num_matches = r.num_matches;\n'
              '        used_gpu = r.used_gpu;\n'
              '        if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '          NoteGpuMatchFailure(s, mrc, j, frame_id);\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        s->gpu_match_fail_streak = 0;\n'
              '        ++s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7;\n'
              '        if (num_matches > 0) {\n'
              '          pair_buf.assign(r.pairs.begin(),\n'
              '                          r.pairs.begin() + static_cast<size_t>(num_matches) * 2);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // [EPI-PRIOR 2026-07-27] Experiment arm (default OFF): ARKit epipolar\n'
              '      // band first; any failure/collapse falls through to the unchanged\n'
              '      // full-GEMM path below.\n'
              '      if (!ov_consumed && EpiPriorMatchEnabled() && gpu_match_avail) {\n'
              '        const double t2_m0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        const int gap = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) - j;\n'
              '        const bool ok = ArkitGuidedMatchPair(s, prev, rec, gap, ratio,\n'
              '                                             pair_buf.data(), cap,\n'
              '                                             &num_matches);\n'
              '        t2_gpu_ms += NowMs() - t2_m0;\n'
              '        if (ok) {\n'
              '          mrc = 0;\n'
              '          used_gpu = true;\n'
              '          s->gpu_match_fail_streak = 0;\n'
              '          ++s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!ov_consumed && mrc != 0 && gpu_match_avail) {\n'
              '        // [P1-RC7-RETRY] transient rc=7 gets two backoff retries in-line\n'
              '        // (worst case +~150 ms on a dead pair, bounded) before the pair\n'
              '        // fails closed into the repay/finalize debt.\n'
              '        const double t2_m0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        mrc = GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '            s, prev.frame_id, prev.descriptors.data(), prev.n_keypoints,\n'
              '            rec.frame_id, rec.descriptors.data(), rec.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '            pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '        t2_gpu_ms += NowMs() - t2_m0;\n'
              '        if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '          // [MATCH-FAIL TELEMETRY 2026-07-11] Same fail-closed skip as before\n'
              '          // (no CPU fallback on device — a Metal failure used to stall the\n'
              '          // queue for minutes), but no longer SILENT: rc-bucketed counters +\n'
              '          // segment warning make a thermal matcher collapse visible, and the\n'
              '          // idle-repay + finalize re-match passes repair the db afterwards.\n'
              '          NoteGpuMatchFailure(s, mrc, j, frame_id);\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        s->gpu_match_fail_streak = 0;  // healthy pair ends a failure segment\n'
              '        ++s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7;      // [P1-REPAY-THERMAL2] clean history\n'
              '        used_gpu = true;\n'
              '      } else if (!ov_consumed && !gpu_match_avail) {\n'
              '        const double t2_m0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        mrc = aether_sift_match_pairs(\n'
              '            prev.descriptors.data(), prev.n_keypoints, rec.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '            rec.n_keypoints, ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '        t2_gpu_ms += NowMs() - t2_m0;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] match 调用后打点\n'
              '      if (mrc != 0) continue;\n'
              '      // [POSE-DIRECT-E 2026-08-11 定位修复] 原始匹配为 0 的配对以前在这一行\n'
              '      // 直接丢弃 —— 而这恰恰是最需要自举的"饿死对":真机 cap_1786414194441541\n'
              '      // 的 frame_000041 有 12 个候选,9 个死在这里,根本没机会进引导链。\n'
              '      // 开启第二级且两帧都有 ARKit 位姿时放行;进链后救不回来仍旧丢弃,\n'
              '      // 下游任何门都不放宽。第二级关闭时行为逐字节不变。\n'
              '      const bool zero_raw_matches = num_matches <= 0;\n'
              '      if (zero_raw_matches &&\n'
              '          !(PoseDirectEEnabled() && prev.has_pose && rec.has_pose)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (num_matches > 0) {\n'
              '        if (used_gpu) gpu_matches += num_matches; else cpu_matches += num_matches;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      const double t2_v0 = NowMs();  // [AETHER-T2] TVG + db-write section\n'
              '      colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '      for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      colmap::TwoViewGeometry two_view_geometry;\n'
              '      // [GUIDED-TEMPORAL 2026-07-12] Weak-texture recall lever, DEFAULT OFF.\n'
              '      // These frames carry ARKit poses (rec.has_pose / prev.has_pose set above),\n'
              '      // so the pair has an epipolar prior; but rather than derive E from the\n'
              '      // (drift-prone) ARKit poses, we reuse the exact chain the spatial-\n'
              '      // enrichment path already ships and verifies: estimate the seed geometry\n'
              '      // from the raw cross-checked matches, then re-match INSIDE that E/F band\n'
              '      // at the relaxed ratio (0.8, vs the temporal 0.7). Epipolar\n'
              '      // disambiguation rescues the repetitive/low-texture correspondences the\n'
              '      // plain Lowe ratio judges to death (2nd-NN ≈ 1st-NN).\n'
              '      // RED LINE: guided candidates are NOT trusted. They pass a fresh TVG\n'
              '      // RANSAC and are adopted ONLY when their inlier count STRICTLY exceeds the\n'
              '      // raw pair\'s, so nothing downstream is relaxed — the same TVG geometry,\n'
              '      // tri-angle (2.0°), reproj, ghost L1/L2, and finalize min_num_matches=15\n'
              '      // gates run unchanged, and view-dependent reflection matches (appearance\n'
              '      // wrong, epipolar can\'t fix) still fall exactly as before.\n'
              '      // [GUIDED-CHAIN 2026-08-11] 两级回退链。两个旋钮都不设 ⇒ 走 else,逐字节\n'
              '      // 等同出货二进制。第一级=原始匹配自估种子(07-12 已实现,够不着饿死对);\n'
              '      // 第二级=ARKit 位姿自举(08-11,专治饿死对)。\n'
              '      if ((GuidedTemporalEnabled() || PoseDirectEEnabled()) && gpu_match_avail &&\n'
              '          aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs_guided != nullptr) {\n'
              '        bool raw_seed_ok = false;\n'
              '        if (!zero_raw_matches) {\n'
              '          const auto initial_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '              prev, prev.points, rec, rec.points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '          raw_seed_ok = MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(initial_tvg);\n'
              '          if (raw_seed_ok) two_view_geometry = initial_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        bool pair_ok = raw_seed_ok;\n'
              '\n'
              '        // ── 第一级:原始匹配估得出几何 ⇒ 在自己的 E/F 精带内放宽比值重配 ──\n'
              '        if (raw_seed_ok && GuidedTemporalEnabled()) {\n'
              '          colmap::FeatureMatches guided;\n'
              '          if (RunGuidedMatch(s, prev, rec, two_view_geometry, &guided) &&\n'
              '              !guided.empty()) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_guided_temporal_attempted;\n'
              '            const auto guided_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '                prev, prev.points, rec, rec.points, guided, tvg_options);\n'
              '            if (MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(guided_tvg) &&\n'
              '                guided_tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size() >\n'
              '                two_view_geometry.inlier_matches.size()) {\n'
              '              ++s->stat_guided_temporal_upgraded;\n'
              '              s->stat_guided_temporal_extra_inliers +=\n'
              '                  static_cast<int64_t>(\n'
              '                      guided_tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size()) -\n'
              '                  static_cast<int64_t>(two_view_geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '              matches = std::move(guided);\n'
              '              num_matches = static_cast<int>(matches.size());\n'
              '              two_view_geometry = guided_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        // ── 第二级:原始匹配饿死(第一级够不着)⇒ ARKit 位姿自举 ──\n'
              '        // (a) 宽带(128px,实测容纳 100.000% 真对应)只为拿到足够候选让 RANSAC\n'
              '        //     反解几何;(b) 再用这个数据自证的 E 走标准 4px 精带做最终重配,\n'
              '        //     消歧能力回到出货口径。两步的产物都必须过 mandatory-gravity TVG。\n'
              '        if (!raw_seed_ok && PoseDirectEEnabled()) {\n'
              '          colmap::TwoViewGeometry seed;\n'
              '          if (BuildPoseDirectGuideGeometry(prev, rec, &seed)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_pose_direct_attempted;\n'
              '            colmap::FeatureMatches boot;\n'
              '            if (RunGuidedMatch(s, prev, rec, seed, &boot,\n'
              '                               PoseDirectBandPixels()) &&\n'
              '                !boot.empty()) {\n'
              '              const auto boot_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '                  prev, prev.points, rec, rec.points, boot, tvg_options);\n'
              '              if (MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(boot_tvg)) {\n'
              '                colmap::FeatureMatches refined;\n'
              '                bool took_refined = false;\n'
              '                if (RunGuidedMatch(s, prev, rec, boot_tvg.geometry, &refined) &&\n'
              '                    !refined.empty()) {\n'
              '                  const auto refined_tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '                      prev, prev.points, rec, rec.points, refined, tvg_options);\n'
              '                  if (MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(refined_tvg) &&\n'
              '                      refined_tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size() >\n'
              '                          boot_tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size()) {\n'
              '                    matches = std::move(refined);\n'
              '                    two_view_geometry = refined_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '                    took_refined = true;\n'
              '                  }\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                if (!took_refined) {\n'
              '                  matches = std::move(boot);\n'
              '                  two_view_geometry = boot_tvg.geometry;\n'
              '                }\n'
              '                num_matches = static_cast<int>(matches.size());\n'
              '                pair_ok = true;\n'
              '                ++s->stat_pose_direct_rescued;\n'
              '                s->stat_pose_direct_inliers += static_cast<int64_t>(\n'
              '                    two_view_geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        // 两级都没救回 ⇒ 与出货行为一致,丢弃该对(不放宽任何下游门)。\n'
              '        if (!pair_ok) continue;\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        if (zero_raw_matches) continue;  // 出货路径:与既有行为一致\n'
              '        const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '            prev, prev.points, rec, rec.points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '        if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '        two_view_geometry = tvg.geometry;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const bool tail_pair_first_write =\n'
              '          TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, prev.image_id, image_id);\n'
              '      s->db->WriteMatches(prev.image_id, image_id, matches);\n'
              '      s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(prev.image_id, image_id, two_view_geometry);\n'
              '      TailCacheCommitPair(s, prev.image_id, image_id, two_view_geometry,\n'
              '                          tail_pair_first_write);\n'
              '      // A pair becomes resolved only after an upright-valid/planar TVG is\n'
              '      // persisted. Pending geometry stays eligible for idle/finalize retry.\n'
              '      s->live_pairs_done.insert(FramePairKey(frame_id, j));\n'
              '      t2_tvg_ms += NowMs() - t2_v0;\n'
              '      // [P1-LIVE-REPAY] Per-frame valid-window-pair counters, mirroring the\n'
              '      // FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames win_valid rule (gap <= production K,\n'
              '      // TVG inliers >= gate) so the idle repay pass can detect starvation\n'
              '      // without a db scan.\n'
              '      {\n'
              '        if (static_cast<int>(s->live_win_valid.size()) <= frame_id) {\n'
              '          s->live_win_valid.resize(frame_id + 1, 0);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const int window_k =\n'
              '            s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12;\n'
              '        if (frame_id - j <= window_k &&\n'
              '            static_cast<int>(two_view_geometry.inlier_matches.size()) >=\n'
              '                kRematchValidInlierGate) {\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[j];\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[frame_id];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      // ── Incremental track growth into the live Reconstruction ──\n'
              '      // [LIVE-GROW-EXTRACT 2026-08-08] Body moved verbatim to\n'
              '      // GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers so the finalize-time probe-debt replay\n'
              '      // (ApplyProbeDebtGrowth) authors points through the IDENTICAL gates\n'
              '      // instead of a second, drifting triangulator. kSelfDevTri (②d) and the\n'
              '      // stat_tvg_inlier_pairs accounting stay here, where they always were.\n'
              '      if (rec.has_pose && prev.has_pose && s->live_recon_ready) {\n'
              '        const colmap::FeatureMatches& inliers =\n'
              '            two_view_geometry.inlier_matches;\n'
              '        s->stat_tvg_inlier_pairs += static_cast<int>(inliers.size());\n'
              '        if (kSelfDevTri) {\n'
              '          const LiveGrowView v_prev{prev.image_id, &prev.points, &prev.camera,\n'
              '                                    prev.cam_from_world};\n'
              '          const LiveGrowView v_cur{image_id, &rec.points, &rec.camera,\n'
              '                                   rec.cam_from_world};\n'
              '          GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers(\n'
              '              s, s->live_recon.get(), v_prev, v_cur, inliers, kMinTriAngleRad,\n'
              '              kMaxCreateReprojPx, kMaxGrowReprojPx, kMaxMergeReprojPx,\n'
              '              kMergeRequireDisjointImages, /*allow_merge=*/true,\n'
              '              /*allow_grow=*/true, &merge_trials, &touched);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ── Official COLMAP local refinement ───────────────────────────────────\n'
              '    // Run the upstream mapper\'s two-round local refinement. It selects the six\n'
              '    // most-connected images (not the six most recent frames), completes/merges\n'
              '    // tracks, filters observations, and keeps every per-image ARKit PINHOLE\n'
              '    // intrinsic fixed while optimizing poses and 3D points.\n'
              '    // [PAIR-CLOUD 2026-08-09 用户签"为什么第2张不出云"] 这道门原本是为局部 BA\n'
              '    // 设的(两帧窗口没什么可精化),但生产的唯一建点入口 TriangulateImage 也被\n'
              '    // 圈在了门内 —— 于是第 2 张照片匹配/TVG/位姿全就绪(实测 1,805 匹配)却\n'
              '    // 一个点不建,AR 到第 3 张才突然出云。两视图三角化是完全成立的(用户删掉\n'
              '    // 第 3 张后幸存的 721 个 2-view 支撑点就是活证,且生产本就允许 2-view\n'
              '    // track:TRI_IGNORE_2VIEW=0)。拆门:块从 >=2 进入(建点),局部精化仍\n'
              '    // 只在 >=3 跑(块内二次门,原行为逐位保留)。回退:OFFICIAL_AETHER_PAIR_CLOUD=0。\n'
              '    const bool pair_cloud_enabled = [] {\n'
              '      static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '        const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_PAIR_CLOUD");\n'
              '        // [2026-08-09 host 4 对定案] 默认关。拆门(三角化>=2)机制上成立\n'
              '        // (第 2 帧出 1,293 点),但**入模型**的早建 2-view 点改变后续演化:\n'
              '        // 交付 -1.78%、live 终态 -3.1%,四跑零散布=确定性代价,违反双重无损。\n'
              '        // 正确形态是"显示层临时配对云"(克隆模型建点只供预览,不碰真模型,\n'
              '        // 第 3 帧起逐位回到旧行为)——待实现。此 env 仅留作实验臂。\n'
              '        return e && e[0] == \'1\' && e[1] == \'\\0\';\n'
              '      }();\n'
              '      return cached;\n'
              '    }();\n'
              '    const size_t live_reg_n =\n'
              '        (s->live_recon_ready && s->live_recon) ? s->live_recon->NumRegImages()\n'
              '                                               : 0;\n'
              '    // [PAIR-DRAFT] 每个被接受的帧都先作废旧草稿;仅当本帧后恰为两图时在下方\n'
              '    // 重建。第 3 帧起这里清一次即永久为空,previewTracked 兜底自动退位。\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_points.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs.clear();\n'
              '    bool live_local_ba_attempted = false;\n'
              '    bool live_local_ba_succeeded = false;\n'
              '    if (rec.has_pose && s->live_recon_ready &&\n'
              '        live_reg_n >= (pair_cloud_enabled ? 2u : 3u) &&\n'
              '        StreamingLocalBaEnabled() &&\n'
              '        (frame_id % StreamingLocalBaPeriod(s->ba_every_n)) == 0) {\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        colmap::DatabaseCache::Options cache_options;\n'
              '        cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '        cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '        const double t2_cache0 = tail_cache_observed ? NowMs() : 0.0;\n'
              '        std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> cache;\n'
              '        std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> tail_shadow_cache;\n'
              '        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> tail_cache_use_lock;\n'
              '        if (TailCacheMode() == TailCacheModeV1::kOn) {\n'
              '          tail_cache_use_lock =\n'
              '              std::unique_lock<std::mutex>(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '          if (!s->tail_cache_master || !s->tail_cache_epoch.readable()) {\n'
              '            if (s->tail_cache_epoch.state() !=\n'
              '                aether::sfm::TailCacheEpochStateV1::kDirty) {\n'
              '              TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '                  s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            TailCacheRebuildLocked(s);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          if (s->tail_cache_master && s->tail_cache_epoch.readable()) {\n'
              '            cache = s->tail_cache_master;\n'
              '            tail_cache_reused = 1;\n'
              '            tail_cache_generation = s->tail_cache_epoch.generation();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!cache) {\n'
              '          cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, cache_options);\n'
              '          if (TailCacheMode() == TailCacheModeV1::kShadow) {\n'
              '            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> shadow_lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '            if (!s->tail_cache_master || !s->tail_cache_epoch.readable()) {\n'
              '              if (s->tail_cache_epoch.state() !=\n'
              '                  aether::sfm::TailCacheEpochStateV1::kDirty) {\n'
              '                TailCacheMarkDirtyLockedV1(\n'
              '                    s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '              }\n'
              '              TailCacheRebuildLocked(s);\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            tail_cache_generation = s->tail_cache_epoch.generation();\n'
              '            if (s->tail_cache_master && s->tail_cache_epoch.readable()) {\n'
              '              tail_shadow_cache = s->tail_cache_master;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (tail_cache_observed) t2_cache_ms += NowMs() - t2_cache0;\n'
              '\n'
              '        colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions official_options;\n'
              '        official_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '        official_options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks =\n'
              '            TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '        official_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '        official_options.ba_refine_focal_length = false;\n'
              '        official_options.ba_refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '        official_options.ba_refine_extra_params = false;\n'
              '        official_options.mapper.ba_local_num_images =\n'
              '            s->ba_window > 0 ? s->ba_window : 6;\n'
              '        // [LIVE-LBA-THREADS 2026-07-29 SHIPPED] Host A/B on cap7_day, four arms,\n'
              '        // all four BYTE-IDENTICAL in geometry (n_points 141758 / track3plus\n'
              '        // 53239 / n_obs 411770 / mean_reproj 1.0553 on every arm — thread count\n'
              '        // is provably lossless here, matching the finalize-side precedent):\n'
              '        //   all-single (two independent routes)  35.8s  ← noise band 0.4%\n'
              '        //   shipped default (floor 50k, -1)      34.9s  -2.5%\n'
              '        //   LiveBaThreads()==4 + floor 6000      26.4s  -26.2%   ← ship\n'
              '        //   all cores (host 12) + floor 6000     31.0s  -13.4%\n'
              '        // ⚠️ MORE THREADS IS WORSE: 12 gives back half the gain to sync\n'
              '        // overhead on a 6-image window. Do not "improve" this to -1.\n'
              '        // The 4 that won on host is also exactly LiveBaThreads() on an A16\n'
              '        // (min(6, hw-2)), i.e. the same value that deliberately reserves two\n'
              '        // cores for the camera/GPU-matcher/UI mid-capture — the speed optimum\n'
              '        // and the thermal/camera-safety choice coincide. Device must still\n'
              '        // confirm the optimum on 6 cores; env overrides both knobs.\n'
              '        official_options.num_threads = LiveBaThreads();\n'
              '        official_options.ba_min_num_residuals_for_cpu_multi_threading = 6000;\n'
              '        if (const int t = LiveLocalBaThreadsOverride(); t > 0) {\n'
              '          official_options.num_threads = t;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (const int f = LiveLocalBaMtFloorOverride(); f >= 0) {\n'
              '          official_options.ba_min_num_residuals_for_cpu_multi_threading = f;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ApplyBirthGateAB(&official_options);  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07]\n'
              '        // [LOCAL-FTOL-AB 2026-08-08] COLMAP ships ba_local_function_tolerance =\n'
              '        // 0.0 (controllers/incremental_pipeline.h:115) and LocalBundleAdjustment()\n'
              '        // hands it straight to Ceres\' solver_options.function_tolerance\n'
              '        // (incremental_pipeline.cc:171). A ZERO function tolerance means Ceres can\n'
              '        // never terminate on "the objective stopped improving" — only on\n'
              '        // gradient_tolerance (10.0 here) or max_num_iterations (15 here). cap201:\n'
              '        // 198 of 398 live solves (49.7%) end in NO_CONVERGENCE, i.e. they burn the\n'
              '        // iteration cap. The identical defect on the GLOBAL side was worth -30%\n'
              '        // wall at +-0.003 reproj (2026-06-24: ba_global_function_tolerance 0 ->\n'
              '        // 1e-6, shipped). This arms the same knob locally for an A/B; unset or 0\n'
              '        // == byte-identical to the shipped path.\n'
              '        official_options.ba_local_function_tolerance = LiveLocalBaFtol();\n'
              '        // [2026-08-10 三臂 A/B,见各自旋钮注释] 未设 env 时以下三行都不碰\n'
              '        // 字段 == 逐字节出货路径。\n'
              '        if (const int lt = LiveLocalBaLossType(); lt >= 0) {\n'
              '          official_options.ba_local_loss_type = lt;\n'
              '          official_options.ba_local_loss_scale = 1.0;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (const int mi = LiveLocalBaMaxIter(); mi > 0) {\n'
              '          official_options.ba_local_max_num_iterations = mi;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (const double ma = LiveTriMinAngleDegOverride(); ma > 0.0) {\n'
              '          official_options.triangulation.min_angle = ma;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache);\n'
              '        mapper.BeginReconstruction(s->live_recon);\n'
              '        // [OFFICIAL-TRIANGULATE 2026-07-26] Upstream calls TriangulateImage on\n'
              '        // a freshly registered image BEFORE the local refinement\n'
              '        // (controllers/incremental_pipeline.cc:817). We never have: this route\n'
              '        // registers via AddImageWithTrivialFrame instead of RegisterNextImage,\n'
              '        // so the official point-CREATION entry was simply never reached and the\n'
              '        // hand-written create/grow/merge filled that hole. The triangulator does\n'
              '        // not care where a pose came from — it triangulates this image\'s\n'
              '        // observations against the already-registered images using whatever\n'
              '        // poses the model holds, which here are ARKit\'s. Env-gated so the arm is\n'
              '        // a clean single variable; unset == byte-identical to the shipped path.\n'
              '        if (OfficialTriangulateImageEnabled()) {\n'
              '          const double t2_t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '          const size_t n_off =\n'
              '              mapper.TriangulateImage(official_options.Triangulation(), image_id);\n'
              '          s->stat_official_tri_added += static_cast<int64_t>(n_off);\n'
              '          t2_tri_ms += NowMs() - t2_t0;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // [TAIL-CACHE-FIRST V1] In shadow mode, run the mutable cache on an\n'
              '        // isolated Reconstruction copy and a dedicated thread before the\n'
              '        // authoritative fresh-cache refinement. The copy prevents output\n'
              '        // mutation; the dedicated thread prevents its thread_local PRNG state\n'
              '        // from advancing the production caller. Shadow is diagnostic-only and\n'
              '        // intentionally receives no speed credit.\n'
              '        LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1 tail_shadow_trace;\n'
              '        bool tail_shadow_executed = false;\n'
              '        bool tail_shadow_failed = false;\n'
              '        // [PAIR-CLOUD] 局部精化(含 shadow 对拍)保持原有的 >=3 门;N==2 时本块\n'
              '        // 只做上面的 TriangulateImage 建点,直接走 EndReconstruction。\n'
              '        if (live_reg_n >= 3 && tail_shadow_cache) {\n'
              '          auto tail_shadow_recon =\n'
              '              std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>(*s->live_recon);\n'
              '          ResetLocalBundleTraceV1();\n'
              '          std::thread shadow_thread([&] {\n'
              '            try {\n'
              '              colmap::IncrementalMapper shadow_mapper(tail_shadow_cache);\n'
              '              shadow_mapper.BeginReconstruction(tail_shadow_recon);\n'
              '              shadow_mapper.IterativeLocalRefinement(\n'
              '                  official_options.ba_local_max_refinements,\n'
              '                  official_options.ba_local_max_refinement_change,\n'
              '                  official_options.Mapper(),\n'
              '                  official_options.LocalBundleAdjustment(),\n'
              '                  official_options.Triangulation(), image_id);\n'
              '              shadow_mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '              tail_shadow_executed = true;\n'
              '            } catch (...) {\n'
              '              tail_shadow_failed = true;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          });\n'
              '          shadow_thread.join();\n'
              '          tail_shadow_trace = CaptureLocalBundleTraceV1();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        // [AETHER-T2 2026-07-29] Zero the local-path counters for THIS frame,\n'
              '        // then drain them into the frame_split record below. Pure observation.\n'
              '        aether_ilr_find_ms = aether_ilr_setup_ms = aether_ilr_solve_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_merge_ms = aether_ilr_filter_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_preproc_ms = aether_ilr_minim_ms = aether_ilr_postproc_ms =\n'
              '            0.0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_jac_ms = aether_ilr_lin_ms = aether_ilr_resid_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_num_resid = aether_ilr_num_param = 0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_rounds = aether_ilr_solves = aether_ilr_iters = 0;\n'
              '        aether_ilr_term_conv = aether_ilr_term_nocnv = aether_ilr_term_other =\n'
              '            0;\n'
              '        ResetLocalBundleTraceV1();\n'
              '        const double t2_l0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        if (live_reg_n >= 3) {\n'
              '          live_local_ba_attempted = true;\n'
              '          mapper.IterativeLocalRefinement(\n'
              '              official_options.ba_local_max_refinements,\n'
              '              official_options.ba_local_max_refinement_change,\n'
              '              official_options.Mapper(),\n'
              '              official_options.LocalBundleAdjustment(),\n'
              '              official_options.Triangulation(), image_id);\n'
              '          live_local_ba_succeeded = true;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        t2_lba_ms += NowMs() - t2_l0;\n'
              '        const LocalBundleTraceSnapshotV1 fresh_trace =\n'
              '            CaptureLocalBundleTraceV1();\n'
              '        AppendTailLocalBundleTraceV1(s, frame_id, tail_cache_reused,\n'
              '                                     tail_cache_generation, "fresh",\n'
              '                                     fresh_trace);\n'
              '        if (tail_shadow_cache) {\n'
              '          AppendTailLocalBundleTraceV1(s, frame_id, /*cache_reused=*/1,\n'
              '                                       tail_cache_generation, "mutable",\n'
              '                                       tail_shadow_trace);\n'
              '          const bool exact = tail_shadow_executed && !tail_shadow_failed &&\n'
              '                             LocalBundleTraceExactV1(fresh_trace,\n'
              '                                                     tail_shadow_trace);\n'
              '          std::string compare_line =\n'
              '              "{\\"t\\":" +\n'
              '              std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '              ",\\"type\\":\\"tail_shadow_compare_v1\\",\\"fid\\":" +\n'
              '              std::to_string(frame_id) + ",\\"status\\":\\"" +\n'
              '              (exact ? "EXACT" : "INVALID_TAIL_BUNDLE_SEQUENCE") +\n'
              '              "\\",\\"fresh_calls\\":" +\n'
              '              std::to_string(fresh_trace.raw_calls) +\n'
              '              ",\\"mutable_calls\\":" +\n'
              '              std::to_string(tail_shadow_trace.raw_calls) + "}";\n'
              '          AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, compare_line);\n'
              '          if (!exact) {\n'
              '            TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '                s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kCacheInconsistency);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '      } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '        // A degenerate local bundle must not kill capture. The next accepted\n'
              '        // frame retries through the same official path.\n'
              '        TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '            s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kExceptionRetry);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // [PAIR-DRAFT 2026-08-09] 第 2 张:在真模型的克隆上跑官方 TriangulateImage,\n'
              '    // 结果只进 session 草稿区(previewTracked 兜底供 AR)。三重隔离保证逐位无损:\n'
              '    //  1) 克隆 Reconstruction —— 真模型零触碰;\n'
              '    //  2) 独立 DatabaseCache —— 不读不写 tail-cache(epoch/generation 不动);\n'
              '    //  3) 专属 std::thread —— RANSAC 的 thread_local PRNG 不推进生产线程状态\n'
              '    //     (与上面 tail-shadow 同一手法)。\n'
              '    // 失败=无草稿,拍摄不受影响。pair_cloud_enabled(实验臂)开时真模型已在\n'
              '    // 第 2 帧建点,草稿多余,跳过。\n'
              '    if (PairDraftEnabled() && !pair_cloud_enabled && rec.has_pose &&\n'
              '        s->live_recon_ready && s->live_recon && live_reg_n == 2 &&\n'
              '        StreamingLocalBaEnabled()) {\n'
              '      const double t_draft0 = NowMs();\n'
              '      bool draft_failed = false;\n'
              '      auto draft_recon =\n'
              '          std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>(*s->live_recon);\n'
              '      // ⚠️ DatabaseCache 必须在本(worker)线程建 —— SQLite 连接跨线程使用\n'
              '      // 会把 s->db 搞进 "database is locked"(host 回归 r2 实测:第 3 帧起\n'
              '      // 全场写失败只剩 4 帧)。tail-shadow 的先例同款:影子线程只消费在\n'
              '      // 主线程建好的 cache,绝不碰 db。cache 构建不消耗 RANSAC PRNG,\n'
              '      // 隔离目标(三角化的 thread_local PRNG)不受影响。\n'
              '      std::shared_ptr<colmap::DatabaseCache> draft_cache;\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        colmap::DatabaseCache::Options draft_cache_options;\n'
              '        draft_cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '        draft_cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '        draft_cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, draft_cache_options);\n'
              '      } catch (...) {\n'
              '        draft_failed = true;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!draft_failed && draft_cache) {\n'
              '        std::thread draft_thread([&] {\n'
              '          try {\n'
              '            colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions draft_options;\n'
              '            draft_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '            draft_options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks =\n'
              '                TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '            draft_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '            colmap::IncrementalMapper draft_mapper(draft_cache);\n'
              '            draft_mapper.BeginReconstruction(draft_recon);\n'
              '            draft_mapper.TriangulateImage(draft_options.Triangulation(),\n'
              '                                          image_id);\n'
              '            draft_mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '          } catch (...) {\n'
              '            draft_failed = true;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        draft_thread.join();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!draft_failed && draft_recon->NumPoints3D() > 0) {\n'
              '        const colmap::Reconstruction& dr = *draft_recon;\n'
              '        const auto& dpts = dr.Points3D();\n'
              '        std::vector<aether_sfm_point_t> pts_out;\n'
              '        std::vector<int32_t> offs_out;\n'
              '        std::vector<aether_sfm_track_obs_t> obs_out;\n'
              '        pts_out.reserve(dpts.size());\n'
              '        offs_out.reserve(dpts.size() + 1);\n'
              '        for (const auto& [pid, pt] : dpts) {\n'
              '          aether_sfm_point_t o;\n'
              '          o.x = static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.x());\n'
              '          o.y = static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.y());\n'
              '          o.z = static_cast<float>(pt.xyz.z());\n'
              '          o.r = pt.color(0);\n'
              '          o.g = pt.color(1);\n'
              '          o.b = pt.color(2);\n'
              '          o._pad[0] = o._pad[1] = 0;\n'
              '          offs_out.push_back(static_cast<int32_t>(obs_out.size()));\n'
              '          for (const auto& el : pt.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '            if (!dr.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '            const auto& xy =\n'
              '                dr.Image(el.image_id).Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy;\n'
              '            aether_sfm_track_obs_t t;\n'
              '            t.frame_id = static_cast<int32_t>(el.image_id) - 1;\n'
              '            t.x = static_cast<float>(xy.x());\n'
              '            t.y = static_cast<float>(xy.y());\n'
              '            obs_out.push_back(t);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          pts_out.push_back(o);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        offs_out.push_back(static_cast<int32_t>(obs_out.size()));\n'
              '        s->draft_pair_points.swap(pts_out);\n'
              '        s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.swap(offs_out);\n'
              '        s->draft_pair_obs.swap(obs_out);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(\n'
              '          s, "{\\"t\\":" + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '                 ",\\"type\\":\\"pair_draft\\",\\"fid\\":" + std::to_string(frame_id) +\n'
              '                 ",\\"n\\":" + std::to_string(s->draft_pair_points.size()) +\n'
              '                 ",\\"failed\\":" + std::to_string(draft_failed ? 1 : 0) +\n'
              '                 ",\\"ms\\":" + std::to_string(NowMs() - t_draft0) + "}");\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // [INCREMENTAL-GLOBAL-BA 2026-07-13] Rolling capture-time global BA over\n'
              '    // live_recon (DEFAULT OFF; OFFICIAL_AETHER_INCREMENTAL_GLOBAL_BA=1). No-op unless the\n'
              '    // env switch is on and the cadence/thermal gates pass; mutates live_recon in\n'
              '    // place, so the unconditional preview snapshot just below reflects it.\n'
              '    const int64_t incremental_refines_before = s->stat_incremental_refines;\n'
              '    MaybeIncrementalGlobalRefine(s);\n'
              '    const bool incremental_global_refine_succeeded =\n'
              '        s->stat_incremental_refines > incremental_refines_before;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Publish the BA-refined live cloud into preview_points (served unchanged by\n'
              '    // the getter). Short lock; the getter never blocks on the BA itself.\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon) {\n'
              '      std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> snap;\n'
              '      snap.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '      for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) snap.push_back(pt.xyz);\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->preview_mutex);\n'
              '      s->preview_points.swap(snap);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    s->frames.push_back(std::move(rec));\n'
              '    // The real record owns this ordinal before any observation-only work. A\n'
              '    // telemetry allocation or write failure must never add a withdrawn record\n'
              '    // or change the accepted-frame result.\n'
              '    ordinal_guard.committed = true;\n'
              '    // [LIVE-CLOUD-DIAG V1] Compare the published live model\'s optimized\n'
              '    // camera centers against each frame\'s immutable ARKit seed. Read-only:\n'
              '    // this summary is never fed back to BA, point creation, or display.\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      if (s->live_recon) {\n'
              '        std::vector<std::array<double, 3>> center_deltas;\n'
              '        center_deltas.reserve(s->frames.size());\n'
              '        int latest_compared_fid = -1;\n'
              '        for (const FrameRecord& frame : s->frames) {\n'
              '          if (!frame.has_pose || frame.image_id == 0 ||\n'
              '              !s->live_recon->ExistsImage(frame.image_id)) {\n'
              '            continue;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          const colmap::Image& image = s->live_recon->Image(frame.image_id);\n'
              '          if (!image.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector3d prior_center =\n'
              '              frame.cam_from_world.TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector3d optimized_center =\n'
              '              image.CamFromWorld().TgtOriginInSrc();\n'
              '          const Eigen::Vector3d delta = optimized_center - prior_center;\n'
              '          if (!delta.allFinite()) continue;\n'
              '          center_deltas.push_back({delta.x(), delta.y(), delta.z()});\n'
              '          latest_compared_fid = frame.frame_id;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const aether::sfm::LiveCloudBaDeltaSummaryV1 diag =\n'
              '            aether::sfm::SummarizeLiveCloudBaDeltasV1(center_deltas);\n'
              '        if (diag.valid) {\n'
              '          char dline[1152];\n'
              '          std::snprintf(\n'
              '              dline, sizeof(dline),\n'
              '              "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"ba_arkit_center_delta_v1\\","\n'
              '              "\\"contract\\":\\"PW_LIVE_CLOUD_DIAG_V1_20260810\\","\n'
              '              "\\"stage\\":\\"post_live_update\\",\\"fid\\":%d,\\"n\\":%zu,"\n'
              '              "\\"local_ba_attempted\\":%s,\\"local_ba_succeeded\\":%s,"\n'
              '              "\\"incremental_global_refine_succeeded\\":%s,"\n'
              '              "\\"median_dx_m\\":%.9g,\\"median_dy_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"median_dz_m\\":%.9g,\\"coherent_median_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"p50_m\\":%.9g,\\"p90_m\\":%.9g,\\"max_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"latest_fid\\":%d,\\"latest_dx_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"latest_dy_m\\":%.9g,\\"latest_dz_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"latest_m\\":%.9g,\\"residual_rms_m\\":%.9g,"\n'
              '              "\\"observation_only\\":true}",\n'
              '              static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), frame_id, diag.count,\n'
              '              live_local_ba_attempted ? "true" : "false",\n'
              '              live_local_ba_succeeded ? "true" : "false",\n'
              '              incremental_global_refine_succeeded ? "true" : "false",\n'
              '              diag.median_delta_m[0], diag.median_delta_m[1],\n'
              '              diag.median_delta_m[2], diag.coherent_median_norm_m,\n'
              '              diag.p50_norm_m, diag.p90_norm_m, diag.max_norm_m,\n'
              '              latest_compared_fid, diag.latest_delta_m[0],\n'
              '              diag.latest_delta_m[1], diag.latest_delta_m[2],\n'
              '              diag.latest_norm_m, diag.residual_rms_m);\n'
              '          AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, dline);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (...) {\n'
              '      // Diagnostics are fail-open by contract. In particular, allocation or\n'
              '      // sidecar-write failures cannot change accepted frames or return codes.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    AppendLiveCloudSnapshotDiagnosticsV2(s, "post_local_ba");\n'
              '    // [LIVE-POSE-DUMP 2026-08-20] HOST-bench-only evolution snapshots,\n'
              '    // env-gated (default off; the app never sets it): every K accepted\n'
              '    // frames dump the current live_recon so host schedulers can replay\n'
              '    // evolving inputs. Same-count re-dumps overwrite idempotently.\n'
              '    if (const char* dump_dir = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_POSE_DUMP");\n'
              '        dump_dir && dump_dir[0] && s->live_recon) {\n'
              '      static const int kDumpEvery = [] {\n'
              '        const char* v = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_POSE_DUMP_EVERY");\n'
              '        const int k = v ? std::atoi(v) : 0;\n'
              '        return k > 0 ? k : 0;\n'
              '      }();\n'
              '      const size_t nreg = s->live_recon->NumRegImages();\n'
              '      if (kDumpEvery > 0 && nreg > 0 &&\n'
              '          nreg % static_cast<size_t>(kDumpEvery) == 0) {\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          char sub[64];\n'
              '          std::snprintf(sub, sizeof(sub), "/live_reg%05zu", nreg);\n'
              '          const std::string snap_dir = std::string(dump_dir) + sub;\n'
              '          std::filesystem::create_directories(snap_dir);\n'
              '          s->live_recon->Write(snap_dir);\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] live evolution dump failed: "\n'
              '                       << e.what();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->last_extract_ms = extract_ms;\n'
              '    s->last_match_ms = NowMs() - t_match0;\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T2] frame_split: one compact pullable line per frame (~150 B;\n'
              '    // a 155-frame capture adds ~25 KB to the sidecar).\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      const double t2_total = s->last_match_ms;\n'
              '      char fline[1024];\n'
              '      const double* ex = s->last_extract_stages;\n'
              '      const int active_components = CountActiveComponentsV1(*s);\n'
              '      const double t2_tail_ms =\n'
              '          t2_total - t2_gpu_ms - t2_tvg_ms - t2_tri_ms - t2_lba_ms;\n'
              '      // Keep the absent-flag record byte-schema identical to the established\n'
              '      // frame_split. Cache fields are emitted only for explicit off/shadow/on\n'
              '      // experiment launches, so installing the dormant code does not enlarge\n'
              '      // every production-frame log.\n'
              '      if (tail_cache_observed) {\n'
              '        std::snprintf(\n'
              '            fline, sizeof(fline),\n'
              '            "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"frame_split\\",\\"fid\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"cand\\":%d,\\"comp\\":%d,\\"gpu_ms\\":%.0f,\\"tvg_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '            "\\"tri_ms\\":%.0f,\\"lba_ms\\":%.0f,\\"tail_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '            "\\"cache_ms\\":%.1f,\\"tail_cache\\":%d,\\"tail_gen\\":%llu,"\n'
              '            "\\"ex\\":[%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f],"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_find\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_setup\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_solve\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_merge\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_filter\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_pre\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_min\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_post\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_rounds\\":%d,\\"ilr_solves\\":%d,\\"ilr_iters\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_conv\\":%d,\\"ilr_nocnv\\":%d,\\"ilr_oth\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_jac\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_lin\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_res\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_nres\\":%lld,\\"ilr_npar\\":%lld,"\n'
              '            "\\"tvg_up\\":%.1f,\\"tvg_h\\":%.1f,\\"tvg_upn\\":%d,\\"tvg_hn\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"m_gpu\\":%.0f,\\"m_sleep\\":%.0f,\\"m_chunks\\":%d,\\"ab\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"pf\\":%d,\\"pf_wait\\":%.1f}",\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), frame_id, n_cand,\n'
              '            active_components, t2_gpu_ms,\n'
              '            t2_tvg_ms, t2_tri_ms, t2_lba_ms, t2_tail_ms, t2_cache_ms,\n'
              '            tail_cache_reused,\n'
              '            static_cast<unsigned long long>(tail_cache_generation), ex[0],\n'
              '            ex[1], ex[2], ex[3], ex[4], ex[5], ex[6], ex[7], ex[8],\n'
              '            aether_ilr_find_ms, aether_ilr_setup_ms, aether_ilr_solve_ms,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_merge_ms, aether_ilr_filter_ms, aether_ilr_preproc_ms,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_minim_ms, aether_ilr_postproc_ms, aether_ilr_rounds,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_solves, aether_ilr_iters, aether_ilr_term_conv,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_term_nocnv, aether_ilr_term_other,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_jac_ms, aether_ilr_lin_ms, aether_ilr_resid_ms,\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(aether_ilr_num_resid),\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(aether_ilr_num_param),\n'
              '            aether_tvg_upright_ms, aether_tvg_homography_ms,\n'
              '            aether_tvg_upright_calls, aether_tvg_homography_calls,\n'
              '            aether_match_gpu_ms, aether_match_sleep_ms, aether_match_chunks,\n'
              '            ab_phase, s->last_pf_state, s->last_pf_wait_ms);\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        std::snprintf(\n'
              '            fline, sizeof(fline),\n'
              '            "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"frame_split\\",\\"fid\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"cand\\":%d,\\"comp\\":%d,\\"gpu_ms\\":%.0f,\\"tvg_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '            "\\"tri_ms\\":%.0f,\\"lba_ms\\":%.0f,\\"tail_ms\\":%.0f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ex\\":[%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f],"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_find\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_setup\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_solve\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_merge\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_filter\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_pre\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_min\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_post\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_rounds\\":%d,\\"ilr_solves\\":%d,\\"ilr_iters\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_conv\\":%d,\\"ilr_nocnv\\":%d,\\"ilr_oth\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_jac\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_lin\\":%.1f,\\"ilr_res\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '            "\\"ilr_nres\\":%lld,\\"ilr_npar\\":%lld,"\n'
              '            "\\"tvg_up\\":%.1f,\\"tvg_h\\":%.1f,\\"tvg_upn\\":%d,\\"tvg_hn\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"m_gpu\\":%.0f,\\"m_sleep\\":%.0f,\\"m_chunks\\":%d,\\"ab\\":%d,"\n'
              '            "\\"pf\\":%d,\\"pf_wait\\":%.1f}",\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), frame_id, n_cand,\n'
              '            active_components, t2_gpu_ms,\n'
              '            t2_tvg_ms, t2_tri_ms, t2_lba_ms, t2_tail_ms, ex[0], ex[1], ex[2],\n'
              '            ex[3], ex[4], ex[5], ex[6], ex[7], ex[8], aether_ilr_find_ms,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_setup_ms, aether_ilr_solve_ms, aether_ilr_merge_ms,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_filter_ms, aether_ilr_preproc_ms, aether_ilr_minim_ms,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_postproc_ms, aether_ilr_rounds, aether_ilr_solves,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_iters, aether_ilr_term_conv, aether_ilr_term_nocnv,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_term_other,\n'
              '            aether_ilr_jac_ms, aether_ilr_lin_ms, aether_ilr_resid_ms,\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(aether_ilr_num_resid),\n'
              '            static_cast<long long>(aether_ilr_num_param),\n'
              '            aether_tvg_upright_ms, aether_tvg_homography_ms,\n'
              '            aether_tvg_upright_calls, aether_tvg_homography_calls,\n'
              '            aether_match_gpu_ms, aether_match_sleep_ms, aether_match_chunks,\n'
              '            ab_phase, s->last_pf_state, s->last_pf_wait_ms);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, fline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [PROBE-GATE 2026-08-07] One compact per-frame gate record, emitted only\n'
              '    // when the gate is armed so the default-off sidecar stays byte-identical.\n'
              '    if (probe_gate_min > 0) {\n'
              '      char pline[288];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(pline, sizeof(pline),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"probe_gate\\",\\"fid\\":%d,"\n'
              '                    "\\"min\\":%d,\\"att\\":%d,\\"skip\\":%d,\\"probe_ms\\":%.1f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"cum_att\\":%lld,\\"cum_skip\\":%lld,"\n'
              '                    // [PROBE-BATCH/DEBT 2026-08-08] batch-form flag + live\n'
              '                    // debt-ledger size (delivery-lossless bookkeeping).\n'
              '                    "\\"batch\\":%d,\\"debt\\":%lld}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), frame_id,\n'
              '                    probe_gate_min, frame_probe_attempted, frame_probe_skipped,\n'
              '                    frame_probe_ms,\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(s->stat_probe_attempted),\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(s->stat_probe_skipped),\n'
              '                    probe_batch_ready ? 1 : 0,\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(s->probe_skipped_pairs.size()));\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, pline);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    AppendGpuTimestampFrameRecord(s, frame_id);\n'
              '    // [IDLE-PREPAY 2026-08-07] frame-idle clock for the idle-prepay leg.\n'
              '    s->last_add_frame_done_ms = NowMs();\n'
              '    s->last_n_cand = n_cand;\n'
              '    s->last_gpu_matches = gpu_matches;\n'
              '    s->last_cpu_matches = cpu_matches;\n'
              '    const int accepted_thermal =\n'
              '        s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '    const bool accepted_thermal_valid =\n'
              '        accepted_thermal >= 0 && accepted_thermal <= 3;\n'
              '    aether_preclamp_instr_v1::FrameCounts preclamp_row;\n'
              '    if (aether_preclamp_instr_v1::FinalizeAcceptedFrame(\n'
              '            frame_id, static_cast<uint32_t>(s->frames.size()),\n'
              '            accepted_thermal_valid\n'
              '                ? aether_preclamp_instr_v1::FieldStatus::kValid\n'
              '                : aether_preclamp_instr_v1::FieldStatus::kUnavailable,\n'
              '            accepted_thermal_valid ? accepted_thermal : -1,\n'
              '            &preclamp_row)) {\n'
              '      (void)aether_preclamp_instr_v1::AppendSessionRecord(\n'
              '          &s->preclamp_instr_records, preclamp_row);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // ⛔ [SPATIAL-REVIVE 2026-08-05 已撤回] 此处曾加一段"每帧打印描述子驻留命中率"\n'
              '    // 的日志,**构造性永不执行**,已删。\n'
              '    // 根因(nm 实测):`aether_gpu_match_descriptor_residency_stats` 在出货\n'
              '    // PWOfficialSfm.framework 里是 **`t` = 本地符号,未导出** —— 按 ABI 边界\n'
              '    // 设计如此:README 明确"exactly the frozen 26-symbol public surface,\n'
              '    // **no exported self/internal symbols**",而 `pwofficial_abi_symbols.txt`\n'
              '    // 那 26 个里没有任何 `aether_gpu_match_*`。所以产品核这侧的 weak 引用恒为\n'
              '    // nullptr,日志块永远被跳过。\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 别据此以为"驻留没实现":同一份 framework 内 `nm -a` 可见 33 个驻留\n'
              '    // 符号(DescriptorResidencyPolicyV1 / MetalSessionV1 / LRU 全在)。\n'
              '    // **驻留在 framework 内部完整工作,只是统计不跨边界。**\n'
              '    // 合法的跨边界通道是 env(`OFFICIAL_AETHER_*` 配置命名空间),已在插件设\n'
              '    // `OFFICIAL_AETHER_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1=1` 启用。\n'
              '    // ⇒ 要观测驻留效果,只能看**外部效应**(每帧匹配耗时、热曲线),不能靠内部计数器。\n'
              '    if (out_frame_id) *out_frame_id = frame_id;\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kExceptionRetry);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kExceptionRetry);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [EXTRACT-PREFETCH 2026-08-08] ————————————————————————————————\n'
              '// 默认关:env 未设时不 spawn 线程、不碰任何新状态,add_frame 逐字节走老路。\n'
              'static bool ExtractPrefetchEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_EXTRACT_PREFETCH");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\' && e[1] == \'\\0\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// 便宜的内容摘要:FNV-1a over (w,h,len,首末 64B,素数步长抽样)。用途只是\n'
              '// "预取的和送来的是不是同一张图"的守卫(撤回/乱序防污染),不是密码学。\n'
              'static uint64_t PfDigestV1(const uint8_t* gray, int w, int h) {\n'
              '  uint64_t hash = UINT64_C(1469598103934665603);\n'
              '  const auto mix = [&hash](uint64_t v) {\n'
              '    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {\n'
              '      hash ^= (v >> (k * 8)) & 0xff;\n'
              '      hash *= UINT64_C(1099511628211);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  };\n'
              '  const size_t len = static_cast<size_t>(w) * static_cast<size_t>(h);\n'
              '  mix(static_cast<uint64_t>(w));\n'
              '  mix(static_cast<uint64_t>(h));\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; i < 64 && i < len; ++i) mix(gray[i]);\n'
              '  for (size_t i = len >= 64 ? len - 64 : 0; i < len; ++i) mix(gray[i]);\n'
              '  for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i += 4093) mix(gray[i]);\n'
              '  return hash;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// 提取本体:与 add_frame 原内联块逐字判等价(gpu_v2 → gpu → CPU 回退、\n'
              '// 九段计时、GPU 时间戳拉取)。跑在哪个线程由调用方决定。\n'
              'static void PfExtractInto(aether_sfm_session_t* s, const uint8_t* gray,\n'
              '                          int width, int height,\n'
              '                          aether_sfm_session_t::PfResult* out) {\n'
              '  const int max_features =\n'
              '      s->options.max_features > 0 ? s->options.max_features : 2048;\n'
              '  out->xy.assign(static_cast<size_t>(max_features) * 2, 0.0f);\n'
              '  out->desc.assign(static_cast<size_t>(max_features) * 128, 0);\n'
              '  out->scales.assign(static_cast<size_t>(max_features), 0.0f);\n'
              '  out->n = 0;\n'
              '  const bool gpu_v2_avail = s->options.use_gpu_extract &&\n'
              '                            (aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu_v2 != nullptr);\n'
              '  const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '      s->options.use_gpu_extract && (aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu != nullptr);\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  if (gpu_v2_avail) {\n'
              '    out->erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu_v2(\n'
              '        gray, width, height, max_features, /*num_threads=*/0, out->xy.data(),\n'
              '        out->desc.data(), out->scales.data(), /*out_orientations=*/nullptr,\n'
              '        max_features, &out->n);\n'
              '    out->have_scales = (out->erc == 0);\n'
              '  } else if (gpu_avail) {\n'
              '    out->erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu(gray, width, height, max_features,\n'
              '                                           /*num_threads=*/0, out->xy.data(),\n'
              '                                           out->desc.data(), max_features,\n'
              '                                           &out->n);\n'
              '    out->have_scales = false;\n'
              '  } else {\n'
              '    out->erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_v2(\n'
              '        gray, width, height, max_features, out->xy.data(), out->desc.data(),\n'
              '        out->scales.data(), /*out_orientations=*/nullptr, max_features,\n'
              '        &out->n);\n'
              '    out->have_scales = (out->erc == 0);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  out->extract_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '  for (double& v : out->stages) v = 0.0;\n'
              '  if ((gpu_v2_avail || gpu_avail) && out->erc == 0 &&\n'
              '      aether_sed_last_stages != nullptr) {\n'
              '    aether_sed_last_stages(out->stages, 9);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (gpu_v2_avail || gpu_avail) {\n'
              '    PullGpuTimestampAfterExtraction(s);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              'static void PfThreadMain(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '  pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '  for (;;) {\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t::PfJob job;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(s->pf_mu);\n'
              '      s->pf_cv.wait(lk, [&] { return s->pf_stop || s->pf_job.valid; });\n'
              '      if (s->pf_stop) return;\n'
              '      job = std::move(s->pf_job);\n'
              '      s->pf_job = {};\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t::PfResult r;\n'
              '    r.digest = job.digest;\n'
              '    r.w = job.w;\n'
              '    r.h = job.h;\n'
              '    const uint8_t* gray = job.borrow ? job.borrow : job.gray.data();\n'
              '    PfExtractInto(s, gray, job.w, job.h, &r);\n'
              '    r.valid = true;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->pf_mu);\n'
              '      s->pf_result = std::move(r);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->pf_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              'static void PfEnsureThread(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '  if (s->pf_started) return;\n'
              '  s->pf_started = true;\n'
              '  s->pf_thread = std::thread(PfThreadMain, s);\n'
              '}\n'
              '// add_frame 侧领取:命中直取 / 在算等尾 / 未命中经线程转发(保持载具单一\n'
              '// 所有权)。返回 false = 流水未启用或线程不可用 ⇒ 调用方走原内联路径。\n'
              'static bool PfAdoptOrRun(aether_sfm_session_t* s, const uint8_t* gray,\n'
              '                         int width, int height,\n'
              '                         aether_sfm_session_t::PfResult* out) {\n'
              '  s->last_pf_state = 0;\n'
              '  if (!ExtractPrefetchEnabled()) return false;\n'
              '  // [INTERLEAVED-AB] 相位 1 = 对照臂:本帧不领取,走内联原路(结果逐字节同,\n'
              '  // 只有时序差)。相位按"即将成为的帧序"算,与 Dart 侧 prefetch 调用一致。\n'
              '  if (AbPeriod() > 0 &&\n'
              '      ((static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) / AbPeriod()) & 1) == 1) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  PfEnsureThread(s);\n'
              '  if (!s->pf_thread.joinable()) return false;\n'
              '  const uint64_t digest = PfDigestV1(gray, width, height);\n'
              '  std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(s->pf_mu);\n'
              '  if (s->pf_result.valid && s->pf_result.digest == digest &&\n'
              '      s->pf_result.w == width && s->pf_result.h == height) {\n'
              '    *out = std::move(s->pf_result);\n'
              '    s->pf_result = {};\n'
              '    ++s->stat_pf_hits;\n'
              '    s->last_pf_wait_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    s->last_pf_state = 1;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (s->pf_job.valid && s->pf_job.digest == digest) {\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    s->pf_cv.wait(lk, [&] {\n'
              '      return s->pf_stop || (s->pf_result.valid && s->pf_result.digest == digest);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    if (s->pf_stop) return false;\n'
              '    *out = std::move(s->pf_result);\n'
              '    s->pf_result = {};\n'
              '    ++s->stat_pf_waits;\n'
              '    s->last_pf_wait_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    s->last_pf_state = 2;\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // 未命中(没预取过这张,或预取的是别帧):丢弃陈旧结果,借指针转发给\n'
              '  // 线程同步跑 —— 语义与内联相同,只是保持了载具的单线程所有权。\n'
              '  s->pf_result = {};\n'
              '  s->pf_job = {};\n'
              '  s->pf_job.borrow = gray;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.w = width;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.h = height;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.digest = digest;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.valid = true;\n'
              '  s->pf_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '  const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '  s->pf_cv.wait(lk, [&] {\n'
              '    return s->pf_stop || (s->pf_result.valid && s->pf_result.digest == digest);\n'
              '  });\n'
              '  if (s->pf_stop) return false;\n'
              '  *out = std::move(s->pf_result);\n'
              '  s->pf_result = {};\n'
              '  ++s->stat_pf_misses;\n'
              '  s->last_pf_wait_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '  s->last_pf_state = 3;\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'extern "C" int aether_sfm_prefetch_frame(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                         const uint8_t* gray, int width,\n'
              '                                         int height) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !gray || width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1;\n'
              '  if (!ExtractPrefetchEnabled()) return 1;\n'
              '  if (AbPeriod() > 0 &&\n'
              '      ((static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()) / AbPeriod()) & 1) == 1) {\n'
              '    return 1;  // [INTERLEAVED-AB] 对照臂帧不预取\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  PfEnsureThread(s);\n'
              '  if (!s->pf_thread.joinable()) return 1;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->pf_mu);\n'
              '  if (s->pf_job.valid) return 2;  // 深度 1:上一单还没被取走\n'
              '  s->pf_job.gray.assign(gray, gray + static_cast<size_t>(width) * height);\n'
              '  s->pf_job.borrow = nullptr;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.w = width;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.h = height;\n'
              '  s->pf_job.digest = PfDigestV1(s->pf_job.gray.data(), width, height);\n'
              '  s->pf_job.valid = true;\n'
              '  s->pf_cv.notify_all();\n'
              '  return 0;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_add_frame(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                         const uint8_t* gray, int width,\n'
              '                                         int height, float fx, float fy,\n'
              '                                         float cx, float cy,\n'
              '                                         const double pose_qwxyz[4],\n'
              '                                         const double pose_t[3],\n'
              '                                         int* out_frame_id) {\n'
              '  aether_preclamp_instr_v1::DiscardPending(\n'
              '      aether_preclamp_instr_v1::PendingDiscardReason::kSessionRejected);\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || !gray || width <= 0 || height <= 0) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // pose_qwxyz/pose_t = ARKit CamFromWorld (world->camera), quaternion [w,x,y,z],\n'
              '  // ARKit camera axes (+X right, +Y up, -Z forward). Consumed by the impl to\n'
              '  // build the throwaway live-preview cloud only; the authoritative finalize\n'
              '  // still self-estimates CamFromWorld (this prior does NOT feed the v1 solve).\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // [GPU-HANG-B1] add_frame 入口 Arm、所有出口(含异常)Disarm(RAII)。\n'
              '    aether_gpu_watchdog_v1::WatchdogV1::ArmScope watchdog_armed(\n'
              '        s->gpu_watchdog);\n'
              '    s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = false;\n'
              '    const int max_features =\n'
              '        s->options.max_features > 0 ? s->options.max_features : 2048;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // 1) DSP-SIFT extraction. use_gpu_extract routes to the GPU DSP-SIFT\n'
              '    //    (Dawn/WGSL, f16 on A16) which is output-equivalent (xy/128-d/UBC/\n'
              '    //    RootSIFT) and falls back to the CPU _threaded path IN-ABI on any GPU\n'
              '    //    failure — so this call site is unaware which path ran. Default = CPU.\n'
              '    //    [SCALE-PERSIST 2026-08-06] prefer the _v2 exits (extra per-keypoint\n'
              '    //    detection-scale output). GPU: gpu_v2 when the linked archive has it,\n'
              '    //    else the old symbol without scales (unit-affine persist — the\n'
              '    //    pre-change behaviour, so a new core over an old carrier only loses\n'
              '    //    the new column, never correctness). CPU: _v2 is in this same target\n'
              '    //    (official_dsp_sift_c.cc), always available.\n'
              '    std::vector<float> xy(static_cast<size_t>(max_features) * 2);\n'
              '    std::vector<uint8_t> desc(static_cast<size_t>(max_features) * 128);\n'
              '    std::vector<float> scales(static_cast<size_t>(max_features));\n'
              '    int n = 0;\n'
              '    const bool gpu_v2_avail = s->options.use_gpu_extract &&\n'
              '                              (aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu_v2 != nullptr);\n'
              '    const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '        s->options.use_gpu_extract && (aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu != nullptr);\n'
              '    // [EXTRACT-PREFETCH 2026-08-08] armed 时向流水线领取(命中/等尾/经线程\n'
              '    // 转发三态,输出与内联逐字等价);未启用(默认)走下面的原路,一字未动。\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t::PfResult pf_res;\n'
              '    const bool pf_adopted = PfAdoptOrRun(s, gray, width, height, &pf_res);\n'
              '    double extract_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '    int erc = 1;\n'
              '    bool have_scales = false;\n'
              '    if (pf_adopted) {\n'
              '      s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] 领取路径同样打点\n'
              '      xy = std::move(pf_res.xy);\n'
              '      desc = std::move(pf_res.desc);\n'
              '      scales = std::move(pf_res.scales);\n'
              '      n = pf_res.n;\n'
              '      erc = pf_res.erc;\n'
              '      have_scales = pf_res.have_scales;\n'
              '      extract_ms = pf_res.extract_ms;\n'
              '      std::memcpy(s->last_extract_stages, pf_res.stages,\n'
              '                  sizeof(s->last_extract_stages));\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '    const double t_extract0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] extract 调用前打点\n'
              '    if (gpu_v2_avail) {\n'
              '      erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu_v2(\n'
              '          gray, width, height, max_features, /*num_threads=*/0, xy.data(),\n'
              '          desc.data(), scales.data(), /*out_orientations=*/nullptr,\n'
              '          max_features, &n);\n'
              '      have_scales = (erc == 0);\n'
              '    } else if (gpu_avail) {\n'
              '      erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_gpu(gray, width, height, max_features,\n'
              '                                        /*num_threads=*/0, xy.data(),\n'
              '                                        desc.data(), max_features, &n);\n'
              '      have_scales = false;\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      erc = aether_dsp_sift_extract_v2(\n'
              '          gray, width, height, max_features, xy.data(), desc.data(),\n'
              '          scales.data(), /*out_orientations=*/nullptr, max_features, &n);\n'
              '      have_scales = (erc == 0);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    extract_ms = NowMs() - t_extract0;\n'
              '    s->gpu_watchdog.InProgress();  // [GPU-HANG-B1] extract 调用后打点\n'
              '    // [AETHER-T-EXTRACT] Stash the extractor\'s per-stage split for the\n'
              '    // frame_split record (zeroed when the CPU path / old archive ran).\n'
              '    for (double& v : s->last_extract_stages) v = 0.0;\n'
              '    if ((gpu_v2_avail || gpu_avail) && erc == 0 &&\n'
              '        aether_sed_last_stages != nullptr) {\n'
              '      aether_sed_last_stages(s->last_extract_stages, 9);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (gpu_v2_avail || gpu_avail) {\n'
              '      PullGpuTimestampAfterExtraction(s);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    }  // [EXTRACT-PREFETCH] 内联原路结束\n'
              '    if (erc != 0 || n <= 0) {\n'
              '      // Record the (possibly large) extract time even on failure so the log\n'
              '      // reflects a stalled/fallback extractor; zero the match-side counters.\n'
              '      s->last_extract_ms = extract_ms;\n'
              '      s->last_match_ms = 0.0;\n'
              '      s->last_n_cand = s->last_gpu_matches = s->last_cpu_matches = 0;\n'
              '      // [EXTRACT-SELFHEAL 2026-08-10] 失败原因落逐帧账(此前只进 stderr,\n'
              '      // 真机丢失 ⇒ 未命名(2) 的 GPU 瞬断无从定罪)。\n'
              '      {\n'
              '        const char* reason =\n'
              '            (aether_sed_last_fail_reason != nullptr)\n'
              '                ? aether_sed_last_fail_reason()\n'
              '                : "";\n'
              '        std::string sanitized(reason ? reason : "");\n'
              '        for (char& c : sanitized) {\n'
              '          if (c == \'"\' || c == \'\\\\\' || c == \'\\n\') c = \' \';\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        AppendMatchFailJsonl(\n'
              '            s, "{\\"t\\":" +\n'
              '                   std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(EpochMs())) +\n'
              '                   ",\\"type\\":\\"extract_fail\\",\\"fid\\":" +\n'
              '                   std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(s->frames.size())) +\n'
              '                   ",\\"erc\\":" +\n'
              '                   std::to_string(erc) + ",\\"n\\":" + std::to_string(n) +\n'
              '                   ",\\"ms\\":" + std::to_string(extract_ms) +\n'
              '                   ",\\"reason\\":\\"" + sanitized + "\\"}");\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_EXTRACT;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_result_t add_result = AddFrameFeaturesImpl(\n'
              '        s, xy.data(), desc.data(), have_scales ? scales.data() : nullptr, n,\n'
              '        width, height, fx, fy, cx, cy, pose_qwxyz, pose_t, extract_ms,\n'
              '        out_frame_id);\n'
              '    if (add_result != AETHER_SFM_OK) {\n'
              '      aether_preclamp_instr_v1::DiscardPending(\n'
              '          aether_preclamp_instr_v1::PendingDiscardReason::kSessionRejected);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return add_result;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    aether_preclamp_instr_v1::DiscardPending(\n'
              '        aether_preclamp_instr_v1::PendingDiscardReason::kSessionRejected);\n'
              '    // [DIAG 2026-07-26] This catch used to swallow the message, so a device\n'
              '    // capture that lost 25 consecutive frames to errInternal (every one of\n'
              '    // them surfaced to the user as a red "unconnected photo, reshoot nearby"\n'
              '    // card) left no evidence of WHAT threw. Log it: the reason is the whole\n'
              '    // difference between a genuine coverage problem and an internal fault.\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] add_frame threw: " << e.what();\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      // Also land it in the pull-able jsonl next to the db: detached device\n'
              '      // runs lose stderr/os_log, and this is exactly the forensic that was\n'
