              '      // missing.\n'
              '      char line[512];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '                    "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"add_frame_throw\\",\\"what\\":\\"%.300s\\"}",\n'
              '                    static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()), e.what());\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    aether_preclamp_instr_v1::DiscardPending(\n'
              '        aether_preclamp_instr_v1::PendingDiscardReason::kSessionRejected);\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] add_frame threw a non-std exception";\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Feature-injection sibling of aether_sfm_add_frame: skips extraction and\n'
              '// feeds precomputed keypoints (xy, +0.5 half-pixel convention, same as the\n'
              '// extractor output) + 128-d UBC RootSIFT u8 descriptors straight into the\n'
              '// shared streaming core. Purpose-built for HOST replay of a device capture\n'
              '// (keypoints/descriptors read back from a pulled sfm_live.db + the fed ARKit\n'
              '// poses) so streaming-path changes are verifiable off-device against real\n'
              '// captures. Production apps keep calling aether_sfm_add_frame.\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_add_frame_features(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t* s, const float* xy, const uint8_t* desc,\n'
              '    int n_keypoints, int width, int height, float fx, float fy, float cx,\n'
              '    float cy, const double pose_qwxyz[4], const double pose_t[3],\n'
              '    int* out_frame_id) {\n'
              '  aether_preclamp_instr_v1::DiscardPending(\n'
              '      aether_preclamp_instr_v1::PendingDiscardReason::kNonGpuRoute);\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || !xy || !desc || n_keypoints <= 0 || width <= 0 ||\n'
              '      height <= 0) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  s->gpu_timestamp_pending_frame_ready = false;\n'
              '  // [SCALE-PERSIST 2026-08-06] injection ABI carries no scales (host replay\n'
              '  // feeds xy+desc only) — scales=nullptr reproduces the unit-affine write.\n'
              '  return AddFrameFeaturesImpl(s, xy, desc, /*scales=*/nullptr, n_keypoints,\n'
              '                              width, height, fx, fy, cx, cy, pose_qwxyz,\n'
              '                              pose_t, /*extract_ms=*/0.0, out_frame_id);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [REMOVE-FRAME 2026-07-20] 用户删照片 → 撤回该帧的全部重建贡献。\n'
              '// 用户签决:"照片删了,那数据也必须删了"(拍虚/有人经过的照片产生的不良点云\n'
              '// 本来就该被纠正)。三步全部是 COLMAP 现成操作,本函数只做转发,零算法。\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_remove_frame(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                            int frame_id, char* out_json,\n'
              '                                            int out_cap) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // frame_id 是 add_frame 返回的序号 = s->frames 下标。\n'
              '    if (frame_id < 0 || frame_id >= static_cast<int>(s->frames.size())) {\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const colmap::image_t image_id = s->frames[frame_id].image_id;\n'
              '    if (image_id == 0) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '    // [WRITE-AHEAD 2026-08-04] Durably record the withdrawal BEFORE any\n'
              '    // destructive step. The snapshot write is the only fallible part of this\n'
              '    // function, and it used to run last — after the reconstruction had been\n'
              '    // de-registered and the db matches deleted. A failure there (or a kill in\n'
              '    // that window) left the sidecar claiming the frame was still active while\n'
              '    // its geometry was already gone, so recovery resurrected a deleted photo.\n'
              '    // Marking first is the safe direction: if the destructive half is\n'
              '    // interrupted, the frame stays withdrawn and the leftover match rows are\n'
              '    // inert (the frame is excluded from every candidate and rematch path).\n'
              '    if (!PersistArkitPoseSnapshotWithWithdrawal(*s, frame_id)) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] remove_frame aborted: could not persist the "\n'
              '                      "withdrawal marker for frame " << frame_id;\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Keep the portable visual-retrieval state consistent with the production\n'
              '    // frame lifecycle. Existing call-site checks remain defense in depth, but\n'
              '    // a removed frame is no longer scored or returned by the index itself.\n'
              '    s->visual_loop_index.RemoveFrame(frame_id);\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(s,\n'
              '                       aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kRemoveFrame);\n'
              '\n'
              '    size_t removed_obs = 0, deleted_points = 0, cleared_pairs = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ① 重建侧:ObservationManager::DeRegisterFrame 一次做完撤观测 + 删孤儿点\n'
              '    //    (track 只剩 2 元时整点删除,见 observation_manager.h 的文档)+ 维护\n'
              '    //    correspondence-graph 可见计数 + 取消注册。图以 trivial rig 加入,\n'
              '    //    frame_id == image_id(见 AddImageWithTrivialFrame 处注释)。\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon && s->live_recon_ready) {\n'
              '      const size_t pts_before = s->live_recon->NumPoints3D();\n'
              '      const colmap::frame_t fid = static_cast<colmap::frame_t>(image_id);\n'
              '      if (s->live_recon->ExistsFrame(fid) &&\n'
              '          s->live_recon->Frame(fid).HasPose()) {\n'
              '        if (s->live_recon->ExistsImage(image_id)) {\n'
              '          removed_obs = s->live_recon->Image(image_id).NumPoints3D();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        colmap::ObservationManager obs_mgr(*s->live_recon);\n'
              '        obs_mgr.DeRegisterFrame(fid);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const size_t pts_after = s->live_recon->NumPoints3D();\n'
              '      deleted_points = pts_before > pts_after ? pts_before - pts_after : 0;\n'
              '      // reg_order 是窗口 BA 的输入,必须同步剔除,否则窗口会引用已注销帧。\n'
              '      s->reg_order.erase(\n'
              '          std::remove(s->reg_order.begin(), s->reg_order.end(), image_id),\n'
              '          s->reg_order.end());\n'
              '      // [PAIR-DRAFT] 删帧后草稿的两图前提不再成立(2→1),无条件作废;\n'
              '      // 用户再拍下一张时 add_frame 会按当时的注册数重建。\n'
              '      s->draft_pair_points.clear();\n'
              '      s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.clear();\n'
              '      s->draft_pair_obs.clear();\n'
              '      // [REMOVE-REFRESH 2026-08-09] preview_points 快照此前只在 add_frame 末尾\n'
              '      // 刷新 —— 删除最后一张后没有下一次 add_frame,读快照的消费者会永远看到\n'
              '      // 删除前的云。此处与 add_frame 同款地重发快照(此刻 live_recon 已是\n'
              '      // 删除后的状态),空了也照发。\n'
              '      {\n'
              '        std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> snap;\n'
              '        snap.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '        for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) {\n'
              '          snap.push_back(pt.xyz);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->preview_mutex);\n'
              '        s->preview_points.swap(snap);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ② db 侧:COLMAP 没有"删单张图"接口(只有 ClearImages 清空全部),\n'
              '    //    官方支持的等价做法是把它孤立 —— 删掉所有涉及它的配对,任何 db 驱动\n'
              '    //    的重建(断点续跑 / refine 失败的 full-rerun 兜底)都无法再注册它。\n'
              '    //    图行留着但完全惰性;不写任何裸 SQL。\n'
              '    if (s->db) {\n'
              '      for (const auto& other : s->frames) {\n'
              '        if (other.image_id == 0 || other.image_id == image_id) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::image_t a = image_id, b = other.image_id;\n'
              '        // ExistsMatches 有,但没有对应的 ExistsTwoViewGeometry 查询接口;\n'
              '        // DeleteTwoViewGeometry 是 SQL DELETE,对不存在的行本就是空操作,\n'
              '        // 无条件调用即可。cleared_pairs 只统计确实存在过的原始匹配对。\n'
              '        if (s->db->ExistsMatches(a, b)) {\n'
              '          s->db->DeleteMatches(a, b);\n'
              '          ++cleared_pairs;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        s->db->DeleteTwoViewGeometry(a, b);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ③ 先使该帧的设备端描述子失效，再释放 host storage。这样相同\n'
              '    //    frame ordinal 不会在撤回后继续命中旧的 Metal buffer。\n'
              '#if AETHER_COMPILE_DESCRIPTOR_RESIDENCY_V1\n'
              '    if (aether_gpu_match_descriptor_residency_invalidate != nullptr &&\n'
              '        s->descriptor_residency_nonce != 0) {\n'
              '      aether_gpu_match_descriptor_residency_invalidate(\n'
              '          s->descriptor_residency_nonce, static_cast<uint32_t>(frame_id));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ④ 标记该帧已撤回,后续 add_frame 的候选选择不得再选它。\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].image_id = 0;\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].descriptors.clear();\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].descriptors.shrink_to_fit();\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].points.clear();\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].points.shrink_to_fit();\n'
              '    s->frames[frame_id].n_keypoints = 0;\n'
              '    // The withdrawal marker is already durable (write-ahead at the top of this\n'
              '    // function) and the in-memory mutation above reproduces exactly what that\n'
              '    // snapshot recorded, so there is deliberately no second persist here — a\n'
              '    // failure at this point would have nothing left to roll back.\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '      std::snprintf(\n'
              '          out_json, static_cast<size_t>(out_cap),\n'
              '          "{\\"removed_obs\\":%zu,\\"deleted_points\\":%zu,\\"cleared_pairs\\":%zu,"\n'
              '          "\\"n_registered\\":%zu,\\"n_points3d\\":%zu}",\n'
              '          removed_obs, deleted_points, cleared_pairs,\n'
              '          s->live_recon ? s->live_recon->NumRegImages() : 0,\n'
              '          s->live_recon ? s->live_recon->NumPoints3D() : 0);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] remove_frame failed: " << e.what();\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_finalize(aether_sfm_session_t* s, char* out_json,\n'
              '                                        int out_cap) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // [PAIR-DRAFT] 拍摄结束即作废草稿:finish 期任何 previewTracked 读取都\n'
              '    // 只能看到真模型,草稿绝不能被当成终态云交付。\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_points.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs.clear();\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kFinalizeMove);\n'
              '    if (!RebuildFrameRecordsForResume(s) || !s->live_recon_ready ||\n'
              '        !s->live_recon || s->live_recon->NumRegImages() < 2 ||\n'
              '        s->live_recon->NumPoints3D() == 0) {\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> work = std::move(s->live_recon);\n'
              '    s->live_recon_ready = false;\n'
              '    work->UpdatePoint3DErrors();\n'
              '    // The same known-pose refinement used by the async production route. It\n'
              '    // may triangulate and optimize, but it never calls RegisterNextImage or a\n'
              '    // P3P-based absolute-pose solver.\n'
              '    RefineGlobalBA(s, std::move(work), /*live_reuse=*/true);\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '      recon = s->recon;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!recon || s->finalize_status.load() != 2) {\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '      std::snprintf(out_json, out_cap,\n'
              '                    "{\\"solve_ms\\":%.1f,\\"n_models\\":1,"\n'
              '                    "\\"n_registered\\":%zu,\\"n_points3d\\":%zu,"\n'
              '                    "\\"reproj_px\\":%.4f,\\"track_len\\":%.3f,"\n'
              '                    "\\"phase1\\":\\"mandatory_arkit_reuse\\"}",\n'
              '                    NowMs() - t0, recon->NumRegImages(), recon->NumPoints3D(),\n'
              '                    recon->ComputeMeanReprojectionError(),\n'
              '                    recon->ComputeMeanTrackLength());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Two-phase async finalize. Phase 1 (this call, now milliseconds on the live\n'
              '// path): hand the capture-time live local-BA reconstruction to the refine\n'
              '// worker (status = LOCAL_READY). Phase 2 (background worker): finish-time db\n'
              '// enrichment (GPU) in parallel with a stage-1 refinement (CPU), then the\n'
              '// stage-2 Cauchy global BA + track completion over the enriched db; the\n'
              '// refined model is published into *recon (status = REFINED). On the live path\n'
              '// the getters serve nothing until REFINED (LOCAL is unpublished by sign-off);\n'
              '// on the resume path they serve the LOCAL model under the mutex as before.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// [S3.5 RESTORE 2026-07-11] The live streaming reconstruction IS the phase-1\n'
              '// result; finalize adds only the global refinement. Historically phase 1\n'
              '// re-ran the FULL IncrementalPipeline from the db (RunIncremental local_only)\n'
              '// because async finalize (2026-06-22, 5f7350ba) predates the live local-BA\n'
              '// recon (2026-07-09, 74d5f477) and was never rewired — on device that full\n'
              '// re-run alone cost 112-154 s (captures 42/43) and re-computed registrations\n'
              '// the live path already held in memory, occasionally SPLITTING the capture\n'
              '// into n_models=2 and dropping every frame of the smaller model (capture 43:\n'
              '// 96/128 vs live 128/128). Reusing the live recon:\n'
              '//   - phase 1 becomes a shared_ptr MOVE (instant; [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY] — the\n'
              '//     original S3.5 cut deep-copied here and again in the worker, +17%/+11%\n'
              '//     peak RSS on the host 42/43 A/B) — no duplicate reconstruction;\n'
              '//   - the model keeps ARKit-world gravity alignment + metric scale, and one\n'
              '//     connected model (pose-registered, never split by match topology);\n'
              '//   - the finalize-written db pairs (AddSpatialRevisitMatches +\n'
              '//     FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames) are consumed INCREMENTALLY by phase 2:\n'
              '//     RefineReconstruction\'s IterativeGlobalRefinement runs\n'
              '//     CompleteAndMergeTracks + Retriangulate over the enriched correspondence\n'
              '//     graph (track extension + new triangulations from the new pairs) before\n'
              '//     and between the Cauchy global BA rounds, and RestoreTemporalDetail\n'
              '//     consumes the re-matched temporal-window TVGs afterwards — nothing about\n'
              '//     phase 2 changed, it always worked this way on the phase-1 model.\n'
              '// RESUME sessions must restore the mandatory ARKit pose snapshot and rebuild a\n'
              '// registered live model. Missing or invalid pose identity blocks explicitly;\n'
              '// the historical db-driven P3P reconstruction fallback is forbidden.\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_finalize_async(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                              char* out_json, int out_cap) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  if (s->refine_thread.joinable()) s->refine_thread.join();  // drain prior run\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // [PAIR-DRAFT] 同步 finalize 同款:拍摄结束即作废草稿。\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_points.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.clear();\n'
              '    s->draft_pair_obs.clear();\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kFinalizeMove);\n'
              '    const double t_enter = NowMs();\n'
              '    if (!RebuildFrameRecordsForResume(s)) {\n'
              '      s->finalize_status.store(3);\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // ── Normal completion path: live recon in memory → reuse it. ──\n'
              '    // A degenerate restored model is blocked. It must never be repaired by\n'
              '    // silently discarding the mandatory ARKit pose contract.\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY + FINALIZE-OVERLAP 2026-07-11] Three changes vs the\n'
              '    // first S3.5 cut, all grounded on the host 42/43 A/B (RSS +17%/+11%, the\n'
              '    // 17-40 s GPU enrichment serial in front of the 30-47 s CPU phase 2):\n'
              '    //   1. The live recon is MOVED into the worker (shared_ptr transfer), not\n'
              '    //      deep-copied — and phase 2 refines it IN PLACE. Zero extra copies.\n'
              '    //   2. NOTHING is published at LOCAL_READY on this path (user signed off:\n'
              '    //      the LOCAL model is never displayed; the Dart worker only reads the\n'
              '    //      getters after REFINED). get_points/get_poses between LOCAL_READY\n'
              '    //      and REFINED now return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED here.\n'
              '    //   3. The finish-time db enrichment (spatial revisit + starved re-match,\n'
              '    //      GPU) moved INTO the worker, where it runs in parallel with the\n'
              '    //      stage-1 CPU refinement — see RefineGlobalBA. This call therefore\n'
              '    //      returns in milliseconds. Consequence: stream-stats read right\n'
              '    //      after this call no longer include the enrichment counters (they\n'
              '    //      are logged natively by the worker instead).\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon_ready && s->live_recon &&\n'
              '        s->live_recon->NumRegImages() >= 2 &&\n'
              '        s->live_recon->NumPoints3D() > 0) {\n'
              '      TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '          s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kFinalizeMove);\n'
              '      std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> work = std::move(s->live_recon);\n'
              '      s->live_recon.reset();          // moved-from shared_ptr is null already; be explicit\n'
              '      s->live_recon_ready = false;    // live getters now gate off\n'
              '      work->UpdatePoint3DErrors();    // live path fills errors lazily\n'
              '      // [LIVE-POSE-DUMP 2026-08-20] HOST-bench-only, env-gated (default off;\n'
              '      // the app never sets it): dump the exact end-of-capture live_recon\n'
              '      // (poses + points, COLMAP bin) BEFORE the worker refines it in place —\n'
              '      // after this call the pre-refine state is unrecoverable.\n'
              '      if (const char* dump_dir = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_POSE_DUMP");\n'
              '          dump_dir && dump_dir[0]) {\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          const std::string end_dir = std::string(dump_dir) + "/live_end";\n'
              '          std::filesystem::create_directories(end_dir);\n'
              '          work->Write(end_dir);\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] live pose dump failed: " << e.what();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '        std::snprintf(\n'
              '            out_json, out_cap,\n'
              '            "{\\"solve_ms\\":%.1f,\\"n_models\\":1,\\"n_registered\\":%zu,"\n'
              '            "\\"n_points3d\\":%zu,\\"reproj_px\\":%.4f,\\"track_len\\":%.3f,"\n'
              '            "\\"phase1\\":\\"live_reuse\\"}",\n'
              '            NowMs() - t_enter, work->NumRegImages(), work->NumPoints3D(),\n'
              '            work->ComputeMeanReprojectionError(),\n'
              '            work->ComputeMeanTrackLength());\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      {\n'
              '        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '        s->recon_manager.reset();  // no mapper manager on this path\n'
              '        s->recon.reset();          // LOCAL model intentionally unpublished\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->finalize_status.store(1);  // LOCAL_READY (worker refining)\n'
              '      s->refine_thread =\n'
              '          std::thread(RefineGlobalBA, s, std::move(work), /*live_reuse=*/true);\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    s->finalize_status.store(3);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    s->finalize_status.store(3);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Poll the background refinement: 0=IDLE, 1=LOCAL_READY (local live, refining),\n'
              '// 2=REFINED (global BA done, *recon swapped), 3=ERROR.\n'
              'int aether_sfm_finalize_status(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return 3;\n'
              '  return s->finalize_status.load();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── outputs ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_frame_health(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                            int32_t* out_valid_pairs, int max,\n'
              '                                            int* out_n) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  const int n = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '  const int m = n < max ? n : max;\n'
              '  if (out_valid_pairs) {\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {\n'
              '      // live_win_valid 由拍摄期匹配路径实时维护;越界(尚未落项)按 0 处理,\n'
              '      // 与 starved() 谓词的取值方式逐字一致。\n'
              '      out_valid_pairs[i] = i < static_cast<int>(s->live_win_valid.size())\n'
              '                               ? static_cast<int32_t>(s->live_win_valid[i])\n'
              '                               : 0;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (out_n) *out_n = m;\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_get_poses(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                         aether_sfm_pose_t* out_poses, int cap,\n'
              '                                         int* out_count) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !out_count) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '    recon = s->recon;  // stable snapshot; survives an async swap\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!recon) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  const auto& images = recon->Images();\n'
              '  int written = 0;\n'
              '  int total = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [image_id, image] : images) {\n'
              '    ++total;\n'
              '    if (out_poses && written < cap) {\n'
              '      aether_sfm_pose_t& p = out_poses[written];\n'
              '      // image_id is 1-based from WriteImage; expose 0-based frame index.\n'
              '      p.frame_id = static_cast<int>(image_id) - 1;\n'
              '      p.registered = image.HasPose() ? 1 : 0;\n'
              '      if (image.HasPose()) {\n'
              '        const colmap::Rigid3d c_from_w = image.CamFromWorld();\n'
              '        const Eigen::Quaterniond q = c_from_w.rotation();  // [4.0.4] member -> method\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[0] = q.w();\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[1] = q.x();\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[2] = q.y();\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[3] = q.z();\n'
              '        p.t[0] = c_from_w.translation().x();  // [4.0.4] member -> method\n'
              '        p.t[1] = c_from_w.translation().y();\n'
              '        p.t[2] = c_from_w.translation().z();\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[0] = 1.0;\n'
              '        p.qwxyz[1] = p.qwxyz[2] = p.qwxyz[3] = 0.0;\n'
              '        p.t[0] = p.t[1] = p.t[2] = 0.0;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ++written;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  *out_count = total;\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [DELIVER-TRI-ANGLE 2026-08-05 用户签决"3°签装机"] 交付层视差角过滤。\n'
              '// 现象:成品 3D viewer 里主体外围一圈散点壳(cap_1785934283657550 实测壳带\n'
              '// 1.3-2.0m 占 9.46%)。逐点元数据实测(host 重放,201 帧):三个候选判据里\n'
              '// 只有"track 内最大成对视差角"有判别力(AUC 0.647;track 长度 0.326 反向、\n'
              '// 重投影误差 0.456 无信号,SOR/半径滤波已判死——壳自撑 k=20 邻域)。\n'
              '// 阈值 3.0° 不是本项目自标定:AliceVision 官方默认 minAngleForTriangulation\n'
              '// =3.0°,OpenMVG 硬编码 2.0°,RC 的 ill flag("apical angle is smaller than\n'
              '// a minimal requirement")同口径。两臂真彩并排页用户肉眼终审后签 3°。\n'
              '// 语义:纯交付层(显示层≠求解层,同 08-04 AR 稳定性裁决)——只影响\n'
              '// s->recon 的两个 finalize 导出口(get_points / get_points_tracked);\n'
              '// points3D、BA、流式路径、live 预览(previewTracked sibling)、DB、resume\n'
              '// 一概不动。回滚 = env 设 0 或删本块。\n'
              '// ⚠️ 与"全量交付铁律"(ghost-mask 注释/feedback_no_downsampled_pointcloud_\n'
              '// delivery)的关系:该铁律禁的是降采样/性能性删点;本过滤是用户签决的质量\n'
              '// 性交付口径(RC/Metashape/COLMAP GUI 同做法)。若未来重开 GHOST_MASK\n'
              '// (E25-C 已停用),其 flag 序按全量模型枚举,与过滤后交付序不对齐,须一并改。\n'
              'double DeliverMinTriAngleDeg() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_DELIVER_MIN_TRI_ANGLE");\n'
              '    if (e && e[0]) return std::atof(e);  // <=0 关闭过滤\n'
              '    return 3.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// 判定:track 内任意两个观测相机对该点的夹角(顶角)达到阈值即通过。\n'
              '// min_cos = cos(阈值);夹角越大 cos 越小,所以判据是 dot <= min_cos。\n'
              '// 有效观测 <2(理论不该存在)按不通过处理——与标定用的 python 口径一致。\n'
              'static bool DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(const colmap::Reconstruction& recon,\n'
              '                                       const colmap::Point3D& point,\n'
              '                                       double min_cos) {\n'
              '  // track 长度 p50=2、p90=5,栈上小缓冲足够;超长 track 截断到 32 个观测\n'
              '  // (夹角最大的对极大概率已在其中;截断只可能漏杀不会误杀)。\n'
              '  constexpr int kMaxObs = 32;\n'
              '  Eigen::Vector3d dirs[kMaxObs];\n'
              '  int k = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '    if (k >= kMaxObs) break;\n'
              '    if (!recon.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '    const auto& image = recon.Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '    if (!image.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d v = image.ProjectionCenter() - point.xyz;\n'
              '    const double norm = v.norm();\n'
              '    if (!(norm > 0.0)) continue;\n'
              '    dirs[k++] = v / norm;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (k < 2) return false;\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {\n'
              '    for (int j = i + 1; j < k; ++j) {\n'
              '      if (dirs[i].dot(dirs[j]) <= min_cos) return true;  // 早退:一对够开即过\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return false;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [MIRROR-GHOST V1 2026-08-06 用户网页并排批准] 反光地板镜像鬼点过滤,算法\n'
              '// 单一事实源在 official_mirror_ghost.h(host parity 工具与产品共用同一份头,\n'
              '// cap5 逐点 52/52 一致)。跑在视差角过滤幸存者上,与两个交付入口同口径。\n'
              '// 开关:OFFICIAL_AETHER_MIRROR_GHOST=0 关闭。\n'
              'bool MirrorGhostEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_MIRROR_GHOST");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// 视差角幸存者集合上算判虚 point_id 集;date 每次交付调用重算(与视差角\n'
              '// 过滤同一"无状态、同口径"契约:count-only 与全量必然一致)。\n'
              'std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> MirrorGhostKillSet(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, const colmap::Reconstruction& recon, bool filter_on,\n'
              '    double min_cos) {\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> killed;\n'
              '  if (!MirrorGhostEnabled()) return killed;\n'
              '  const auto& pts = recon.Points3D();\n'
              '  std::vector<aether_mirror_ghost::P3> mp;\n'
              '  std::vector<colmap::point3D_t> ids;\n'
              '  mp.reserve(pts.size());\n'
              '  ids.reserve(pts.size());\n'
              '  for (const auto& [pid, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if (filter_on && !DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(recon, point, min_cos)) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    mp.push_back({static_cast<float>(point.xyz.x()),\n'
              '                  static_cast<float>(point.xyz.y()),\n'
              '                  static_cast<float>(point.xyz.z())});\n'
              '    ids.push_back(pid);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const auto res = aether_mirror_ghost::Detect(mp);\n'
              '  for (const uint32_t i : res.kill) killed.insert(ids[i]);\n'
              '  const int nk = static_cast<int>(killed.size());\n'
              '  if (s && nk != s->stat_mirror_ghost_logged) {\n'
              '    s->stat_mirror_ghost_logged = nk;\n'
              '    char line[256];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '                  "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"mirror_ghost_summary\\",\\"floor_y\\":%.3f,"\n'
              '                  "\\"candidates\\":%d,\\"clusters\\":%d,\\"killed_clusters\\":%d,"\n'
              '                  "\\"killed\\":%d,\\"guard\\":%d}",\n'
              '                  static_cast<long long>(NowMs()), res.floor_y, res.candidates,\n'
              '                  res.clusters, res.killed_clusters, nk, res.guard);\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return killed;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [ISOLATED-FLOATER 2026-08-07 用户签决装机] 孤立浮点过滤(最强安全版),算法\n'
              '// 单一事实源在 official_isolated_floater.h(host parity 工具与产品共用同一份\n'
              '// 头)。与镜像鬼点并列的第二把交付层刀:同样只跑在视差角幸存者上,两把刀的\n'
              '// kill 集取并,在 get_points / get_points_tracked 两个入口同口径生效。\n'
              '// 开关:OFFICIAL_AETHER_ISOLATED_FLOATER=0 关闭。\n'
              'bool IsolatedFloaterEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ISOLATED_FLOATER");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// 视差角幸存者集合上算判浮 point_id 集。每个点还要一条"平均视线":遍历\n'
              '// track 的 image_id → recon.Image(id).ProjectionCenter() → 单位化 (C − X)\n'
              '// 求均值再单位化。无有效观测 ⇒ 零向量,该点 cos 记 0(fail-safe:算不出\n'
              '// 方向就不按方向删)。注意这里**不做** DeliverPointPassesTriAngle 的 32 观测\n'
              '// 截断 —— 那个截断只是视差角判据的早退优化,平均视线要用全部观测。\n'
              'std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> IsolatedFloaterKillSet(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, const colmap::Reconstruction& recon, bool filter_on,\n'
              '    double min_cos) {\n'
              '  std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> killed;\n'
              '  if (!IsolatedFloaterEnabled()) return killed;\n'
              '  const auto& pts = recon.Points3D();\n'
              '  std::vector<aether_isolated_floater::P3> mp, vd;\n'
              '  std::vector<colmap::point3D_t> ids;\n'
              '  mp.reserve(pts.size());\n'
              '  vd.reserve(pts.size());\n'
              '  ids.reserve(pts.size());\n'
              '  for (const auto& [pid, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if (filter_on && !DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(recon, point, min_cos)) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Eigen::Vector3d acc = Eigen::Vector3d::Zero();\n'
              '    for (const auto& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '      if (!recon.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& image = recon.Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '      if (!image.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '      const Eigen::Vector3d v = image.ProjectionCenter() - point.xyz;\n'
              '      const double nrm = v.norm();\n'
              '      if (!(nrm > 0.0)) continue;\n'
              '      acc += v / nrm;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const double an = acc.norm();\n'
              '    aether_isolated_floater::P3 dir{0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};\n'
              '    if (an > 0.0) {\n'
              '      dir = {static_cast<float>(acc.x() / an), static_cast<float>(acc.y() / an),\n'
              '             static_cast<float>(acc.z() / an)};\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    mp.push_back({static_cast<float>(point.xyz.x()),\n'
              '                  static_cast<float>(point.xyz.y()),\n'
              '                  static_cast<float>(point.xyz.z())});\n'
              '    vd.push_back(dir);\n'
              '    ids.push_back(pid);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const auto res = aether_isolated_floater::Detect(mp, vd);\n'
              '  for (const uint32_t i : res.kill) killed.insert(ids[i]);\n'
              '  const int nk = static_cast<int>(killed.size());\n'
              '  if (s && nk != s->stat_isolated_floater_logged) {\n'
              '    s->stat_isolated_floater_logged = nk;\n'
              '    char line[256];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '                  "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"isolated_floater_summary\\","\n'
              '                  "\\"eps\\":%.6f,\\"main\\":%d,\\"clusters\\":%d,"\n'
              '                  "\\"killed_clusters\\":%d,\\"killed\\":%d}",\n'
              '                  static_cast<long long>(NowMs()), res.eps, res.main_size,\n'
              '                  res.clusters, res.killed_clusters, nk);\n'
              '    AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return killed;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// 两把交付层刀的 kill 集取并(get_points / get_points_tracked 共用,保证\n'
              '// 两个入口 count-only 与全量四处完全同口径)。\n'
              '// [DELIVER-KILL-CACHE] 缓存时限(ms),env 可调;0 = 关缓存(每次全量重算,\n'
              '// 恢复 08-08 前行为)。仅影响 live 预览新点的过滤延迟上限,不影响交付语义。\n'
              'double DeliverKillCacheMs() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_DELIVER_KILL_CACHE_MS");\n'
              '    if (e && e[0]) return std::atof(e);\n'
              '    return 2000.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> DeliverKillSet(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session* s, const colmap::Reconstruction& recon, bool filter_on,\n'
              '    double min_cos, int* out_mg, int* out_if) {\n'
              '  const double cache_ms = DeliverKillCacheMs();\n'
              '  const size_t npts = recon.Points3D().size();\n'
              '  if (s && cache_ms > 0.0) {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->deliver_kill_mutex);\n'
              '    // 地址相同还须点数相同:防旧快照析构后新对象落在同一地址(ABA)。\n'
              '    const bool same_snapshot =\n'
              '        (s->deliver_kill_recon == &recon) && (s->deliver_kill_npts == npts);\n'
              '    const double age = NowMs() - s->deliver_kill_ms;\n'
              '    const double drift =\n'
              '        s->deliver_kill_npts\n'
              '            ? std::fabs(static_cast<double>(npts) -\n'
              '                        static_cast<double>(s->deliver_kill_npts)) /\n'
              '                  static_cast<double>(s->deliver_kill_npts)\n'
              '            : 1.0;\n'
              '    if (s->deliver_kill_recon &&\n'
              '        (same_snapshot || (age < cache_ms && drift < 0.02))) {\n'
              '      if (out_mg) *out_mg = s->deliver_kill_mg;\n'
              '      if (out_if) *out_if = s->deliver_kill_if;\n'
              '      return s->deliver_kill_cache;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  auto kill = MirrorGhostKillSet(s, recon, filter_on, min_cos);\n'
              '  const int n_mg = static_cast<int>(kill.size());\n'
              '  if (out_mg) *out_mg = n_mg;\n'
              '  const auto fl = IsolatedFloaterKillSet(s, recon, filter_on, min_cos);\n'
              '  const int n_if = static_cast<int>(fl.size());\n'
              '  if (out_if) *out_if = n_if;\n'
              '  kill.insert(fl.begin(), fl.end());\n'
              '  if (s && cache_ms > 0.0) {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->deliver_kill_mutex);\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_cache = kill;\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_recon = &recon;\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_npts = npts;\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_ms = NowMs();\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_mg = n_mg;\n'
              '    s->deliver_kill_if = n_if;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return kill;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_get_points(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                          aether_sfm_point_t** out_points,\n'
              '                                          int* out_count) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !out_count) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '    recon = s->recon;  // stable snapshot; survives an async swap\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!recon) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  const auto& pts = recon->Points3D();\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [DELIVER-TRI-ANGLE] 交付层过滤;count-only 与全量查询必须同口径。\n'
              '  const double thr_deg = DeliverMinTriAngleDeg();\n'
              '  const bool filter_on = thr_deg > 0.0;\n'
              '  const double min_cos = std::cos(thr_deg * M_PI / 180.0);\n'
              '  int n_mg = 0, n_if = 0;\n'
              '  const auto mg_kill = DeliverKillSet(s, *recon, filter_on, min_cos, &n_mg, &n_if);\n'
              '\n'
              '  int n = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if ((!filter_on || DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(*recon, point, min_cos)) &&\n'
              '        !mg_kill.count(point_id)) {\n'
              '      ++n;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  *out_count = n;\n'
              '  if (!out_points) return AETHER_SFM_OK;  // count-only query\n'
              '\n'
              '  auto* arr = static_cast<aether_sfm_point_t*>(\n'
              '      std::malloc(static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(aether_sfm_point_t)));\n'
              '  if (!arr && n > 0) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  int i = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if (filter_on && !DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(*recon, point, min_cos)) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mg_kill.count(point_id)) continue;\n'
              '    aether_sfm_point_t& o = arr[i++];\n'
              '    o.x = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.x());\n'
              '    o.y = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.y());\n'
              '    o.z = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.z());\n'
              '    o.r = point.color(0);\n'
              '    o.g = point.color(1);\n'
              '    o.b = point.color(2);\n'
              '    o._pad[0] = o._pad[1] = 0;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (filter_on || !mg_kill.empty()) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] deliver-filter(get_points): kept " << n\n'
              '                 << "/" << pts.size() << " (min_tri_angle=" << thr_deg\n'
              '                 << "deg, mirror_ghost=" << n_mg << ", isolated_floater=" << n_if\n'
              '                 << ", killed_union=" << mg_kill.size() << ")";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  *out_points = arr;\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void aether_sfm_points_free(aether_sfm_point_t* points) {\n'
              '  std::free(points);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_get_points_tracked(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t* s, aether_sfm_point_t** out_points, int* out_count,\n'
              '    int32_t** out_obs_offsets, aether_sfm_track_obs_t** out_obs,\n'
              '    int64_t* out_obs_count) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !out_points || !out_count || !out_obs_offsets || !out_obs ||\n'
              '      !out_obs_count) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '    recon = s->recon;  // stable snapshot; survives an async swap\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!recon) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  const auto& pts = recon->Points3D();\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [DELIVER-TRI-ANGLE] 交付层过滤;与 get_points 完全同口径。\n'
              '  const double thr_deg = DeliverMinTriAngleDeg();\n'
              '  const bool filter_on = thr_deg > 0.0;\n'
              '  const double min_cos = std::cos(thr_deg * M_PI / 180.0);\n'
              '  int n_mg = 0, n_if = 0;\n'
              '  const auto mg_kill = DeliverKillSet(s, *recon, filter_on, min_cos, &n_mg, &n_if);\n'
              '\n'
              '  int n = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t total_obs = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if (filter_on && !DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(*recon, point, min_cos)) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mg_kill.count(point_id)) continue;\n'
              '    ++n;\n'
              '    total_obs += static_cast<int64_t>(point.track.Length());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  auto* arr = static_cast<aether_sfm_point_t*>(\n'
              '      std::malloc(static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(aether_sfm_point_t)));\n'
              '  auto* offs = static_cast<int32_t*>(\n'
              '      std::malloc((static_cast<size_t>(n) + 1) * sizeof(int32_t)));\n'
              '  auto* obs = static_cast<aether_sfm_track_obs_t*>(std::malloc(\n'
              '      static_cast<size_t>(total_obs) * sizeof(aether_sfm_track_obs_t)));\n'
              '  if ((n > 0 && (!arr || !offs)) || (total_obs > 0 && !obs)) {\n'
              '    std::free(arr);\n'
              '    std::free(offs);\n'
              '    std::free(obs);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int i = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t w = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    if (filter_on && !DeliverPointPassesTriAngle(*recon, point, min_cos)) {\n'
              '      continue;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mg_kill.count(point_id)) continue;\n'
              '    aether_sfm_point_t& o = arr[i];\n'
              '    o.x = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.x());\n'
              '    o.y = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.y());\n'
              '    o.z = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.z());\n'
              '    o.r = point.color(0);\n'
              '    o.g = point.color(1);\n'
              '    o.b = point.color(2);\n'
              '    o._pad[0] = o._pad[1] = 0;\n'
              '    offs[i] = static_cast<int32_t>(w);\n'
              '    for (const auto& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '      // Track elements always reference existing images in a valid model,\n'
              '      // but guard anyway — a dropped element only shortens this point\'s run.\n'
              '      if (!recon->ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& xy = recon->Image(el.image_id).Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy;\n'
              '      aether_sfm_track_obs_t& t = obs[w++];\n'
              '      // image_id is 1-based from WriteImage; frame_id mirrors get_poses.\n'
              '      t.frame_id = static_cast<int32_t>(el.image_id) - 1;\n'
              '      t.x = static_cast<float>(xy.x());\n'
              '      t.y = static_cast<float>(xy.y());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ++i;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  offs[n] = static_cast<int32_t>(w);\n'
              '  if (filter_on || !mg_kill.empty()) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] deliver-filter(get_points_tracked): kept "\n'
              '                 << n << "/" << pts.size() << " (min_tri_angle=" << thr_deg\n'
              '                 << "deg, mirror_ghost=" << n_mg << ", isolated_floater=" << n_if\n'
              '                 << ", killed_union=" << mg_kill.size() << ")";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  *out_points = arr;\n'
              '  *out_count = n;\n'
              '  *out_obs_offsets = offs;\n'
              '  *out_obs = obs;\n'
              '  *out_obs_count = w;\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void aether_sfm_track_obs_free(int32_t* offsets, aether_sfm_track_obs_t* obs) {\n'
              '  std::free(offsets);\n'
              '  std::free(obs);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Sibling of aether_sfm_get_points_tracked that reads the LIVE streaming\n'
              '// local-BA reconstruction (s->live_recon) instead of the finalize output\n'
              '// (s->recon), so the worker can true-color the streaming cloud through the SAME\n'
              '// colorize path. Identical output contract: points via aether_sfm_points_free,\n'
              '// offsets+obs via aether_sfm_track_obs_free.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// THREADING: live_recon is single-writer, owned by aether_sfm_add_frame on the\n'
              '// capture worker isolate, and — unlike s->recon — is NEVER swapped by an async\n'
              '// thread. This getter therefore takes NO lock and MUST be called on that same\n'
              '// worker isolate, serially between add_frame calls. If the Dart binding ever\n'
              '// calls it off that thread, wrap live_recon\'s mutation in add_frame AND this\n'
              '// read in a shared mutex (a torn read of a live Reconstruction is UB).\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_get_preview_tracked(\n'
              '    aether_sfm_session_t* s, aether_sfm_point_t** out_points, int* out_count,\n'
              '    int32_t** out_obs_offsets, aether_sfm_track_obs_t** out_obs,\n'
              '    int64_t* out_obs_count) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !out_points || !out_count || !out_obs_offsets || !out_obs ||\n'
              '      !out_obs_count) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!s->live_recon_ready || !s->live_recon) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const colmap::Reconstruction& recon = *s->live_recon;\n'
              '  const auto& pts = recon.Points3D();\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [PAIR-DRAFT 2026-08-09] live 模型还没有任何点(两帧阶段)而草稿非空时,\n'
              '  // 服务显示层临时配对云。第 3 帧起 live 模型出点 + add_frame 已清草稿,\n'
              '  // 这条兜底自动失效 —— 对既有行为的唯一改变是"原本返回 0 点的窗口期返回\n'
              '  // 草稿点"。同线程契约(worker isolate 串行),无锁。\n'
              '  if (pts.empty() && !s->draft_pair_points.empty()) {\n'
              '    const int dn = static_cast<int>(s->draft_pair_points.size());\n'
              '    const int64_t dobs = static_cast<int64_t>(s->draft_pair_obs.size());\n'
              '    auto* darr = static_cast<aether_sfm_point_t*>(\n'
              '        std::malloc(static_cast<size_t>(dn) * sizeof(aether_sfm_point_t)));\n'
              '    auto* doffs = static_cast<int32_t*>(\n'
              '        std::malloc((static_cast<size_t>(dn) + 1) * sizeof(int32_t)));\n'
              '    auto* dob = static_cast<aether_sfm_track_obs_t*>(std::malloc(\n'
              '        std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(dobs)) *\n'
              '        sizeof(aether_sfm_track_obs_t)));\n'
              '    if (!darr || !doffs || !dob) {\n'
              '      std::free(darr);\n'
              '      std::free(doffs);\n'
              '      std::free(dob);\n'
              '      return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    std::memcpy(darr, s->draft_pair_points.data(),\n'
              '                static_cast<size_t>(dn) * sizeof(aether_sfm_point_t));\n'
              '    std::memcpy(doffs, s->draft_pair_obs_offsets.data(),\n'
              '                (static_cast<size_t>(dn) + 1) * sizeof(int32_t));\n'
              '    if (dobs > 0) {\n'
              '      std::memcpy(dob, s->draft_pair_obs.data(),\n'
              '                  static_cast<size_t>(dobs) * sizeof(aether_sfm_track_obs_t));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    *out_points = darr;\n'
              '    *out_count = dn;\n'
              '    *out_obs_offsets = doffs;\n'
              '    *out_obs = dob;\n'
              '    *out_obs_count = dobs;\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const int n = static_cast<int>(pts.size());\n'
              '\n'
              '  int64_t total_obs = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts)\n'
              '    total_obs += static_cast<int64_t>(point.track.Length());\n'
              '\n'
              '  auto* arr = static_cast<aether_sfm_point_t*>(\n'
              '      std::malloc(static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(aether_sfm_point_t)));\n'
              '  auto* offs = static_cast<int32_t*>(\n'
              '      std::malloc((static_cast<size_t>(n) + 1) * sizeof(int32_t)));\n'
              '  auto* obs = static_cast<aether_sfm_track_obs_t*>(std::malloc(\n'
              '      static_cast<size_t>(total_obs) * sizeof(aether_sfm_track_obs_t)));\n'
              '  if ((n > 0 && (!arr || !offs)) || (total_obs > 0 && !obs)) {\n'
              '    std::free(arr);\n'
              '    std::free(offs);\n'
              '    std::free(obs);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int i = 0;\n'
              '  int64_t w = 0;\n'
              '  for (const auto& [point_id, point] : pts) {\n'
              '    aether_sfm_point_t& o = arr[i];\n'
              '    o.x = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.x());\n'
              '    o.y = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.y());\n'
              '    o.z = static_cast<float>(point.xyz.z());\n'
              '    o.r = point.color(0);\n'
              '    o.g = point.color(1);\n'
              '    o.b = point.color(2);\n'
              '    o._pad[0] = o._pad[1] = 0;\n'
              '    offs[i] = static_cast<int32_t>(w);\n'
              '    for (const auto& el : point.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '      if (!recon.ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '      const auto& xy = recon.Image(el.image_id).Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy;\n'
              '      aether_sfm_track_obs_t& t = obs[w++];\n'
              '      // image_id is 1-based from WriteImage; frame_id mirrors get_poses / get_points_tracked.\n'
              '      t.frame_id = static_cast<int32_t>(el.image_id) - 1;\n'
              '      t.x = static_cast<float>(xy.x());\n'
              '      t.y = static_cast<float>(xy.y());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ++i;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  offs[n] = static_cast<int32_t>(w);\n'
              '  *out_points = arr;\n'
              '  *out_count = n;\n'
              '  *out_obs_offsets = offs;\n'
              '  *out_obs = obs;\n'
              '  *out_obs_count = w;\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Official COLMAP iterative global refinement over the streaming model.\n'
              '// This is the inner solver used by the RS-style V20 / +10% outer schedule:\n'
              '// CompleteAndMergeTracks, Retriangulate, global BA, point filtering, and frame\n'
              '// filtering use the pinned COLMAP implementation and defaults. Per-image ARKit\n'
              '// [A1B-ASYNC-PVBA 2026-07-27, experiment arm, DEFAULT OFF] Async preview BA.\n'
              '// Provenance: [调查 lever A1b] the streaming-BA publish is a SERIAL\n'
              '// checkpoint inside the worker\'s frame event and its cost is superlinear in\n'
              '// registered poses (measured 495ms@20 → 9.5s@140 → 15.7s@145; ~40s\n'
              '// extrapolated at 300 frames — the single blocker for the signed 20-300\n'
              '// frame range). This arm keeps the SAME BA (same DatabaseCache options,\n'
              '// same IterativeGlobalRefinement arguments) but runs it on a SNAPSHOT COPY\n'
              '// on a background thread; the worker merges the refined result back at the\n'
              '// next publish trigger (poses for frames both sides know, xyz for point ids\n'
              '// both sides know — ids created/deleted by either side are left alone to\n'
              '// avoid id-collision hazards; finalize re-derives everything anyway).\n'
              '// Classification: NOISE-BAND (the live model receives each global\n'
              '// refinement a few frames late = shifted linearization points) — ships only\n'
              '// through the host matrix + sign-off, like every arm before it.\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA=1        master switch (default OFF)\n'
              '//   OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA_THREADS  bg ceres threads (default 2 —\n'
              '//                                             the worker keeps feeding)\n'
              'bool AsyncPreviewBaEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA");\n'
              '    return e && e[0] == \'1\';\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'int AsyncPreviewBaThreads() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_ASYNC_PREVIEW_BA_THREADS");\n'
              '    const int v = e ? std::atoi(e) : 2;\n'
              '    return v > 0 ? v : 2;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '// [A1B-V5] Propagate / cadence knobs are defined earlier in this TU (next to\n'
              '// TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks) because the finalize apvba_summary emit needs them.\n'
              '\n'
              '// [A1B-V2 WHOLESALE 2026-07-27] Worker-side harvest, whole-model variant.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// The v1 field-merge (adopt poses + xyz for ids both sides know) was killed\n'
              '// by its own matrix: it silently DISCARDED the background pass\'s deletion\n'
              '// decisions (FilterFrames / FilterPoints), so the live model kept growing on\n'
              '// frames the BA had already judged dead — cap3_eve delivered -5.65%/-6.33%\n'
              '// points against a 0.059% noise band, and the arm\'s own repeatability\n'
              '// (B<->B2 2.9% on 3+-view tracks) was worse than the band it had to fit in.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// This variant ADOPTS THE REFINED MODEL WHOLESALE — every BA decision,\n'
              '// deletions included — and then re-registers the frames the worker accepted\n'
              '// while the background pass was running, through the SAME official path\n'
              '// add_frame uses (per-image PINHOLE + trivial rig, AddImageWithTrivialFrame\n'
              '// with the ARKit pose, then IncrementalMapper::TriangulateImage over the db\n'
              '// matches). Nothing is hand-merged; the post-snapshot frames are simply\n'
              '// replayed onto the refined model exactly as they were first registered.\n'
              '// Their point ids are re-issued, which is fine: nothing outside the live\n'
              '// model keys on them (the finalize provenance census is telemetry).\n'
              'bool AdoptAsyncPreviewRefinement(aether_sfm_session* s,\n'
              '                                 std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> refined,\n'
              '                                 int snapshot_nframes) {\n'
              '  if (!refined) return false;\n'
              '  const int n_now = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '  // 1) Wholesale adopt (the whole point of this variant).\n'
              '  TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '      s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kModelReplacement);\n'
              '  s->live_recon = std::move(refined);\n'
              '  if (snapshot_nframes >= n_now) return true;  // nothing to replay\n'
              '  // [A1B-V4] Correction propagation (ORB-SLAM2\'s merge rule, adapted).\n'
              '  //\n'
              '  // The background pass moved the frames it optimized; the frames accepted\n'
              '  // while it ran still carry their raw ARKit poses. Replaying them as-is\n'
              '  // (v2/v3) leaves a SEAM: one side of the model is BA-corrected, the other\n'
              '  // is not, so their relative geometry is wrong by exactly the correction.\n'
              '  // ORB-SLAM2 solves this by "propagating the correction of updated\n'
              '  // keyframes (i.e. the transformation from the non-optimized to the\n'
              '  // optimized pose) to non-updated keyframes"; the reference is the\n'
              '  // spanning-tree parent (max shared observations), which in a sequential\n'
              '  // K-window capture is simply the newest frame the pass did optimize.\n'
              '  //\n'
              '  // Algebra (colmap Rigid3d, cam_from_world, A*B = apply B then A):\n'
              '  //   reference r has C_old (pre-BA) and C_new (post-BA)\n'
              '  //   world_new_from_world_old  S = C_new^-1 * C_old\n'
              '  //   a new frame\'s corrected pose  C\' = C * S^-1 = C * (C_old^-1 * C_new)\n'
              '  // Sanity: substituting the reference itself gives C_old * C_old^-1 * C_new\n'
              '  // = C_new, as it must.\n'
              '  std::optional<colmap::Rigid3d> world_old_from_world_new;\n'
              '  for (int i = AsyncPreviewBaPropagate() ? snapshot_nframes - 1 : -1; i >= 0;\n'
              '       --i) {\n'
              '    const colmap::frame_t fid = static_cast<colmap::frame_t>(\n'
              '        s->frames[i].image_id);  // trivial frames: frame_id == image_id\n'
              '    const auto snap_it = s->apvba_snapshot_poses.find(fid);\n'
              '    if (snap_it == s->apvba_snapshot_poses.end()) continue;\n'
              '    if (!s->live_recon->ExistsFrame(fid)) continue;  // filtered by the BA\n'
              '    const colmap::Frame& refined_ref = s->live_recon->Frame(fid);\n'
              '    if (!refined_ref.HasPose()) continue;\n'
              '    world_old_from_world_new =\n'
              '        colmap::Inverse(snap_it->second) * refined_ref.RigFromWorld();\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d t = world_old_from_world_new->translation();\n'
              '    s->stat_apvba_last_corr_mm = t.norm() * 1000.0;\n'
              '    const double qw = std::abs(world_old_from_world_new->rotation().w());\n'
              '    s->stat_apvba_last_corr_deg =\n'
              '        2.0 * std::acos(std::min(1.0, qw)) * 180.0 / M_PI;\n'
              '    break;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // 2) Replay the frames accepted during the background pass.\n'
              '  std::vector<colmap::image_t> replayed;\n'
              '  replayed.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(n_now - snapshot_nframes));\n'
              '  for (int i = snapshot_nframes; i < n_now; ++i) {\n'
              '    const FrameRecord& rec = s->frames[i];\n'
              '    if (!rec.has_pose || rec.image_id == 0) continue;\n'
              '    if (s->live_recon->ExistsImage(rec.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '    s->live_recon->AddCameraWithTrivialRig(rec.camera);\n'
              '    colmap::Image rimg;\n'
              '    rimg.SetImageId(rec.image_id);\n'
              '    char name[64];\n'
              '    std::snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "frame_%06d.jpg", rec.frame_id);\n'
              '    rimg.SetName(name);\n'
              '    rimg.SetCameraId(rec.camera_id);\n'
              '    rimg.SetPoints2D(rec.points);\n'
              '    // [A1B-V4] The ARKit pose carried into the BA\'s corrected world frame.\n'
              '    // Without this the frame lands in the pre-BA frame and the seam is the\n'
              '    // whole error. s->frames keeps the RAW ARKit prior untouched — it is the\n'
              '    // candidate-selection and gravity-align record, not a model pose.\n'
              '    const colmap::Rigid3d pose =\n'
              '        world_old_from_world_new\n'
              '            ? rec.cam_from_world * (*world_old_from_world_new)\n'
              '            : rec.cam_from_world;\n'
              '    s->live_recon->AddImageWithTrivialFrame(std::move(rimg), pose);\n'
              '    replayed.push_back(rec.image_id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (replayed.empty()) return true;\n'
              '  // 3) Official triangulation for the replayed frames (one mapper pass; the\n'
              '  //    matches are already in the db, so this is the same work add_frame did).\n'
              '  colmap::DatabaseCache::Options cache_options;\n'
              '  cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '  cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '  auto cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, cache_options);\n'
              '  colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions official_options;\n'
              '  official_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '  official_options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks =\n'
              '      TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '  official_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '  ApplyBirthGateAB(&official_options);  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07]\n'
              '  colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache);\n'
              '  mapper.BeginReconstruction(s->live_recon);\n'
              '  for (const colmap::image_t image_id : replayed) {\n'
              '    mapper.TriangulateImage(official_options.Triangulation(), image_id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '  return true;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '#if defined(AETHER_TAIL_CACHE_FAULT_TEST_HOOKS)\n'
              '// Host replay only. This symbol is intentionally absent from every default\n'
              '// and device build. It drives the real production invalidation paths without\n'
              '// mutating the source fixture, so the next bundle read must rebuild from the\n'
              '// session DB and can be compared with the fresh oracle.\n'
              'int aether_sfm_test_tail_cache_inject_v1(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int kind) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !TailCacheActive()) return -1;\n'
              '  if (kind == 1) {  // overwrite an already-seen pair\n'
              '    colmap::image_pair_t pair_id = 0;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '      if (s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs.empty()) return -2;\n'
              '      pair_id = *s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs.begin();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    const auto [image1, image2] = colmap::PairIdToImagePair(pair_id);\n'
              '    return TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, image1, image2) ? -3 : 0;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (kind == 2) {  // admit a previously unseen pair as a late write\n'
              '    colmap::image_t image1 = 0;\n'
              '    colmap::image_t image2 = 0;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s->tail_cache_mutex);\n'
              '      for (size_t i = 0; i < s->frames.size() && image1 == 0; ++i) {\n'
              '        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < s->frames.size(); ++j) {\n'
              '          const colmap::image_t a = s->frames[i].image_id;\n'
              '          const colmap::image_t b = s->frames[j].image_id;\n'
              '          if (a == 0 || b == 0 || a == b) continue;\n'
              '          const colmap::image_pair_t candidate =\n'
              '              colmap::ImagePairToPairId(a, b);\n'
              '          if (s->tail_cache_seen_db_pairs.count(candidate) == 0) {\n'
              '            image1 = a;\n'
              '            image2 = b;\n'
              '            break;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (image1 == 0 || image2 == 0) return -4;\n'
              '    return TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, image1, image2,\n'
              '                                   /*late_write=*/true)\n'
              '               ? 0\n'
              '               : -5;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (kind == 3) {  // exercise the real wholesale model-adoption path\n'
              '    if (!s->live_recon) return -6;\n'
              '    auto exact_copy =\n'
              '        std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>(*s->live_recon);\n'
              '    return AdoptAsyncPreviewRefinement(\n'
              '               s, std::move(exact_copy), static_cast<int>(s->frames.size()))\n'
              '               ? 0\n'
              '               : -7;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (kind == 4) {  // exercise the shared production exception-retry path\n'
              '    TailCacheMarkDirty(\n'
              '        s, aether::sfm::TailCacheDirtyReasonV1::kExceptionRetry);\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return -8;\n'
              '}\n'
              '#endif  // AETHER_TAIL_CACHE_FAULT_TEST_HOOKS\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t AsyncPreviewBaTick(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '  // 1) HARVEST a finished background run (worker thread owns live_recon, so\n'
              '  //    the merge is race-free by construction).\n'
              '  bool merged = false;\n'
              '  if (s->async_pvba_done.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {\n'
              '    std::shared_ptr<colmap::Reconstruction> refined;\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->async_pvba_mutex);\n'
              '      refined.swap(s->async_pvba_result);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (s->async_pvba_thread.joinable()) s->async_pvba_thread.join();\n'
              '    s->async_pvba_done.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '    s->async_pvba_running.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '    if (refined) {\n'
              '      const double t_m = NowMs();\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        merged = AdoptAsyncPreviewRefinement(s, std::move(refined),\n'
              '                                             s->apvba_snapshot_nframes);\n'
              '      } catch (...) {\n'
              '        // Adoption failed mid-replay: the live model may be the refined one\n'
              '        // without its replayed frames — those frames\' matches are all in the\n'
              '        // db, so finalize still sees them; count it and carry on.\n'
              '        merged = false;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->stat_apvba_merge_ms_total += NowMs() - t_m;\n'
              '      if (merged) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_apvba_merges;\n'
              '        s->apvba_consecutive_drops = 0;\n'
              '        // [A1B-V5] A successful harvest publishes, which resets the Dart\n'
              '        // publish policy\'s growth baselines — that is exactly the moment the\n'
              '        // sync arm would be allowed to start its next BA.\n'
              '        s->apvba_kick_armed = true;\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_apvba_dropped;\n'
              '        ++s->apvba_consecutive_drops;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_apvba_dropped;\n'
              '      ++s->apvba_consecutive_drops;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [A1B-V3] Back off after repeated drops instead of re-kicking every\n'
              '    // frame: 2 consecutive drops ⇒ skip the next 2 kick opportunities, 3+ ⇒\n'
              '    // skip 4. Breaks v2\'s failure→retry→contention→failure loop.\n'
              '    if (s->apvba_consecutive_drops >= 3) {\n'
              '      s->apvba_skip_kicks = 4;\n'
              '    } else if (s->apvba_consecutive_drops == 2) {\n'
              '      s->apvba_skip_kicks = 2;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  // 2) KICK a fresh run when idle: snapshot copy on the worker, solve on a\n'
              '  //    background thread with its OWN db read connection (WAL concurrent\n'
              '  //    reads are safe against the worker\'s writes).\n'
              '  if (s->apvba_skip_kicks > 0) {\n'
              '    --s->apvba_skip_kicks;  // [A1B-V3] backoff window\n'
              '  } else if (AsyncPreviewBaMatchCadence() && !s->apvba_kick_armed) {\n'
              '    // [A1B-V5] cadence-matched: one background pass per publish, no more.\n'
              '  } else if (!s->async_pvba_running.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      const double t_c = NowMs();\n'
              '      auto snap_model =\n'
              '          std::make_shared<colmap::Reconstruction>(*s->live_recon);\n'
              '      s->stat_apvba_copy_ms_total += NowMs() - t_c;\n'
              '      // [A1B-V2] Frames present at snapshot time; anything accepted after\n'
              '      // this index is replayed onto the refined model at harvest.\n'
              '      s->apvba_snapshot_nframes = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '      // [A1B-V4] Pre-BA poses for the correction propagation at harvest.\n'
              '      s->apvba_snapshot_poses.clear();\n'
              '      for (const auto& [fid, frame] : snap_model->Frames()) {\n'
              '        if (frame.HasPose()) s->apvba_snapshot_poses[fid] = frame.RigFromWorld();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      s->async_pvba_running.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '      s->apvba_kick_armed = false;  // [A1B-V5] consumed by this kick\n'
              '      ++s->stat_apvba_kicks;\n'
              '      // [A1B-V3 2026-07-27] The cache is built HERE, on the worker, from the\n'
              '      // worker\'s own db connection — the background thread never touches\n'
              '      // sqlite. v2 had it call Database::Open itself and the resulting lock\n'
              '      // contention with the worker\'s writes silently ate 4 frames\n'
              '      // (add_frame_features ... ERR_INTERNAL), which is a P0 against\n'
              '      // "采集必出点云". Build cost is the finalize-measured cache_pre\n'
              '      // (19-52 ms) and it is serialized with add_frame by construction.\n'
              '      colmap::DatabaseCache::Options kick_cache_options;\n'
              '      kick_cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '      kick_cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '      auto kick_cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db,\n'
              '                                                      kick_cache_options);\n'
              '      s->async_pvba_thread = std::thread([s, snap_model, kick_cache] {\n'
              '#if defined(__APPLE__)\n'
              '        pthread_set_qos_class_self_np(QOS_CLASS_UTILITY, 0);\n'
              '#endif\n'
              '        const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          auto cache = kick_cache;\n'
              '          colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions official_options;\n'
              '          official_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '          official_options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks =\n'
              '              TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '          official_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '          official_options.ba_refine_focal_length = false;\n'
              '          official_options.ba_refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '          official_options.ba_refine_extra_params = false;\n'
              '          official_options.mapper.ba_local_num_images = 6;\n'
              '          official_options.mapper.ba_global_ignore_redundant_points3D = true;\n'
              '          ApplyBirthGateAB(&official_options);  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07]\n'
              '          auto ba_opts = official_options.GlobalBundleAdjustment();\n'
              '          if (ba_opts.ceres) {\n'
              '            ba_opts.ceres->solver_options.num_threads = AsyncPreviewBaThreads();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache);\n'
              '          mapper.BeginReconstruction(snap_model);\n'
              '          if (AsyncPreviewBaParamOnly()) {\n'
              '            // [A1B-V6] parameters only: one official global solve, zero\n'
              '            // structural mutation (see the knob\'s provenance comment).\n'
              '            mapper.AdjustGlobalBundle(official_options.Mapper(), ba_opts);\n'
              '          } else {\n'
              '            mapper.IterativeGlobalRefinement(\n'
              '                official_options.ba_global_max_refinements,\n'
              '                official_options.ba_global_max_refinement_change,\n'
              '                official_options.Mapper(), ba_opts,\n'
              '                official_options.Triangulation(),\n'
              '                /*normalize_reconstruction=*/false);\n'
              '            mapper.FilterFrames(official_options.Mapper());\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '          snap_model->UpdatePoint3DErrors();\n'
              '          {\n'
              '            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->async_pvba_mutex);\n'
              '            s->async_pvba_result = snap_model;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '          // [A1B-V3] v2 swallowed the type: 8-11 of its drops had no evidence\n'
              '          // at all. Record it so a drop is diagnosable from the sidecar.\n'
              '          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->async_pvba_mutex);\n'
              '          s->apvba_last_error = e.what();\n'
              '        } catch (...) {\n'
              '          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->async_pvba_mutex);\n'
              '          s->apvba_last_error = "(non-std exception)";\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        s->stat_apvba_last_ba_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '        s->async_pvba_done.store(true, std::memory_order_release);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (...) {\n'
