              '      s->async_pvba_running.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!merged) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;  // Dart: "stable cloud\n'
              '                                                      // unchanged", no publish\n'
              '  // 3) Republish preview_points from the merged live model (sync-path tail).\n'
              '  std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> snap;\n'
              '  snap.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '  for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) snap.push_back(pt.xyz);\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->preview_mutex);\n'
              '    s->preview_points.swap(snap);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// PINHOLE intrinsics stay fixed; poses and 3D points remain free.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Refines live_recon in place and republishes preview_points atomically. On any\n'
              '// failure the previous stable AR cloud remains visible and the next frame retries.\n'
              '// THREADING: like get_preview_tracked, must run on the capture worker isolate\n'
              '// (mutates live_recon); the getter is called after this returns.\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_global_refine(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  if (!s->live_recon_ready || !s->live_recon ||\n'
              '      s->live_recon->NumRegImages() < 3)\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED;\n'
              '  // [A1B-ASYNC-PVBA 2026-07-27] Experiment arm (default OFF): kick/harvest\n'
              '  // instead of the in-line solve. Unset env ⇒ the sync body below runs\n'
              '  // byte-identically.\n'
              '  if (AsyncPreviewBaEnabled()) {\n'
              '    const aether_sfm_result_t result = AsyncPreviewBaTick(s);\n'
              '    if (result == AETHER_SFM_OK) {\n'
              '      AppendLiveCloudSnapshotDiagnosticsV2(s, "post_global_ba");\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return result;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    colmap::DatabaseCache::Options cache_options;\n'
              '    cache_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    cache_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '    auto cache = colmap::DatabaseCache::Create(*s->db, cache_options);\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalPipelineOptions official_options;\n'
              '    official_options.min_num_matches = 15;\n'
              '    official_options.triangulation.ignore_two_view_tracks =\n'
              '        TriIgnoreTwoViewTracks();\n'
              '    official_options.load_all_images = true;\n'
              '    official_options.ba_refine_focal_length = false;\n'
              '    official_options.ba_refine_principal_point = false;\n'
              '    official_options.ba_refine_extra_params = false;\n'
              '    official_options.mapper.ba_local_num_images = 6;\n'
              '    // [OOM-126 2026-07-26] Upstream\'s own global-BA size reducer, ON for the\n'
              '    // capture-time solve. IncrementalMapper::AdjustGlobalBundle (upstream\n'
              '    // sfm/incremental_mapper.cc:1131-1141, 1174-1181) then splits one whole-\n'
              '    // model problem into (a) a joint solve over the cameras plus only the\n'
              '    // non-redundant points — FindRedundantPoints3D drops points whose\n'
              '    // coverage gain is below the threshold — and (b) a second pass that\n'
              '    // optimizes the dropped points alone with every other parameter fixed.\n'
              '    // Nothing is discarded, so this is lossless by construction; it exists to\n'
              '    // keep the joint Jacobian / Schur workspace small, which is exactly the\n'
              '    // block that OOM-killed a 126-frame capture on device. Upstream gates it\n'
              '    // behind >=10 registered frames (kMinNumRegFramesForFastBA), and this\n'
              '    // solve only runs from 20 frames up, so the gate is always satisfied.\n'
              '    official_options.mapper.ba_global_ignore_redundant_points3D = true;\n'
              '    ApplyBirthGateAB(&official_options);  // [BIRTH-GATE-AB 2026-08-07]\n'
              '\n'
              '    colmap::IncrementalMapper mapper(cache);\n'
              '    mapper.BeginReconstruction(s->live_recon);\n'
              '    mapper.IterativeGlobalRefinement(\n'
              '        official_options.ba_global_max_refinements,\n'
              '        official_options.ba_global_max_refinement_change,\n'
              '        official_options.Mapper(), official_options.GlobalBundleAdjustment(),\n'
              '        official_options.Triangulation(),\n'
              '        /*normalize_reconstruction=*/false);\n'
              '    mapper.FilterFrames(official_options.Mapper());\n'
              '    mapper.EndReconstruction(/*discard=*/false);\n'
              '    s->live_recon->UpdatePoint3DErrors();\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Republish the collapsed cloud into preview_points.\n'
              '    std::vector<Eigen::Vector3d> snap;\n'
              '    snap.reserve(s->live_recon->NumPoints3D());\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) snap.push_back(pt.xyz);\n'
              '    {\n'
              '      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->preview_mutex);\n'
              '      s->preview_points.swap(snap);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    AppendLiveCloudSnapshotDiagnosticsV2(s, "post_global_ba");\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;  // keep the windowed cloud on failure\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Rough live-preview point cloud accumulated during capture (throwaway; built by\n'
              '// triangulating per-frame matches with the ARKit poses — NOT the authoritative\n'
              '// finalize model). World frame = ARKit world. Fills out_xyz with up to `cap`\n'
              '// points (cap*3 floats); *out_count = TOTAL available (may exceed cap — call once\n'
              '// with out_xyz=NULL to size, then again to fill).\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_get_preview_points(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                                  float* out_xyz, int cap,\n'
              '                                                  int* out_count) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !out_count) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->preview_mutex);\n'
              '  const int n = static_cast<int>(s->preview_points.size());\n'
              '  *out_count = n;\n'
              '  if (!out_xyz) return AETHER_SFM_OK;  // count-only sizing query\n'
              '  const int m = (n < cap) ? n : cap;\n'
              '  for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {\n'
              '    const Eigen::Vector3d& p = s->preview_points[i];\n'
              '    out_xyz[3 * i + 0] = static_cast<float>(p.x());\n'
              '    out_xyz[3 * i + 1] = static_cast<float>(p.y());\n'
              '    out_xyz[3 * i + 2] = static_cast<float>(p.z());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Per-frame timing/counters of the LAST aether_sfm_add_frame (perf diagnostics;\n'
              '// see aether_sfm_c.h). Pure read-back of the values add_frame stashes on the\n'
              '// session — the numbers behind the device log line\n'
              '//   extract=<extract_ms>ms match=<match_ms>ms cand=<n_cand> gpuM=<..> cpuM=<..>\n'
              '// Any out-ptr may be NULL. Safe before the first add_frame (fields zero-init).\n'
              'void aether_sfm_debug_last(aether_sfm_session_t* s, double* extract_ms,\n'
              '                           double* match_ms, int* n_cand, int* gpu_matches,\n'
              '                           int* cpu_matches) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (extract_ms) *extract_ms = s->last_extract_ms;\n'
              '  if (match_ms) *match_ms = s->last_match_ms;\n'
              '  if (n_cand) *n_cand = s->last_n_cand;\n'
              '  if (gpu_matches) *gpu_matches = s->last_gpu_matches;\n'
              '  if (cpu_matches) *cpu_matches = s->last_cpu_matches;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Cumulative streaming-quality counters over the whole capture (see the session\n'
              '// stat_* fields). Lets the worker log which floater filter did what:\n'
              '//   tvg_pairs/raw_pairs — geometric-inlier vs raw-fallback grow/create pairs\n'
              '//   grow_accepted/rejected — track-growth observations kept vs gated out\n'
              '//   reproj_filtered/tri_filtered — points/obs culled by each post-BA filter\n'
              '// Any out-ptr may be NULL. Safe before the first add_frame (fields zero-init).\n'
              'void aether_sfm_stream_stats(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int64_t* tvg_pairs,\n'
              '                             int64_t* raw_pairs, int64_t* grow_accepted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* grow_rejected, int64_t* reproj_filtered,\n'
              '                             int64_t* tri_filtered,\n'
              '                             int64_t* grow_reject_cheirality,\n'
              '                             int64_t* grow_reject_reproj,\n'
              '                             int64_t* create_reject_cheirality,\n'
              '                             int64_t* create_reject_tri_angle,\n'
              '                             int64_t* create_reject_reproj,\n'
              '                             int64_t* already_assigned,\n'
              '                             int64_t* merge_needed,\n'
              '                             int64_t* merge_accepted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* merge_rejected,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_considered,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_attempted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_written,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_inliers,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_anchor_attempted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_anchor_passed,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_regions_confirmed,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_expanded_attempted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_guided_pairs,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_guided_inliers,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_quadratic_attempted,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_quadratic_written,\n'
              '                             int64_t* spatial_budget_skipped,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_pairs,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_matches,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_created,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_grown,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_reject_cheirality,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_reject_reproj,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle,\n'
              '                             int64_t* temporal_detail_conflicts) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (tvg_pairs) *tvg_pairs = s->stat_tvg_inlier_pairs;\n'
              '  if (raw_pairs) *raw_pairs = s->stat_raw_pairs;\n'
              '  if (grow_accepted) *grow_accepted = s->stat_grow_accepted;\n'
              '  if (grow_rejected) *grow_rejected = s->stat_grow_rejected;\n'
              '  if (reproj_filtered) *reproj_filtered = s->stat_reproj_filtered;\n'
              '  if (tri_filtered) *tri_filtered = s->stat_tri_filtered;\n'
              '  if (grow_reject_cheirality)\n'
              '    *grow_reject_cheirality = s->stat_grow_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '  if (grow_reject_reproj) *grow_reject_reproj = s->stat_grow_reject_reproj;\n'
              '  if (create_reject_cheirality)\n'
              '    *create_reject_cheirality = s->stat_create_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '  if (create_reject_tri_angle)\n'
              '    *create_reject_tri_angle = s->stat_create_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '  if (create_reject_reproj)\n'
              '    *create_reject_reproj = s->stat_create_reject_reproj;\n'
              '  if (already_assigned) *already_assigned = s->stat_already_assigned;\n'
              '  if (merge_needed) *merge_needed = s->stat_merge_needed;\n'
              '  if (merge_accepted) *merge_accepted = s->stat_merge_accepted;\n'
              '  if (merge_rejected) *merge_rejected = s->stat_merge_rejected;\n'
              '  if (spatial_considered)\n'
              '    *spatial_considered = s->stat_spatial_pairs_considered;\n'
              '  if (spatial_attempted) *spatial_attempted = s->stat_spatial_pairs_attempted;\n'
              '  if (spatial_written) *spatial_written = s->stat_spatial_pairs_written;\n'
              '  if (spatial_inliers) *spatial_inliers = s->stat_spatial_inliers;\n'
              '  if (spatial_anchor_attempted)\n'
              '    *spatial_anchor_attempted = s->stat_spatial_anchor_attempted;\n'
              '  if (spatial_anchor_passed)\n'
              '    *spatial_anchor_passed = s->stat_spatial_anchor_passed;\n'
              '  if (spatial_regions_confirmed)\n'
              '    *spatial_regions_confirmed = s->stat_spatial_regions_confirmed;\n'
              '  if (spatial_expanded_attempted)\n'
              '    *spatial_expanded_attempted = s->stat_spatial_expanded_attempted;\n'
              '  if (spatial_guided_pairs)\n'
              '    *spatial_guided_pairs = s->stat_spatial_guided_pairs;\n'
              '  if (spatial_guided_inliers)\n'
              '    *spatial_guided_inliers = s->stat_spatial_guided_inliers;\n'
              '  if (spatial_quadratic_attempted)\n'
              '    *spatial_quadratic_attempted = s->stat_spatial_quadratic_attempted;\n'
              '  if (spatial_quadratic_written)\n'
              '    *spatial_quadratic_written = s->stat_spatial_quadratic_written;\n'
              '  if (spatial_budget_skipped)\n'
              '    *spatial_budget_skipped = s->stat_spatial_budget_skipped;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_pairs)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_pairs = s->stat_temporal_detail_pairs;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_matches)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_matches = s->stat_temporal_detail_matches;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_created)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_created = s->stat_temporal_detail_created;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_grown)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_grown = s->stat_temporal_detail_grown;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_reject_cheirality) {\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_reject_cheirality =\n'
              '        s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_cheirality;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_reject_reproj)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_reject_reproj = s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_reproj;\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle) {\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle =\n'
              '        s->stat_temporal_detail_reject_tri_angle;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (temporal_detail_conflicts)\n'
              '    *temporal_detail_conflicts = s->stat_temporal_detail_conflicts;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Live-recon quality snapshot + merge-gate reject reasons. Additive diagnostic\n'
              '// sibling of aether_sfm_stream_stats — same threading contract (call from the\n'
              '// add_frame worker thread; the live recon is single-writer). mean_reproj_px is\n'
              '// computed directly over every (point, observation) pair with the recon\'s own\n'
              '// (possibly BA-refined) camera, NOT from Point3D::error (never populated on\n'
              '// the live path).\n'
              'void aether_sfm_live_diag(aether_sfm_session_t* s, double* mean_reproj_px,\n'
              '                          int64_t* n_points, int64_t* n_track3plus,\n'
              '                          int64_t* n_obs, int64_t* merge_reject_shared_image,\n'
              '                          int64_t* merge_reject_reproj,\n'
              '                          int64_t* merge_reject_missing) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (merge_reject_shared_image)\n'
              '    *merge_reject_shared_image = s->stat_merge_reject_shared_image;\n'
              '  if (merge_reject_reproj) *merge_reject_reproj = s->stat_merge_reject_reproj;\n'
              '  if (merge_reject_missing)\n'
              '    *merge_reject_missing = s->stat_merge_reject_missing;\n'
              '  int64_t pts = 0, track3 = 0, obs = 0;\n'
              '  double err_sum = 0.0;\n'
              '  int64_t err_n = 0;\n'
              '  // [FINALIZE-ZEROCOPY 2026-07-11] finalize_async moves the live recon into\n'
              '  // the refine worker; after that this diagnostic reports zeros.\n'
              '  if (!s->live_recon) {\n'
              '    if (n_points) *n_points = 0;\n'
              '    if (n_track3plus) *n_track3plus = 0;\n'
              '    if (n_obs) *n_obs = 0;\n'
              '    if (mean_reproj_px) *mean_reproj_px = 0.0;\n'
              '    return;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : s->live_recon->Points3D()) {\n'
              '      ++pts;\n'
              '      const size_t len = pt.track.Length();\n'
              '      obs += static_cast<int64_t>(len);\n'
              '      if (len >= 3) ++track3;\n'
              '      for (const auto& el : pt.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        if (!s->live_recon->ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Image& image = s->live_recon->Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '        if (!image.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= image.NumPoints2D()) continue;\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam = image.CamFromWorld() * pt.xyz;\n'
              '        if (x_cam.z() <= 0.0) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Camera* cam = image.CameraPtr();\n'
              '        if (!cam) continue;\n'
              '        const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> px = cam->ImgFromCam(x_cam);\n'
              '        if (!px) continue;\n'
              '        err_sum += (*px - image.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy).norm();\n'
              '        ++err_n;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    // Diagnostic only — never throw across the ABI.\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (n_points) *n_points = pts;\n'
              '  if (n_track3plus) *n_track3plus = track3;\n'
              '  if (n_obs) *n_obs = obs;\n'
              '  if (mean_reproj_px) *mean_reproj_px = err_n > 0 ? err_sum / err_n : 0.0;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [SPATIAL-FIRST 2026-07-11] Capture-time candidate-selection attribution.\n'
              '// spatial_first_pairs = candidates chosen by the spatial K-NN ∩ view-angle\n'
              '// rule; temporal_fallback_pairs = candidates from the temporal fill (spatial\n'
              '// set short) or the full no-pose fallback. Sum = total pairs attempted by\n'
              '// add_frame over the capture. Same threading contract as\n'
              '// aether_sfm_stream_stats. Nullable; zero before the first add_frame.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_candidate_stats(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                int64_t* spatial_first_pairs,\n'
              '                                int64_t* temporal_fallback_pairs) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (spatial_first_pairs)\n'
              '    *spatial_first_pairs = s->stat_cand_spatial_first_pairs;\n'
              '  if (temporal_fallback_pairs)\n'
              '    *temporal_fallback_pairs = s->stat_cand_temporal_fallback_pairs;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [MATCH-FAIL TELEMETRY + FINALIZE-REMATCH 2026-07-11] Capture-time GPU\n'
              '// matcher failure accounting + the finalize starved-frame re-match counters.\n'
              '// Additive diagnostic sibling of aether_sfm_stream_stats (same threading\n'
              '// contract). gpu_fail_by_rc, if non-null, receives 8 int64 buckets indexed by\n'
              '// the pwofficial_gpu_match.mm return code (bucket 0 = out-of-range rc). Nullable.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_match_fail_stats(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* gpu_fail_total,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* gpu_fail_by_rc,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* gpu_fail_max_streak,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_starved_frames,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_candidates,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_attempted,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_written,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_inliers,\n'
              '                                 int64_t* rematch_failed) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (gpu_fail_total) *gpu_fail_total = s->stat_gpu_match_fail_total;\n'
              '  if (gpu_fail_by_rc) {\n'
              '    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) gpu_fail_by_rc[i] = s->stat_gpu_match_fail_by_rc[i];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (gpu_fail_max_streak)\n'
              '    *gpu_fail_max_streak = s->stat_gpu_match_fail_max_streak;\n'
              '  if (rematch_starved_frames)\n'
              '    *rematch_starved_frames = s->stat_finalize_rematch_starved_frames;\n'
              '  if (rematch_candidates)\n'
              '    *rematch_candidates = s->stat_finalize_rematch_candidates;\n'
              '  if (rematch_attempted)\n'
              '    *rematch_attempted = s->stat_finalize_rematch_attempted;\n'
              '  if (rematch_written) *rematch_written = s->stat_finalize_rematch_written;\n'
              '  if (rematch_inliers) *rematch_inliers = s->stat_finalize_rematch_inliers;\n'
              '  if (rematch_failed) *rematch_failed = s->stat_finalize_rematch_failed;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [THERMAL-THROTTLE 2026-07-11] Platform push of the ProcessInfo thermal\n'
              '// bucket (0 nominal · 1 fair · 2 serious · 3 critical). The Dart worker calls\n'
              '// this right before each add_frame; state >= 2 halves the live match\n'
              '// candidate window (see add_frame; env OFFICIAL_AETHER_LIVE_CAND_K_HOT tunes/disables).\n'
              '// Values outside [0,3] are treated as unknown and never throttle. Safe on any\n'
              '// thread; no-op on a null session.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_set_thermal_state(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int state) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  s->thermal_state.store((state >= 0 && state <= 3) ? state : -1,\n'
              '                         std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [THERMAL-THROTTLE 2026-07-11] Telemetry: frames fed with a reduced live K\n'
              '// this capture (0 = throttle never engaged). Same threading contract as\n'
              '// aether_sfm_stream_stats.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_thermal_throttle_stats(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                       int64_t* throttled_frames) {\n'
              '  if (throttled_frames) *throttled_frames = s ? s->stat_thermal_throttled_frames : 0;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [P1-LIVE-REPAY 2026-07-11] Capture-idle debt repayment. The worker calls\n'
              '// this from the SAME thread as add_frame whenever its queue has slack (offer\n'
              '// interval > processing time); the pass re-matches up to max_pairs missing\n'
              '// temporal-window pairs of currently-starved frames through the SAME matcher\n'
              '// route add_frame used, and persists them with the exact WriteMatches →\n'
              '// EstimateTwoViewGeometry → WriteTwoViewGeometry sequence — i.e. it prepays\n'
              '// the debt FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames would otherwise pay at ~410 ms/pair\n'
              '// on a hot finish-time GPU (cap46: 336 pairs → 137.9 s enrichment). A healthy\n'
              '// capture-time GPU pair costs ~16 ms, so repaying during idle windows is an\n'
              '// order of magnitude cheaper than repaying at finalize.\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Rules:\n'
              '//   - thermal critical (state >= 3) → refuses outright;\n'
              '//   - thermal serious (state == 2) → conditional repayment:\n'
              '//     [P1-REPAY-THERMAL2 2026-07-11 · PHASE-A 2026-07-12] the old ">= 2\n'
              '//     refuses outright" rule never repaid anything in practice — on cap47 the\n'
              '//     device sat at thermal=2 from min 2 of a 6.5-min capture (采集常态), so\n'
              '//     all 140 repay offers were refused and the whole debt hit the finalize\n'
              '//     re-match at ~410 ms/pair on a hot GPU. serious+idle with a HEALTHY\n'
              '//     matcher is exactly the cheap window; serious+struggling GPU is the\n'
              '//     freeze precondition (rc=7 forensic). Gate: allowed when the last\n'
              '//     kRepayThermal2CleanN(=4) GPU pairs all succeeded (gpu_pairs_since_rc7,\n'
              '//     rc=7 resets — i.e. recent no rc=7), budget clamped to\n'
              '//     kRepayThermal2MaxPairs(=8) per call, and ANY matcher failure aborts the\n'
              '//     pass on the spot (heat self-throttles the yield; failure is safe —\n'
              '//     healthy takes the full budget, struggling degrades to the old\n'
              '//     finalize-debt path). Kill switch: OFFICIAL_AETHER_REPAY_THERMAL2_CLEAN_N=0\n'
              '//     restores the old full refusal; N>0 overrides the clean-history length;\n'
              '//   - starved = the live win_valid mirror of the finalize rule (< 4 valid\n'
              '//     window pairs, or fed_throttled), candidates = missing window pairs\n'
              '//     (gap <= K, either side starved; gap <= 2 chain holes always),\n'
              '//     gap-ascending — identical topology to the finalize re-match;\n'
              '//   - each pair is attempted AT MOST once by repay (failed pairs are left\n'
              '//     for the finalize pass, which re-attempts anything still missing);\n'
              '//   - db-only: the pass never touches the live preview reconstruction, so\n'
              '//     the delivered model is identical whether a pair was repaid live or at\n'
              '//     finalize (same descriptors, same matcher, same write sequence).\n'
              '// Returns pairs written this call (0 = nothing to do / refused), -1 on bad\n'
              '// args. Counters via aether_sfm_repair_stats.\n'
              '// [P1-REPAY-THERMAL2] thermal=2 conditional-repay knobs.\n'
              '// [PHASE-A 2026-07-12] AGGRESSIVE-REPAY re-tuning (即时出云战役 Phase A):\n'
              '// the original 2-pair / 16-clean gate almost never fired — cap47 sat at\n'
              '// thermal=2 (采集常态) from min 2 of a 6.5-min capture, so the debt (112 s of\n'
              '// finalize re-match at ~410 ms/pair on a hot GPU) was never prepaid. serious +\n'
              '// a HEALTHY matcher (recent no rc=7) IS the cheap idle window; the freeze\n'
              '// precondition is serious + a STRUGGLING GPU (rc=7 forensic), which the\n'
              '// per-pair rc=7 reset + first-failure abort still catch. So Phase A widens the\n'
              '// per-call clamp 2→8 (~130 ms healthy GPU, still << the 2 s single-call red\n'
              '// line) and shortens the required clean run 16→4 (recent-no-rc=7 rather than a\n'
              '// long streak). MaxPairs is the per-call budget clamp at serious; CleanN is\n'
              '// the required healthy-GPU run since the last rc=7 (0 = thermal=2 refuses\n'
              '// outright, the old rule; env OFFICIAL_AETHER_REPAY_THERMAL2_CLEAN_N overrides).\n'
              'constexpr int kRepayThermal2MaxPairs = 8;\n'
              'int RepayThermal2CleanN() {\n'
              '  static const int cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_REPAY_THERMAL2_CLEAN_N")) {\n'
              '      const int v = std::atoi(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 4;  // recent-no-rc=7: ≥4 consecutive healthy GPU pairs since rc=7\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ── [QUAD-PREPAY 2026-07-26, signed] Capture-idle official quadratic prepay\n'
              '// OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUADRATIC_PREPAY=0 kills the prepay; the finalize\n'
              '// AddOfficialQuadraticPairs pass is the unconditional backstop either way\n'
              '// (it skips pairs that already exist in the db).\n'
              'static bool QuadraticPrepayEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_QUADRATIC_PREPAY");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Prepays the upstream quadratic-overlap pairs (i, i+2^k), 2^k > live K\n'
              '// window — the EXACT candidate set, matcher, ratio, and colmap-DEFAULT\n'
              '// TwoViewGeometry verification the finalize AddOfficialQuadraticPairs pass\n'
              '// would run at finish time, just executed earlier, in capture idle gaps\n'
              '// (the Dart facade only drives this when the frame queue is empty).\n'
              '// Union(prepaid, finalize backstop) is the same pair set with identical\n'
              '// per-pair results — descriptors are frozen at extraction — so finish-time\n'
              '// work shrinks without any data change. Writes are db-only: the live\n'
              '// in-memory mapper is not fed (same classification as the finalize\n'
              '// enrichment passes; the periodic capture-time global BA rebuilds its\n'
              '// DatabaseCache from the db, so prepaid long-range edges reach the preview\n'
              '// solve earlier — the signed FINALIZE-OVERLAP semantics, one stage earlier).\n'
              '// Returns matcher invocations consumed (0 = nothing due / disabled), so the\n'
              '// idle driver can chain calls while work remains.\n'
              'static int PrepayQuadraticTick(aether_sfm_session* s, int max_pairs) {\n'
              '  if (!QuadraticPrepayEnabled()) return 0;\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db || max_pairs <= 0) return 0;\n'
              '  const int overlap = OfficialQuadraticOverlap();\n'
              '  if (overlap <= 0 || s->camera_id == 0 || s->frames.size() < 2) return 0;\n'
              '  // Never add GPU load at thermal critical. Serious is normal operating\n'
              '  // condition for the paced matcher (cap_1785070530166049: K12 with 112\n'
              '  // serious frames, zero rc=7).\n'
              '  if (s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) >= 3) return 0;\n'
              '  const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '      s->options.use_gpu_match && (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs != nullptr);\n'
              '  // Fail-closed parity with the finalize pass: GPU requested but absent →\n'
              '  // never degrade into O(N²) CPU matching.\n'
              '  if (s->options.use_gpu_match && !gpu_avail) return 0;\n'
              '  int attempted = 0;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    // 1) Extend the due queue with newly arrived frames\' pairs. The live\n'
              '    //    window already matches gaps 1..K, so only 2^k > K is due here;\n'
              '    //    anything the live window missed is the finalize passes\' job.\n'
              '    const int K = s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12;\n'
              '    const int n = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    for (; s->prepay_scan < static_cast<size_t>(n); ++s->prepay_scan) {\n'
              '      const int j = static_cast<int>(s->prepay_scan);\n'
              '      for (int k = 0; k < overlap; ++k) {\n'
              '        const int64_t gap = 1ll << k;\n'
              '        if (gap <= K) continue;\n'
              '        if (gap > j) break;  // larger k ⇒ larger gap ⇒ also out of range\n'
              '        s->prepay_due.emplace_back(j - static_cast<int>(gap), j);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // 2) Attempt up to max_pairs (budget = matcher invocations). Pair\n'
              '    //    orientation, options, and persistence mirror the finalize pass\n'
              '    //    exactly.\n'
              '    const double ratio =\n'
              '        s->options.match_max_ratio > 0 ? s->options.match_max_ratio : 0.8;\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;  // colmap defaults\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf;\n'
              '    while (attempted < max_pairs && !s->prepay_due.empty()) {\n'
              '      const auto [i, j] = s->prepay_due.front();\n'
              '      s->prepay_due.pop_front();\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& fa = s->frames[i];\n'
              '      const FrameRecord& fb = s->frames[j];\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t img1 = fa.image_id;\n'
              '      const colmap::image_t img2 = fb.image_id;\n'
              '      if (img1 == 0 || img2 == 0 || img1 == img2) continue;\n'
              '      if (fa.descriptors.empty() || fb.descriptors.empty() ||\n'
              '          fa.n_keypoints <= 0 || fb.n_keypoints <= 0) {\n'
              '        continue;  // no in-memory features (resume-style) → finalize backstop\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (s->db->ExistsMatches(img1, img2) ||\n'
              '          s->db->ExistsTwoViewGeometry(img1, img2)) {\n'
              '        continue;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const int cap =\n'
              '          fa.n_keypoints < fb.n_keypoints ? fa.n_keypoints : fb.n_keypoints;\n'
              '      pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '      int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '      ++attempted;\n'
              '      ++s->stat_prepay_attempted;\n'
              '      // [P1-RC7-RETRY] transient rc=7 on a hot GPU gets two backoff retries.\n'
              '      const int mrc =\n'
              '          gpu_avail\n'
              '              ? GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                    s, fa.frame_id, fa.descriptors.data(), fa.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                    fb.frame_id, fb.descriptors.data(), fb.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                    pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches)\n'
              '              : aether_sift_match_pairs(fa.descriptors.data(), fa.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                                        fb.descriptors.data(), fb.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                                        ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap,\n'
              '                                        &num_matches);\n'
              '      if (mrc != 0) continue;          // struggling now: finalize backstop\n'
              '      if (num_matches <= 0) continue;  // legitimate zero-match pair\n'
              '      colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '      for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '        matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '          fa, fa.points, fb, fb.points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '      if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '      const bool tail_pair_first_write =\n'
              '          TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, img1, img2, /*late_write=*/true);\n'
              '      s->db->WriteMatches(img1, img2, matches);\n'
              '      s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(img1, img2, tvg.geometry);\n'
              '      TailCacheCommitPair(s, img1, img2, tvg.geometry,\n'
              '                          tail_pair_first_write);\n'
              '      ++s->stat_prepay_written;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    // Prepay is opportunistic — a failure must never take the worker down.\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] quadratic prepay aborted: " << e.what();\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] quadratic prepay aborted";\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return attempted;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [IDLE-PREPAY 2026-08-07] Root cause of stat_repay_calls == 0 in every\n'
              '// production capture: aether_sfm_live_repay (the only caller of the\n'
              '// starved-window repay below) short-circuits at the production official\n'
              '// endpoint into PrepayQuadraticTick and RETURNS — the starved-window pass\n'
              '// became unreachable by construction when [QUAD-PREPAY 2026-07-26] claimed\n'
              '// the idle channel. The quadratic debt is small (gaps 16/32/… only) and\n'
              '// drains in a few ticks, after which every remaining idle window was thrown\n'
              '// away while the starved-frame debt still hit FinalizeRematchStarvedFrames at\n'
              '// ~410 ms/pair on a hot finish-time GPU. Fix: once the quadratic tick reports\n'
              '// nothing due, spend the same (facade-verified: spool empty + nothing in\n'
              '// flight) idle window on the starved-window repay — but only when the worker\n'
              '// has ALSO been frame-idle for a while and the device is cool:\n'
              '//   - OFFICIAL_AETHER_IDLE_PREPAY=0 kills the leg (default ON);\n'
              '//   - frame-idle gate: NowMs() - last_add_frame_done_ms >\n'
              '//     OFFICIAL_AETHER_IDLE_PREPAY_IDLE_MS (default 2000 ms) — a queue that is\n'
              '//     momentarily empty between shutter frames does NOT count as idle, so the\n'
              '//     leg cannot add tail latency to a keyframe cadence that is keeping up;\n'
              '//   - thermal serious+ never prepays from this leg (counted via the existing\n'
              '//     stat_repay_skipped_thermal) — stricter than the legacy conditional\n'
              '//     clean-history rule, because idle prepay is pure opportunism;\n'
              '//   - per-call budget clamped to kRepayThermal2MaxPairs (= 8) pairs.\n'
              '// Sessions that never fed a frame (resume-from-db) keep\n'
              '// last_add_frame_done_ms == 0 and are refused outright — and the resume\n'
              '// bench never drives the repay channel at all, so resume output is unchanged\n'
              '// by construction.\n'
              'static bool IdlePrepayEnabled() {\n'
              '  static const bool cached = [] {\n'
              '    const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_IDLE_PREPAY");\n'
              '    return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              'static double IdlePrepayIdleMs() {\n'
              '  static const double cached = [] {\n'
              '    if (const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_IDLE_PREPAY_IDLE_MS")) {\n'
              '      const double v = std::atof(e);\n'
              '      if (v >= 0.0) return v;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 2000.0;\n'
              '  }();\n'
              '  return cached;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [IDLE-PREPAY 2026-08-07] The pre-existing starved-window repay body,\n'
              '// extracted verbatim from aether_sfm_live_repay so both the legacy endpoint\n'
              '// and the new idle-prepay leg share one implementation. Thermal rules,\n'
              '// counters, and per-pair semantics are unchanged.\n'
              'static int LiveRepayStarvedWindowTick(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int max_pairs) {\n'
              '  if (s->frames.size() < 3 || s->camera_id == 0) return 0;\n'
              '  const int thermal_entry =\n'
              '      s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '  bool thermal2_mode = false;\n'
              '  if (thermal_entry >= 3) {  // critical: never add GPU load\n'
              '    ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '    return 0;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (thermal_entry == 2) {\n'
              '    const int clean_n = RepayThermal2CleanN();\n'
              '    if (clean_n <= 0 || s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7 < clean_n) {\n'
              '      ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '      return 0;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    thermal2_mode = true;\n'
              '    max_pairs = std::min(max_pairs, kRepayThermal2MaxPairs);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  const bool gpu_avail =\n'
              '      s->options.use_gpu_match && (aether_gpu_match_gemm_pairs != nullptr);\n'
              '  // Fail-closed parity with the finalize pass: GPU requested but absent →\n'
              '  // never degrade into CPU brute force.\n'
              '  if (s->options.use_gpu_match && !gpu_avail) return 0;\n'
              '  ++s->stat_repay_calls;\n'
              '  int written = 0;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const int num_frames = static_cast<int>(s->frames.size());\n'
              '    const int K = s->options.k_neighbors > 0 ? s->options.k_neighbors : 12;\n'
              '    const auto starved = [&](int i) {\n'
              '      const int valid = i < static_cast<int>(s->live_win_valid.size())\n'
              '                            ? s->live_win_valid[i]\n'
              '                            : 0;\n'
              '      return s->frames[i].n_keypoints > 0 &&\n'
              '             (valid < kRematchMinValidWindowPairs ||\n'
              '              s->frames[i].fed_throttled);\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    const double ratio =\n'
              '        s->options.match_max_ratio > 0 ? s->options.match_max_ratio : 0.8;\n'
              '    const colmap::TwoViewGeometryOptions tvg_options;  // colmap defaults\n'
              '    std::vector<uint32_t> pair_buf;\n'
              '    int attempted = 0;\n'
              '    for (int gap = 1; gap <= K && attempted < max_pairs; ++gap) {\n'
              '      for (int f = gap; f < num_frames && attempted < max_pairs; ++f) {\n'
              '        const int j = f - gap;\n'
              '        if (gap > kRematchNearGap && !starved(f) && !starved(j)) continue;\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& fj = s->frames[j];\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& ff = s->frames[f];\n'
              '        if (fj.n_keypoints <= 0 || ff.n_keypoints <= 0) continue;\n'
              '        if (fj.descriptors.empty() || ff.descriptors.empty()) continue;\n'
              '        const uint64_t key = FramePairKey(f, j);\n'
              '        if (s->live_pairs_done.count(key)) continue;\n'
              '        if (s->db->ExistsMatches(fj.image_id, ff.image_id)) {\n'
              '          s->live_pairs_done.insert(key);\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // Mid-call thermal check: a state push can arrive between pairs.\n'
              '        // critical always aborts; a cool→serious transition aborts too (the\n'
              '        // thermal2 clean-history gate was not evaluated for this call) —\n'
              '        // only a call that ENTERED at serious keeps its small clamped budget.\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          const int th_now =\n'
              '              s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '          if (th_now >= 3 || (th_now >= 2 && !thermal2_mode)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '            return written;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ++attempted;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_attempted;\n'
              '        const int cap =\n'
              '            fj.n_keypoints < ff.n_keypoints ? fj.n_keypoints : ff.n_keypoints;\n'
              '        pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '        int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '        const int mrc =\n'
              '            gpu_avail\n'
              '                ? GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                      s, fj.frame_id, fj.descriptors.data(), fj.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                      ff.frame_id, ff.descriptors.data(), ff.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                      ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches)\n'
              '                : aether_sift_match_pairs(fj.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '                                          fj.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                                          ff.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '                                          ff.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                                          pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '        if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_repay_failed;\n'
              '          if (gpu_avail && mrc == 7) s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7 = 0;\n'
              '          // [P1-REPAY-THERMAL2] at serious, the FIRST struggling pair ends\n'
              '          // the pass — repay must never become the load that tips a hot GPU.\n'
              '          if (thermal2_mode) return written;\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (gpu_avail) ++s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7;\n'
              '        if (num_matches <= 0) continue;  // legitimate empty pair — resolved\n'
              '        colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '        for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '            fj, fj.points, ff, ff.points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '        if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '        const bool tail_pair_first_write =\n'
              '            TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, fj.image_id, ff.image_id,\n'
              '                                    /*late_write=*/true);\n'
              '        s->db->WriteMatches(fj.image_id, ff.image_id, matches);\n'
              '        s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(fj.image_id, ff.image_id, tvg.geometry);\n'
              '        TailCacheCommitPair(s, fj.image_id, ff.image_id, tvg.geometry,\n'
              '                            tail_pair_first_write);\n'
              '        s->live_pairs_done.insert(key);\n'
              '        ++written;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_written;\n'
              '        s->stat_repay_inliers +=\n'
              '            static_cast<int64_t>(tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '        if (static_cast<int>(tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size()) >=\n'
              '            kRematchValidInlierGate) {\n'
              '          if (static_cast<int>(s->live_win_valid.size()) <= f) {\n'
              '            s->live_win_valid.resize(f + 1, 0);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[j];\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[f];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [PROBE-DEBT 2026-08-08] Drain probe-skipped pairs with whatever budget\n'
              '    // the starved-window pass left. The ledger is the delivery-lossless hard\n'
              '    // line for the probe gate: skipped pairs on HEALTHY frames (and skipped\n'
              '    // spatial/loop pairs, whose index gap exceeds K) are invisible to the\n'
              '    // starved-window rule above, so they are drained here explicitly — same\n'
              '    // matcher route, same TVG gates, same persistence. A pair leaves the\n'
              '    // ledger after ONE completed (rc==0) full-match attempt regardless of\n'
              '    // outcome: that is exactly the single live attempt a gate-off run gave\n'
              '    // it (a legitimately dead pair is dead, not lost). rc!=0 keeps the debt\n'
              '    // for the finalize backstop. Sorted drain order keeps runs deterministic.\n'
              '    if (!s->probe_skipped_pairs.empty() && attempted < max_pairs) {\n'
              '      std::vector<uint64_t> debt(s->probe_skipped_pairs.begin(),\n'
              '                                 s->probe_skipped_pairs.end());\n'
              '      std::sort(debt.begin(), debt.end());\n'
              '      for (const uint64_t key : debt) {\n'
              '        if (attempted >= max_pairs) break;\n'
              '        const int f = static_cast<int>(key >> 32);\n'
              '        const int j = static_cast<int>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);\n'
              '        if (f < 0 || f >= num_frames || j < 0 || j >= num_frames) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // malformed — void the entry\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& fj = s->frames[j];\n'
              '        const FrameRecord& ff = s->frames[f];\n'
              '        if (fj.image_id == 0 || ff.image_id == 0) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // withdrawn frame → debt void\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (fj.n_keypoints <= 0 || ff.n_keypoints <= 0 ||\n'
              '            fj.descriptors.empty() || ff.descriptors.empty()) {\n'
              '          continue;  // no in-memory features → leave for the finalize pass\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (s->live_pairs_done.count(key) ||\n'
              '            s->db->ExistsMatches(fj.image_id, ff.image_id)) {\n'
              '          s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);  // already resolved elsewhere\n'
              '          s->live_pairs_done.insert(key);\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // Mid-call thermal check — identical to the starved-window loop.\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          const int th_now =\n'
              '              s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n'
              '          if (th_now >= 3 || (th_now >= 2 && !thermal2_mode)) {\n'
              '            ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '            return written;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        ++attempted;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_attempted;\n'
              '        const int cap =\n'
              '            fj.n_keypoints < ff.n_keypoints ? fj.n_keypoints : ff.n_keypoints;\n'
              '        pair_buf.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cap) * 2);\n'
              '        int num_matches = 0;\n'
              '        const int mrc =\n'
              '            gpu_avail\n'
              '                ? GpuMatchGemmPairsRetry(\n'
              '                      s, fj.frame_id, fj.descriptors.data(), fj.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                      ff.frame_id, ff.descriptors.data(), ff.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                      ratio, pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches)\n'
              '                : aether_sift_match_pairs(fj.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '                                          fj.n_keypoints,\n'
              '                                          ff.descriptors.data(),\n'
              '                                          ff.n_keypoints, ratio,\n'
              '                                          pair_buf.data(), cap, &num_matches);\n'
              '        if (mrc != 0) {\n'
              '          ++s->stat_repay_failed;\n'
              '          if (gpu_avail && mrc == 7) s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7 = 0;\n'
              '          if (thermal2_mode) return written;\n'
              '          continue;  // keep the debt — finalize backstop re-attempts it\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (gpu_avail) ++s->gpu_pairs_since_rc7;\n'
              '        s->probe_skipped_pairs.erase(key);\n'
              '        ++s->stat_probe_debt_repaid_live;\n'
              '        if (num_matches <= 0) continue;  // legitimate empty pair — settled\n'
              '        colmap::FeatureMatches matches(num_matches);\n'
              '        for (int m = 0; m < num_matches; ++m) {\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx1 = pair_buf[2 * m];\n'
              '          matches[m].point2D_idx2 = pair_buf[2 * m + 1];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        const auto tvg = EstimateMandatoryFrameTwoViewGeometry(\n'
              '            fj, fj.points, ff, ff.points, matches, tvg_options);\n'
              '        if (!MandatoryGravityTvgPersistable(tvg)) continue;\n'
              '        const bool tail_pair_first_write =\n'
              '            TailCacheBeginPairWrite(s, fj.image_id, ff.image_id,\n'
              '                                    /*late_write=*/true);\n'
              '        s->db->WriteMatches(fj.image_id, ff.image_id, matches);\n'
              '        s->db->WriteTwoViewGeometry(fj.image_id, ff.image_id, tvg.geometry);\n'
              '        TailCacheCommitPair(s, fj.image_id, ff.image_id, tvg.geometry,\n'
              '                            tail_pair_first_write);\n'
              '        s->live_pairs_done.insert(key);\n'
              '        ++written;\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_written;\n'
              '        s->stat_repay_inliers +=\n'
              '            static_cast<int64_t>(tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size());\n'
              '        // [PROBE-DEBT-GROW 2026-08-08] Parking site for the finalize leg.\n'
              '        //\n'
              '        // [LIVE-GROW-NOW 2026-08-08, user-signed "先去修空闲补账"] The original\n'
              '        // design parked EVERY repaid pair for one deterministic consumption site\n'
              '        // after the enrichment join, so the delivered cloud would not depend on\n'
              '        // capture idleness or thermal state. That traded the LIVE cloud away: a\n'
              '        // probe-skipped pair contributes no points to the AR preview for the\n'
              '        // whole capture, even after this idle pass has already matched and\n'
              '        // verified it. cap201 measures the cost at -1256 live points (-0.89%),\n'
              '        // and the product requirement is that the live cloud must NOT degrade —\n'
              '        // feeling coverage grow shot by shot is the point of the AR preview.\n'
              '        //\n'
              '        // With this arm armed, the idle repay grows the live model the moment\n'
              '        // the pair is verified, through the SAME entry, gates and parameters as\n'
              '        // both the live add_frame path and the finalize replay\n'
              '        // (GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers) — a point born here is\n'
              '        // indistinguishable from one born at finalize. The pair is then NOT\n'
              '        // parked, so it can never be grown twice.\n'
              '        //\n'
              '        // Declared cost: the delivered cloud becomes a function of how idle the\n'
              '        // capture was (this pass is budget-capped and returns early at thermal\n'
              '        // serious). That is a real loss of replay determinism, taken knowingly.\n'
              '        bool grown_live_now = false;\n'
              '        if (ProbeDebtGrowLiveEnabled() && s->live_recon_ready && s->live_recon &&\n'
              '            !tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.empty()) {\n'
              '          colmap::Reconstruction* recon = s->live_recon.get();\n'
              '          if (fj.has_pose && ff.has_pose && !fj.points.empty() &&\n'
              '              !ff.points.empty() && recon->ExistsImage(fj.image_id) &&\n'
              '              recon->ExistsImage(ff.image_id)) {\n'
              '            const colmap::Image& im1 = recon->Image(fj.image_id);\n'
              '            const colmap::Image& im2 = recon->Image(ff.image_id);\n'
              '            if (im1.HasPose() && im2.HasPose() &&\n'
              '                im1.NumPoints2D() == fj.points.size() &&\n'
              '                im2.NumPoints2D() == ff.points.size()) {\n'
              '              const LiveGrowView v1{fj.image_id, &fj.points, &fj.camera,\n'
              '                                    im1.CamFromWorld()};\n'
              '              const LiveGrowView v2{ff.image_id, &ff.points, &ff.camera,\n'
              '                                    im2.CamFromWorld()};\n'
              '              std::unordered_set<PointIdPair, PointIdPairHash> merge_trials;\n'
              '              std::unordered_set<colmap::point3D_t> touched;\n'
              '              GrowLiveTracksFromTvgInliers(\n'
              '                  s, recon, v1, v2, tvg.geometry.inlier_matches,\n'
              '                  LiveCreateTriMinAngleRad(), LiveCreateMaxReprojPx(),\n'
              '                  LiveGrowMaxReprojPx(), /*max_merge_reproj_px=*/8.0,\n'
              '                  /*merge_require_disjoint_images=*/false, ProbeDebtGrowMerge(),\n'
              '                  ProbeDebtGrowObs(), &merge_trials, &touched);\n'
              '              ++s->stat_probe_debt_grow_live_pairs;\n'
              '              grown_live_now = true;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!grown_live_now && !tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.empty()) {\n'
              '          s->probe_debt_grow.push_back(\n'
              '              ProbeDebtGrowPair{j, f, tvg.geometry.inlier_matches});\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (static_cast<int>(tvg.geometry.inlier_matches.size()) >=\n'
              '            kRematchValidInlierGate) {\n'
              '          if (static_cast<int>(s->live_win_valid.size()) <= f) {\n'
              '            s->live_win_valid.resize(f + 1, 0);\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[j];\n'
              '          ++s->live_win_valid[f];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return written;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] live repay aborted: " << e.what();\n'
              '    return written;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'int aether_sfm_live_repay(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int max_pairs) {\n'
              '  if (!s || !s->db) return -1;\n'
              '  if (max_pairs <= 0) return 0;\n'
              '  // [QUAD-PREPAY 2026-07-26, signed] At the official endpoint the idle\n'
              '  // channel performs the OFFICIAL quadratic-overlap prepay (pair generation\n'
              '  // + matching + colmap-default TVG, db-only — the same official-semantics\n'
              '  // classification that un-gated the quadratic and re-match passes) first.\n'
              '  if (kProductionOfficialEndpointOnly) {\n'
              '    const int consumed = PrepayQuadraticTick(s, max_pairs);\n'
              '    if (consumed > 0) return consumed;\n'
              '    // [IDLE-PREPAY 2026-08-07] Quadratic debt clear → same idle window pays\n'
              '    // the starved-window debt (rationale + gates: block comment above\n'
              '    // IdlePrepayEnabled). Default ON; OFFICIAL_AETHER_IDLE_PREPAY=0 kills.\n'
              '    if (!IdlePrepayEnabled()) return 0;\n'
              '    // [STARVED-ALWAYS 2026-08-14 用户签] 拍摄期断联补配对不再等空闲、不再避热。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // 为什么改:原设计要求"距上一帧处理完 >2000ms 的帧空闲 + 热档 < serious",\n'
              '    // 而真实拍摄里用户一直在按快门,2 秒空闲窗**根本凑不满** —— 两场真机遥测\n'
              '    // `idle_prepay_ticks=0 / repay_calls=0`,这条腿从未跑过。断联帧因此一路带到\n'
              '    // finalize 才补,而那时用户已经离开现场,补的是配对不是观测。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // 现在:只要有断联帧就在下一次匹配机会里补(预算见调用处)。\n'
              '    //   · OFFICIAL_AETHER_STARVED_ALWAYS=0 → 回到旧的空闲+热门(可一键回滚);\n'
              '    //   · 仍在 thermal critical(3)停手 —— 那一档系统自己会杀进程,\n'
              '    //     且内存里有"热压 GPU 丢命令导致相机冻结"的定罪记录;\n'
              '    //   · OFFICIAL_AETHER_STARVED_THERMAL_STOP 可改这道门(3=默认,4=永不停)。\n'
              '    const bool starvedAlways = [] {\n'
              '      const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STARVED_ALWAYS");\n'
              '      return !(e && e[0] == \'0\');  // 默认开\n'
              '    }();\n'
              '    const int thermalStop = [] {\n'
              '      const char* e = std::getenv("OFFICIAL_AETHER_STARVED_THERMAL_STOP");\n'
              '      const int v = e && e[0] ? std::atoi(e) : 3;\n'
              '      return v <= 0 ? 3 : v;\n'
              '    }();\n'
              '    if (!starvedAlways) {\n'
              '      if (s->last_add_frame_done_ms <= 0.0 ||\n'
              '          NowMs() - s->last_add_frame_done_ms < IdlePrepayIdleMs()) {\n'
              '        return 0;  // 旧路径:不是帧空闲就不碰匹配器\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) >= 2) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '        return 0;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      if (s->last_add_frame_done_ms <= 0.0) return 0;  // 从未喂帧(resume)不碰\n'
              '      if (s->thermal_state.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) >= thermalStop) {\n'
              '        ++s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '        return 0;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ++s->stat_idle_prepay_ticks;\n'
              '    const int64_t attempted_before = s->stat_repay_attempted;\n'
              '    const int64_t written_before = s->stat_repay_written;\n'
              '    const int n =\n'
              '        LiveRepayStarvedWindowTick(s, std::min(max_pairs,\n'
              '                                               kRepayThermal2MaxPairs));\n'
              '    if (s->stat_repay_attempted != attempted_before) {\n'
              '      char line[224];\n'
              '      std::snprintf(\n'
              '          line, sizeof(line),\n'
              '          "{\\"t\\":%lld,\\"type\\":\\"idle_prepay\\",\\"tick\\":%lld,"\n'
              '          "\\"attempted\\":%lld,\\"written\\":%lld,\\"cum_written\\":%lld}",\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(EpochMs()),\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(s->stat_idle_prepay_ticks),\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(s->stat_repay_attempted - attempted_before),\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(s->stat_repay_written - written_before),\n'
              '          static_cast<long long>(s->stat_repay_written));\n'
              '      AppendMatchFailJsonl(s, line);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return n;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return LiveRepayStarvedWindowTick(s, max_pairs);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [P1 2026-07-11] Finalize-speedup package counters: idle repay, rc=7 retry,\n'
              '// enrichment time-budget truncation. Same threading contract as\n'
              '// aether_sfm_stream_stats. All out-params nullable.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_repair_stats(aether_sfm_session_t* s, int64_t* repay_calls,\n'
              '                             int64_t* repay_attempted, int64_t* repay_written,\n'
              '                             int64_t* repay_inliers, int64_t* repay_failed,\n'
              '                             int64_t* repay_skipped_thermal,\n'
              '                             int64_t* gpu_retry_attempts,\n'
              '                             int64_t* gpu_retry_recovered,\n'
              '                             int64_t* enrich_budget_stopped) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  if (repay_calls) *repay_calls = s->stat_repay_calls;\n'
              '  if (repay_attempted) *repay_attempted = s->stat_repay_attempted;\n'
              '  if (repay_written) *repay_written = s->stat_repay_written;\n'
              '  if (repay_inliers) *repay_inliers = s->stat_repay_inliers;\n'
              '  if (repay_failed) *repay_failed = s->stat_repay_failed;\n'
              '  if (repay_skipped_thermal)\n'
              '    *repay_skipped_thermal = s->stat_repay_skipped_thermal;\n'
              '  if (gpu_retry_attempts) *gpu_retry_attempts = s->stat_gpu_retry_attempts;\n'
              '  if (gpu_retry_recovered) *gpu_retry_recovered = s->stat_gpu_retry_recovered;\n'
              '  if (enrich_budget_stopped)\n'
              '    *enrich_budget_stopped = s->stat_enrich_budget_stopped;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// [L1-ARBITRATE 2026-07-12] Ghost-layer L1 CasDiffMVS 1-bit arbitration —\n'
              '// fully FILE-driven over the session\'s run_dir: consumes the finalize-tail\n'
              '// sidecars (arbitration_plan.bin + arbitration_points.bin + ghost_mask.bin,\n'
              '// written when OFFICIAL_AETHER_GHOST_MASK=1) plus the per-ref depth bins the platform\n'
              '// CoreML runner wrote (l1_depth_<frameId>.bin), applies the calibrated\n'
              '// terminal rules (height-domain per-view votes, 3x3-patch median reads,\n'
              '// SUP>=2/SEE=0 hysteresis, mirror defense: below-floor evidence never\n'
              '// rescues, abstain -> visible), and rewrites ghost_mask.bin with the\n'
              '// kFlagL1Rescued / kFlagL1Confirmed bits + a ghost_arbitration.json stats\n'
              '// sidecar. The session is only the run_dir carrier — no reconstruction state\n'
              '// is touched, so this is safe on any thread once the runner finished.\n'
              '// Returns NOT_REGISTERED when the inputs are absent (plan not written /\n'
              '// runner never ran) — the caller treats that as a no-op, never an error.\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_arbitrate(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                         char* out_json, int out_cap) {\n'
              '  if (out_json && out_cap > 0) out_json[0] = \'\\0\';\n'
              '  if (!s) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    const double t0 = NowMs();\n'
              '    const std::string dir =\n'
              '        std::filesystem::path(s->db_path).parent_path().string();\n'
              '    aether_l1::ArbStats st;\n'
              '    std::string err;\n'
              '    if (!aether_l1::RunArbitration(dir, &st, &err)) {\n'
              '      LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_arbitrate: " << err;\n'
              '      const bool absent = err.find("missing") != std::string::npos;\n'
              '      return absent ? AETHER_SFM_ERR_NOT_REGISTERED : AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    st.pass_ms = NowMs() - t0;\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_arbitrate: refs=" << st.n_refs_used << "/"\n'
              '                 << st.n_refs_planned << " band=" << st.n_band\n'
              '                 << " rescue=" << st.n_rescue << " confirm=" << st.n_confirm\n'
              '                 << " abstain=" << st.n_abstain\n'
              '                 << " degraded=" << (st.degraded ? 1 : 0) << " ("\n'
              '                 << static_cast<int>(st.pass_ms) << "ms)";\n'
              '    if (out_json && out_cap > 0) {\n'
              '      std::snprintf(\n'
              '          out_json, static_cast<size_t>(out_cap),\n'
              '          "{\\"refs_planned\\":%d,\\"refs_used\\":%d,\\"n_band\\":%d,"\n'
              '          "\\"rescue\\":%d,\\"confirm\\":%d,\\"abstain\\":%d,"\n'
              '          "\\"delta_mm\\":%.3f,\\"delta_ray_mm\\":%.3f,\\"degraded\\":%s,"\n'
              '          "\\"pass_ms\\":%d}",\n'
              '          st.n_refs_planned, st.n_refs_used, st.n_band, st.n_rescue,\n'
              '          st.n_confirm, st.n_abstain, st.delta_mm, st.delta_ray_mm,\n'
              '          st.degraded ? "true" : "false", static_cast<int>(st.pass_ms));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] l1_arbitrate failed: " << e.what();\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  } catch (...) {\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Finalize-output quality snapshot: the aether_sfm_live_diag fields computed\n'
              '// over the CURRENT finalize reconstruction (LOCAL or REFINED — whichever the\n'
              '// getters serve). mean_reproj_px is computed over every observation with the\n'
              '// recon\'s own (BA-refined) camera. Zeros before finalize.\n'
              'void aether_sfm_final_diag(aether_sfm_session_t* s, double* mean_reproj_px,\n'
              '                           int64_t* n_points, int64_t* n_track3plus,\n'
              '                           int64_t* n_obs) {\n'
              '  if (mean_reproj_px) *mean_reproj_px = 0.0;\n'
              '  if (n_points) *n_points = 0;\n'
              '  if (n_track3plus) *n_track3plus = 0;\n'
              '  if (n_obs) *n_obs = 0;\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '    recon = s->recon;  // stable snapshot; survives the async LOCAL→REFINED swap\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!recon) return;\n'
              '  int64_t pts = 0, track3 = 0, obs = 0;\n'
              '  double err_sum = 0.0;\n'
              '  int64_t err_n = 0;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    for (const auto& [pid, pt] : recon->Points3D()) {\n'
              '      ++pts;\n'
              '      const size_t len = pt.track.Length();\n'
              '      obs += static_cast<int64_t>(len);\n'
              '      if (len >= 3) ++track3;\n'
              '      for (const auto& el : pt.track.Elements()) {\n'
              '        if (!recon->ExistsImage(el.image_id)) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Image& image = recon->Image(el.image_id);\n'
              '        if (!image.HasPose() || el.point2D_idx >= image.NumPoints2D()) continue;\n'
              '        const Eigen::Vector3d x_cam = image.CamFromWorld() * pt.xyz;\n'
              '        if (x_cam.z() <= 0.0) continue;\n'
              '        const colmap::Camera* cam = image.CameraPtr();\n'
              '        if (!cam) continue;\n'
              '        const std::optional<Eigen::Vector2d> px = cam->ImgFromCam(x_cam);\n'
              '        if (!px) continue;\n'
              '        err_sum += (*px - image.Point2D(el.point2D_idx).xy).norm();\n'
              '        ++err_n;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception&) {\n'
              '    // Diagnostic only — never throw across the ABI.\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (n_points) *n_points = pts;\n'
              '  if (n_track3plus) *n_track3plus = track3;\n'
              '  if (n_obs) *n_obs = obs;\n'
              '  if (mean_reproj_px) *mean_reproj_px = err_n > 0 ? err_sum / err_n : 0.0;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'aether_sfm_result_t aether_sfm_debug_dump_model(aether_sfm_session_t* s,\n'
              '                                                const char* dir) {\n'
              '  // [AETHER BA-MIXED A/B 2026-07-11] Debug/bench-only: write the current\n'
              '  // authoritative reconstruction as a COLMAP binary model\n'
              '  // (cameras.bin/images.bin/points3D.bin) so host A/B harnesses can score it\n'
              '  // with the pycolmap 9-gate scorer verbatim. Never called by the app.\n'
              '  if (!s || !dir || !dir[0]) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n'
              '  std::shared_ptr<const colmap::Reconstruction> recon;\n'
              '  {\n'
              '    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(s->recon_mutex);\n'
              '    recon = s->recon;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (!recon) return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  try {\n'
              '    std::filesystem::create_directories(dir);\n'
              '    recon->Write(dir);\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_OK;\n'
              '  } catch (const std::exception& e) {\n'
              '    LOG(WARNING) << "[aether_sfm] debug_dump_model failed: " << e.what();\n'
              '    return AETHER_SFM_ERR_INTERNAL;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'void aether_sfm_free(aether_sfm_session_t* s) {\n'
              '  if (!s) return;\n'
